### [70] [Hypergraph Partitioning on GPU with Distinct Incident Hyperedges and Size Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20497)
*Marco Ronzani,Cristina Silvano*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种针对具有特定约束的超图划分问题的GPU并行算法，通过利用高效的内存管理、并行集合运算和节点移动路径优化，在保持高质量的同时，实现了比传统任务划分器快数百倍的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超图划分是工程中的NP难问题，高效的大规模划分依赖并行处理；现有的方法在处理具有特殊约束（如有限大小和每个分区不同的入站超边）的超图时面临计算性能和并行效率的挑战。

Method: 1. 针对GPU的层次化并行结构设计算法；2. 将超图的关联结构和唯一邻域实例化到内存中，利用集合稀疏性并在共享内存中处理节点配对分数；3. 在分区细化阶段，将节点移动串联为路径和循环，通过并行的累积集合大小差异验证其有效性。

Result: 1. 与顺序多级划分器相比，平均加速比达到380倍，连接度降低了1.2-2.0倍；2. 支持k-way平衡划分，比CPU方法快5倍（k=2时质量损失约5%）；3. 在相近运行时间下优于现有的GPU划分器，且处理额外约束逻辑几乎没有带来测量开销。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种以GPU为中心的超图划分算法，通过利用层次化的并行性和针对特定约束的设计，实现了显著的性能提升和连接度优化，证明了其在解决特定约束下的超图划分问题上的高效性与可行性。

Abstract: Hypergraph partitioning is a recurring NP-hard problem in engineering; its efficient solution at scale hinges on parallelism. This work proposes a GPU-centric algorithm for multi-level hypergraph partitioning aimed at a specific set of problem constraints: limited size and distinct inbound hyperedges per partition. Manipulating hypergraphs requires deeply nested traversals and concurrent decision-making; our constraints impose further set operations amidst that. In turn, we design algorithms around the GPU's hierarchical parallelism and our problem's specifics. When forming partitions, we materialize the hypergraph's incidence structure and unique neighborhoods in memory to exploit set sparsity and batch node-pairing scores in shared memory. Upon refining partitions, we chain node moves into improving paths and cycles, checking their validity via cumulative set size variations reduced in parallel over moves. Thus, our dominant kernels exhibit a span linear in local hypergraph parameters. Results show an average 380x speedup and a 1.2-2.0x reduction in connectivity compared to a sequential multi-level partitioner. With minor changes, we also support k-way balanced partitioning, running 5x faster than CPU methods with a ~5% quality loss for k=2, outperforming an existing GPU partitioner at comparable runtime, with no measurable overhead from the added constraints handling logic.

</details>


### [71] [Cloud-Native Operation of Roadside Infrastructure Enabling Demand-Driven Collective Perception via V2X](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21145)
*Lukas Zanger,Fabian Thomsen,Guido Linden,Jean-Pierre Busch,Lennart Reiher,Lutz Eckstein*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Kubernetes的云原生按需编排架构，通过仅在车辆出现时动态启用路侧感知服务，显著提升了协同智能交通系统（C-ITS）的可扩展性与能源效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着路侧单元（RSU）数量增加和功能复杂化，传统固定式部署导致资源浪费、能耗增加、硬件损耗及频谱资源占用，亟需一种可扩展且高效的部署与运行管理方案。

Method: 提出并实现了一种结合边缘路侧单元（RSU）与中心云服务器的Kubernetes原生集群架构，并设计了一种“按需编排（demand-driven orchestration）”策略，实现资源密集型服务（如集体感知应用）仅在车辆接近时动态部署。

Result: 真实场景测试验证了该系统可在车辆行驶至服务区前及时启动感知应用；通过基于V2X记录的评估分析，证实了按需运行模式在降低能耗、缓解无线通信拥塞及减少硬件磨损方面的巨大潜力。

Conclusion: 基于云原生的按需协同感知架构在提高路侧基础设施的可扩展性、能源效率及资源利用率方面具有显著优势，是未来协同智能交通系统（C-ITS）的实用解决方案。

Abstract: Intelligent roadside infrastructure is a key enabler for cooperative intelligent transport systems (C-ITS), supporting vehicles equipped with automated driving systems (ADS), e.g., through enhanced environment perception. With a growing number and an expanding functional scope of roadside units, scalable and efficient operation becomes a challenge. This paper presents a cloud-native architecture for the operation of distributed roadside infrastructure based on a Kubernetes cluster spanning roadside units and a cloud server. Building on this architecture, a demand-driven orchestration approach is implemented to dynamically deploy resource-intensive services only when required. As a representative use case, a V2X-based collective perception application is deployed on-demand when a connected vehicle is nearby. The approach is validated in a real-world experiment in our test field in Aachen, demonstrating that the collective perception application starts in time for the vehicle to benefit from it. Without any demand, the application remains inactive, reducing energy consumption, channel congestion, and hardware wear. Beyond the primary evaluation, V2X recordings from the test field are analyzed to estimate the energy-saving potential of demand-driven operation. In summary, the results demonstrate the practical feasibility of cloud-native, demand-driven operation of roadside infrastructure and indicate its potential to improve scalability and (energy) efficiency in future C-ITS deployments.

</details>


### [72] [Llamas on the Web: Memory-Efficient, Performance-Portable, and Multi-Precision LLM Inference with WebGPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20706)
*Reese Levine,Rithik Sharma,Nikhil Jain,Abhijit Ramesh,Zheyuan Chen,Neha Abbas,James Contini,Tyler Sorensen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: LlamaWeb 是一个基于 WebGPU 的 llama.cpp 后端，通过创新的内存管理与内核优化，实现了在浏览器中高效、便携地运行大模型，其性能和内存效率显著优于现有前端方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在浏览器中运行大模型能提升 AI 应用的隐私性、便携性和易用性，但受限于浏览器严苛的内存配额和多样化的硬件环境。

Method: 1. 构建基于 WebGPU 的 llama.cpp 后端；2. 实施静态内存规划以减少内存开销；3. 开发可调节的内核库以适配异构硬件；4. 采用模板化 GPU 内核支持多种量化格式。

Result: 在 16 种设备上测试表现优异：相比其他 Web LLM 框架，内存使用率降低 29-33%，解码吞吐量提升 45-69%；在某些设备上其性能甚至超过了特定的厂家原生后端。

Conclusion: LlamaWeb 通过 WebGPU 有效解决了浏览器运行大模型面临的内存限制与硬件碎片化挑战，其内存管理和推理性能均优于现有的 Web LLM 框架，且在性能上可与原生后端竞争。

Abstract: Running language models in the browser presents a unique opportunity to build efficient, private, and portable AI applications, but requires contending with constrained memory availability and heterogeneous hardware targets. To realize this opportunity, we present Llamas on the Web (LlamaWeb), a WebGPU backend for llama.cpp that enables memory-efficient and performance-portable LLM inference across a wide range of model weight formats in the browser. Our design significantly reduces memory overhead through static memory planning and efficient model loading, addresses cross-device variability through a tunable kernel library, and introduces templated GPU kernels that support performant implementations of numerous quantization formats, enabling broad model support and extensibility to new formats.
  We evaluate LlamaWeb on 16 devices from 8 vendors, collecting data from 10 language models and four model weight formats. We compare LlamaWeb against existing browser-based LLM frameworks and find that LlamaWeb requires 29-33% less memory across several combinations of device, browser, and operating system. We also evaluate LlamaWeb's performance against these frameworks and find that it increases decode throughput by 45-69% across four GPUs from separate vendors. In addition, we compare LlamaWeb's performance against other llama.cpp backends, where it is competitive with and even beats vendor-specific backend performance on some devices.

</details>


### [73] [PlexRL: Cluster-Level Orchestration of Serviceized LLM Execution for RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20863)
*Yiqi Zhang,Fangzheng Jiao,Tian Tang,Boyu Tian,Hangyu Wang,Qiaoling Chen,Guoteng Wang,Zhen Jiang,Peng Sun,Ping Zhang,Xiaohe Hu,Ziming Liu,Menghao Zhang,Yanmin Jia,Yang You,Siyuan Feng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: PlexRL 是一个集群级运行时系统，通过将多个 RLVR 作业的时间片多路复用在同一组 GPU 上，解决了 RLVR 训练中结构性的资源闲置问题，在大幅降低成本的同时提升了集群利用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR 训练中存在结构性的低效问题（如长尾 Rollout、工具调用导致的停顿及资源需求不对称），导致单个作业内存在大量难以通过局部优化消除的空闲时间。

Method: 提出 PlexRL，这是一个集群级别的运行时系统，通过集中管理模型放置、状态转换和函数级调度，在严格的亲和性约束下实现跨作业的 LLM 执行时间切片（Time-slicing），从而避免昂贵的模型迁移并填补空闲期。

Result: 实验结果显示，PlexRL 显著提高了集群有效容量，并将用户 GPU 使用成本降低了最高 37.58%。

Conclusion: PlexRL 通过多路复用（Multiplexing）技术，在集群层面有效填补了 RLVR 训练中的资源空隙，在保持算法灵活性和低开销的前提下，显著提升了集群利用率并降低了计算成本。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has recently unlocked strong reasoning capabilities in large language models (LLMs), triggering rapid exploration of new algorithms and data. However, RLVR training is notoriously inefficient: long-tailed rollouts, tool-induced stalls, and asymmetric resource requirements between rollout and training introduce substantial idle time that cannot be eliminated by job-local optimizations such as synchronous pipelining, asynchronous rollout, or colocated execution.
  We argue that this inefficiency is structural. While idle gaps are unavoidable within individual RLVR jobs, they are largely anti-correlated across jobs and therefore exploitable at the cluster level. Leveraging this observation, we present PlexRL, a cluster-level runtime for multiplexing unified LLM services across RLVR jobs. By centrally managing model placement, state transitions, and function-level scheduling under strict affinity constraints, PlexRL time-slices LLM execution across jobs to fill otherwise idle periods without expensive model migration. Our implementation and evaluations demonstrate that PlexRL significantly improves effective cluster capacity and reduces user GPU hour cost by maximum 37.58% while preserving algorithmic flexibility and introducing minimal per-job overhead.

</details>


### [74] [Diagnosing Overhead in Dispatch Operations: Cross-architecture Observatory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20982)
*Bole Ma,Jan Eitzinger,Harald Koestler,Gerhard Wellein*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文通过DODOCO测试证实，当前MoE互联优化的核心理论基础（系统层可修复偏差、模拟Token有效）在真实生产场景下失效；研究发现不同架构对通信路由影响深远，应按架构特性分类进行互联设计，而非仅依赖EP规模调整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型中的AlltoAll通信是瓶颈，现有针对该瓶颈的四类优化方案均基于“路由不平衡可由系统修复”及“模拟Token基准测试准确”这两大假设，作者旨在测试这些假设的有效性。

Method: 开发了DODOCO测试工具；基于五个不同架构的MoE检查点（涵盖MLA、MHA、GQA、Mamba-2、GDN），在多种数据条件和专家并行（EP）规模（4-32 rank）下，在H100集群上进行实测，对比分析真实数据与模拟Token的表现。

Result: 1. 现有的两大假设均被证伪：EP规模对负载均衡的影响极小（路由不平衡本质上源于模型决策）；模拟Token大幅高估了路由基尼系数（Gini）并虚构了扩展趋势。2. 发现了架构依赖模式：MHA与Mamba-2表现出较高的数据弹性（基尼系数较低），而MLA与GDN表现出持续的集中性（基尼系数较高）。

Conclusion: 现有的MoE通信优化假设（路由不平衡可由系统层修正、模拟Token基准测试有效）是错误的。MoE架构本质上分为“数据弹性”和“持续集中”两类，这种架构设计才是互联带宽规划和AlltoAll调度设计的核心依据。

Abstract: AlltoAll dispatch is the dominant bottleneck of MoE expert parallelism, and the interconnect community has responded with four families of mitigations: predictive sample placement, adaptive expert relayout, hierarchical collectives, and EP-aware topology. All four rest on two assumptions about the workload. The first is that routing imbalance is correctable by the system layer. The second is that the mock-token benchmarks evaluating them faithfully represent production routing. We introduce DODOCO to test both assumptions.
  We instrument five MoE checkpoints spanning five sequence-mixer designs (DeepSeek-V2-Lite MLA, DeepSeek-MoE-16B MHA, Qwen3-30B GQA, Nemotron-30B Mamba-2, Qwen3.5-35B GDN) under a 5 by 6 grid of data conditions plus a matched EP scan from 4 to 32 ranks on H100s; both assumptions fail. Scaling EP changes the per-expert max/mean token ratio by at most 5% within every architecture's measurable range: the straggler is intrinsic to the routing decision the model makes, not to how its experts land on ranks. Mock tokens overestimate routing Gini by up to a factor of 2.35 and fabricate a batch-size scaling trend that vanishes the moment real text replaces random IDs.
  A third pattern, unexpected, emerges from the same matrix: the five architectures cleave into two stable bands. MHA and Mamba-2 (data-resilient) drop to Gini 0.105 and 0.150 on wikitext. MLA and GDN (persistently concentrated) stay above 0.24 on every real-text condition and reach 0.29 to 0.38 on mock. GQA is the intermediate case. These bands, not the EP degree or the mock-data profile, are the right workload input to AlltoAll-aware interconnect and dispatch design.

</details>


### [75] [NanoCP: Request-Level Dynamic Context Parallelism for Data-Expert Parallel Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21100)
*Jiefei Chen,Binbin Lin,Jinming Ma,Jiangfei Duan,Haojie Duanmu,Hao Liu,Qinxiu Cheng,Xiuhong Li,Zhilin Pei,Hui Wang,Xingcheng Zhang,Dahua Lin*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对 MoE 模型推理中传统并行架构导致的负载不均与 KV 缓存碎片化问题，\work 提出了动态上下文并行（DCP）技术，实现了计算与存储资源的灵活调度，显著提升了吞吐量并降低了长上下文下的尾延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 MoE 部署系统将注意力计算、MoE 通信和 KV 缓存绑定在单一实例中，导致难以同时平衡注意力延迟（受 KV 缓存大小影响）和 MoE 通信延迟（受 batch 大小影响），产生 EP 短板效应，并因 KV 存储碎片化导致长上下文下的尾延迟增加。现有的上下文并行（CP）策略因采用统一的并行度而引入了额外的通信与计算开销。

Method: 提出动态上下文并行（DCP, Dynamic Context Parallelism）：通过解耦 MoE 通信与 KV 缓存放置，根据请求的 KV 需求动态分配上下文并行度（即长请求跨实例分布，短请求本地化）。同时引入了提前（AOT）编译图引擎和定制化的基于路由的通信后端，以支持动态执行。

Result: \work 通过 DCP 有效地“液化”了集群中的 KV 缓存，在不增加额外负载均衡成本的前提下，实现了 per-instance KV 占用率和 batch size 的平衡，显著减轻了 EP 瓶颈。

Conclusion: \work 在保持严格的每输出 token 时间 (TPOT) SLA 的前提下，能够实现高达 1.88 倍至 3.27 倍的请求吞吐量提升，并将 P99 尾延迟降低了 1.79 倍至 2.12 倍，有效解决了 MoE 模型推理中的负载不均衡和 KV 缓存碎片化问题。

Abstract: Modern serving systems for Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models adopt hybrid data-expert parallelism: expert parallelism (EP) shards experts across GPUs to scale capacity, while data parallelism (DP) replicates attention layers across instances to process independent requests. Existing systems bind each request's attention, MoE communication, and KV cache to a single instance. Because attention latency scales with KV cache size while MoE communication latency scales with batch size, this binding cannot balance both simultaneously, producing EP stragglers; it also fragments KV memory across instances, inflating tail latency under long contexts. While existing context parallelism (CP) mitigates these constraints, its uniform parallelism degree incurs prohibitive communication and attention-side overheads.
  We present \work, which decouples MoE communication from KV cache placement and achieves dual balance through dynamic context parallelism (DCP). DCP assigns each request a context-parallel degree sized to its KV footprint: long requests distribute attention across multiple instances; short requests remain local.
  This dynamic parallelism effectively liquefies the KV cache across the cluster, balancing both the per-instance KV cache occupancy and batch sizes without unnecessary load-balancing costs. To bridge DCP with static execution, \work introduces an ahead-of-time (AOT) graph engine paired with a custom routing-based communication backend. Experimental results show that \work maintains up to $1.88\times$--$3.27\times$ higher request rates under strict time-per-output-token (TPOT) service level objectives (SLOs). Furthermore, \work significantly mitigates stragglers, reducing P99 tail latency by up to $1.79\times$--$2.12\times$.

</details>


### [76] [Automated Byzantine-Resilient Clustered Decentralized Federated Learning for Battery Intelligence in Connected EVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21115)
*Mouhamed Amine Bouchiha,Abdelaziz Amara Korba,Yacine Ghamri-Doudane*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对电动汽车联邦学习中的安全与信任挑战，本文提出了 ABC-DFL 框架，通过引入区块链、动态拜占庭容错机制和 FLECA 聚合算法，实现了去中心化、高鲁棒性的电池数据隐私保护方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的电动汽车电池数据联邦学习框架多采用中心化聚合模式，在安全性和信任机制上存在局限性，难以有效应对拜占庭攻击。

Method: 提出了一种自动化、拜占庭鲁棒的集群式去中心化联邦学习框架（ABC-DFL），主要包含：1. 基于开放许可区块链的去中心化架构（替代中心服务器）；2. 动态共识协议（QBFT）与预言机聚合层；3. 核心算法 FLECA（过滤分层增强聚类聚合），利用自适应阈值进行恶意更新过滤；4. 基于预言机节点的鲁棒集群聚合策略。

Result: 实验表明，在正常情况下，FLECA 的收敛性能与 FedProx 持平；在自适应对抗场景下，攻击影响分数低于 0.10，显著优于现有防御方法；同时验证了激励机制的公平性和系统整体的实用性。

Conclusion: ABC-DFL 通过去中心化架构、拜占庭容错机制及高效的聚类聚合策略，解决了传统联邦学习在电动汽车电池数据管理中的安全性和信任问题，在保证收敛性的同时显著增强了抗攻击能力。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) has emerged as a promising paradigm for managing electric vehicle (EV) battery data in intelligent transportation systems (ITS), enabling privacy-preserving tasks such as anomaly detection and capacity estimation. However, most existing frameworks rely on centralized aggregation schemes, which pose critical limitations in terms of security and trust. To address these challenges, we propose ABC-DFL, an automated Byzantine-resilient clustered decentralized federated learning (C-DFL) framework for connected EVs. The proposed incentive-driven C-DFL system replaces the central server with an open-permissioned blockchain, featuring a new dynamic Quorum Byzantine Fault Tolerance (QBFT) protocol and an oracle-based aggregation layer, to enhance trust, security, and automation. At the core of ABC-DFL lies FLECA (Filtered Layered Enhanced Clustering Aggregation), a robust hierarchical aggregation protocol that mitigates Byzantine attacks by having each EV filter malicious updates using an adaptive threshold based on deviations from its reference model update. Oracle nodes, responsible for inter-group aggregation, employ robust clustering to isolate and aggregate model updates from trustworthy EV groups. Comprehensive experimental evaluations demonstrate that FLECA matches FedProx convergence under benign conditions and significantly outperforms existing defenses with attack impact scores below 0.10 in adaptive adversarial scenarios. Furthermore, several learning experiments with multitask models confirm the effectiveness and fairness of the incentive mechanism. Finally, on-chain and off-chain benchmarks validate the practicality of ABC-DFL.

</details>

### [77] [Supporting Dynamic Control-Flow Execution for Runtime Reconfigurable Processors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21203)
*Hassan Nassar,Rafik Youssef,Lars Bauer,Jörg Henkel*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种支持运行时动态控制流（循环、跳转等）的微代码执行架构，通过优化可重构处理器，显著提升了处理多领域计算密集型任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着计算需求增长，单一的专用硬件加速器存在空间浪费且利用率低的问题。可重构处理器通过支持运行时切换加速器功能来提高资源利用率和性能。

Method: 提出了针对运行时可重构处理器微代码的动态控制流执行技术，该技术允许微代码支持循环、条件跳转和异常处理等复杂逻辑。研究使用了四个不同领域的计算密集型应用进行基准测试和评估。

Result: 该研究证明了动态控制流技术能够成功支持多种计算密集型应用，并实现了相对于通用处理器显著的性能加速。

Conclusion: 通过引入动态控制流（循环、条件跳转、异常处理），可重构处理器能够高效支持计算密集型任务，相比通用处理器在特定应用领域具有显著的加速比。

Abstract: As the need for more computing power grows, traditional methods are hitting limits. To boost performance, we're expanding Central Processing Unit (CPU) capabilities and using specialized hardware accelerators. For example, mobile devices usually have cameras, video encoding, and audio accelerators. To perform the different tasks, these accelerators execute microcode programs. These accelerators, however, take up space and often sit idle. Reconfigurable processors offer a solution. They have a normal core connected to several accelerator slots. These accelerator slots can be filled during runtime to accommodate the application running. Once one application finishes and another application is running, the accelerators can be switched. For example, playing music after using the camera.
  In this work, we introduce dynamic control-flow execution for the microcode of runtime reconfigurable processors, i.e., support for loops, conditional jumps, and exception handling. We benchmark using four different applications from four domains (object detection, ocean movement simulation, artificial intelligence and security) that all are compute-intensive and would require the dynamic control-flow when executed on reconfigurable processors. We show that the dynamic control-flow allows different applications to be executed with significant speedup in comparison with execution on general-purpose processors.

</details>

### [78] [An $O(n^5)$-Time Algorithm for Optimal Broadcast Domination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20526)
*Kleitos Papadopoulos*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种改进的路径图广播支配算法，通过构建更高效的 DAG 状态表示，将该子问题的复杂度降低至 $O(n^3)$，进而将一般连通无权图的最优广播支配问题求解速度提升至 $O(n^5)$，解决了该领域的长久猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已证明广播支配问题在一般连通无权图上是多项式时间可解的，但此前 Heggernes 和 Lokshtanov 在其研究中给出的 $O(n^6)$ 算法不够高效，且学术界存在关于该问题能否达到 $O(n^5)$ 时间复杂度的猜想。本文旨在优化路径情形的算法，从而进一步提升整体问题的求解效率。

Method: 1. 针对路径图情形，构建了一个新的单状态有向无环图（DAG），通过引入“带残余侧的定向广播球”状态，将路径情形的求解复杂度优化至 $O(n^3)$ 时间和空间。2. 将上述算法与 Heggernes 和 Lokshtanov 的“剥离一个球（peel-one-ball）”约简技术相结合，提升了处理一般图问题的效率。

Result: 1. 提出了一个 $O(n^3)$ 时间复杂度的路径图广播支配算法。2. 将整体问题的求解复杂度从 $O(n^6)$ 降低至 $O(n^5)$。

Conclusion: 该论文成功证明并实现了最优广播支配问题的 $O(n^5)$ 时间复杂度算法，解决了关于该问题通用图时间复杂度的“五次方程猜想（quintic-time conjecture）”。

Abstract: Broadcast domination assigns a nonnegative integer power to every vertex of a graph so that every vertex is within the assigned power of some broadcasting vertex, and the objective is to minimize the sum of the powers. Heggernes and Lokshtanov proved that the problem is polynomial-time solvable on arbitrary connected unweighted graphs by showing that some optimal efficient broadcast has a domination graph that is a path or a cycle, and by reducing the general case to an $O(n^6)$-time algorithm. This paper gives an efficient algorithm of the path-case. Instead of building one auxiliary acyclic graph for every possible left endpoint vertex, we build a single directed acyclic graph whose states are oriented broadcast balls together with their two possible residual sides. The resulting path-case algorithm runs in $O(n^3)$ time and $O(n^3)$ space on an $n$-vertex graph. Combining this routine with the same peel-one-ball reduction of Heggernes and Lokshtanov yields an exact $O(n^5)$-time algorithm for optimal broadcast domination on arbitrary connected unweighted graphs. This resolves the quintic-time conjecture for general graphs attributed to Heggernes and Sæther and recorded in subsequent surveys of broadcast domination.

</details>


### [79] [Distributed Stochastic Graph Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21248)
*Keren Censor-Hillel,Aditi Dudeja,George Giakkoupis*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 研究引入了一种新的分布式随机图优化框架，证明了通过利用图的边缘随机性，分布式随机算法在求解最大匹配、最小顶点覆盖和最小支配集时，在通信轮数上比非随机算法具有显著的性能优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在处理大规模图优化（如最大匹配、最小顶点覆盖、最小支配集）时，传统的集中式算法存在查询延迟，而现有的分布式算法存在通信瓶颈。作者旨在探索利用图的随机特性在分布式环境下加速这些优化问题的求解。

Method: 将经典的随机图优化问题引入分布式设置，并利用每个节点仅感知自身关联边的分布式特性；通过 rounds（同步通信轮数）衡量计算复杂度，而非传统的查询次数。

Result: 研究表明，对于最大匹配、最小顶点覆盖和最小支配集，分布式随机算法在通信轮数上远优于对应的非随机算法，并克服了现有的下界限制。

Conclusion: 分布式随机算法可以通过利用图结构的随机性，显著提高分布式优化问题的处理效率，并突破非随机算法的特定下界。

Abstract: We study stochastic graph optimization problems in a novel distributed setting. As in the standard centralized setting, a random subgraph $G^*$ of a known base graph $G$ is realized by including each edge $e$ independently with a known probability $p_e$, and we must solve an optimization problem on $G^*$ despite uncertainty about its edges. In the standard setting, to cope with this uncertainty, the algorithm can query any edge of $G$ to learn if the edge exists in $G^*$, and its complexity is the number of queried edges. The distributed setting incorporates uncertainty in a natural manner, by having each vertex know only about its own edges in $G^*$ (and only communicate over them), and the complexity is measured by the number of synchronous communication rounds.
  We establish that distributed stochastic algorithms can be drastically faster than their non-stochastic counterparts and overcome known lower bounds, by showing fast distributed approximation algorithms for maximum matching, minimum vertex cover, and minimum dominating set.

</details>


### [80] [pace-Time Trade-off in Integer Linear Scaling Rounded to the Nearest Integer through Multiplicative and Additive Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21400)
*Kyeong Soo Kim*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种解决时钟偏斜补偿的整数线性缩放新方法，通过 MDID 和 ADDS 两种算法，实现了在避免浮点精度损失的前提下，在计算复杂度与数值溢出之间的高度可控权衡，并证明其精度足以替代标准高精度浮点运算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 Bresenham 算法的增量式方法和浮点数运算存在局限：浮点运算容易产生精度丢失，且现有整数近似方法在处理大数值时存在溢出或精度不足的问题，需要一种避免浮点误差且处理逻辑严谨的整数补偿方案。

Method: 1. 将时钟偏斜补偿问题定义为 iD/A 形式的整数线性缩放；2. 提出两种算法：乘法分解法 (MDID) 和 加法分解法 (ADDS)；3. 通过复杂度分析研究时空权衡与非溢出条件；4. 在 32 位和 64 位整数不同场景下进行数值验证。

Result: MDID 在 D 较小时性能最优 (O(1))，但在处理最大整数范围时易溢出；ADDS 在所有测试场景下均能避免溢出，且 32 位整数下的 ADDS 效果等同于双精度浮点数，64 位整数下等同于四倍精度浮点数。

Conclusion: 本文提出两种整数线性缩放算法（MDID 和 ADDS）用于时钟偏斜补偿。MDID 在特定条件下具有 O(1) 的最优复杂度，但在大数值下易溢出；ADDS 具有全场景适用性但计算复杂度随数值增加而上升。研究证明了基于整数运算的算法在保持与高精度浮点数等效精度的同时，具备更低的空间复杂度和可控的误差边界。

Abstract: We formulate the problem of clock skew compensation as a special case of the integer linear scaling in the form of iD/A and propose two algorithms -- i.e., the multiplicative decomposition of integer division (MDID) and the additive decomposition of direct search (ADDS) -- for its nearest integer solution, which are not only immune to floating-point precision loss but also non-incremental unlike our prior approaches based on Bresenham's algorithm. Having theoretically established both decomposition algorithms based on a unified and rigorous formulation of the problem of the integer linear scaling rounded to the nearest integer, we discuss the space-time trade-off through the analysis of their computational complexities and non-overflow conditions. Through the numerical examples in a practical context of clock skew compensation under two different scenarios based on 32-bit and 64-bit integers, we observe that MDID can obtain the nearest integer solutions with the complexity of O(1) when D is much smaller than the maximum value of the underlying integer type but overflows otherwise; in comparison, ADDS can handle all the cases under both scenarios without overflows but at the expense of increased computational complexity when i approaches the maximum value of the underlying integer type. We also observe that ADDS based on 32-bit integers is equivalent to the clock skew compensation based on 64-bit double-precision floating-point arithmetic, while both algorithms based on 64-bit integers are equivalent to the clock skew compensation based on 128-bit quadruple-precision floating-point arithmetic, which highlights another trade-off between the bounded compensation errors and lower space complexity of the integer-based decomposition algorithms and the lower chances of overflows resulting from the wide ranges of numbers of the clock skew compensation based on floating-point arithmetic.

</details>

### [81] [Clove: Object-Level CXL Memory Management in Managed Runtimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20370)
*Sam Son,Zhihong Luo,Wen Zhang,Sylvia Ratnasamy,Scott Shenker*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: Clove 是一种针对托管语言的对象级 CXL 分层内存管理系统，它通过增强运行时（JVM）的热度追踪与对象重定位能力，显著优于传统的页级分层内存管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于页的 CXL 分层内存管理效率较低，而现有的对象级方案多局限于非托管语言；作者旨在利用托管语言运行时自身已有的对象管理机制，解决 CXL 分层内存中的热度监测与重定位难题。

Method: 开发了一个名为 Clove 的系统，通过扩展现有的托管运行时（如 JVM），集成了性能导向的对象热度追踪（profile-guided hotness tracking）和对象重定位策略，从而在对象粒度上对 CXL 分层内存进行管理。

Result: Clove 在 JVM 原型上表现优异，实现了快速层内存的高利用率，且控制了运行时开销，对比页级管理方案，应用程序运行减速降低了 22-84%。

Conclusion: 通过在托管语言运行时中集成对象级内存管理，Clove 有效弥补了 CXL 分层内存系统中的管理缺失，证明了利用运行时自身机制（如对象重定位）来实现分层内存管理的优越性。

Abstract: Object-level management of tiered memory has been studied to address the inefficiencies in page-based systems. However, object-level management for CXL-tiered memory remains underexplored due to CXL's tight performance budget and load/store interface. As a result, existing approaches remain limited in scope, primarily targeting unmanaged-language applications with bespoke runtimes or compiler support.
  This paper identifies and explores a new design point for object-level CXL management: managed languages and their runtimes. The key observation is that existing managed runtimes already provide highly optimized mechanisms for problems closely related to object-level management, including object relocation and dynamic code generation. However, they still lack the features needed for tiered memory management, such as hotness tracking and relocation policies, and thus must be carefully extended to fully realize this direction.
  We present Clove, a system that extends existing managed runtimes to support object-level CXL management for managed-language applications. Clove combines profile-guided object hotness tracking with object relocation techniques and policies. Our JVM prototype demonstrates that this extension enables high utilization of fast-tier memory while bounding runtime overhead, reducing application slowdown by 22-84% compared to page-based systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [82] [Adaptive Human-Robot Collaboration for Masonry Construction Under Material and Assembly Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20264)
*Jutang Gao,Arash Adel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种结合空间投影引导（辅助人）和激光扫描闭环校正（辅助机器人）的人机协作砌筑架构，成功解决了施工环境中的公差累积难题，在提升施工精度的同时改善了整体协作效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决施工过程中因人机沟通不畅及材料/装配不确定性导致的公差累积问题，以提高人机协作的施工效率与质量。

Method: 提出一套人机协作砌筑流程：1. 利用末端执行器集成的投影仪实现胶粘剂涂布的实时空间引导；2. 利用激光扫描技术对砖块进行实时反馈与位姿校正。

Result: 全尺寸实验表明，投影引导改善了胶粘剂涂布的涂布一致性并缩短了操作时间；基于激光的反馈校正机制有效保持了砌筑层级，避免了开环控制常见的碰撞失败，实现了对作业误差的实时动态调整。

Conclusion: 通过集成空间投影引导与基于反馈的实时适应机制，能够有效缓解建筑施工中材料和装配公差的累积问题，显著提升人机协作砌筑的精度及鲁棒性。

Abstract: Human-robot collaboration in construction is often challenged by limited robot-to-human communication and the need to adapt to tolerance accumulation arising from material and assembly uncertainties. We present an adaptive human-robot collaborative workflow for masonry construction that addresses communication limitations and tolerance accumulation, demonstrated through a brickwork case study in which a robot places bricks while a human applies adhesive. This workflow is enabled by two complementary mechanisms: 1) an end-effector-mounted projector that provides spatially registered, just-in-time projection guidance for manual adhesive application, and 2) laser scanning for feedback-driven grasping and placement pose correction. Together, these mechanisms enable adjustment of human and robotic actions in response to material variability and accumulated assembly tolerances. Full-scale experiments across conventional running-bond and nonstandard configurations demonstrate that projection guidance improves adhesive application consistency and reduces application time, while laser-based correction maintains level courses and avoids collision-prone failures associated with open-loop execution. These results indicate that integrating spatial projection with feedback-driven adaptation, enabled by material and as-built sensing, can mitigate tolerance accumulation and improve precision and robustness in human-robot collaborative construction.

</details>


### [83] [Terrestrial Soft Mobile Robots: A Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20304)
*Dimuthu D. K. Arachchige*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对无轮式陆地软体移动机器人的发展现状进行了全面综述，涵盖了驱动、建模与控制等核心维度，并探讨了该领域未来面临的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机器人因其在搜救、服务、探测及制造等领域的潜力，成为研究热点；作者旨在通过综述当前技术水平，明确发展瓶颈，促进该领域的技术进步与应用普及。

Method: 综述研究法（Comprehensive Review），对无轮式陆地软体移动机器人的运动策略、驱动方法、建模方式及控制系统进行了系统性归纳与分析。

Result: 总结了软体移动机器人在无轮陆地运动方面的现有技术成果，涵盖了从基础驱动到系统控制的全面框架，并明确了影响该技术大规模商业化和实际应用的关键挑战。

Conclusion: 本文梳理了软体移动机器人的技术现状，旨在为该领域的科研人员与实践者提供系统性的研究指南，并指出未来广泛应用的关键挑战。

Abstract: Soft mobile robots have emerged as a promising area of research with potential applications in various disciplines including but not limited to search-and-rescue, service, surveillance, explorations, and manufacturing. In this article, we provide a comprehensive review of the current state of soft mobile robot research, focusing on wheelless terrestrial locomotive systems. We include past and present developments in locomotion strategies, actuation methods, modeling approaches, and control systems. Further, we identify key research challenges that must be overcome to enable the widespread adoption of soft mobile robots in various applications. Overall, this article provides a valuable resource for researchers and practitioners interested in the field of soft mobile robots and soft robotics.

</details>


### [84] [Proximal State Nudging: Reducing Skill Atrophy from AI Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20355)
*Megha Srivastava,Jonathan Ouyang,Eric Zhou,Andrew Silva,Emily Sumner,Dorsa Sadigh,Yuchen Cui,Deepak Gopinath,Guy Rosman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 为解决人机协作中的“技能萎缩”问题，本文提出了PSN算法，通过在任务执行过程中引导用户进入易于学习的状态，在保证任务表现的同时显著提升了操作员的自主技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在半自动系统中，人类过度依赖AI辅助会导致“技能萎缩”（Skill Atrophy），即人类丧失在脱离辅助时的自主操作能力，从而带来安全隐患。现有方法往往只关注任务表现，而忽视了用户的技能培养。

Method: 提出了一种名为“近端状态微调”（Proximal State Nudging, PSN）的共享自主控制算法。该算法通过识别并引导用户进入“最易学习”的状态，在执行任务的同时优化用户的技能习得。

Result: 在LunarLander仿真环境下，PSN表现优于现有的共享自主基准；在CARLA模拟器的真人驾驶实验（n=60）中，PSN使受试者在脱离辅助下的技能增益提升高达7倍，且比独立练习减少了50%的碰撞率。

Conclusion: 该研究证明通过在共享自主控制中整合对技能学习的优化，可以在不显著牺牲任务表现的前提下，有效缓解“技能萎缩”问题，并促进人机协作中的操作员技能提升。

Abstract: Skill atrophy, the gradual decline of human capability under AI assistance, poses a safety risk in shared-control of semi-autonomous systems, where operators may be unable to distinguish their own inputs from autonomous corrections. We propose Proximal State Nudging (PSN), a shared autonomy algorithm that jointly optimizes for skill development and task performance by nudging users toward states estimated to be most learnable. We first show that PSN outperforms existing shared autonomy baselines in balancing student improvement in unassisted reward with overall shared performance, using simulated students in the classic LunarLander environment. We then present, to the best of our knowledge, the first human subject studies of a planner incorporating learning-compatible shared autonomy: across two driving tasks in the CARLA simulator (High Performance Racing and Parallel Parking, n = 60), PSN produces up to 7x larger gains in unassisted skill than standard blended shared autonomy, while incurring 50% fewer collisions than unassisted self-practice.

</details>


### [85] [SUGAR: A Scalable Human-Video-Driven Generalizable Humanoid Loco-Manipulation Learning Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20373)
*Tianshu Wu,Xiangqi Kong,Yue Chen,Qize Yu,Hang Ye,Jia Li,Yizhou Wang,Hao Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SUGAR 是一个无需任务奖励工程的框架，通过从海量人类视频中提取并修正运动先验，训练类人机器人学习通用的全身操纵技能，并成功在真实物理硬件上实现了零样本部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的类人机器人运动控制方法普遍存在奖励工程繁琐、泛化性差或依赖昂贵的远程操作等问题，且直接使用人类视频数据作为运动先验时存在遮挡和接触误差，难以直接用于策略学习。

Method: 提出 SUGAR 框架：(1) 自动化提取模块：从视频中提取人体-物体运动轨迹及接触标签；(2) 物理修正模块：利用特权物理仿真器将不完美的运动先验转化为物理可行的技能；(3) 策略蒸馏模块：通过分层策略（命令生成器+跟踪器）实现自主控制。

Result: 在6项典型任务中表现优异，大幅超越基准方法；验证了数据量与性能的正相关性；在现实世界中实现了零样本迁移，具备自主恢复力及应对外部干扰的稳定性。

Conclusion: SUGAR 提供了一种有效的、可扩展的从人类视频学习机器人全身运动控制的框架，证明了利用海量未标注人类视频可以实现稳健的零样本现实世界部署，无需繁琐的任务专用奖励工程。

Abstract: Building humanoid robots capable of generalizable whole-body loco-manipulation in the real world remains a fundamental challenge. Existing methods either rely on laborious task-specific reward engineering, rigidly replay reference motions that fail to generalize, or depend on costly teleoperation that limits scalability. While human videos capture diverse human behaviors, motion priors inferred from them are inherently imperfect, suffering from occlusion, contact artifacts, and retargeting errors that render them unsuitable for direct policy learning. To address this, we present SUGAR, a scalable data-driven framework that converts diverse human videos into deployable humanoid loco-manipulation skills, without any task-specific reward engineering or reference-motion conditioning at inference. SUGAR proceeds in three stages. First, a fully automated pipeline extracts kinematic interaction priors including human-object motion trajectories and contact labels from unstructured human videos. Second, a privileged physics-based refiner uses a unified mimic reward and progressive state pool to transform imperfect priors into physically feasible, high-fidelity skills. Third, refined skills are distilled into a hierarchical autonomous policy consisting of a command generator and a command tracker. We evaluate SUGAR on six representative loco-manipulation tasks in simulation and real-world humanoid hardware. Our method substantially outperforms reference-tracking baselines, and performance scales clearly with the amount of human video data. It also achieves zero-shot real-world transfer with reliable closed-loop execution, autonomous failure recovery, and stable long-horizon performance under external perturbations. Project Page: https://tianshuwu.github.io/sugar-humanoid/

</details>


### [86] [Scalable Multi-robot Motion Planning via Hierarchical Subproblem Expansion and Workspace Decomposition Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20395)
*Isaac Ngui,Courtney McBeth,James D. Motes,Marco Morales,Nancy M. Amato*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于工作空间分解和迭代细化的多机器人运动规划方法，通过在解耦的较小配置空间内进行搜索，显著提高了规划效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人运动规划面临“维度灾难”，在大空间中搜索联合配置空间计算开销极大，现有的协调方法往往在效率与复杂性之间难以平衡。

Method: 提出了一种基于工作空间分解（workspace decomposition）的规划方法。不同于以往先判断冲突再构建联合配置空间的做法，该方法通过迭代细化工作空间表示，引导系统在解耦的小型配置空间中进行搜索，从而实现多机器人协调。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在规划时间上实现了高达一个数量级的提升。

Conclusion: 该方法通过将工作空间分解与迭代细化相结合，证明了无需搜索整个联合配置空间即可实现多机器人协调路径规划，在保证规划质量的同时显著降低了计算成本。

Abstract: A fundamental challenge in multi-robot motion planning is achieving sufficient coordination to avoid inter-robot conflicts without incurring the large computational expense of searching the joint configuration space of the robot group. In this work, we present a method for multiple mobile robot motion planning that achieves an improvement in planning time up to an order of magnitude by leveraging the insight that we can use discrete search over a workspace decomposition to provide coordination between robots during planning. While prior work uses workspace topology to inform when coordination between robots is needed and then composes robots into their joint configuration space, we take a step further by iteratively refining our workspace representation to allow our planner to search smaller, decoupled configuration spaces.

</details>


### [87] [Enhancing Graph-Based SLAM in GNSS-Denied environments by leveraging leg odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20484)
*Léon Perruchot-Triboulet,Luc Jaulin,Kai Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种将腿部里程计融合进LIO-SAM的因子图框架，有效修正了足式机器人在无GPS场景下的高程漂移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 足式机器人在GNSS拒止环境下自主导航时，单纯依赖激光雷达（LiDAR）在几何稀疏或重复场景中容易产生高程漂移。

Method: 利用因子图架构，将基于本体感的腿部里程计整合至LIO-SAM框架中；通过包含选择性噪声模型的单位相对位姿约束将腿部里程计与激光雷达-惯性里程计（LIO）链耦合。

Result: 在Linxai D50四足机器人上完成了超过1公里的室外实地测试，高程漂移从30米以上降低至30厘米以内，并在基准算法失效的场景中实现了稳定收敛。

Conclusion: 本体感（proprioceptive）腿部里程计是解决足式机器人在无GPS环境下进行三维SLAM时，垂直维度（高程）漂移问题的轻量级且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation in GNSS-denied environments remains a core challenge for legged robots, where exteroceptive sensors such as LiDAR are prone to elevation drift in geometrically sparse or repetitive scenes. We present a factor graph architecture that augments the LIO-SAM framework with a parallel kinematic lane driven by proprioceptive leg odometry, coupled to the main LiDAR-inertial lane via an identity relative pose constraint with a selective noise model. Applied to a Linxai D50 quadruped platform across two outdoor loops totaling over one kilometer, our approach reduces elevation drift from over 30m to under 30cm and enables convergence in a scene where the baseline pipeline fails entirely. These results suggest that proprioceptive data, already computed onboard for gait control, constitutes a lightweight and effective vertical anchor for SLAM in GNSS-denied settings.

</details>


### [88] [The Yes-Man Syndrome: Benchmarking Abstention in Embodied Robotic Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20544)
*Doguhan Yeke,Elif Su Temirel,Ananth Shreekumar,Brandon Lee,Dongyan Xu,Z Berkay Celik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对具身机器人场景，文章提出了RoboAbstention框架，用于测评并提升VLM模型在面对无法执行指令时的弃权能力，弥补了现有研究在物理环境感知与约束方面的缺失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型语言模型（LLM）的弃权研究多集中于纯文本领域，缺乏对具身机器人场景中物理约束、感知接地失效及指令歧义性等问题的考量，导致VLM作为具身智能规划器时无法有效处理不可执行的指令。

Method: 构建了包含具身机器人弃权分类法的RoboAbstention框架，并通过三个阶段生成数据集：(1)结构化视觉接地；(2)确定性约束推导；(3)基于模板的受控指令生成。此外，评估了多种前沿VLM，并测试了防御性提示及上下文学习等干预策略。

Result: 测评显示前沿VLM模型在弃权任务上表现欠佳（例如Gemini 2.5 Flash仅弃权39.0%），但通过防御性提示和上下文学习等干预措施，弃权率可分别提升至93.6%和88.6%。

Conclusion: 现有视觉语言模型（VLM）在具身机器人场景下的“弃权”（Abstention）能力较弱。尽管通过防御性提示和上下文学习等干预手段可显著提升表现，但尚未完全解决该问题，且该领域仍需更深入的研究以应对复杂的物理环境约束。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are used as high-level planners for embodied agents, translating natural language instructions and visual observations into action plans. While prior work has studied abstention in LLMs, existing benchmarks are largely text-only and do not capture the perceptual grounding and physical constraints inherent to embodied robotics environments. In such settings, abstention requires recognizing when instructions are ambiguous, physically infeasible, based on false premises, or otherwise unresolvable given the available sensory modalities and context. To address this gap, we introduce a taxonomy to categorize abstention in the context of embodied robotics and present RoboAbstention, a scalable and auditable framework for generating abstention instructions grounded in images gathered from five robotics datasets. RoboAbstention instantiates the taxonomy through a three-phase pipeline: (1) structured visual grounding, (2) deterministic constraint derivation, and (3) controlled instruction generation via category-specific templates. This enables the construction of a diverse dataset with verifiable abstention conditions. We evaluate several frontier VLMs and find that all models exhibit significant weaknesses in abstention, including those with advanced reasoning capabilities. The best-performing model, Gemini 2.5 Flash, abstains on only 39.0% of our 6,069 benchmark instructions, while the embodied planner Gemini Robotics ER 1.6 Preview abstains on just 16.5%. We further explore methods for improving abstention in VLM planners, such as defensive prompting and in-context learning, and find that these interventions substantially improve performance, reaching 93.6% abstention rate for Gemini Robotics ER 1.6 Preview and 88.6% for GPT 5.4 Mini, yet no approach fully solves the problem. We open-source RoboAbstention at https://purseclab.github.io/RoboAbstention/.

</details>


### [89] [Fault-Tolerant, Rigidity-Preserving Control of Inflatable Truss Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20561)
*James Wade,Isaac Weaver,Mihai Stanciu,Nathan Usevitch*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文为等周机器人桁架设计了一套容错控制框架，通过运动学路径规划约束、DTCBF刚性保障及闭环反馈控制，解决了电机故障导致的结构失效问题，实现了高效稳定的容错运动控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等周机器人桁架在实际运行中易受电机故障影响，导致操作能力受限，亟需一套具备容错功能的控制框架以确保其在执行器受损时的任务执行可靠性。

Method: 1. 扩展运动学优化算法：通过引入等式约束屏蔽故障执行器；2. 引入离散时间控制障碍函数（DTCBF）：在保证结构刚性的同时最大化工作空间；3. 闭环位置控制：利用板载编码器反馈及基于正运动学的状态估计器，提升抗干扰能力。

Result: 在6执行器2D桁架测试平台通过仿真与硬件实验验证：单电机故障下保持了超过69%的工作空间，闭环系统相比开环系统轨迹追踪精度提升了25%以上。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的容错控制框架有效提升了等周机器人桁架在电机故障情况下的鲁棒性，通过约束优化与控制障碍函数，在牺牲最小工作空间的前提下保障了结构的刚性与位置控制精度。

Abstract: Isoperimetric robotic trusses can adapt to different tasks and environments because they have a high strength-to-weight ratio, can change their own shape dramatically, and can be reconfigured into a variety of different shapes. However, motor failures in operational environments can severely limit operational capabilities if not properly addressed. This paper presents a fault-tolerant control framework for an inflatable robotic truss that maintains functionality despite motor failures, shown through three key contributions. First, we extend the kinematic optimization to handle arbitrary combinations of motor failures by imposing equality constraints to ensure failed actuators are not used. Second, we introduce discrete-time control barrier function (DTCBF) constraints that mathematically guarantee structural rigidity while maximizing workspace utilization, a critical requirement for reliable operation of truss robots under discrete-time control. Third, we implement closed-loop position control using onboard encoder feedback and a forward kinematics-based state estimator, improving positional accuracy in the presence of disturbances. We validate our approach through simulation and hardware experiments on a 2D isoperimetric truss testbed. For a 2D configuration with 6 actuators, we demonstrate >69% workspace preservation under single-motor failures and a >25% improvement in tracking accuracy with closed-loop control. These results establish a foundation for more robust and resilient isoperimetric truss robots operating under degraded actuation.

</details>


### [90] [Conflict-Aware Active Perception and Control in 3D Gaussian Splatting Fields via Control Barrier Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20566)
*Amirhossein Mollaei Khass,Athanasios Cosse,Vivek Pandey,Nader Motee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于3DGS的抗冲突主动感知与控制框架，通过引入AV@R控制障碍函数和感知障碍函数，构建统一的二次规划模型，有效平衡了安全性与信息获取的最优权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在不确定环境下，机器人进行主动感知（获取信息）的目标往往与安全性（避障）存在冲突。现有方法在处理3DGS环境下的这种权衡时效果不佳。

Method: 1. 利用3DGS进行环境建模；2. 采用基于AV@R（平均风险值）的控制障碍函数（CBF）保障安全性；3. 提出风险感知下的期望信息增益（EIG）及感知障碍函数引导相机观测；4. 构建统一的安全临界、感知感知二次规划（QP）问题，将安全作为硬约束，感知作为松弛约束。

Result: 模拟实验证明，该方法在保证高安全性的前提下，显著提升了信息获取的效能，优于现有的3DGS主动感知方法。

Conclusion: 提出了一种能够同时处理安全约束与信息获取需求的统一框架，克服了两者在不确定环境下的竞争矛盾，且在3DGS环境下表现优于现有方案。

Abstract: Active perception in uncertain environments requires robots to navigate safely while acquiring informative observations to reduce map uncertainty. These objectives inherently conflict, as informative viewpoints often lie near uncertain regions with higher collision risk. To address this challenge, we develop a conflict-aware active perception and control framework for robotic systems operating in environments represented by 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS). Safety is enforced using a Control Barrier Function (CBF) derived from an Average Value-at-Risk AV@R collision-risk metric that accounts for geometric uncertainty and guarantees forward invariance of a safe set. To improve perception, we propose a risk-aware Expected Information Gain (EIG) formulation for selecting the next-best-view and introduce perception barrier functions that align the camera orientation with the local information-ascent direction. To obtain a tractable formulation for these conflicting safety and perception objectives, we propose a unified safety-critical, perception-aware quadratic program that enforces safety as a hard constraint while relaxing perception constraints through slack variables. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed method improves both safety and information acquisition compared to existing 3DGS-based approaches.

</details>


### [91] [Intent-First Aerial V2V for Tactical Coordination and Separation: Protocol and Performance Under Density and Disturbance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20595)
*Mehrnaz Sabet*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对低空无人机交通管理，提出了一种全新的意图优先V2V战术通信协议栈，通过本地化意图共享与协同机制，解决了从预先协调到紧急避撞之间的战术分离空白，验证了其提升高密度空域协同效率的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低空无人机群面临快速变化的扰动，现有的预航线协调（战略层）和避撞机制（应急层）无法满足实时、高密度的交通管理需求，缺乏中间层的战术分离手段。

Method: 提出并实现了一种全空基、侧链路（Sidelink）级、意图优先的无人机对无人机（V2V）战术邻域交换协议栈，结合了刷新状态、意图信标、协同感知及基于事件触发的协调机制，并采用带认证的新鲜度检查（Freshness Check）机制。

Result: 测试表明，该V2V协议栈能有效减少陈旧信念分歧，通过协同感知保持可观测性，拒绝无效指令，抑制错误推理，并实现了共享资源的结构化协调。

Conclusion: 基于意图优先的空基V2V通信栈是解决低空无人机大规模战术协调的可行方案，但在高密度、高干扰或复杂环境下仍需采取受限的故障回退策略。

Abstract: Dense low-altitude aerial operations require more than pre-flight route coordination and last-resort collision avoidance. Once aircraft are airborne, disturbances can emerge on timescales shorter than strategic reauthorization can absorb, while collision avoidance is too late and disruptive to serve as routine traffic management. Although tactical separation is recognized as the intermediate layer, realizing it at scale requires a deployable neighborhood communication mechanism that provides fresh, trusted information for local coordination. This paper presents what is, to our knowledge, the first controller-coupled characterization of an all-airborne, sidelink-class, intent-first vehicle-to-vehicle (V2V) tactical neighborhood exchange stack for dense Unmanned Aircraft System Traffic Management (UTM) operations. Unlike awareness-only broadcast, the proposed exchange combines refreshed state and intent beacons for local awareness, cooperative perception, and degraded-mode assessment with event-triggered messages for yielding, sequencing, release, and contingency coordination. We implement and evaluate this model on an all-airborne V2V stack using sidelink-class C-V2X modules with authenticated freshness checks. Evaluation uses a scenario-driven, high-volume stress campaign supported by real-time, field-anchored infrastructure. Results show that V2V reduces stale-belief divergence, preserves observability through cooperative perception, rejects invalid tactical messages, suppresses false local inference, and structures shared-resource coordination. The implemented stack provides a viable communication layer for tactical separation in lower-to-moderate regimes, but transitions toward guarded fallback as density, impairment, and complexity increase. These findings position intent-first aerial V2V as a bounded enabler for scaling tactical coordination in disturbance-driven urban airspace.

</details>


### [92] [Jointly Learning Predicates and Actions Enables Zero-Shot Skill Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20648)
*Benedict Quartey,Sebastian Castro,Eric Rosen,Wil Thomason,George Konidaris,Stefanie Tellex*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PACTS 提出了一种将动作与符号结果（谓词）联合建模的机器人策略，显著提升了机器人对已知技能进行零样本重组的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的模仿学习（LfD）方法通常仅建模动作分布，缺乏对行为产生的符号结果的推理能力，导致在面对已知技能的新组合时，零样本泛化能力较弱。

Method: 提出了“谓词动作技能”（Predicate Action Skills, PACTS），这是一种闭环视觉运动策略。该方法将技能建模为动作路径（action trajectories）与谓词信念路径（predicate belief trajectories）的联合生成过程，并利用在线谓词预测作为符号接口进行规划与排序。

Result: 实验表明，PACTS 能够学习到改进动作生成与谓词分类的内部表示，并支持通过在线规划实现已学技能的零样本组合（Zero-shot composition）。

Conclusion: PACTS 通过建模动作与谓词的联合概率分布，弥补了现有生成式策略在组合性上的不足，证明了将动作与符号结果联合建模可实现零样本（Zero-shot）技能组合与更鲁棒的执行监控。

Abstract: Learning from Demonstration (LfD) enables robots to learn complex behaviors from expert examples, yet existing approaches often fail to generalize to new compositions of known skills without retraining. Modern generative policies model distributions over action trajectories alone, thus are unable to reason about the symbolic outcomes required for robust composition. We propose that skills should jointly model action trajectories and the symbolic outcomes they induce. To address this gap, we introduce Predicate Action Skills (PACTS), a class of closed-loop visuomotor policies that model skills as a joint generative process over action and predicate belief trajectories, producing coherent action-outcome rollouts within a single model. Jointly generating actions and predicates enables PACTS to learn internal representations that improve both action generation and predicate classification. Furthermore, we demonstrate zero-shot composition of learned skills via planning by leveraging online predicate predictions from PACTS as a symbolic interface for sequencing and monitoring execution. Project website: https://planpacts.github.io/

</details>


### [93] [A Semantic and Occlusion-Aware GM-PHD Filter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20666)
*Jovan Menezes,Mark Campbell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文提出了一种基于深层语义信息和遮挡感知的GM-PHD滤波器出生模型，优化了复杂驾驶场景下新目标的初始化过程，显著提升了跟踪效率与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GM-PHD滤波器的出生模型多基于简单的均匀假设，在复杂的遮挡和高密度驾驶场景中无法有效表征新目标的出现位置，导致跟踪初始化延迟高。

Method: 提出了一种语义感知遮挡（S-OA）出生模型，利用深度学习提取的语义信息与遮挡区域分析来指导高斯混合概率假设密度（GM-PHD）滤波器的初始化。

Result: 在KITTI数据集等实验中，该模型减少了遮挡环境下的初始化延迟，在约70%的测试案例中达到或优于最优基准，并降低了平均基数误差。

Conclusion: 将语义先验与遮挡推理集成到贝叶斯跟踪框架中，能显著提升复杂驾驶场景下的多目标跟踪性能。

Abstract: This paper proposes a new birth model including semantic information derived from deep learning to create an occlusion-aware Gaussian Mixture Probability Hypothesis Density (GM-PHD) filter. Unlike prior approaches that rely on simplistic or uniform assumptions, the proposed Semantic-Occlusion Aware (S-OA) birth model defines initialization terms by explicitly considering regions of occlusion and by leveraging semantic information about the environment. This enables the filter to accurately represent where new objects are more likely to appear, thereby improving tracking performance in complex and high-density driving scenarios. The method is evaluated through Monte Carlo simulations and experiments on the KITTI dataset. Performance is assessed by measuring the latency between first detection and track initiation, along with the mean absolute cardinality error and the Optimal Subpattern Assignment (OSPA) metric. Results demonstrate that the S-OA birth model reduces initialization delay in occlusion-heavy settings, matching or outperforming the strongest baseline in approximately 70% of cases. A sensitivity analysis of birth model weights is also provided. Overall, the findings underscore the benefits of integrating occlusion reasoning and semantic priors into Bayesian tracking frameworks for autonomous driving.

</details>


### [94] [GaussianDream: A Feed-Forward 3D Gaussian World Model for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20752)
*Zijian Zhang,Yuqing Jiang,Qian Cheng,Si Liu,Ding Zhao,Ping Luo,Weitao Zhou,Haibao Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GaussianDream 是一种用于机器人 VLA 策略的 3D 高斯世界模型插件，通过在训练时引入几何和时空监督来提升物理操纵精度，推理阶段则剔除复杂计算，以保持高效执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在处理物体几何、密集视觉结构及短程环境演变方面的监督不足，难以满足高精度物理操纵的需求。

Method: 提出一种前馈 3D 高斯世界模型插件 GaussianDream；在训练期间耦合当前场景重建与视界条件下的未来场景预测，强制学习紧凑的时空前缀；利用高斯渲染提供 RGB、深度和 3D 流监督。推理时移除解码头，仅保留前缀辅助行动生成，实现零额外推断开销。

Result: 在 LIBERO 任务中达到 98.4% 的成功率，在 RoboCasa Human-50 中达到 52.6%，并在真实机器人任务中达到 50.0% 的成功率，性能优于对比模型。

Conclusion: GaussianDream 通过引入基于 3D 高斯的时空世界模型插件，显著增强了 VLA 模型在物理精确操纵任务中的空间感知与预测能力，在多个仿真及现实任务中表现优异。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies have advanced language-conditioned robotic manipulation by transferring semantic priors from pretrained vision-language models to action generation. Yet, standard action-imitation training often provides limited explicit supervision for 3D geometry, dense visual structure, and short-horizon environment evolution, which are critical for physically precise manipulation. We introduce \textbf{GaussianDream}, a feed-forward 3D Gaussian world-model plug-in that turns robot trajectories into structured spatial-temporal supervision. The key idea is to couple current Gaussian reconstruction with horizon-conditioned future Gaussian prediction during training, forcing a compact spatio-temporal prefix to be decodable into renderable 3D Gaussian states. This enables dense RGB rendering, depth, and pseudo 3D scene-flow supervision without requiring test-time Gaussian decoding. At inference, GaussianDream discards all auxiliary decoding heads and retains only the learned prefix to condition action generation, avoiding rendering, video rollout, or additional planning during closed-loop control. Experiments on LIBERO, RoboCasa Human-50, and real-robot tasks demonstrate strong and highly competitive performance, achieving \textbf{98.4\%} average success on LIBERO, \textbf{52.6\%} on RoboCasa Human-50, and \textbf{50.0\%} in real-world evaluation.

</details>


### [95] [VLA-REPLICA: A Low-Cost, Reproducible Benchmark for Real-World Evaluation of Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20774)
*Alex S. Huang,Jiahui Zhang,Shiqing Tang,Yu Xiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对VLA机器人模型缺乏统一、可复现真实测试基准的问题，本文提出了VLA-REPLICA，这是一个基于低成本、易于复制组件的标准化评估平台，填补了模拟与昂贵真实基准之间的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器人VLA模型评估存在局限：模拟环境无法捕捉真实世界复杂性；而现有的真实世界基准测试存在硬件昂贵、难以复现、缺乏多样性或评估中心化等痛点。

Method: 提出了一套低成本、基于现成组件（off-the-shelf components）的可复现机器人硬件评估系统；构建了一套包含多样化操作任务的基准，并提供了目标域适应的小规模演示数据集；设计了评估模型In-Distribution和Out-of-Distribution能力的统一实验协议。

Result: 成功构建并验证了VLA-REPLICA系统；在多个独立实验室环境中测试显示结果具有高度一致性；实验揭示了当前最先进VLA模型在真实场景下的实际操作能力与瓶颈。

Conclusion: VLA-REPLICA通过实验证明了其作为一套低成本、高复现性基准的可行性，能够有效评估VLA模型在不同分布下的性能，并揭示了现有模型在真实机器人操作中的优劣势。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown strong promise for general-purpose robotic manipulation, but their real-world evaluation remains limited by a lack of accessible, reproducible, and consistent benchmarks. Simulation benchmarks fail to capture real-world complexity, while existing real-world benchmarks often require expensive hardware, centralized evaluation, or are limited in task diversity. We introduce VLA-REPLICA, a low-cost, easily reproducible real-world benchmark for evaluating VLA models. Built from off-the-shelf components, our system can be quickly assembled and replicated across laboratories, providing a consistent environment for policy evaluation anywhere in the world. VLA-REPLICA includes a diverse suite of manipulation tasks and a small-scale demonstration dataset for target-domain adaptation, with real-world evaluation protocols for both in-distribution and out-of-distribution settings. Experiments with imitation learning and state-of-the-art VLA models reveal model strengths and limitations, while consistent results across independently constructed setups demonstrate the reproducibility of our benchmark.

</details>


### [96] [CMC-Opt: Constraint Manifold with Corners for Inequality-Constrained Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20796)
*Yetong Zhang,Frank Dellaert*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于“带角的约束流形”的优化框架，将复杂的机器人运动规划问题转化为流形上的无约束优化问题，有效提升了复杂约束下轨迹生成的鲁棒性与成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人领域中存在难以处理的非线性等式和不等式约束优化问题，传统方法在解决大规模动力学规划时往往表现不佳或无法收敛。

Method: 将带约束的原始优化问题转化为约束状态空间上的无约束优化问题；提出了“带角的约束流形”来表征满足混合非线性等式和不等式约束的状态空间，并在此结构上扩展了流形优化算法。

Result: 在该框架下成功生成了动态可行的轨迹，在处理大规模动力学规划问题时，性能优于传统方法。

Conclusion: 通过引入“带角的约束流形（constraint manifolds with corners）”并扩展相应的流形优化算法，能有效解决机器人领域复杂的混合非线性约束优化问题，且在处理大规模运动规划任务时比传统方法表现出更强的鲁棒性与可行性。

Abstract: We introduce a manifold-based framework for addressing optimization problems with equality and inequality constraints found in robotics. Our approach transforms the original problem into an unconstrained optimization problem directly on the constrained state space. To achieve this, we introduce ``constraint manifolds with corners" to represent the state space satisfying mixed nonlinear equality and inequality constraints. We further extend manifold optimization algorithms to operate on this new topological structure. We demonstrate the power and robustness of our framework in the context of a large-scale kinodynamic planning problem, successfully generating dynamically feasible trajectories where standard methods fail.

</details>


### [97] [Demo-JEPA: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture for One-shot Cross-Embodiment Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20811)
*Jingyang He,Guangrun Li,Jieyu Zhang,Chengkai Hou,Zhengping Che,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Demo-JEPA 是一种跨形态模仿学习框架，通过将演示视为目标而非动作序列，结合 JEPA 世界模型，实现了在异构形态机器人间无需动作对齐的鲁棒性仿效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的模仿学习通常依赖于动作复现，但在面对不同形态、运动学结构或动作空间的演示者时遇到障碍，现有的对齐方法或大规模协同训练成本高且灵活性差。需要一种摆脱“动作即演示”思维、关注“演示意图”的跨形态模仿方法。

Method: 提出 Demo-JEPA 框架：1) 基于 JEPA（Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture）构建世界模型；2) 将视觉演示转化为目标物体的潜在轨迹（latent trajectories）；3) 目标智能体利用自身学习到的前向动力学模型，通过规划（planning）实现这些潜在轨迹目标。

Result: 在 RLBench 和真实环境操作任务中，Demo-JEPA 表现出与特定领域规划器相当的性能，并具备在未见任务及不同形态配置下的泛化能力，优于现有同类方法。

Conclusion: Demo-JEPA 成功解决了机器人跨形态（cross-embodiment）模仿学习中动作空间不匹配的问题，通过将演示转化为目标导向的潜在轨迹而非动作序列，实现了高效且通用的跨形态迁移。

Abstract: Robotic imitation learning is often treated as reproducing demonstrated actions, but actions are inherently embodiment-specific. When demonstrations come from humans or robots with different morphology, kinematics, or action spaces, this action-centric view requires shared action spaces, heuristic retargeting, or large-scale multi-embodiment co-training. We instead view demonstrations as implicit specifications of future goals: the target agent should infer what state the demonstrator is trying to realize, rather than how the demonstrator executes it. We propose Demo-JEPA, a cross-embodiment imitation framework that decouples demonstration intent from embodiment-specific execution. Built on a JEPA-based world model, Demo-JEPA translates source visual demonstrations into target-compatible future latent trajectories in a shared predictive representation space. The target agent then uses these latent trajectories as subgoals and realizes them through planning under its own learned forward dynamics. Because Demo-JEPA avoids action-level correspondence and requires only visual demonstrations plus the target agent's own interaction experience, it supports flexible imitation across heterogeneous embodiments. Experiments on RLBench and real-world manipulation tasks show that Demo-JEPA matches specialized in-domain planners and generalizes to unseen tasks and embodiment configurations where prior methods fail.

</details>


### [98] [Mobile UMI: Cross-View Diffusion Policy with Decoupled Kinematics for Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20894)
*Haoran Huang,Haonan Dong,Huixu Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Mobile UMI 是一种解决移动机器人模仿学习中动作耦合与执行延迟的框架，通过双摄解耦运动轨迹和异步状态对齐，提升了机器人执行复杂任务的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动模仿学习在便携接口上存在两个耦合瓶颈：一是动作标签受肢体移动污染（难以区分身体运动与手部动作），二是推理延迟导致基座在执行过程中位置偏移，造成轨迹执行偏差。

Method: 提出了一套 Mobile UMI 框架，包含三个核心组件：1) 双摄像头系统，获取胸部全局环境与腕部局部交互；2) 基于 ChArUco 的空间锚定，将坐标系对齐并解耦为 SE(3) 操作动作和 SE(2) 基座轨迹；3) 异步滚动时域执行器，通过实时状态匹配丢弃过期动作点，解决推理延迟导致的滞后问题。

Result: 在四项长视距家居任务中，100次试验平均成功率达 83.8%；对比 ACT 和 Diffusion Policy，该方法表现更优，验证了该方案在解决移动机器人模仿学习问题上的有效性。

Conclusion: Mobile UMI 通过明确的运动学因子分解（将手部姿态相对于胸部坐标系重构）和状态级延迟对齐（异步滚动时域执行），有效解决了移动模仿学习中的操作/导航耦合及推理延迟问题，无需修改底层策略架构。

Abstract: Mobile imitation learning on portable demonstration interfaces faces two coupled bottlenecks: locomotion-contaminated action labels and inference-induced execution latency on a continuously moving base. Recent wrist-mounted interfaces lower the cost of tabletop data collection, yet a single wrist view does not capture the global context required for base navigation. Adding a body-mounted camera entangles human walking with hand motion. Meanwhile, generative policies introduce hundreds of milliseconds of inference latency, during which the base advances past predicted waypoints, forcing backward corrections at action splices. This paper presents Mobile UMI, a hardware-free demonstration framework that addresses both gaps through three components. First, a dual-camera capture system records chest-centric global context and wrist-centric local interaction without any robot present. Second, a one-shot ChArUco-based spatial anchor unifies the chest and hand visual-inertial frames; the hand pose is then re-expressed relative to the chest to extract decoupled SE(3) manipulation and SE(2) base trajectories. Third, an asynchronous receding-horizon executor performs online state matching: each generated action chunk is realigned with the current physical pose so that expired waypoints are discarded before execution. The full system is evaluated on four long-horizon household tasks, achieving an average success rate of 83.8% over 100 trials per task. Controlled comparisons against ACT and Diffusion Policy show that the chest-relative label alone closes much of the gap; online state matching closes the remainder. These results indicate that, for mobile imitation learning under the tested conditions, explicit kinematic factorization combined with state-level latency alignment provides an effective solution without requiring architectural changes to the underlying policy class.

</details>


### [99] [SubTGraph: Large-Scale Subterranean Environment Synthesis with Controllable Topological Variability for Robotic Autonomy Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20917)
*F. Labra Caso,A. Saradagi,S. Fredriksson,S. Nordström,A. Koval,G. Nikolakopoulos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了 SubTGraph，这是一个用于快速合成大规模、高变异性地下（SubT）环境的框架，旨在通过自动化、规模化的场景生成，为机器人自主性算法提供严谨的模拟基准测试平台并已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地下（SubT）环境难以进入，现有的机器人研究往往缺乏大规模、统计意义上的模拟基准测试，导致算法验证仅限于少数环境，评估不够严谨。

Method: 提出 SubTGraph 框架，基于拓扑及用户定义的约束条件（拓扑、维度、材质等），构建成本矩阵，利用改进的 Dijkstra 算法结合 DARPA 世界生成器中的拓扑瓦片（topometric tiles）程序化生成高度可变的地下环境。

Result: 成功构建了包含 150 个高度可变地下场景的数据库，并通过结构语义分割、多智能体路径规划、LIO SLAM 三个典型案例，验证了该方案在评估自主导航堆栈不同层级性能方面的有效性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SubTGraph 为地下机器人自主性的严谨测试提供了一种高效的生成手段，填补了大规模基准测试基础设施的空白，促进了复杂环境（如矿井、溶洞）下的机器人算法验证与性能分析。

Abstract: Subterranean (SubT) environments have been a frontier for autonomous robotics, driven by the push for automation of mining operations and the interest in planetary exploration (Martian Lava Tubes). Due to the challenges involved in accessing real SubT environments, rigorous hardening of autonomy stacks in realistic simulation environments is critical. This article fills a well-known gap, which relates to the unavailability of a large-scale simulation-based benchmarking infrastructure for rigorous statistical evaluation of robotic autonomy, due to which it is common for SubT research articles to present validation results in a few environments at best.
  This article presents SubTGraph, a novel framework for rapid synthesis of multi-level SubT environments with high variability, incorporating user specifications related to topology, dimensionality, textures, etc., to generate distinct environments such as operational mines, natural caves and lava tubes. SubTGraph builds a cost matrix from user-specified structural constraints to guide the classical Dijkstra algorithm to procedurally generate SubT worlds utilizing topometric tiles from the DARPA World Generator. Three robotics case-studies are investigated to demonstrate the utility of SubTGraph for rigorous validation of different layers in the robotic autonomy stack. Structural semantic segmentation is validated against topometric ground truths, multi-agent path planning is widely tested for identification of patterns and trends in the algorithm behavior and LIO SLAM is stress-tested in challenging subterranean sections to identify failure cases. The SubTGraph world creation codebase is open-sourced (https://github.com/LTU-RAI/SubTGraph.git) along with a database consisting of 150 highly variable underground worlds.

</details>


### [100] [WiXus: A Wheeled-Legged Robot with Wire-Driven Environmental Utilizing to Integrate Mobility and Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20932)
*Shintaro Inoue,Kento Kawaharazuka,Temma Suzuki,Sota Yuzaki,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型轮腿机器人 WiXus，通过引入绳索驱动机构与环境交互，成功将机器人从单一的移动平台扩展为具备复杂三维移动与物体操作能力的通用作业平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的轮腿机器人大多仅专注于移动能力，缺乏对象操纵或工具使用等执行任务的额外功能。本研究旨在通过外部环境支撑来解放腿足，以挖掘其潜在的任务执行能力。

Method: 提出并开发了WiXus机器人，将传统的轮腿驱动机构与外部支持的绳索驱动机构相结合。通过绳索悬挂机体，将腿部从支撑移动中解放出来，使其能够作为机械臂执行操作任务。

Result: WiXus不仅保持了平面移动能力，还能通过绳索与轮腿协同实现攀爬等三维移动；同时，在绳索悬挂模式下，成功演示了物体操纵（如救援）和工具使用（如采摘）功能。

Conclusion: 利用绳索驱动与环境交互的设计原则，有效地扩展了轮腿机器人的作业能力边界，实现了从单纯移动平台向多功能操作机器人的跨越。

Abstract: Wheeled-legged robots, which have wheels at their feet and achieve high mobility by coordinating wheel drive and leg drive, have been developed. These robots have been developed purely as platforms specialized for locomotion. Therefore, they do not have a means to repurpose their legs for roles other than locomotion, such as object manipulation or tool utilization. In this paper, we address the problem of how to draw out the potential task-execution capability of the legs by freeing them from the roles of locomotion through external body support. To this end, we propose and develop a new robot, WiXus, which fuses a wheeled-legged mechanism with a wire-driven mechanism that utilizes the external environment. The developed WiXus demonstrates not only planar locomotion with wheeled-legged drive, but also three-dimensional mobility such as cliff climbing by coordinating wire-driven and wheeled-legged actuation. Furthermore, by suspending the body with wire-driven actuation, WiXus successfully repurpose its legs as arms to perform object manipulation, (e.g., rescuing a dog (stuffed animal)), and tool utilization (e.g., harvesting an apple (mockup) with loppers). This study demonstrates that the approach of utilizing the environment with wire-driven actuation is a new design principle that extends the operational domain of wheeled-legged robots.

</details>


### [101] [Component Influence-Driven Fastener Reduction for Robotic Disassemblability-Aware Design Simplification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21026)
*Takuya Kiyokawa,Tomoki Ishikura,Shingo Hamada,Genichiro Matsuda,Kensuke Harada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于CAD模型的自动化框架，通过量化分析识别并建议移除阻碍机器人拆解的冗余紧固件，从而优化产品设计，提升自动化再制造的效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了加速自动化再制造，需要在产品设计阶段考虑机器人拆解需求；然而，设计人员目前缺乏定量反馈工具来识别阻碍机器人操作的结构元素（尤其是紧固件）。

Method: 1. 基于CAD模型自动生成接触-连接-约束（CCC）图；2. 利用机器人拆解序列规划结果计算“组件影响分数”；3. 通过3D热力图将影响分数映射至CAD几何模型；4. 对高影响力的紧固件进行模拟移除，并评估结构稳定性、工具切换频率和机器人移动距离。

Result: 在七种家用电器上的实验表明，该框架能有效识别冗余紧固件。移除这些紧固件后，结构约束减少了8至132个，机器人工具切换次数减少，移动距离缩短了165至1675毫米，且未损害结构完整性。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种有效的方法，通过定量分析促进工业产品的精简设计（DFD），在保证结构稳定性的前提下，减少紧固件数量，显著提升了机器人拆解的效率，为实现自动化再制造提供了工程化指导。

Abstract: To accelerate automated remanufacturing, robotic disassembly must be considered during the product design phase. However, designers currently lack quantitative feedback to identify which structural elements hinder robotic operations. To address this, this study proposes an analytical framework that provides actionable redesign guidance focused on fastener reduction, as fasteners are numerous and ubiquitous components found in almost all manufactured products. Using a Computer-Aided Design (CAD) model and its automatically generated Contact-Connection-Constraint (CCC) graph, the framework translates robotic disassembly sequence planning outcomes into component influence scores. These scores reflect how often a component causes structural constraint violations or evaluation objective deteriorations in the robotic disassembly sequence. To visually highlight structural hindrances, the framework projects these scores onto the CAD geometry as 3D heatmaps. The system then analytically simulates the removal of highly influential fasteners. It reports the expected reductions in structural constraints, tool changes, and robot travel distances, while preventing structurally unsafe modifications by evaluating geometric stability metrics. Experiments on seven household appliances demonstrate that the framework successfully targets redundant fasteners. Removing the recommended fasteners simplified the structural dependencies by eliminating between 8 and 132 structural constraints on the graph depending on each product's structural configuration. Furthermore, it improved robotic operational efficiency by eliminating unnecessary tool change operations and shortening travel distances by 165 to 1675 millimeters wherever structurally permissible.

</details>


### [102] [Modeling and Control of a Pneumatic Morphing Soft Quadrotor based on the SOFA Framework for Dynamic Soft Robotic Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21031)
*F. Labra Caso,V. Sumathy,P. Ferrentino,V. Vanderborght,J. Haluska,G. Nikolakopoulos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于SOFA有限元法的气动软体四旋翼建模与控制框架，通过引入物理可解释的动力学模型和PI控制器，实现了对软体臂变形行为的精确模拟与位置控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有四旋翼模型难以准确描述气动软体臂复杂且随时间变化的变形行为，缺乏既具备物理可解释性又能进行精确动力学控制的建模方案。

Method: 基于SOFA框架的有限元方法（FEM），将气动软体臂离散化为四面体网格，应用弹性材料定律计算内部力，并结合比例-积分（PI）控制器实现对气动驱动信号的闭环控制。

Result: 仿真实验表明，所提出的建模框架和PI控制器能够准确捕捉软体变形动力学，并有效控制气动软体臂达到预期的目标位置。

Conclusion: 该方法成功结合了物理可解释的动力学模型与SOFA有限元仿真，有效实现了气动软体四旋翼的建模、动态模拟及位置控制，验证了该框架在描述软体变形行为上的有效性。

Abstract: This article presents a novel SOFA based finite element method for the soft body modeling and the corresponding dynamic simulation and control of a pneumatic morphing soft quadrotor. The proposed modeling preserves the physical interpretability and control structure of traditional quadrotor dynamics, while capturing the complex, time-varying behavior of pneumatically actuated soft arms. In SOFA, the soft pneumatically actuated arms are discretized as a tetrahedral mesh following an elastic material law that produces internal forces adequate to the real dynamic behavior of the body. Pneumatic actuation governed by both periodic and error-based control signals is applied within the internal cavities to analyze the morphing capability. Finally, a proportional-integral controller is proposed to study the controlled dynamic behavior and morphing capabilities of the pneumatic arm, wherein the pneumatic actuation to the soft arm is controlled to achieve the desired target position. The simulation results show the effectiveness of the proposed novel modeling framework and the related controller design.

</details>


### [103] [Perception of Social Robots as Communication Partners in Healthcare for Older Adults](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21053)
*Hana Yamamoto,Carlotta Julia Mayer,Charlotte Raithel,Theresa Buchner,Christian Werner,Yasuhisa Hirata,Monika Eckstein,Katja Mombaur*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究通过多维度对比分析，证明了社交机器人能作为压力适度的老年人交互对象，在减轻护理负担方面表现出潜力，但也指出了外观与内容匹配性的设计优化空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应对全球护理人员严重短缺的现状，评估社交辅助机器人在老年人交互过程中的心理和生理影响，并探讨机器人是否能替代人类提供同等有效的互动。

Method: 对35名70岁以上的老年人进行对比研究，采用多模态分析方法，结合面部表情识别、心率变异性（生理指标）及主观问卷调查。

Result: 研究发现，与人类互动相比，老年人在与机器人互动时并无显著的压力差异；生理指标显示心率略低（更放松）；面部表情分析确认了机器人已被接受为有效的互动对象。

Conclusion: 社交机器人可作为有效的互动伙伴，无需担忧引发老年人的心理压力，且在执行结构化任务（如健康调查）时具备减轻护理负担的潜力。未来的研究需改进机器人的外观与内容匹配度，以优化交互体验。

Abstract: Addressing the global caregiver shortage through socially assistive robots necessitates a deep understanding of their psychological and physiological impacts on older adults during human-robot interaction (HRI). This study addresses whether social robots can serve as effective interaction partners compared to humans, and if "positive prompts" can similarly enhance these interactions. We conducted a comparative study with 35 participants (aged 70+). Our multi-modal analysis, integrating facial expression data, heart rate variability, and subjective questionnaires, revealed no significant differences in overall stress levels between human and robot interactions. Facial expression analysis confirmed that the robot was accepted as a valid interaction partner, while physiological data showed slightly lower heart rates during robot interactions, suggesting a more relaxed state compared to human-led sessions. These findings indicate that social robots can engage older adults without inducing psychological strain and are capable of alleviating caregiver burden by performing structured tasks, such as health-sensing surveys. Future work should address the identified "appearance-content mismatch" in robot design to facilitate even more natural and effective interactions.

</details>


### [104] [Anomaly-Informed Confidence Calibration for Vision-Based Safety Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21109)
*Zhenjiang Mao,Jiawen Wu,Gabriel Wagner,Zhongzheng Zhang,Ivan Ruchkin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种融合感知与动力学异常分数的在线校准方法，通过监测世界模型的状态，有效缓解了自动驾驶控制器在未知分布偏移下的过度自信问题，显著降低了安全风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉控制器在自动驾驶中常因分布偏移（Distribution Shift）产生危险的过度自信。现有的异常检测方案（如重构误差）仅关注图像视觉层面的伪影，无法捕捉动力学异常（如执行器偏差、处理延迟），导致在图像外观正常但车辆行为异常时产生误判。

Method: 提出了一种名为“异常感知在线校准”（Anomaly-Informed Online Calibration）的方法。该方法基于世界模型（World Model）提取两个互补的异常分数：1. 感知分数（来自重构误差）；2. 动力学分数（来自认知不确定性和控制流统计量）。随后利用轻量级的温度缩放校准器和测试时增强（Test-time Augmentation）在不修改核心模型架构的前提下执行在线置信度校准。

Result: 在物理DonkeyCar（驴车）平台上进行的四类真实世界异常测试（黑暗、模糊、执行器偏差、处理延迟）表现显示，该方法将平均预期校准误差（ECE）从0.184降低至0.116，性能提升了37%。

Conclusion: 该方法能够有效评估自动驾驶中的模型置信度，显著缓解了分布偏移下的过拟合问题，证明了通过融合感知与动力学异常分数可以更全面地检测未知风险，且无需重新训练现有模型。

Abstract: Reliable confidence estimates are important for safely deploying vision-based controllers in autonomous racing, where safety predictions must be derived from camera images, yet modern predictors become dangerously overconfident under test-time distribution shifts. We identify a critical perception-dynamics gap in existing anomaly signals: widely used scores, such as autoencoder reconstruction error, capture visual corruptions but miss dynamics anomalies (e.g., actuation bias, latency), where images remain plausible while the trajectory degrades. To address this, we propose an Anomaly-Informed Online Calibration approach that, without retraining any model component, fuses two complementary anomaly scores extracted from a world model: a perceptual score from reconstruction error and a dynamics score from epistemic uncertainty and control-stream statistics. Based on these fused scores, a lightweight temperature-scaling calibrator leverages test-time augmentation to selectively reduce overconfidence under shift while preserving nominal-condition performance. Experiments on a physical DonkeyCar under four real-world anomaly protocols unseen during training (darkness, blur, actuation bias, processing latency) reduce average expected calibration error from 0.184 to 0.116, a 37% improvement over the best baseline, without modifying the base safety predictor.

</details>


### [105] [Benchmarking Empirical and Learning-Based Approaches for Feedforward Steering Control in Autonomous Racing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21111)
*Georg Jank,Mattia Piccinini,Sebastian Wenk,Phillip Pitschi,Johannes Betz,Boris Lohmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对自动驾驶赛车，本文对比了学习型与经验型前馈转向控制策略，发现基于多项式拟合的经验模型（EHD）虽开环精度非最优，但在闭环测试中展现了最强的鲁棒性和最佳的单圈时间性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈转向控制是自动驾驶赛车层级控制架构的核心，旨在通过预测车辆逆向横向动力学来减轻反馈控制器的负担。研究目的是评估不同前馈控制策略在实际性能表现上的差异，并确定最优方案。

Method: 提出了一种基于多项式曲面拟合的新型经验横向动力学（EHD）公式；对比了两种学习型控制器与两种经验型（解析型）控制器；利用高保真双轨车辆动力学模拟器（基于阿布扎比自动驾驶赛车联盟环境）进行了开环预测误差评估与完整闭环路径跟踪测试。

Result: 尽管学习型控制器在开环预测中误差最低，但在闭环测试中并未表现出性能优势；相比之下，本文提出的EHD方法在闭环鲁棒性和单圈时间上表现最佳。

Conclusion: 在自动驾驶赛车的完整控制架构中，简单且鲁棒的经验模型（如本文提出的EHD）通常优于基于学习的模型。闭环测试对于评估前馈控制策略至关重要，单纯追求开环预测精度并不能转化为实际的赛道性能优势。

Abstract: Feedforward steering control is a key component of hierarchical control architectures for autonomous racing. The goal is to reduce steering corrections from the feedback controllers by predicting the vehicle's inverse lateral dynamics. This paper presents a systematic benchmark of two learning-based and two empirical (analytical) feedforward steering controllers. We introduce a new \acf{ehd} formulation based on a polynomial surface fit that captures velocity-dependent nonlinear steering behavior with minimal parametrization. We test the feedforward controllers in a high-fidelity simulation framework based on the real-world Abu Dhabi Autonomous Racing League competition, using a high-fidelity double-track vehicle dynamics simulator. Open-loop evaluation shows that the learning-based controllers achieve the lowest prediction errors; however, closed-loop testing reveals that this improved accuracy does not translate into superior path tracking performance or lap times, even after iterative fine-tuning. In contrast, the proposed EHD approach achieves the best overall closed-loop robustness and lap time, highlighting the necessity of evaluating feedforward strategies within the complete trajectory planning and control software stack. Our code is available at https://github.com/TUMRT/steering_ff_control.

</details>


### [106] [Humanoid Whole-Body Manipulation via Active Spatial Brain and Generalizable Action Cerebellum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21133)
*Zhizhao Liang,Yi-Lin Wei,Xuhang Chen,Mu Lin,Yi-Xiang He,Zhexi Luo,Jun-Hui Liu,Kun-Yu Lin,Wei-Shi Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对人形机器人全身操作面临的空间理解难与数据依赖性强的问题，本文提出了一种基于多智能体大模型的框架（含“主动空间大脑”与“通用动作小脑”），实现了无需特定任务真实数据的高效空间感知与泛化动作生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人全身操作在复杂3D场景中面临两个核心挑战：一是在多样化空间关系下的空间理解难度大；二是受限于昂贵且稀缺的真实机器人数据，动作生成的泛化能力较差。

Method: 提出了一个包含两个组件的通用人形全身操作框架：1. Active Spatial Brain（主动空间大脑）：利用多智能体大模型进行主动空间感知、任务规划和子任务分解；2. Generalizable Action Cerebellum（通用动作小脑）：根据大脑的决策生成可执行的机器人全身动作，无需任务特定的真实机器人数据即可泛化。

Result: 实验验证表明，该框架在复杂的空间操作任务中展现了强大的空间感知与理解能力，并在多类多样化环境中表现出优异的真实机器人任务执行性能。

Conclusion: 本研究提出的多智能体大模型框架成功实现了通用人形全身操作，无需任务特定的真实机器人数据即可完成复杂的空间操作任务，在感知和执行层面均表现出色。

Abstract: In this paper, we explore spatial-aware humanoid whole-body manipulation task.
