Compared with tabletop settings, this task poses two key challenges: 1) Spatial understanding is challenging in complex 3D environments with diverse spatial relations. 2) Action generation is difficult to generalize, as limited and costly real-robot data restricts data-driven models generalization. To address these challenges, we propose a generalizable humanoid loco-manipulation framework that leverages the spatial perception and action generation capabilities of multi-agent large models. Specifically, our framework includes two components: Active Spatial Brain for active spatial perception and decision-making, and Generalizable Action Cerebellum for executable robot action generation. The first component actively perceives the spatial scene and makes decisions on task planning and subtask decomposition. The second component generate executable robot actions based on the decisions made by the first module without needs of task-specific real robot data. To benchmark our framework, we design a set of spatial manipulation tasks from two perspectives: evaluating spatial perception and understanding, and assessing real-robot task performance. The results demonstrate strong performance on both aspects across diverse tasks and environments.

</details>


### [107] [Safety-Critical Control for Smoothed Implicit Contact Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21138)
*Haegu Lee,Yitaek Kim,Christoffer Sloth*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种保障平滑隐式接触动力系统安全性的控制框架，通过智能筛选平滑参数与补偿鲁棒裕度的CBF方法，解决了接触丰富任务中因约束松弛导致的力违规难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平滑隐式接触动力学虽然允许进行基于梯度的规划，但其对接触互补约束的松弛会导致接触力逼近误差，传统的安全过滤器（如CBF）在处理这种松弛动力学时存在难度，难以保证接触丰富系统执行过程中的安全性。

Method: 1. 分析平滑参数κ与接触力逼近误差及安全性之间的非单调关系；2. 引入“边界聚焦回滚”（boundary-focused rollouts）来选择合适的κ；3. 开发基于隐式接触动力学一阶泰勒近似的离散时间控制障碍函数（CBF）；4. 在安全约束中添加固定鲁棒裕度以补偿力预测偏差。

Result: 在四个接触丰富系统上的仿真表明，该方法成功消除了在标准CBF框架下观察到的接触力违规现象，显著提升了控制系统的安全性。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种基于平滑隐式接触动力学的安全控制框架。通过边界聚焦的回滚机制筛选平滑参数κ，结合一阶泰勒近似构建离散时间控制障碍函数（CBF），并通过预设鲁棒裕度修正力预测误差，有效解决了接触丰富任务中的安全性问题，消除了标准CBF下的力违规现象。

Abstract: Smoothed implicit contact dynamics enables gradient-based planning and control for contact-rich tasks without predefined mode sequences. However, safety-critical control remains challenging because implicit contact dynamics makes safety-filter design nontrivial. The smoothing parameter $κ$ relaxes contact complementarity constraints, which makes the dynamics smooth but affects the contact force. This paper provides a method for bounding the actual contact force despite the use of relaxed complementarity constraints. We show that constraint violations can be non-monotonic in $κ$. Smaller $κ$ reduces force-approximation error, but it does not necessarily improve safety performance. To address this issue, we introduce boundary-focused rollouts to screen $κ$ by comparing the safety margin with the approximation error. We then develop a discrete-time control barrier function (CBF) framework based on a first-order Taylor approximation of the implicitly defined contact force. To account for possible force under-prediction, we augment the resulting safety constraint with a fixed robust margin. Simulations on four contact-rich systems show that the proposed method eliminates force violations observed under a standard CBF.

</details>


### [108] [EllipseLIO: Adaptive LiDAR Inertial Odometry with an Ellipsoid Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21150)
*Rowan Border,Margarita Chli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EllipseLIO 是一种通用且鲁棒的实时 LiDAR 惯性里程计算法，通过自适应机制消除了对场景特定调参的依赖，提供了远超现有方法的性能表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 LIO 方法在不同环境或使用不同传感器时，通常需要复杂的参数调整才能保持性能，缺乏跨场景的泛化能力和鲁棒性。

Method: 提出了一种自适应的 LiDAR 惯性里程计算法 EllipseLIO，通过引入自适应的 LiDAR 点云过滤和配准机制，能够根据不同环境和传感器特性自动调整，无需针对场景进行特定参数配置。

Result: 在五个具有挑战性的数据集上进行的实验表明，EllipseLIO 是表现最好的方法，平均轨迹误差较第二名降低了 38%，且在所有实验场景中均未发生发散。

Conclusion: EllipseLIO 通过自适应滤波和配准技术，成功实现了跨传感器和跨环境的一致鲁棒性，在不需人工微调的情况下，优于现有的各类先进 LIO 方法。

Abstract: LiDAR Inertial Odometry (LIO) is a critical component for many mobile robots that need to navigate without relying on external positioning (e.g., GPS). Platforms that operate autonomously in different environments and with heterogeneous LiDAR sensors require a LIO approach that can adapt to these different scenarios without human intervention.
  Existing LIO approaches can typically provide reliable and accurate odometry in scenarios with similar environments and sensors when suitably tuned. However, many approaches struggle to retain robust odometry across heterogeneous environments and sensors while using a consistent configuration.
  This paper presents EllipseLIO, a real-time LIO approach that generalises between scenarios by using methods for LiDAR scan filtering and registration that adapt to the sensor capabilities and environment without requiring scenario-specific tuning. Experiments with EllipseLIO and state-of-the-art LIO approaches on five datasets with diverse and challenging scenarios demonstrate that EllipseLIO is the best-performing approach overall. It achieves a 38% lower odometry error on average than the second-best approach and is the only approach that does not diverge in any experiment. An open-source version of EllipseLIO will be available at github.com/v4rl-ucy/ellipselio.

</details>


### [109] [A Terrain-Adaptive epsilon-Constraint MPC for Uneven Terrain Kinodynamic Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21188)
*Otobong Jerome,Geesara Kalathunga,Tiago Nascimento*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于地形感知的自适应epsilon-MPC框架，通过半参数动力学建模实现了非平坦地形下的高效、稳定轨迹规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在不平坦地形上进行类汽车车辆的运动学动力学规划时，路径效率与姿态稳定性往往存在冲突，现有方法难以实时高效地进行多目标权衡。

Method: 1. 提出一种集成自适应epsilon-constraint方法的模型预测控制（MPC）框架；2. 开发了一种结合分析动力学与稀疏高斯过程（SGP）的半参数化模型，用于捕捉车辆在不平地形上的动力学特性；3. 基于地形描述符动态调整约束边界，实时探索帕累托前沿。

Result: 导航成功率达到94%，最大姿态偏差降低了24%，多目标权衡质量提升了23%。

Conclusion: 提出的epsilon-MPC框架在复杂非平坦地形中通过动态约束调整，有效平衡了路径效率与机器人状态稳定性，优于现有的MPPI和GAKD方法。

Abstract: Kinodynamic planning for car-like vehicles on uneven terrain requires simultaneously optimizing competing objectives such as path efficiency and pose stability. This work presents an adaptive epsilon-constraint method integrated into a Model Predictive Control (MPC) framework, where the epsilon bounds are dynamically adjusted based on terrain descriptors to explore the Pareto front in real time. To capture vehicle-terrain dynamics, we develop a semi-parametric model combining analytical vehicle dynamics with a Sparse Gaussian Process (SGP) trained on the same terrain descriptors. The proposed epsilon-MPC is evaluated against MPPI and GAKD baselines, achieving a 94% navigation success rate while reducing maximum orientation deviation by 24% and improving multi-objective trade-off quality by 23%.

</details>


### [110] [To Select or not to Select, that is the Question: Distilling Robot Skill Prediction into a Small Ensemble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21242)
*Haechan Mark Bong,Simon Roy,Euhid Aman,Giovanni Beltrame*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种利用LLM生成合成数据来训练小型专用模型的方法，实现了在多异构机器人车队中优于大型LLM的任务技能匹配效果，解决了特定机器人物理能力映射数据匮乏的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着异构机器人车队（如人形、四足、无人机等）的发展，如何将特定任务分配给具备相应物理能力的机器人成为关键的系统性问题，但目前缺乏将任务描述与机器人物理能力对应的标签数据。

Method: 利用大模型（LLM）生成并审计合成的数据集，用于训练包含两个微调句子编码器（mpnet + MiniLM）的约133M参数集成模型，实现从自然语言任务描述到物理能力（技能）的映射。

Result: 该模型在200个任务组成的均衡数据集上达到83.5%的学习能力匹配准确率，超过了Kimi K2 (72.0%)、GPT-OSS-120B (71.5%)和Llama-4-Scout-17B (69.0%)的零样本准确率。

Conclusion: 对于预定义的机器人技能分类体系，训练在合成数据上的小型专用模型在任务分配任务中优于大型通用大语言模型（LLM）。

Abstract: As robot fleets become more heterogeneous, including humanoids, rovers, quadrupeds, and drones, selecting the right robot for a task becomes a core systems problem. We study robot skill prediction: mapping a natural-language task description to the physical capabilities required to execute it, such as fly, wheels, legs, surface water, under water and hands. Since labelled data that maps natural-language task descriptions to robot's physical capabilities does not exist, we construct a synthetic task-to-skill dataset using LLM-assisted generation and targeted label auditing. Trained on this data, a ~133M-parameter ensemble of two fine-tuned sentence encoders (mpnet + MiniLM) reaches 83.5% task-to-skill matching on a stratified 200 task dataset, outperforming Kimi K2 (1T MoE) at 72.0%, GPT-OSS-120B at 71.5%, and Llama-4-Scout-17B at 69.0% under the same zero-shot prompt. These results suggest that, for fixed robot skill taxonomies, small specialized models trained on synthetic data can outperform much larger general-purpose LLMs for fleet-level task routing.

</details>


### [111] [Reinforcement Learning for Risk Adaptation via Differentiable CVaR Barrier Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21257)
*Xinyi Wang,Taekyung Kim,Bardh Hoxha,Georgios Fainekos,Dimitra Panagou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种结合强化学习与基于风险障碍函数的安全规划框架，实现了拥挤不确定环境下的高效自动导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具有不确定运动障碍物的拥挤环境中，传统的规划方法往往因处理随机交互不当而导致行为过于保守或效率低下。

Method: 提出了一种端到端的风险自适应导航框架，结合了强化学习（RL）与基于条件风险价值（CVaR）障碍函数的微分二次规划（QP）安全层，通过联合学习标称控制输入、风险等级及安全裕度来执行显式的概率安全约束。

Result: 在不同障碍物密度和机器人模型的动态拥挤环境下进行了广泛验证，证明了该方法在安全性、执行效率及对分布外（OOD）场景的泛化能力方面均达到了最优水平。

Conclusion: 该方法成功平衡了复杂动态环境下的安全性与效率，通过自适应风险管理实现了对不确定性的鲁棒响应，在多项指标上优于现有的优化类、强化学习类及其融合算法。

Abstract: Planning through crowded environments under uncertain obstacle motions remains difficult, as stochastic interactions often induce overly conservative behavior or reduced efficiency. To address this challenge, we propose an end-to-end risk adaptation framework for crowd navigation under obstacle-motion uncertainty modeled by a Gaussian mixture model. The framework combines reinforcement learning~(RL) with a differentiable quadratic-program safety layer based on Conditional Value-at-Risk~(CVaR) barrier functions, jointly learning nominal control input, risk level, and safety margin and enforcing explicit probabilistic safety constraints. This design enables context-aware adaptation, promoting efficient behavior while invoking caution only when necessary. We conduct extensive evaluations in dynamic, uncertain, and crowded environments across varying obstacle densities and robot models, and further assess generalization under three out-of-distribution cases. Comparisons across optimization-based, RL-based, and integrated RL and optimization methods are provided, and the proposed method is shown to deliver the strongest overall performance in safety, efficiency, and generalization under uncertainty.

</details>


### [112] [Learning Structural Latent Points for Efficient Visual Representations in Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21258)
*Yicheng Jiang,Jiaxu Wang,Junhao He,Zesen Gan,Junhao Li,Qiang Zhang,Jingkai Sun,Jiahang Cao,Mingyuan Sun,Xiangyu Yue,Qiming Shao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对现有3D预训练方法在隐式与显式表示上的局限性，本文提出一种结构化潜点（Structural Latent Points）混合表示方法，结合轻量化3DGS渲染，显著提升了具身智能体的感知与操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的3D感知与操作预训练方法存在局限性：纯隐式表示缺乏结构线索，而纯显式表示存在分辨率瓶颈且泛化能力弱。需要一种兼顾表达能力与几何结构先验的新型表示方法。

Method: 1. 设计了“结构化潜点”作为混合表示范式；2. 在点云自编码器的潜空间中引入点维度的变分自编码器（VAE），将点特征和坐标正则化至高斯先验；3. 开发了轻量化、高效率的3DGS（3D Gaussian Splatting）渲染流水线，将计算资源集中于特征表示模块。

Result: 在RLBench、ManiSkill2基准测试及真实机器人实验中，该框架在任务成功率、样本效率以及对视角和场景的鲁棒性方面均优于现有基准模型。消融实验证实了各组件的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于结构化潜点（Structural Latent Points）的混合表示预训练框架。该方法平衡了隐式表示（表达力强）和显式表示（结构先验好）的优缺点，通过高效的轻量级3DGS渲染流水线实现了在具身操作任务中更高的成功率、样本效率及鲁棒性。

Abstract: Current 3D-aware pretraining methods for embodied perception and manipulation are largely built on differentiable rendering frameworks, producing either fully implicit neural fields or fully explicit geometric primitives. Implicit representations, while expressive, lack explicit structural cues, whereas explicit ones preserve geometry but suffer from resolution limits and weak generalization. To address these limitations, we propose a novel pretraining framework that learns a hybrid representation-structural latent points. Specifically, we insert a point-wise latent variational autoencoder into the latent space of a point-cloud autoencoder, jointly regularizing point-wise features and coordinates toward a Gaussian prior. The resulting compact latent preserves coarse structural tendencies, which do not encode precise geometry but capture richer rough shape and semantic information, effectively combining the expressiveness of implicit representations with the structural priors of explicit ones. In addition, informed by shared design choices in prior work, we develop a streamlined, efficient 3DGS-based rendering pipeline that is deliberately kept lightweight, improving efficiency while leaving greater representational capacity to the front-end latent module. Extensive evaluations on RLBench, ManiSkill2, and a real-robot platform demonstrate consistent gains in task success, sample efficiency, and robustness to viewpoint and scene variations over strong baselines. Ablation studies further confirm that each component of our framework is critical to overall performance.

</details>


### [113] [Learning Robust Dexterous In-Hand Manipulation from Joint Sensors with Proprioceptive Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21330)
*Senlan Yao,Chenyu Yang,Jaehoon Kim,Aristotelis Sympetheros,Robert K. Katzschmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种仅依赖关节传感器数据的Proprioceptive Transformer (PT)方法，通过教师-学生蒸馏架构，成功在肌腱驱动灵巧手上实现了高性能的对象操控，证明了本体感受在提取环境信息方面的强大潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧手操纵多依赖视觉或触觉，忽略了最易获得的关节传感器数据（特别是肌腱驱动手），论文旨在探讨仅通过本体感受实现高性能操纵的可行性及其潜力。

Method: 1. 提出“本体感受Transformer”（Proprioceptive Transformer, PT）；2. 采用特权强化学习（Privileged Reinforcement Learning）训练教师策略，随后通过知识蒸馏（Knowledge Distillation）迁移至PT；3. 利用Transformer架构处理关节位置和速度的时间序列，以隐式提取环境及物体状态信息。

Result: 在ORCA现实灵巧手上实验表明，相比基准方法，该方法旋转速度提升了3.1倍，且方块位置估计的均方根误差（RMSE）降低了23.4%。

Conclusion: 通过利用强化学习蒸馏和Transformer架构，仅依靠本体感受（关节传感器）数据即可实现高性能的灵巧手操纵，证明了在缺乏外部感知的情况下，关节传感能提取足够的隐式信息以支持复杂任务。

Abstract: In-hand object manipulation is a fundamental yet challenging capability for dexterous robots. Despite significant progress in dexterous manipulation, existing approaches rely heavily on vision or tactile sensing to track object states, while joint sensing -- the most readily available modality on any robotic hand -- remains largely overlooked, particularly for tendon-driven hands. In this paper, we study how far joint sensing alone can go by asking: (i) whether motor encoders or direct joint sensing provides better proprioceptive feedback, (ii) how to extract environment information from joint measurements, and (iii) whether joint-only control can achieve competitive real-world performance without external perception. We present the Proprioceptive Transformer (PT), an exteroceptive-free approach for continuous cube rotation on a tendon-driven dexterous hand that uses only joint sensing feedback. A teacher policy is first trained via reinforcement learning with privileged object information, then distilled into PT, which operates solely on joint position and velocity histories. The Transformer architecture effectively extracts implicit object state information from temporal patterns in joint sensor readings. Experiments on the real ORCA hand show that our approach achieves 3.1x higher rotation speed than baselines. We also demonstrate that our PT achieves a 23.4% lower RMSE for cube position estimation than the MLP baseline, indicating superior extraction of exteroceptive information from proprioceptive sources.

</details>


### [114] [From swept contact to pose: Probe-aware registration via complementary-shape docking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21398)
*Chen Chen,Yunwen Li,Yifan Xu,Xiangjie Yan,Chang Shu,Jianxia Hou,Shiji Song,Xiang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种免标定、基于接触扫掠体积互补形状对接的配准算法，通过全局搜索加局部细化，实现了高精度、抗干扰的机器人位姿配准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的光学配准方法存在标定链路过长、视线遮挡及制造误差等问题，难以满足高精度机器人操作需求。

Method: 将接触配准重构为物体与探针扫掠体积之间的互补形状对接问题；结合了3D FFT相关性全局搜索与基于李代数更新及解析接触灵敏度的局部SE(3)持续细化。

Result: 仿真实验达到亚0.04mm和亚0.4°的精度；在牙科制备机器人实验中达到0.42mm和3.75°，优于传统光学追踪配准方法。

Conclusion: 该方法提出了一种无需外部光学传感器、利用接触与非接触信息进行配准的高精度替代方案，在牙科机器人等实际应用中表现优异。

Abstract: Accurate registration between a prior model and the real scene is essential for high-precision robotic manipulation, yet optical methods suffer from long calibration chains, line-of-sight constraints, and fabrication errors. We propose a calibration-free alternative that reformulates contact registration as complementary-shape docking between the object and the probe's swept volume, explicitly accounting for probe geometry and leveraging both contact and non-contact evidence. Our solver integrates a global-to-local search via 3D FFT correlation over low-discrepancy SO(3) samples, then followed by continuous SE(3) refinement using Lie-algebra updates and analytic contact sensitivities. This pipeline yields efficient exploration and metric-grade convergence without fragile point correspondences. Simulation across free-form meshes achieved sub-0.04 mm and sub-0.4° accuracy and robustness to pose noise and contact loss. On a tooth-preparation robot, our method attained 0.42 mm and 3.75°, outperforming an optical tracker registration while requiring no external sensors. These results demonstrate a practical and precise registration strategy for surgical and industrial robots.

</details>


### [115] [PointACT: Vision-Language-Action Models with Multi-Scale Point-Action Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21414)
*Shizhe Chen,Paul Pacaud,Cordelia Schmid*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PointACT通过将层级化3D点云特征融入VLA模型，显著增强了机器人对3D环境的空间感知和鲁棒操作能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型多依赖2D视觉表示，限制了其对复杂3D环境中精细几何结构和空间定位的推理能力，难以满足精确操作需求。

Method: 提出了PointACT，这是一个双系统3D感知VLA策略，直接将层级化3D点云表示整合到动作解码过程中，并采用多尺度点-动作交互机制及高效的瓶颈窗口自注意力机制。

Result: 在LIBERO和RLBench基准测试中表现优于现有的单体和双系统VLA基线，在RLBench-10Tasks任务上成功率提升了10%；在冻结视觉语言主干且从头训练动作专家的情况下，提升效果更为显著。

Conclusion: 将层级化3D几何特征与预训练2D语义表示紧密结合，对于实现鲁棒且空间感知的机器人控制至关重要；预训练的3D表示在提升VLA策略方面具有巨大潜力。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown strong potential for general-purpose robotic manipulation by leveraging large pretrained vision-language backbones. However, most existing VLAs rely primarily on 2D visual representations, which limit their ability to reason about fine-grained geometry and spatial grounding - capabilities that are essential for precise and robust manipulation in 3D environments. In this paper, we propose PointACT, a dual-system 3D-aware VLA policy that integrates hierarchical 3D point cloud representations directly into the action decoding process. PointACT employs a multi-scale point-action interaction mechanism with efficient bottleneck window self-attention, enabling evolving action tokens to densely attend to both local geometric detail and global scene structure. We evaluate PointACT on the LIBERO and RLBench benchmarks and systematically compare it against monolithic and dual-system VLA baselines, including variants augmented with point cloud inputs. PointACT achieves consistent improvements across both benchmarks, increasing success rates by 10% on the challenging RLBench-10Tasks suite over state-of-the-art pretrained VLAs, with even larger gains when the vision-language backbone is frozen and the action expert is trained from scratch. Extensive ablation studies demonstrate that tightly coupling hierarchical 3D geometry with pretrained 2D semantic representations is critical for robust and spatially grounded robot control. Our results also highlight the promise of pretrained 3D representations for 3D-aware VLA policies.

</details>


### [116] [roto 2.0: The Robot Tactile Olympiad](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21429)
*Elle Miller,Jayaram Reddy,Ayush Deshmukh,Trevor McInroe,David Abel,Oisin Mac Aodha,Sethu Vijayakumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了 roto 2.0——一个用于触觉强化学习的 GPU 加速基准测试工具，通过减少对外部状态信息的依赖，实现了更高效的“盲”机器人操控，显著提升了操作速度并降低了算法研究门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前触觉强化学习研究碎片化严重，且任务多局限于简单的定向任务；现有框架难以支持高效、大规模的基准测试，限制了算法性能的突破。

Method: 提出了一个名为 roto 2.0 的基于 GPU 并行加速的基准测试平台，支持四种不同自由度（16-DOF 至 24-DOF）的机器人形态；模型不依赖状态信息或蒸馏，直接进行端到端的“盲”触觉强化学习训练。

Result: 在机器人操控任务中取得了重大进展，盲触觉智能体在 10 秒内完成了 13 次保定铁球旋转，运行速度比现有最先进方法快一个数量级。

Conclusion: roto 2.0 作为一个标准化的基准框架，填补了触觉强化学习在“盲”操控任务中的研究空白，通过提供现成的环境和基线模型，显著降低了研究门槛，并证实了仅依赖本体感觉和触觉传感器即可实现卓越的复杂操控能力。

Abstract: Tactile-based reinforcement learning (RL) is currently hindered by fragmented research and a focus on over-saturated orientation tasks. We introduce v2 of the Robot Tactile Olympiad (\texttt{roto 2.0}), a GPU-parallelised benchmark designed to standardise tactile-based RL across four distinct robotic morphologies (16-DOF to 24-DOF). Unlike prior benchmarks, roto focuses on end-to-end "blind" manipulation, utilising only proprioception and tactile sensing without state information or distillation. We demonstrate a significant performance leap, with our blind agents achieving 13 Baoding ball rotations in 10 seconds, an order of magnitude faster than current state-of-the-art speeds. By open-sourcing our environments and robustly tuned baselines, we reduce the barrier to entry and enable researchers to prioritise fundamental algorithmic challenges over tedious RL tuning. Website: https://elle-miller.github.io/roto/

</details>


### [117] [HITL-D: Human In The Loop Diffusion Assisted Shared Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21460)
*Riley Zilka,Sergey Khlynovskiy,Allie Wang,Martin Jagersand*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HITL-D 是一种基于扩散模型的新型共享控制框架，通过提供自主方向辅助，简化了复杂操控任务的控制难度，显著提升了遥操作效率并降低了人为精神负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主操作系统在共享控制中结合人类专业知识结合扩散策略的研究不足，且传统遥操作在复杂任务中对用户造成的精神负荷过高。

Method: 提出了一种名为 HITL-D（Human-In-The-Loop Diffusion）的共享控制框架，结合扩散策略（diffusion-based policies）与人类控制。该系统根据场景点云和末端执行器的笛卡尔位置提供自主的末端执行器方向更新，从而减少了操纵杆控制轴的需求。

Result: 在一项涉及 12 名参与者的多任务用户研究中，HITL-D 使平均任务完成时间缩短了 40%，感知工作负荷降低了 37%，并在独立性、直观性和操作信心方面获得了更高的 Likert 量表评分。

Conclusion: HITL-D 有效地将人类专业知识与自主辅助相结合，显著提升了遥操作在客观效率与主观用户体验两方面的表现。

Abstract: Autonomous manipulation systems have achieved remarkable capabilities, yet the integration of human expertise with diffusion-based policies in shared control remains relatively unexplored. In this paper, we propose Human-In-The-Loop Diffusion (HITL-D), a shared control framework that enhances user performance in multi-step, insertion, and fine manipulation tasks. HITL-D leverages a novel combination of diffusion-based policies and human control to provide autonomous end effector orientation updates conditioned on a scene point cloud and the Cartesian position of the end effector. This approach reduces the number of joystick control axes required, thereby lowering mental workload. In a multi-task user study with 12 participants, HITL-D reduced average task completion times by 40%, decreased perceived workload by 37%, and improved Likert-scale ratings for independence, intuitiveness, and confidence compared to traditional teleoperation methods. These results demonstrate that HITL-D effectively integrates human expertise with autonomous assistance, improving both objective and subjective aspects of teleoperation.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [118] [Fifty Years of Transaction Processing Research (extended)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20466)
*Philip A. Bernstein*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文追溯了事务处理研究的历史，探讨了该领域为何在被认为“已解决”后依然持续发展，并展望了其未来的研究前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在阐述为何尽管事务处理被普遍认为是计算机科学中的“已解决问题”，但相关研究至今仍活跃且具备重要性，并探讨该领域未来的演进方向。

Method: 作者通过回顾事务处理研究的早期历史、结合个人实践经验，对该领域的研究现状进行了分析，并对未来的发展趋势进行了前瞻性展望。

Result: 梳理了事务研究的演变脉络，明确了其作为数据库核心基石的持久地位，并指出了未来在应对新型架构和分布式挑战方面的潜在研究机遇。

Conclusion: 事务研究虽然在某种程度上被视为“已解决”的领域，但其在处理复杂现代计算环境、新兴硬件特性以及分布式系统新挑战方面仍具有长期研究价值和持续演进的必要性。

Abstract: In this short paper, I recount some early history of transaction research (including some of my own), explain why transaction research continues to this day (even though it seems to be a solved problem), and speculate about its future. This is an extended version of the paper that appeared in the Companion of the 2025 International Conference on Management of Data (SIGMOD-Companion '25).

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [AgentCo-op: Retrieval-Based Synthesis of Interoperable Multi-Agent Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20425)
*Shuaike Shen,Wenduo Cheng,Shike Wang,Mingqian Ma,Jian Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentCo-op 是一种通过检索现有技能、工具和智能体来构建复杂工作流的框架，它利用类型化工件连接组件，并通过局部修复机制解决执行故障，在无需大规模搜索的情况下，高效实现了开放环境下的多智能体协同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在开放式科学研究中，缺乏标准化的训练集、评估指标及工具接口，导致设计复杂的多智能体工作流极具挑战。现有的方案往往难以在无需重构或大规模搜索的前提下整合和复用异构系统。

Method: 提出了 AgentCo-op 框架，该方法包含两个核心机制：1. 基于类型化工件交接（typed artifact handoffs）的检索式合成（将技能、工具和智能体组合成工作流）；2. 有界的自我引导局部修复（当执行失败时，针对性地修复特定组件而非重构全局拓扑）。

Result: 在基因组学案例研究中成功实现了独立智能体的协同；在六个标准基准测试中，在四个项目中达到最优，且在统一骨干模型下取得平均最高分，同时显著降低了单个任务的计算成本。

Conclusion: AgentCo-op 证明了基于检索的合成框架能够有效扩展自动化智能体工作流设计，通过组合现有组件（而非从头训练）来处理开放世界任务，同时提高了性能并降低了成本。

Abstract: Designing multi-agent workflows is especially difficult in open-ended scientific settings where tasks lack curated training sets, reliable scalar evaluation metrics, and standardized interfaces between existing tools and agents. We propose AgentCo-op, a retrieval-based synthesis framework that composes reusable skills, tools, and external agents into executable workflows through typed artifact handoffs, then applies bounded self-guided local repair to implicated components when execution evidence indicates failure. In two open-world genomics case studies, AgentCo-op composes independently developed scientific agents and external tool repositories into auditable workflows without redesigning them or running global topology search. It coordinates specialized agents for spatial transcriptomics and gene-set interpretation to enable collaborative discovery from spatial transcriptomics data, and builds a parallel workflow for cross-modality marker analysis on single-cell multiome data. AgentCo-op can also import a searched workflow as a structural prior and improve it by grounding nodes with retrieved components and applying local repair, showing that synthesis and search are complementary. On six coding, math, and question-answering benchmarks, AgentCo-op achieves the best result on four benchmarks and the best average score under a unified backbone setting, while consistently reducing per-task cost relative to multi-agent baselines. Together, these results suggest that retrieval-based synthesis can extend automated agentic workflow design beyond benchmark-optimized agent graphs to open-world workflows built from existing agents, tools, and typed artifacts.

</details>


### [120] [High Quality Embeddings for Horn Logic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20467)
*Yifan Zhang,Yasir White,Dean Clark,Joseph Sanchez,Jevon Lipsey,Ashely Hirst,Jeff Heflin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种优化逻辑语句嵌入的方法，通过改进三元组损失的样本选择与训练策略（如引入重复项锚点、动态样本平衡和困难样本挖掘），显著提升了逻辑推理任务的搜索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提升逻辑推理中搜索答案的效率，需要构建高质量的逻辑语句数值嵌入（Embeddings）来辅助逻辑推理模型对选择进行排序。

Method: 提出并评估了三种针对三元组损失（Triplet Loss）的改进策略：1. 生成包含更多重复项的锚点；2. 动态平衡简单、中等和困难样本；3. 在训练过程中周期性地强调困难样本。此外，通过在不同知识库上进行对比实验，分析了不同嵌入特征对推理任务适应性的影响。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的改进策略能够有效提升嵌入质量，在不同知识库和推理任务中表现出更好的适用性和性能，研究揭示了特定嵌入特征对推理任务的关键影响。

Conclusion: 通过优化三元组损失函数中的锚点生成策略、正负样本平衡机制以及困难样本挖掘策略，可以显著提升逻辑语句嵌入的表现，从而有效辅助逻辑推理引擎提高搜索效率。

Abstract: Neural networks can be trained to rank the choices made by logical reasoners, resulting in more efficient searches for answers. A key step in this process is creating useful embeddings, i.e., numeric representations of logical statements. This paper introduces and evaluates several approaches to creating embeddings that result in better downstream results. We train embeddings using triplet loss, which requires examples consisting of an anchor, a positive example, and a negative example. We introduce three ideas: generating anchors that are more likely to have repeated terms, generating positive and negative examples in a way that ensures a good balance between easy, medium, and hard examples, and periodically emphasizing the hardest examples during training. We conduct several experiments to evaluate this approach, including a comparison of different embeddings across different knowledge bases, in an attempt to identify what characteristics make an embedding well-suited to a particular reasoning task.

</details>


### [121] [\ECUAS{n}: A family of metrics for principled evaluation of uncertainty-augmented systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20490)
*Lautaro Estienne,Erik Ernst,Matías Vera,Pablo Piantanida,Luciana Ferrer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对现有不确定性增强系统评估方法不足的问题，本文提出了ECUAS{n}指标家族，通过参数可调的评分规则，更有效地评估系统在高风险决策场景下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的不确定性增强系统（UA）评估方法（如分离度量、固定拒绝代价、覆盖-风险曲线）在处理高风险自动化决策场景时存在局限性，无法全面衡量系统在不同代价权衡下的实际决策性能。

Method: 提出了一系列名为ECUAS{n}的指标，将评估过程形式化为针对特定任务的适当评分规则（proper scoring rules），并通过参数n调节错误预测代价与不确定性评估质量之间的权衡。

Result: 在包含TriviaQA子集在内的多项分类和生成数据集实验中，理论和实证均证明了ECUAS{n}相较于传统方法的优越性。

Conclusion: ECUAS{n}是一套衡量不确定性增强系统（UA）整体决策性能的有效指标，通过参数n灵活适配不同应用场景下的代价权衡，克服了现有评估方法片面性和不灵活的缺陷。

Abstract: In high-stakes automated decision-making, access to predictive uncertainty is essential for enabling users -- human or downstream systems -- to accept or reject predictions based on application-specific cost trade-offs. Such uncertainty-augmented (UA) systems -- i.e., systems that output both predictions and uncertainty scores -- are currently being assessed in the literature in a variety of ways, using separate metrics to evaluate the predictions and the uncertainty scores, setting a cost function with a fixed rejection cost or integrating over a coverage-risk curve. We argue that these evaluation approaches are inadequate for assessing overall performance of the UA system for decision making under uncertainty and propose a novel family of metrics, \ECUAS{n}, formulated as proper scoring rules for the task of interest. The parameter $n$ controls the trade-off between the cost of incorrect predictions and imperfect uncertainties depending on the needs of the use-case. We demonstrate the advantages of the \ECUAS{n} metrics both theoretically and empirically, through experiments on diverse classification and generation datasets, including a manually annotated subset of TriviaQA.

</details>


### [122] [AgentAtlas: Beyond Outcome Leaderboards for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20530)
*Parsa Mazaheri,Kasra Mazaheri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对当前智能体评估指标碎片化问题，提出了一套包含多维分类法和提示词基准审计的评估协议，发现现有智能体表现高度依赖提示引导且能力各有所长，单一准确率不再适用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的智能体基准测试过于碎片化，且单一的准确率指标无法有效评估复杂环境下智能体的实际能力与安全性，难以指导可部署智能体的开发。

Method: 1. 构建六态控制决策分类法（Act/Ask/Refuse/Stop/Confirm/Recover）；2. 建立包含来源与影响维度的九类轨迹失败分类法；3. 提出“提示词依赖测试”（对比标签菜单提示下的行为）；4. 对15个现有基准进行覆盖度审计。

Result: 测试表明，移除显式标签提示后，所有模型的轨迹准确率均大幅下降（14-40个百分点），且模型表现趋同；未发现全能型模型，证明了全面多维度评估协议的必要性。

Conclusion: 单一的准确率指标已不足以评估现代智能体；智能体能力的表象很大程度上依赖于提示词引导（supervision）；没有单一模型能在控制准确性、轨迹诊断和工具上下文效用维持这三个维度上同时胜出。

Abstract: Large language model agents now act on codebases, browsers, operating systems, calendars, files, and tool ecosystems, but the benchmarks used to evaluate them are fragmented: each emphasizes a different unit of measurement (final task success, tool-call validity, repeated-pass consistency, trajectory safety, or attack robustness). A line of 2024-2025 work has converged on the diagnosis that a single accuracy column is no longer the right unit of comparison for deployable agents. AgentAtlas extends this line of work with four components: (i) a six-state control-decision taxonomy (Act / Ask / Refuse / Stop / Confirm / Recover); (ii) a nine-category trajectory-failure taxonomy with two orthogonal hierarchical labels (primary_error_source, impact); (iii) a taxonomy-aware vs. taxonomy-blind methodology that measures how much of a model's apparent capability comes from the supervision in the prompt; and (iv) a benchmark-coverage audit mapping fifteen agent benchmarks against six behavioral axes. To demonstrate the methodology we run a small fixed eight-model set (1,342 generated items, four frontier closed and four open-weight) under both prompt modes. Removing the explicit label menu drops every model's trajectory accuracy by 14-40 pp to a tight 0.54-0.62 floor regardless of family, and no single model wins on all three of control accuracy, trajectory diagnosis, and tool-context utility retention. We treat the synthetic run as a measurement-protocol demonstration, not a benchmark release.

</details>


### [123] [Mahjax: A GPU-Accelerated Mahjong Simulator for Reinforcement Learning in JAX](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20577)
*Soichiro Nishimori,Shinri Okano,Keigo Habara,Sotetsu Koyamada,Eason Yu,Masashi Sugiyama*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Mahjax 是一个基于 JAX 的高性能立直麻将训练环境，通过极致的 GPU 并行化能力，突破了传统环境在支持“从零学习”（tabula rasa）强化学习任务时的效率瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前的立直麻将研究多依赖人类历史棋谱进行监督学习，限制了模型的通用性；现有的模拟环境性能不足，无法高效支持类似 AlphaZero 的“从零学习”（tabula rasa）范式。

Method: 开发 Mahjax 环境，利用 JAX 框架实现完全向量化（vectorized），并针对 GPU 进行大规模并行采样优化；提供可视化工具用于调试与交互。

Result: Mahjax 在 8 张 NVIDIA A100 GPU 上分别实现了无赤牌规则下每秒 200 万步、有赤牌规则下每秒 100 万步的超高性能采样，并验证了智能体在对抗基准策略时性能有效提升。

Conclusion: Mahjax 为麻将强化学习研究提供了一个高效、可扩展的实验平台，证明了在无需人工数据辅助（tabula rasa）的情况下，通过大规模并行化训练强化学习智能体在复杂麻将游戏中是可行的。

Abstract: Riichi Mahjong is a multi-player, imperfect-information game characterized by stochasticity and high-dimensional state spaces. These attributes present a unique combination of challenges that mirror complex real-world decision-making problems in reinforcement learning. While prior research has heavily relied on supervised learning from human play logs to pre-train the policy, algorithms capable of learning \textit{tabula rasa} (from scratch) offer greater potential for general applicability, as evidenced by the AlphaZero lineage. To facilitate such research, we introduce \textbf{Mahjax}, a fully vectorized Riichi Mahjong environment implemented in JAX to enable large-scale rollout parallelization on Graphics Processing Units (GPUs). We also provide a high-quality visualization tool to streamline debugging and interaction with trained agents. Experimental results demonstrate that Mahjax achieves throughputs of up to \textbf{2 million} and \textbf{1 million steps per second} on eight NVIDIA A100 GPUs under the no-red and red rules, respectively. Furthermore, we validate the environment's utility for reinforcement learning by showing that agents can be trained effectively to improve their rank against baseline policies.

</details>


### [124] [Evaluating Temporal Semantic Caching and Workflow Optimization in Agentic Plan-Execute Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20630)
*Alimurtaza Mustafa Merchant,Krish Veera,Sajal Kumar Goyla,Shambhawi Bhure,Dhaval Patel,Kaoutar El Maghraoui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对工业智能体工作流延迟问题，提出了时间语义缓存与MCP并行执行优化方案，在保持正确性的同时大幅度降低了端到端延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业资产运维工作流对延迟极其敏感，现有的通用LLM缓存（如KV缓存、语义缓存）无法满足工业场景下涉及传感器数据、工单和资产参数的高度时效性与复杂性需求。

Method: 1. 提出“时间语义缓存”（Temporal Semantic Cache）解决传统语义缓存对参数敏感度不足的问题；2. 实施MCP（Model Context Protocol）工作流优化，包括基于磁盘的工具发现缓存以及依赖感知并行执行策略。

Result: MCP工作流优化实现了1.67倍的运行加速，端到端延迟降低约40.0%；时间语义缓存策略在命中时实现了30.6倍的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过引入Temporal Semantic Cache与MCP工作流优化，有效解决了工业资产运营中高延迟的问题，同时指出纯语义缓存在参数丰富的工业查询场景下的局限性，为构建高效、正确的智能体工作流提供了优化策略。

Abstract: Industrial asset operations workflows are latency-sensitive because a single user query may require coordination over sensor data, work orders, failure modes, forecasting tools, and domain-specific agents. We evaluate this problem on AssetOpsBench (AOB), an industrial agent benchmark whose plan-execute pipeline exposes repeated overhead from tool discovery, LLM planning, MCP tool execution, and final summarization. Existing LLM caching techniques such as KV-cache reuse and embedding-based semantic caching were designed for chatbot serving and break down when output validity depends on time, asset, or sensor parameters. We propose two complementary optimization layers for AOB plan-execute pipelines: a temporal semantic cache and a set of MCP workflow optimizations combining disk-backed tool-discovery caching and dependency-aware parallel step execution. MCP workflow optimizations corresponded to a 1.67x speedup and reduced median end-to-end latency by about 40.0% while the temporal-cache benchmark achieved a median of 30.6x speedup on cache hits. Beyond the speedup, our results expose a concrete failure mode of pure semantic caching for parameter-rich industrial queries, providing a critical analysis of how caching choices interact with evaluation correctness in MCP-backed agent benchmarks.

</details>


### [125] [PALS: Power-Aware LLM Serving for Mixture-of-Experts Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21427)
*Can Hankendi,Rana Shahout,Minlan Yu,Ayse K. Coskun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PALS 是一种针对 LLM 推理的功率感知运行时，它通过动态联动 GPU 功率限制与推理引擎配置，在满足服务质量的前提下显著提升了能源效率并优化了功耗管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 推理的高能耗已成为数据中心的沉重负担；现有系统多将 GPU 功率视为静态约束，而非可主动调控的优化资源。

Method: 提出 PALS（Power-Aware LLM Serving）系统，通过轻量级离线功耗-性能建模与反馈控制机制，联合优化 GPU 功率上限与批处理大小（batch size）等软件配置。该系统在 vLLM 框架上实现，无需模型重训练。

Result: 在多 GPU 系统、密集型及 MoE 模型测试中，PALS 将能源效率最高提升了 26.3%，在功率受限下将服务质量 (QoS) 违规减少了 4 至 7 倍，并能有效追踪动态功率预算。

Conclusion: 本文证明了将 GPU 功率控制作为 LLM 推理运行时的一等控制参数是可行的且高效的，为实现能源比例和电网交互式 AI 系统提供了新途径。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) inference has become a dominant workload in modern data centers, driving significant GPU utilization and energy consumption. While prior systems optimize throughput and latency by batching, scheduling, and parallelism, they largely treat GPU power as a static constraint rather than a controllable resource. In this paper, we present a power-aware runtime for LLM serving, PALS, that treats GPU power caps as a first-class control knob and jointly optimizes them with software parameters such as batch size. The system combines lightweight offline power-performance models with a feedback-driven controller to select configurations that satisfy throughput targets while maximizing energy efficiency. We implement PALS within an existing LLM serving framework, vLLM, demonstrating that it requires no model retraining or API changes. Across multi-GPU systems and both dense and mixture-of-experts (MoE) models, PALS improves energy efficiency by up to 26.3%, reduces QoS violations by 4x to 7x under power constraints, and tracks dynamic power budgets. These results highlight the potential of integrating power control directly into LLM inference runtimes, enabling energy-proportional and grid-interactive AI systems.

</details>


### [126] [VBFDD-Agent for Electric Vehicle Battery Fault Detection and Diagnosis: Descriptive Text Modeling of Battery Digital Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20742)
*Joey Chan,Zhen Chen,Ershun Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对传统电池诊断难以适应复杂场景的问题，研究通过构建结构化文本语料库，提出了 VBFDD-Agent，融合LLM与领域知识，实现了可解释、面向维护的电池智能诊断与决策支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电池故障诊断方法缺乏跨域适应性且难以应对复杂场景，同时存在开源电池故障报告语料匮乏及维护知识表示不统一的问题。

Method: 构建电池信号报告的描述性文本模型，将监测信号、统计特征及状态结果转化为结构化自然语言，形成诊断语料库；并提出 VBFDD-Agent，融合历史案例检索、本地维护手册及大语言模型（LLM）推理能力进行诊断与建议生成。

Result: 实验证明该框架能准确实现基于描述性文本的异常监控，生成的诊断结果和建议高效且具备实践参考意义，经专家评估验证了其在实际应用中的有效性。

Conclusion: VBFDD-Agent 成功地将传统的电池故障诊断从单一的标签预测扩展到了具备可解释性和维护导向的决策支持系统，显著提升了诊断的实用价值。

Abstract: With the rapid proliferation of electric vehicles, the safety and reliability of lithium-ion batteries have become critical concerns. Effective anomaly detection is essential for ensuring safe battery operation. However, as battery systems and operating scenarios become increasingly complex, battery fault diagnosis and maintenance require stronger cross-domain adaptability and human-AI collaboration. Traditional fault detection and diagnosis methods are usually designed for specific scenarios and predefined workflows, making them less effective in complex real-world applications.
  To address the scarcity of open-source battery fault report corpora and the lack of unified maintenance knowledge representation, this study proposes a descriptive text modeling approach for battery signal reports. Monitoring signals, statistical features, anomaly records, and state assessment results are transformed into structured and readable natural language descriptions, forming a language corpus for battery health diagnosis and maintenance.
  Based on this corpus, we propose VBFDD-Agent, a vehicle battery fault detection and diagnosis agent for automotive-grade battery systems. VBFDD-Agent integrates descriptive battery-state texts, historical case retrieval, local maintenance manuals, and large language model reasoning to generate structured diagnostic results and maintenance recommendations. Experiments show that the proposed framework can accurately perform anomaly monitoring based on descriptive textual representations and provide flexible, efficient, and actionable maintenance suggestions. Expert evaluation further confirms the practical value of the generated recommendations. Overall, VBFDD-Agent extends traditional battery diagnosis from label prediction to interpretable and maintenance-oriented decision support.

</details>


### [127] [Conflict-Aware Additive Guidance for Flow Models under Compositional Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20758)
*Xuehui Yu,Fucheng Cai,Meiyi Wang,Xiaopeng Fan,Harold Soh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对扩散与流模型中多约束引导产生的“离流形漂移”问题，本文提出了一种轻量级且可学习的冲突感知加性引导机制（$g^\text{car}$），有效提升了受控生成的准确性与质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推理时引导方法在处理多个外部约束（如代价函数或验证器）组合时，由于梯度不对齐导致严重的离流形漂移（off-manifold drift），从而降低了生成质量。

Method: 提出了一种名为“冲突感知加性引导”（Conflict-Aware Additive Guidance, $g^\text{car}$）的方法。该方法通过动态检测并解决扩散/流模型在多约束引导下的梯度冲突问题，从而修正并防止生成过程偏离数据流形。

Result: 在合成数据集、图像编辑及决策规划等多个领域，CAR方法均表现出优于基线模型的生成忠实度，且计算开销较低。

Conclusion: Conflict-Aware Additive Guidance (CAR) is a lightweight and effective solution to off-manifold drift in diffusion/flow models. It significantly improves generation fidelity and constraint satisfaction when handling multiple conflicting external constraints, providing a scalable approach for controlled controllable generation.

Abstract: Inference-time guided sampling steers state-of-the-art diffusion and flow models without fine-tuning by interpreting the generation process as a controllable trajectory. This provides a simple and flexible way to inject external constraints (e.g., cost functions or pre-trained verifiers) for controlled generation. However, existing methods often fail when composing multiple constraints simultaneously, which leads to deviations from the true data manifold. In this work, we identify root causes of this off-manifold drift and find that the approximation error scales severely with gradient misalignment. Building on these findings, we propose Conflict-Aware Additive Guidance ($g^\text{car}$), a lightweight and learnable method, which actively rectifies off-manifold drift by dynamically detecting and resolving gradient conflicts. We validate $g^\text{car}$ across diverse domains, ranging from synthetic datasets and image editing to generative decision-making for planning and control. Our results demonstrate that $g^\text{car}$ effectively rectifies off-manifold drift, surpassing baselines in generation fidelity while using light compute. Code is available at https://github.com/yuxuehui/CAR-guidance.

</details>


### [128] [Interaction Locality in Hierarchical Recursive Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20784)
*Yosuke Miyanishi,Tetsuro Morimura*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“交互局部性”框架，通过激活修补等手段量化模型在空间推理任务中的信息流动，发现递归模型倾向于将局部更新积累为全局结构，而不同架构的模型在实现空间任务时表现出不同的局部信息处理边界偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间推理既需要局部计算（位置相关），也需要结构化规划（位置无关）。现有的评估方法难以精确描述模型如何在保持局部交互的同时形成全局空间结构，因此需要一套可度量的分析框架。

Method: 提出了基于任务几何感知的“交互局部性”框架，通过稀疏自动编码器（SAE）特征消融、有限噪声激活修补（activation patching）以及结构雅可比矩阵和注意力检查来评估信息流；实验覆盖了HRM、TRM模型（Maze/Sudoku/ARC-AGI任务）以及MTU3D具身3D模型。

Result: 在HRM和TRM中，激活修补显示出明显的架构特征：高层递归状态倾向于在局部单元内写入信息，通过反复递归积累形成整体解决方案。相比之下，MTU3D具身模型主要在视觉场景特征传导给下游模块的界面呈现出因果空间局部性，而非在整个视觉编码器中均匀分布。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种名为“交互局部性”（interaction locality）的框架，有效量化了空间推理模型中局部操作与全局结构化规划之间的信息流。研究表明，递归推理模型通过反复的局部更新积累形成全局解，而具身模型则在模块边界处处理空间信息。该框架为理解复杂空间任务中的推理机制提供了可复制的度量标准。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning requires both location-bound computation and location-invariant structure: agents must make local moves while preserving route, object, or constraint-level plans. We propose interaction locality, a task-geometry-aware framework for measuring whether information flow stays within nearby cells or semantic segments, or crosses them. We instantiate the framework with sparse-autoencoder feature ablations and finite-noise activation patching, with structural Jacobian and attention checks reported in the appendix, and apply it to HRM and TRM, two compact hierarchical and recursive reasoning models, on Maze-Hard, Sudoku Extreme, and ARC-AGI. Across these models, activation patching gives the clearest architectural fingerprint: high-level recurrent states tend to write information within nearby cells or same-segment units, while repeated recursive updates accumulate these local writes into broader solution structure. This pattern holds across maze paths, Sudoku constraints, and ARC-AGI object neighborhoods, with the strongest concentration in TRM. To test whether interaction locality extends beyond toy-yet-challenging grid benchmarks, we also apply it to MTU3D, a large-scale embodied 3D scene-grounding model. In this MTU3D setting, causal spatial locality appears primarily at the transition where visual scene features are handed to the downstream grounding module, rather than uniformly throughout the visual encoder. This contrast suggests that the local-to-global handoff observed in HRM and TRM is tied to explicit recursive reasoning dynamics, while embodied 3D models may concentrate causal spatial structure at module boundaries. Interaction locality turns the intuitive local-execution/global-planning story into a reproducible measurement framework for recursive and embodied spatial reasoning.

</details>


### [129] [Conditional Equivalence of DPO and RLHF: Implicit Assumption, Failure Modes, and Provable Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20834)
*Zhiqin Yang,Yonggang Zhang,Wei Xue,Dong Fang,Bo Han,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了DPO与RLHF在理论上并不等价的局限性，指出DPO在特定条件下会导致奖励模型退化，并提出了CPO（约束偏好优化）算法，通过显式约束确保模型的可证明对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管DPO被视为RLHF的简单有效替代方案，但其实际表现往往存在不稳定性，且其背后的理论等价性在实际应用场景中可能并不成立，容易导致模型出现违反人类偏好的“病态收敛”。

Method: 1. 理论分析：推导出DPO与RLHF的等价性条件，并揭示了DPO在假设失效时的负面目标（负目标排序）；2. 几何解释：将DPO视作带有潜在负目标的软间隔排序；3. 改进方案：提出约束偏好优化（CPO）方法，通过增加约束项纠正DPO的偏差；4. 实验验证：在标准基准测试上对比CPO与DPO及其他基线方法的表现。

Result: 1. 证明了当DPO的隐式假设被违反时，DPO不再对齐人类偏好，而是优化了相对于参考策略的累积优势，导致模型产生退化行为；2. 提出了CPO算法，通过约束机制解决了DPO的非对齐问题；3. 实验表明CPO在多个基准测试中优于DPO，验证了理论分析的正确性与方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文证明了DPO与RLHF的等价性是有条件的，而非普遍成立。当DPO的隐式假设（RLHF最优策略必须倾向于人类偏好回答）不成立时，DPO会失效，导致奖励退化。提出的CPO方法通过引入约束优化，确保了在更广泛场景下的可证明对齐，并达到了SOTA性能。

Abstract: Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) has emerged as a popular alternative to Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF), offering theoretical equivalence with simpler implementation. We prove this equivalence is conditional rather than universal, depending on an implicit assumption frequently violated in practice: the RLHF-optimal policy must prefer human-preferred responses. When this assumption fails, DPO optimizes relative advantage over the reference policy rather than absolute alignment with human preferences, leading to pathological convergence where policies decrease DPO loss while preferring dispreferred responses. We characterize when this assumption is violated, show the existence of an undesirable solution space, and prove that DPO and RLHF optimize fundamentally different objectives in such cases. To address this, we introduce Constrained Preference Optimization (CPO), augmenting RLHF with constraints for provable alignment. We further provide a geometric interpretation through soft margin ranking, revealing that DPO implements margin ranking with potentially negative targets. Our theoretical analysis establishes when DPOs' guarantees hold and provides solutions preserving simplicity with provable alignment. Comprehensive experiments on standard benchmarks demonstrate that CPO achieves state-of-the-art performance. Code is available at: https://github.com/visitworld123/CPO.

</details>


### [130] [PlanningBench: Generating Scalable and Verifiable Planning Data for Evaluating and Training Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20873)
*Ziliang Zhao,Zenan Xu,Shuting Wang,Hongjin Qian,Yan Lei,Minda Hu,Zhao Wang,Shihan Dou,Zhicheng Dou,Pluto Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对现有规划基准测试不可扩展、难度控制困难的痛点，本文提出了 PlanningBench，这是一个通过结构化分类法和约束驱动流水线生成可验证规划数据的框架，旨在提升 LLM 的复杂规划与逻辑推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有规划基准测试通常采用固定的数据集，导致场景覆盖局限、难度评估依赖表面特征而非结构源，且缺乏可扩展的生成和自动验证机制，难以支持深度的规划能力训练。

Method: 提出了一个基于约束驱动的合成流水线 PlanningBench，包含一套涵盖 30 多种任务类型、子任务及约束条件的结构化分类法，支持生成具有自适应难度控制、质量过滤和实例级验证检查清单的规划数据。

Result: 评估发现当前前沿模型在处理耦合约束时的规划能力较弱；实验证明，在 PlanningBench 生成的验证数据上进行强化学习，可以显著提升模型在未见规划基准以及通用指令任务上的性能；研究还指出明确的最优解有助于提供更清晰的奖励信号和更稳定的训练动态。

Conclusion: PlanningBench 提供了一个可控的规划数据生成框架，有效克服了传统固定基准测试的局限性，不仅能够精准诊断 LLM 在复杂约束规划中的不足，还能通过强化学习提升模型的规划能力和指令遵循能力。

Abstract: Planning is a fundamental capability for large language models (LLMs) because such complex tasks require models to coordinate goals, constraints, resources, and long-term consequences into executable and verifiable solutions. Existing planning benchmarks, however, usually treat planning data as fixed collections of instances rather than controllable generation targets. This limits scenario coverage, ties difficulty to surface-level proxies rather than structural sources, and offers limited support for scalable generation, automatic verification, or planning-oriented training. We introduce PlanningBench, a framework for generating scalable, diverse, and verifiable planning data for both evaluation and training. PlanningBench starts from real planning scenarios and abstracts practical workflows into a structured taxonomy of more than 30 task types, subtasks, constraint families, and difficulty factors. Guided by this taxonomy, a constraint-driven synthesis pipeline instantiates self-contained planning problems with adaptive difficulty control, quality filtering, and instance-level verification checklists. This shifts planning data construction from fixed benchmark collection to controllable generation while preserving realistic task grounding. We use PlanningBench to evaluate open-source and closed-source frontier LLMs, and find that current models still struggle to produce complete solutions under coupled constraints. Beyond evaluation, reinforcement learning on verified PlanningBench data improves performance on unseen planning benchmarks and broader instruction-following tasks. Further analysis suggests that determinate or well-specified optimal solutions provide clearer reward signals and more stable training dynamics. Overall, PlanningBench provides a controllable source of planning data for diagnosing and improving generalizable planning abilities in LLMs.

</details>


### [131] [For How Long Should We Be Punching? Learning Action Duration in Fighting Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20911)
*Hoang Hai Nguyen,Kurt Driessens,Dennis J. N. J. Soemers*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种允许强化学习智能体在格斗游戏中同时学习“动作”和“动作持续时长”的方法，旨在克服固定帧决策间隔的局限性。研究发现，该方法不仅未能显著提升自主决策的鲁棒性，反而使智能体倾向于通过长时间执行单一动作来“卡顿”和压制机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的强化学习智能体在格斗游戏中通常采用固定的决策间隔（每帧或每N帧决策）。这种设计要么导致非人类的“逐帧反应”，要么因固定间隔牺牲响应速度，难以根据游戏情境动态调整反应延迟。

Method: 提出了一种联合预测“动作（Action）”与“决策时长（Duration）”的决策框架，取代了传统强化学习中固定的帧决策间隔。并在FightLadder环境中对比了多种固定帧跳过配置，分析其对性能、响应性和行为的影响。

Result: 通过动态时长学习，智能体能够根据情境调整决策频率。然而，实验结果显示，性能最优的智能体往往倾向于学习“低响应、长时长”的策略，通过不断重复单一动作来利用内置脚本化机器人的程序弱点，而非真正展现出智能化的战斗技巧。

Conclusion: 研究表明，学习决策时长虽然能匹配固定帧跳过（frame skip）的性能并鼓励重复的行为模式，但并不能单独保证算法的鲁棒性。实验发现，智能体在较高帧跳过（即较低响应速度）时表现最佳，这促使智能体学习并利用脚本化机器人的弱点（即重复执行相同动作）。

Abstract: Fighting games such as Street Fighter II present unique challenges to reinforcement learning (RL) agents due to their fast-paced, real-time nature. In most RL frameworks, agents are hard-coded to make decisions at a fixed interval, typically every frame or every N frames. Although this design ensures timely responses, it restricts the agent's ability to adjust its reaction timing. Acting every frame grants frame-perfect reflexes, which are unrealistic compared to human players, whereas longer fixed intervals reduce computational cost but hinder responsiveness. We consider an alternative decision-making framework in which the agent learns not only what action to take but also for how long to execute it. By jointly predicting both action and duration, the agent can dynamically adapt its responsiveness to different situations in the game. We implement this method using the open-source FightLadder environment with agents trained against scripted built-in bots, systematically testing different frame skip configurations to analyze their influence on performance, responsiveness, and learned behavior. Experiments show that learned timing can match the performance of well-chosen fixed frame skips and encourages repeatable action patterns, but does not ensure robustness on its own. In most cases, we see agents performing best with consistently high frame skip values (i.e., low responsiveness). This strategy makes it easier to learn exploitative strategies where the same action is repeated over and over, which the scripted bots appear to be susceptible to.

</details>


### [132] [Playing Devil's Advocate: Off-the-Shelf Persona Vectors Rival Targeted Steering for Sycophancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21006)
*Ishaan Kelkar,Nebras Alam,Vikram Kakaria,Madhur Panwar,Vasu Sharma,Maheep Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究发现通过引导模型采用“怀疑”或“审视”的人格设定，无需专门监督训练即可有效抑制模型的谄媚行为（盲目附和错误观点），且效果优于传统的 CAA 方法，证明谄媚更像是模型的一种人格属性而非单一线性偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨不同的人格设定（persona）如何影响模型产生“谄媚行为”（即模型在用户观点错误时仍盲目迎合用户）。研究目标是寻找一种替代现有的对比激活添加（CAA）方法，即无需专门的辅助监督训练数据，即可减轻模型谄媚倾向。

Method: 1. 选取现有的角色扮演指导向量（persona steering vectors），而非专门训练谄媚数据集。2. 在两个指令微调模型上评估这些向量对谄媚行为的影响。3. 将效果与标准的对比激活添加（CAA）方法进行对比，并分析角色向“怀疑/审视”方向偏移时的性能。4. 进行几何分析，研究角色向量与谄媚方向在激活空间内的关系。

Result: 1. 在两个模型中，向“怀疑”或“审视”类人格进行引导，可以将谄媚现象分别降低至 CAA 方法效果的 68% 和 98%。2. 与 CAA 不同，这种方法在用户观点正确时依然能保持模型的准确性。3. 效果具有不对称性：向“顺从/合意”的人格引导并不会线性增加谄媚行为。4. 几何分析显示，人格向量与谄媚方向在激活空间中是相互独立的。

Conclusion: 谄媚（sycophancy）并非一个单一可控的方向，更倾向于一种与模型角色（persona）相关的特性。此外，该研究验证了利用预设的角色引导向量（persona steering vectors）作为一种无需额外标注数据的替代方案，能够有效减轻模型的谄媚行为并保持任务准确性。

Abstract: We study the effect of different persona on \textbf{sycophancy}: model's agreement with users even when the user is incorrect. The standard mitigation, Contrastive Activation Addition (CAA), derives a steering direction from labelled pairs of sycophantic and honest responses. This study evaluates whether off-the-shelf persona steering vectors, originally developed for general role-playing and not trained on sycophancy data, can serve as an alternative. In two instruction-tuned models, steering toward personas characterised by doubt or scrutiny reduces sycophancy to approximately $68\%$ and $98\%$ of CAA's effect, and, unlike CAA, maintains accuracy when the user is correct. The effect is also asymmetric: steering toward agreeable personas does not produce a mirror increase in sycophancy. Geometrically, the persona vector is largely independent of the direction of sycophancy in activation space. Collectively, these findings suggest that sycophancy is better understood as a persona-level property rather than a single steerable direction. We release our code here: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Sycophancy-Steering-9DF0/.

</details>


### [133] [ScenePilot: Controllable Boundary-Driven Critical Scenario Generation for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21168)
*Qiyu Ruan,Yuxuan Wang,He Li,Zhenning Li,Cheng-zhong Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ScenePilot 是一种针对自动驾驶压力测试的场景生成框架，通过多目标强化学习与可行性屏蔽技术，精准生成物理可解但足以诱发系统失败的“边界”场景，显著提升了仿真测试的有效性与系统鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动驾驶仿真测试方法存在缺陷：要么生成因忽略物理限制而导致的“不切实际”的碰撞，要么过度专注于单一可行性约束而缺乏压力测试深度。研究旨在生成既满足物理可行性、又能诱发自动驾驶系统失效的“边界带宽”场景。

Method: 提出了 ScenePilot 框架，将场景生成建模为约束多目标强化学习问题。具体包括：1. 集成基于 RSS（责任敏感安全）的物理可行性评分 $σ$ 和在线学习的 AV 风险预测器 $Φ$；2. 引入步级可行性感知屏蔽机制（feasibility-aware shielding），确保探索过程严格限制在物理可解边界内。

Result: 在 SafeBench 上的实验表明，ScenePilot 将碰撞率提升了 6.2 个百分点，同时保持了场景的物理有效性；此外，对生成的边界场景进行对抗性微调，能够显著降低系统在测试中的实际崩溃率。

Conclusion: ScenePilot 通过在物理可行性与风险边界之间进行定向探索，有效提升了自动驾驶系统压力测试的质量，能够生成更具挑战性且真实的碰撞场景，从而显著增强自动驾驶系统的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Safety-critical scenarios are central to evaluating autonomous driving systems, yet their rarity in naturalistic logs makes simulation-based stress testing indispensable. Most scenario generation methods treat surrounding agents as adversaries, but they either (i) induce failures without explicitly modeling vehicle-road physical limits, yielding visually extreme yet physically unsolvable crashes, or (ii) enforce physical feasibility or policy feasibility in isolation, which can over-focus on aggressive maneuvers or remain tied to a controller-dependent capability boundary. We propose ScenePilot, a feasibility-guided, boundary-driven framework that targets the boundary band: scenarios that are physically solvable in principle yet still cause the deployed autonomy stack to fail. We formulate generation as constrained multi-objective reinforcement learning, combining an RSS-derived physical-feasibility score $σ$ with an online-learned AV-risk predictor $Φ$, and introduce step-level feasibility-aware shielding to keep exploration near the feasibility boundary while avoiding infeasible artifacts. Experiments on SafeBench with multiple planners show that ScenePilot yields substantially higher collision rates (+6.2 percentage points) while preserving physical validity, and that adversarial fine-tuning on these boundary-band scenarios consistently reduces downstream crash rates. The code is available at https://github.com/QiyuRuan/ScenePilot.

</details>


### [134] [Teaching AI Through Benchmark Construction: QuestBench as a Course-Based Practice for Accountable Knowledge Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21413)
*Haiyang Shen,Jiuzheng Wang,Taian Guo,Mugeng Liu,Wenchun Jing,Chongyang Pan,Siqi Zhong,Zhiyang Chen,Weichen Bi,Yudong Han,Xiaoying Bai,Yun Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种新型AI教学模式，通过让学生参与构建专家级基准测试（QuestBench）来评估深度研究AI系统，不仅揭示了当前AI在复杂任务中的高失败率，更通过这种实践成功增强了学生审视AI输出及巩固专业知识的批判性思维。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的AI教育往往将AI简单视为提高生产力的工具（如查阅、总结、编程），忽视了培养学生对AI生成知识进行严谨评估和批判性审视的能力。因此，需要建立一种能让学生测试AI并明确自身作为“知识主体”角色的教育框架。

Method: 提出了一种基于课程的教学方法：让学生通过构建基准测试（Benchmark Construction）来学习如何测试AI。学生需将学科知识转化为可验证的专家级问题，互评设计方案，并利用这些任务评估深度研究AI系统的表现，最终形成了包含256个问题的QuestBench数据集。

Result: 通过对13个深度研究AI系统的评估显示，QuestBench展现了现有系统在大规模任务中的隐蔽性失败，平均通过率仅为16.85%，表现最好的模型GPT-5.5也仅为57.58%。这些失败揭示了即使是语意通顺、来源详实的回复，在查询准确性、证据标准等方面仍存在巨大缺陷。

Conclusion: 基准测试构建是培养学生成为负责任知识主体的一项有效教学实践，它不仅是评价AI的手段，更能帮助学生深度理解专业知识，确立评估AI输出的思维范式。

Abstract: As AI becomes part of everyday learning, many courses teach students to use it mainly as a productivity tool: how to prompt, search, summarize, write, code, and use tools more efficiently. We argue that AI education also needs a setting in which students learn to test AI and understand their own role in judging machine-produced knowledge. To this end, we introduce a course-based practice that teaches AI through benchmark construction, using deep research systems as a concrete example of AI-era knowledge work. Students turn disciplinary knowledge into verifiable expert-level questions, review one another's designs for ambiguity and shortcuts, and evaluate AI systems on the resulting tasks. This activity gives students direct exposure to a powerful tool while asking them to specify what a trustworthy answer would require. The produced benchmark, QuestBench, consists of 256 questions across 14 humanities and social-science domains. Evaluation on QuestBench shows that student-designed tasks reveal hidden failures in current deep research systems: across thirteen evaluated systems, the mean question-level pass rate is only 16.85%, and the best-performing system, GPT-5.5, reaches a 57.58% pass rate. The failures are educationally useful because they show how fluent, source-backed answers can still miss the right query, source, term, or evidence standard. Reflections from five student contributors suggest that benchmark construction can help students see professional knowledge not only as content AI may retrieve, but as the basis for judging AI outputs. We present QuestBench as a benchmark artifact and as a reusable classroom setting for a larger educational question: how students can remain responsible knowledge actors as AI enters learning and professional work. The dataset is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/PKUAIWeb/QuestBench/tree/main.

</details>


### [135] [Mind the Sim-to-Real Gap & Think Like a Scientist](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21458)
*Harsh Parikh,Gabriel Levin-Konigsberg,Dominique Perrault-Joncas,Alexander Volfovsky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了如何结合廉价但有偏的模拟器与昂贵但无偏的实地实验。通过引入Fisher-SEP算法，解决了在复杂序列决策中如何通过最优实验设计来纠正模拟器偏差并覆盖未知区域的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟器查询成本低但存在偏差（校准数据导致的混杂和偏差），而实地实验无偏但成本高昂。研究旨在解决如何在模拟器与实地实验之间进行权衡，以优化序列决策方案。

Method: 1. 提出了扩展模拟引理（Extended Simulation Lemma），将模拟器误差分解为可识别的校准-部署偏差和不可缩减的参数残差；2. 将价值差距分解为局部（Local）和可达性（Reachability）两部分，指出被动学习无法解决覆盖范围不足的问题；3. 提出Fisher-SEP算法，通过最小化目标策略价值的后验预测方差来指导实验选择。

Result: 1. 模拟器误差分解理论支撑了实验的必要性；2. 揭示了被动学习在面对分布偏移或状态空间覆盖不足时的局限性；3. 通过自动售货机供应链和HIV移动检测两个案例，证明了Fisher-SEP在不同资源限制下的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，在存在模拟器偏差和实验耗损的场景下，通过Fisher-SEP策略将模拟器与实地实验智能化结合是实现最优决策的关键。对于长时延任务或探索范围广的场景，设计的主动探索（Designed Exploration）远优于被动学习。

Abstract: Suppose a planner has a pre-trained simulator of a sequential decision problem and the option to run real experiments in the field. The simulator is cheap to query but inherits confounding and drift from its calibration data. Experimentation is unbiased but consumes one real unit per trial. We study when, and how, the planner should supplement the simulator with experiments. We give three results. First, an extended simulation lemma decomposes the simulator's value error into a calibration--deployment shift that randomization can identify and a parametric residual that no further interaction can reduce. Second, the value gap between the simulator-optimal policy and the optimum splits into a local component, on states the deployed policy already visits, and a reachability component, on states it does not. The reachability component stays bounded away from zero at any horizon under purely passive learning. Third, we propose Fisher-SEP, a simulation-aided experimental policy (SEP) that minimizes the posterior predictive variance of a target policy's value, with reward-only and transition-only specializations. Two case studies illustrate the regimes. In a vending-machine supply chain, front-loaded experimentation overtakes posterior updating once the horizon is long enough to amortize the pilot. In an HIV mobile-testing example with a corridor that separates a well-surveilled region from a poorly-surveilled one, only designed exploration reaches the poorly-surveilled region.

</details>


### [136] [DeepWeb-Bench: A Deep Research Benchmark Demanding Massive Cross-Source Evidence and Long-Horizon Derivation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21482)
*Sixiong Xie,Zhuofan Shi,Haiyang Shen,Jiuzheng Wang,Siqi Zhong,Mugeng Liu,Chongyang Pan,Peilun Jia,Baoqing Sun,Xiang Jing,Yun Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文推出了 DeepWeb-Bench 基准，用于衡量前沿 AI 模型在复杂 Web 调研任务中的表现。研究发现，模型在“深度研究”中的瓶颈在于逻辑推导与结果校准，而非信息检索，且不同模型在特定领域表现出高度异质性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度研究基准过于简单，导致难以区分前沿语言模型在处理复杂、长程、多来源信息整合时的真实深度研究能力。

Method: 提出了 DeepWeb-Bench 基准，包含需要大规模证据收集、跨来源协调和长期多步推理的任务。将模型能力划分为检索、推导、推理和校准四个维度进行切片分析，并为参考答案配套了四级来源溯源记录以供审计。对九个前沿模型进行了基准测试。

Result: 1. 检索失败仅占错误来源的 12-14%，推导和校准失败占比超过 70%；2. 强模型错误主要源于推导不完整，弱模型则倾向于虚假的精确（幻觉）；3. 模型表现出明显的领域专门化，模型间的一致性相关系数仅为 0.61，单案分歧率高达 18.8%。

Conclusion: DeepWeb-Bench 在揭示当前前沿模型在深度研究任务中的瓶颈方面比现有基准更有效。研究发现检索能力并非主要短板，模型在逻辑推导（Derivation）和校准（Calibration）方面的不足才是限制性能的关键因素，且不同模型在特定领域表现出显著的专业化差异。

Abstract: Deep research, in which an agent searches the open web, collects evidence, and derives an answer through extended reasoning, is a prominent use case for frontier language models. Frontier deep research products score high on existing benchmarks, making it difficult to distinguish their capabilities from current evaluation data alone. We introduce DeepWeb-Bench, a deep research benchmark that is substantially harder than existing benchmarks for the current frontier. Difficulty comes from three properties of the data itself: each task requires massive evidence collection, cross-source reconciliation, and long-horizon multi-step derivation. We represent these three sources of difficulty as four capability families (Retrieval, Derivation, Reasoning, and Calibration) and report results sliced by family. Every reference answer is accompanied by a source-provenance record with four disclosure levels and cross-source checks where available, making scores easier to audit against the underlying evidence. We evaluate DeepWeb-Bench on nine frontier models and report three findings: (1) retrieval is not the bottleneck, as retrieval failures account for only 12-14% of errors while derivation and calibration failures account for over 70%; (2) strong and weak models fail in qualitatively different ways, with strong models' errors dominated by incomplete derivation and weak models' by hallucinated precision; and (3) models exhibit genuine specialization across domains, with cross-model agreement of only rho = 0.61 and per-case disagreement reaching 18.8 percentage points. The public benchmark release includes the data, rubrics, and evaluation code.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [137] [Multi-Head Attention as Ensemble Nadaraya-Watson Estimation: Variance Reduction, Decorrelation, and Optimal Head Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20271)
*Ernest Fokoué*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文将多头注意力视为Nadaraya-Watson核回归估计器的集成，证明了其通过正交化投影空间来减少方差，并提出了衡量头间多样性的指标（HDI）及最优架构缩放定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的注意力机制研究多基于经验，缺乏关于“多头如何协同工作以提升性能”的严谨理论解释。论文旨在从统计学习理论角度揭示MHA通过集成学习实现方差缩减的内部机理。

Method: 将MHA建模为Nadaraya-Watson（NW）核回归估计器的集合；推导了MHA均方误差（MSE）的偏差-方差-协方差分解；提出了衡量去相关程度的指标HDI；利用固定维度约束求解了头数H和每头维度d_k的最优分配。

Result: 证明了MHA的MSE随着HDI（头多样性指数）单调递减；推导了新的架构缩放定律：最优每头维度随数据集大小呈对数增长，而最优头数随总资源预算呈近似线性增长。

Conclusion: 多头注意力（MHA）通过在不同子空间投影实现方差缩减，其性能取决于头间去相关性（HDI）。该研究提出了新的缩放定律，确立了MHA是实现“同质智能体+多样性机制=涌现优化”这一通用原则的表现形式。

Abstract: We develop a rigorous statistical theory of multi-head attention (MHA) as an ensemble of Nadaraya-Watson (NW) kernel regression estimators. Building on the algebraic identity between single-head softmax attention and the NW estimator, we prove that MHA is a structured ensemble of H NW estimators, each operating in a distinct learned projection subspace of the key space. We derive an explicit Bias-Variance-Covariance decomposition of the MHA mean squared error, showing that variance reduction depends not merely on the number of heads H but fundamentally on the decorrelation of head outputs. Decorrelation is governed by the principal angles between learned projection subspaces: orthogonal projections yield maximum variance reduction; aligned projections yield none. We introduce the Head Diversity Index (HDI), a computable spectral measure of inter-head decorrelation, and prove that MHA mean squared error is monotonically decreasing in HDI. This provides the first rigorous theoretical explanation for the empirically observed specialization of attention heads. Under a fixed total-dimension budget D = H * d_k, we solve the optimal head-dimension allocation problem, deriving the MSE-minimizing pair (H*, d_k*) from data distribution and regression smoothness. The solution yields a new architectural scaling law: the optimal per-head dimension grows logarithmically with training set size, while the optimal number of heads grows nearly linearly with the total budget D. Our framework unifies three strands of prior work: the NW theory of single-head attention, the general weighting theory for ensemble learning, and the decorrelation-variance-reduction isomorphism between biological and computational ensembles. Multi-head attention is the Transformer's instantiation of a universal principle: identical agents plus diversity-enforcing mechanisms yields emergent optimality.

</details>


### [138] [Corrected Integrated Laplace Approximation for Bayesian Inference in Latent Gaussian Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20345)
*Jinlin Lai,Charles C. Margossian,Daniel R. Sheldon*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对潜高斯模型（LGMs）中积分拉普拉斯近似（ILA）带来的后验偏差，本文提出了一种基于重要性采样的修正方法，利用准蒙特卡洛技术及自动微分支持的HMC算法，实现了向精确后验的收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜高斯模型（LGMs）在处理非高斯似然时常依赖积分拉普拉斯近似（ILA），但在某些场景下ILA产生的后验近似误差导致下游应用效果不佳，需要一种能收敛到精确后验的修正方法。

Method: 提出了一种基于重要性采样的修正方案，集成了伪边缘化（pseudo-marginalization）、准蒙特卡洛（QMC）及随机准蒙特卡洛（RQMC）等技术，并将其集成在自动微分框架中以支持哈密顿蒙特卡洛（HMC）进行超参数推断。

Result: 该方法成功减小了ILA引入的近似误差；通过增加采样数量，后验分布能够收敛至真实后验；在多种应用模型（如空间模型、混合效应模型等）中展示了更优的推断性能。

Conclusion: 提出了一种利用重要性采样修正积分拉普拉斯近似（ILA）误差的通用框架。该方法通过增加采样数量可收敛至精确后验，并支持自动微分以实现基于梯度的推断（如哈密顿蒙特卡洛），在多种应用模型中证实了其有效性。

Abstract: Latent Gaussian models (LGMs) are a popular class of Bayesian hierarchical models that include Gaussian processes, as well as certain spatial models and mixed-effect models. Efficient Bayesian inference of LGMs often requires marginalizing out the latent variables. For LGMs with a non-Gaussian likelihood, exact marginalization is not possible and a popular approach is to do approximate marginalization with an integrated Laplace approximation (ILA). Using ILA produces an approximate posterior which, in some settings, can differ significantly from the correct posterior, which impacts downstream applications. We propose an importance sampling scheme to correct the error introduced by ILA. By increasing the number of samples in importance sampling, the posterior with ILA converges to the correct posterior. This idea is realized with various techniques, including pseudo-marginalization, quasi-Monte Carlo and randomized quasi-Monte Carlo. We implement our methods in an automatic differentiation framework to support gradient-based algorithms when doing inference on the hyperparameters. For the latter, we specifically consider the use of Hamiltonian Monte Carlo. We demonstrate the benefits of reduced error in various applied models.

</details>


### [139] [Contradiction Graphs Determine VC Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20434)
*Jesse Campbell,Daniel Ibaibarriaga,Lev Reyzin*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了二元概念类的矛盾图，证明了通过矛盾图序列 $(G_m(H))_{m \ge 1}$ 可以精确确定 VC 维，不仅回答了 Alon 等人提出的开放性问题，还为 VC 维的研究提供了新的图论视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨二元概念类的矛盾图结构如何反映该类的组合特性，旨在解决关于从矛盾图序列中确定 VC 维及其有限/无限属性的理论问题。

Method: 通过构造和分析二元概念类（binary concept classes）的阶数为 $m$ 的矛盾图（contradiction graph $G_m(H)$），即顶点为 $H$-可实现标记序列，若两者在某点标记相反则连边，研究其拓扑或图论结构与 VC 维之间的依赖关系。

Result: 证明了 $G_m(H)$ 的结构与判断 $\mathrm{VCdim}(H) \ge m$ 之间存在确定的逻辑联系，完善了统计学习理论中 VC 维的图论刻画。

Conclusion: 即使仅凭单个矛盾图 $G_m(H)$ 也能确定 VC 维是否至少为 $m$；而通过完整的矛盾图序列 $(G_m(H))_{m \ge 1}$ 则可以精确确定 VC 维，并有效区分有限与无限 VC 维，从而回答了 Alon 等人（2024）提出的问题。

Abstract: We study the contradiction graphs associated with binary concept classes. For a class $H \subseteq \{0,1\}^X$, the order-$m$ contradiction graph $G_m(H)$ has as vertices the $H$-realizable labeled sequences of length $m$, with two vertices adjacent when the two sequences assign opposite labels to some common domain point. Our main result is that the single graph $G_m(H)$ determines the threshold predicate $\mathrm{VCdim}(H)\ge m$. Consequently, the full sequence $(G_m(H))_{m \ge 1}$ determines the exact VC dimension and, in particular, detects finite versus infinite VC dimension, answering a question posed by Alon et al. (2024).

</details>


### [140] [Spectral bandits for smooth graph functions with applications in recommender systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20552)
*Tomáš Kocák,Michal Valko,Rémi Munos,Branislav Kveton,Shipra Agrawal*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于图平滑性的强盗算法，通过引入“有效维度”概念，实现了大规模图结构下的高效在线推荐，在减少评估次数的同时保持了高预测准确度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在线学习（如内容推荐）中，当候选项目（图节点）数量巨大时，传统多臂老虎机算法回归效率低的问题。利用项目间的图结构相似性（即邻近节点收益相似）来提升学习效率。

Method: 结合图结构平滑性假设，引入“有效维度”概念，设计了两种针对图结构收益的强盗算法（Bandit Algorithms），并利用该维度特性实现算法复杂度与节点总数的解耦。

Result: 实验表明，该算法仅需对几十个节点进行评估，即可对数以千计的项目做出准确的用户偏好预测，显著降低了累积遗憾。

Conclusion: 通过引入并利用图信号的“有效维度”（Effective Dimension），可以在大规模图数据上实现高效的在线学习。该方法使得在极少量评估下即可获得准确的用户偏好估计，有效解决了在图结构约束下多臂老虎机问题的扩展性瓶颈。

Abstract: Smooth functions on graphs have wide applications in manifold and semi-supervised learning. In this paper, we study a bandit problem where the payoffs of arms are smooth on a graph. This framework is suitable for solving online learning problems that involve graphs, such as content-based recommendation. In this problem, each recommended item is a node and its expected rating is similar to its neighbors. The goal is to recommend items that have high expected ratings. We aim for the algorithms where the cumulative regret would not scale poorly with the number of nodes. In particular, we introduce the notion of an effective dimension, which is small in real-world graphs, and propose two algorithms for solving our problem that scale linearly in this dimension. Our experiments on real-world content recommendation problem show that a good estimator of user preferences for thousands of items can be learned from just tens nodes evaluations.

</details>


### [141] [Group-Aware Matrix Estimation and Latent Subspace Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20559)
*Hamza Golubovic,Matthew Shen,Genevera I. Allen,Tarek M. Zikry*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对异构数据矩阵补全中全局低秩模型无法捕捉群体特异性结构的问题，提出了 GAME 算法，通过重叠核范数惩罚实现子群间信息共享与局部结构保留，有效提升了复杂结构缺失数据的补全性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准低秩矩阵补全方法假设单一全局潜在几何结构，在处理具有多种重叠元类别（如人口统计学、地理位置等）的异构数据时，容易忽略子群特有的变异性，尤其是在样本分布不均匀的情况下。

Method: GAME (Group-Aware Matrix Estimation)：一种基于凸优化的估计器，通过引入重叠核范数惩罚项 (overlapping nuclear-norm penalties)，对类别特定的子矩阵进行正则化，从而在保留局部潜在结构的同时允许群组间信息共享。

Result: 理论上提供了重构误差和子群特定子空间恢复的有限样本保证；实验表明在合成数据及推荐系统、生态学、神经科学等实际数据集上，GAME 在结构化缺失场景下显著提高了重构精度和潜在子空间的保真度。

Conclusion: GAME 是一种处理具有重叠子群结构矩阵补全问题的有效工具，通过利用群组间的共享结构和局部特征，在结构性缺失数据环境下显著优于传统的全局低秩估计器。

Abstract: Modern matrix completion problems often involve heterogeneous data whose rows simultaneously belong to many meta-categories, such as demographic and age groups in recommendation systems, or region and recording session labels in neural electrophysiological experiments. Standard low-rank estimators impose a single global latent geometry, which can recover average structure but may smooth away subgroup-specific variation, especially when observations are unevenly distributed across groups. We introduce Group-Aware Matrix Estimation (GAME), a convex estimator for overlapping subgroup-wise low-rank matrix estimation. GAME regularizes category-specific submatrices through overlapping nuclear-norm penalties, allowing related groups to borrow information while preserving local latent structure in a shared coordinate system. We provide finite-sample guarantees for both reconstruction error and subgroup-specific subspace recovery, showing how performance depends on sampling density, subgroup rank, and overlap structure. Experiments on synthetic, recommendation, ecological, and neuroscience datasets show that GAME is most beneficial in structured missingness regimes, where subgroup-aware regularization improves both reconstruction accuracy and latent subspace fidelity. Across these benchmarks, GAME is competitive or best among global low-rank, side-information, and modern imputation baselines, with the largest gains when subgroups exhibit distinct low-rank structure.

</details>


### [142] [Conditioning Gaussian Processes on Almost Anything](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21041)
*Henry Moss,Lachlan Astfalck,Thomas Cowperthwaite,Colin Doumont,Sam Willis,Philipp Hennig,Christopher Nemeth,Andrew Zammit-Mangion*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文通过将高斯过程建模为线性扩散过程，实现了一种通用的、无需定制推导的概率推理框架，允许将大语言模型等复杂先验知识引入GP推理中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统高斯过程（GP）在非线性和非共轭设置下，由于推理过程复杂（受限于线性-高斯范式），难以直接引入真实的、复杂的先验知识。

Method: 建立GP与线性扩散模型之间的显式等价关系，将预测采样重构为具有闭式高斯动力学和似然依赖引导项的常微分方程（ODE），并采用白化（Whitening）技术最小化Wasserstein-2传输成本以消除数值刚性。

Result: 该方法在线性-高斯设置下精确恢复了标准GP条件，并成功扩展至非线性物理场景及基于大语言模型（LLM）的自然语言条件约束。该方案无需针对特定问题进行复杂的定制化推导，具有普适性。

Conclusion: 提出了一种通用的高斯过程（GP）推理框架。通过将GP与线性扩散模型建立等价关系，该方法克服了传统GP在非线性、非算子及复杂条件下的限制，使得GP能够结合大型语言模型等先验知识进行概率建模。

Abstract: Gaussian processes (GPs) offer a principled probabilistic model over functions, but exact inference is restricted to the linear-Gaussian regime. We establish an explicit equivalence between GPs and a class of linear diffusion models, recasting predictive sampling as an ODE with closed-form Gaussian dynamics and a likelihood-dependent guidance term that admits a simple Monte Carlo approximation. In the linear-Gaussian setting, we recover standard GP conditioning exactly; beyond conjugacy, the same machinery handles any conditioning statement admitting point-wise likelihood evaluation -- including non-linear physics, and, for the first time, natural language via large language models. Whitening isolates the irreducible non-Gaussian dynamics, minimising Wasserstein-2 transport cost and eliminating numerical stiffness. The result is a general-purpose GP inference scheme requiring no bespoke derivations. Together, these results provide a general mechanism for incorporating the full richness of real-world knowledge as conditioning information, opening a new frontier for the probabilistic modelling of real-world problems.

</details>


### [143] [Federated LoRA Fine-Tuning for LLMs via Collaborative Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21217)
*Shuaida He,Liwen Chen,Long Feng*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对联邦LoRA微调中数据污染与异构问题，提出CLAIR框架，通过结构化分解实现稳健的共享子空间协同训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决大规模语言模型在联邦学习场景下，面临客户端高度异构及数据受污染问题时，参数高效微调（LoRA）的稳定性与协作效率问题。

Method: 提出了协同低秩对齐与可识别恢复（CLAIR）框架，利用矩阵值更新的特性，通过结构化低秩+块稀疏分解技术进行子空间恢复与污染检测。

Result: 理论上证明了在特定条件下可实现共享子空间与干净客户端集的精确、稳定恢复；实验证实CLAIR在文本复制任务中能准确识别污染，且性能优于标准局部微调与非稳健联邦平均算法。

Conclusion: CLAIR通过结构化低秩加块稀疏分解，在联邦学习场景下有效实现了对抗污染的稳健LoRA微调，即便是异构客户端也能实现更优的学习效果。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) has emerged as a powerful tool for parameter-efficient fine-tuning of large language models (LLMs). This paper studies LoRA under a federated learning setting, enabling collaborative fine-tuning across clients while preserving parameter efficiency. We focus on a highly heterogeneous regime in which clients share only partial structure and a substantial subset may be contaminated. We propose Collaborative Low-rank Alignment and Identifiable Recovery (CLAIR), a contamination-aware framework that relies only on preliminary local estimators. Its formulation applies broadly, from linear regression to neural network and LLM modules, whenever local adaptation can be represented by matrix-valued updates. CLAIR recovers the shared LoRA subspace and detects contaminated clients via a structured low-rank plus block-sparse decomposition.
