We prove exact recovery of the shared LoRA subspace in the noiseless case, stable recovery under preliminary estimation error, and consistent collaborative-set recovery under mild separation conditions. We further quantify the gain from CLAIR refinement: it reduces off-subspace estimation error through cross-client averaging while preserving client-specific variation within the shared LoRA subspace, thus improves over local fine-tuning whenever this oracle gain outweighs the costs of subspace estimation and benign-client heterogeneity. Empirically, we demonstrate the benefits of CLAIR by fine-tuning a Transformer architecture on a text-copying task. The results show accurate contamination detection and improved benign-client performance compared with local fine-tuning and non-robust federated averaging.

</details>


### [144] [Theoretical guidelines for annealed Langevin dynamics in compositional simulation-based inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21253)
*Camille Touron,Gabriel V. Cardoso,Julyan Arbel,Pedro L. C. Rodrigues*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文针对组合分数SBI框架下的Annealed Langevin采样，通过推导Wasserstein误差边界，为超参数的选择提供了理论化、可操作的决策规则，并量化对比了现有两种主流组合分数方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前的组合分数SBI方法在通过逆向SDE采样时存在不可约偏置，而现有的Annealed Langevin采样方案缺乏理论支撑，超参数选择主要依赖经验，导致采样效率和准确性难以保证。

Method: 1. 导出了带有近似分数的Annealed Langevin dynamics的Wasserstein边界；2. 将边界转化为明确的超参数（步长、每层迭代次数、退火层数）决策规则；3. 在高斯假设下推导出闭式解，对比两种组合分数方案的效率。

Result: 1. 建立了一套能够保障预定采样精度的超参数决策规则；2. 理论证明在Gaussian设置下，Linhart等人的方法相比Geffner等人的方法允许更大的步长且所需的总Langevin步数更少；3. 实验验证了高斯设置下的超参数调优策略可推广至更复杂的任务。

Conclusion: 该研究为基于组合分数的SBI提供了理论指导，证明了基于Annealed Langevin dynamics的采样方法能够通过受控的超参数选择实现预期的采样精度，且Linhart等人的方法在效率上优于Geffner等人的方法。

Abstract: Compositional score-based approaches to simulation-based inference (SBI) approximate the posterior over a shared parameter given $n$ independent observations by aggregating individually learned posterior scores: currently, there are two main propositions of such methods (Geffner et al. (2023), Linhart et al. (2026)). As the resulting composite score does not correspond to the score of any distribution along the forward diffusion path of the true multi-observation posterior, sampling from it via a reverse SDE leads to an irreducible bias. Annealed Langevin dynamics provides a principled alternative: it treats the composite score as the genuine score of a sequence of tractable bridging densities and samples from them in succession. When properly tuned, it could lead to a controllable bias. However, its hyperparameters, namely step sizes, the number of steps per level, and the number of annealing levels, have so far been chosen empirically. We derive Wasserstein bounds for annealed Langevin with approximate scores and translate them into explicit decision rules for these hyperparameters that guarantee a prescribed sampling accuracy, while highlighting different theoretical aspects of each composite score formulation. In the Gaussian setting, we obtain closed-form expressions for all relevant quantities and prove that the bridging densities of Linhart et al. (2026) consistently admit larger step sizes and require fewer total Langevin steps than those of Geffner et al. (2023). Furthermore, we show empirically that the tuning obtained in the Gaussian setting generalizes to more complex problems, thus providing a well-understood and theoretically grounded starting point for practitioners using compositional score-based approaches.

</details>


### [145] [Large-Step Training Dynamics of a Two-Factor Linear Transformer Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21292)
*Krishnakumar Balasubramanian*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文通过简化模型证明了：Transformer在大学习率下的有限步训练并非简单的收敛，而是可能演化出周期、混沌或发散等复杂动力学行为，而非总是学会线性回归任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的梯度流分析无法解释Transformer在大学习率下的有限步梯度下降（Gradient Descent）不稳定行为，且观察到其表现与二次回归的立方映射类似。

Method: 将单提示线性Transformer的训练问题简化为可精确求解的二维双因子乘积映射（two-factor product map），并分析其在有效步长参数μ下的动力学特性、不变Chebyshev椭圆轨迹及横向稳定性。

Result: 发现在学习率μ∈(0,2)范围内存在Chebyshev椭圆作为不变量；当系统处于平衡态时存在吸引子，但其横向稳定性决定了训练的最终表现。超阈值的训练往往会导致训练动力学偏离最优解，进入混沌或发散状态。

Conclusion: 大步长学习率不仅仅是加速收敛，更会根本性改变Transformer的训练吸引子。当超过稳定阈值时，训练可能陷入周期律动、有界混沌或发散，而非获得预期的线性回归求解能力。

Abstract: Gradient-flow analyses show that simplified linear transformers can learn the in-context linear-regression algorithm, but they do not explain the finite-step behavior of gradient descent at large learning rates. Motivated by empirical work on high-learning-rate transformer instabilities and by the cubic-map phase diagram for quadratic regression, we study an exactly reducible one-prompt linear-transformer training problem. After normalization, the dynamics reduce to a two-factor product map with an effective step-size parameter \(μ\). On the balanced slice, this map recovers the known scalar cubic transition from monotone convergence to catapult convergence, periodic and chaotic bounded nonconvergence, and divergence. We then analyze the full two-dimensional system and show that, for \(0<μ<2\), it has an explicit invariant Chebyshev ellipse separating forward-invariant regions; this ellipse carries off-balanced chaotic dynamics but is transversely repelling, while balanced scalar attractors can be transversely attracting. These results show that large constant learning rates can change the training attractor of the learned transformer rather than merely accelerating convergence: beyond sharp stability thresholds, finite-step training may settle into cycles, bounded chaos, or divergence instead of a single in-context linear-regression solution. We also discuss the consequences for mini-batch gradient descent based training methods.

</details>


### [146] [Semiparametric Efficient Bilevel Gradient Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21341)
*Fares El Khoury,Houssam Zenati,Nathan Kallus,Michael Arbel,Aurélien Bibaut*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对非参数双层优化中的一阶偏差问题，提出了一种基于有效影响函数的半参数去偏超梯度估计方法，实现了更准确的估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的功能性双层优化方法在下层问题采用非参数学习时，插件梯度（Plug-in gradient）会保留显著的一阶偏差。

Method: 基于有效影响函数（Efficient Influence Function）的半参数去偏理论；构建了交叉拟合（Cross-fitted）的正交超梯度（Orthogonal Hypergradient）估计器；在二次损失下退化为双重鲁棒分数（Doubly Robust Score）。

Result: 在合成及基准测试中，该方法成功追踪到预言机（Oracle）级别的有效梯度基准，表现显著优于插件式功能超梯度和正则化核双层基准方案。

Conclusion: 提出了基于有效影响函数的半参数去偏双层梯度估计器，该方法通过正交化消除了非参数学习带来的首阶偏差，实现了渐近正态性和对外部参数的一致控制，并在实验中优于基准方法。

Abstract: Functional bilevel methods estimate a lower-level function and plug it into a hypergradient, but this plug-in gradient can retain first-order bias when the lower-level problem is learned nonparametrically. To remove this bias, we develop a semiparametric debiasing theory for population bilevel gradients based on the efficient influence function. This perspective leads to a cross-fitted orthogonal hypergradient estimator for which we establish asymptotic normality together with uniform control over the outer parameter. Under quadratic losses, the estimator reduces to a simple doubly robust score based on conditional mean nuisances. On synthetic bilevel benchmarks with known ground truth, the method tracks the oracle efficient-gradient benchmark and improves over plug-in functional hypergradients and regularized kernel bilevel baselines.

</details>


### [147] [Memorisation, convergence and generalisation in generative models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21402)
*Antoine Maillard,Sebastian Goldt*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了生成式模型泛化的双重性：收敛只是匹配了数据分布的“大头”，而识别数据的底层潜在因子则是另一个独立的、更难达到的任务。单纯的收敛并不等同于特征的还原。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对生成式模型究竟是“学习”还是“记忆”的问题，探讨生成模型收敛所需的样本量，以及收敛性到底在多大程度上代表了对数据分布的习得。

Method: 通过线性生成模型提供从记忆到泛化的精确分析刻画，并将其扩展到具有幂律谱的数据，同时在卷积降噪器和现有文献数据上进行实验验证。

Result: 发现在小样本下模型表现为记忆；当样本量与输入维度呈线性关系时，模型收敛。关键发现是收敛性对潜在因子的恢复是不敏感的，潜在因子的恢复表现为尖锐的相变。这一结论在幂律分布数据及卷积降噪器中均成立。

Conclusion: 生成式模型的泛化能力包含两个截然不同的目标：匹配数据分布的主体（对应收敛性）和恢复数据的潜在因子（对应潜在因子的恢复）。仅凭“收敛”并不代表模型真正学习到了数据的潜在结构。

Abstract: Generative neural networks learn how to produce highly realistic images from a large, but finite number of examples - or do they simply memorise their training set? To settle this question, Kadkhodaie, Guth, Simoncelli and Mallat (ICLR '24) trained diffusion models independently on disjoint subsets of a dataset and showed that they converge to nearly the same density when the number of training images is large enough. This result raises two basic questions: how much data do you need for convergence, and what does convergence capture about learning the data distribution? Here, we address these questions by providing an exact analytical characterisation of the transition from memorisation to generalisation in linear generative models. We find that these models memorise at small load, while convergence emerges continuously when the number of samples is linear in the input dimension. Strikingly, we find that convergence is insensitive to recovery of the principal latent factors of the data, which are recovered in a sharp transition. After extending our approach to data with power-law spectra, we find the same distinction between convergence and latent recovery in our experiments with convolutional denoisers and in the data of Kadkhodaie et al. We thus show that generalisation in generative models decomposes into at least two distinct objectives: matching the bulk of the data distribution and recovering the principal latent factors. These objectives correspond to two different distances between true and learnt data distribution, and only the first one is captured by convergence.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [148] [Causal Unlearning in Collaborative Optimization: Exact and Approximate Influence Reversal under Adversarial Contributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20341)
*Ali Mahdavi,Azadeh Zamanifar,Amirfarhad Farhadi,Omid Kashefi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HF-KCU 是一种高效的联邦学习机器遗忘算法，通过 Krylov 子空间近似和因果加权机制，在极低计算开销下实现了接近重新训练的遗忘效果与隐私保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器学习遗忘方法在联邦学习中面临高昂的重新训练计算成本，且需满足隐私合规性要求，同时要处理异步联邦环境下的不稳定性和对抗性摄动。

Method: 提出 HF-KCU 方法，通过 Krylov 子空间中的共轭梯度迭代来近似影响函数（Influence Function），将复杂度从 O(d^3) 降低至 O(kd)；引入因果加权机制，确保仅更新持有被遗忘数据的客户端，并对 Hessian 和梯度的扰动具有鲁棒性。

Result: 在 CIFAR-10 数据集上，相比重新训练，HF-KCU 实现了 47.75 倍的加速，测试准确率仅下降 0.60%，且成员推理攻击成功率降至 0.499（与重新训练一致），证明了其良好的遗忘效果和性能。

Conclusion: HF-KCU 是一种在联邦学习中实现机器遗忘（Machine Unlearning）的高效、精确且可解释的方法，能在满足隐私合规的同时大幅降低计算开销，适用于生产环境。

Abstract: Federated learning systems must support data deletion requests to comply with privacy regulations, yet retraining from scratch after each deletion is computationally prohibitive. We present HF-KCU, a method that removes a client's contribution by approximating the influence function through conjugate gradient iterations in Krylov subspaces, reducing complexity from O(d^3) to O(kd) where k<<d.A causal weighting mechanism ensures that only clients holding the deleted data receive parameter updates, preventing spurious changes to unaffected clients. Our method is designed to handle bounded adversarial perturbations to the Hessian and gradient, providing graceful degradation under realistic threat models. We validate HF-KCU across convolutional (ResNet-18, SimpleCNN) and transformer (ViT-Lite) architectures on CIFAR-10, MNIST, and Fashion-MNIST. On CIFAR-10 under Dirichlet (alpha=0.5) partitioning, HF-KCU achieves 47.75 times speedup over retraining while maintaining test accuracy within 0.60% of the rational baseline(71.16 vs 71.76 %). Membership inference attacks on the forget set yield success rates of 0.499 matching the retrained model and confirming effective privacy restoration. We provide convergence guarantees showing that the Krylov approximation error decreases as O((k ^1/2-1)/(k^1/2+1)) where k is the Hessian condition number. The causal weighting mechanism ensures surgical updates, where only clients holding deleted data are modified, preserving model quality for unaffected participants and avoiding the instability of gradient-based approaches in asynchronous federated settings. This design provides interpretability as each update is directly traceable to the influence of the deleted data. The method's efficiency and precision make it suitable for production federated systems where deletion requests arrive asynchronously and computational budgets are constrained.

</details>


### [149] [Dynamic Shapley Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20620)
*Xuan Yang,Hsi-Wen Chen,Ming-Syan Chen,Jian Pei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: D-Shap 通过将 Shapley 计算建模为动态矩阵维护问题，利用任务与数据的局部性特征，实现了高效的数据贡献价值更新，解决了传统方法难以应对动态场景的痛点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的 Shapley 数据估值方法计算成本高昂，且将计算视作“一次性”过程，导致在动态任务或玩家变更时必须全量重算，无法适应现实中任务和数据不断演化的需求。

Method: 提出 D-Shap 框架：1) 将 Shapley 值建模为“玩家-任务”矩阵；2) 利用“效用局部性”和“联盟局部性”进行结构感知插值（用于任务更新）和局部矩阵块操作（用于玩家更新）；3) 引入“自评估（self-valuation）”机制，通过覆盖感知锚点选择实现无预设任务评估。

Result: 实验表明，D-Shap 在任务更新上达到毫秒级速度，将玩家更新的计算成本降低了三个数量级，且数据估值质量与全量重算方案相当。

Conclusion: D-Shap 框架成功将 Shapley 值计算转化为结构化矩阵维护问题，利用局部性特征实现了毫秒级的任务更新和数量级减小的玩家更新成本。未来该方法可有效支持大规模、动态演进的数据评估场景。

Abstract: Shapley-based data valuation provides a principled way to quantify the contribution of training data, but its high computational cost makes it impractical in dynamic settings where tasks and training players evolve. Existing methods treat Shapley computation as a one-shot process and collapse contributions into aggregated scores, preventing reuse and requiring recomputation under any change. We introduce a new perspective that represents Shapley values as a player-by-task matrix and formulates dynamic valuation as a structured matrix maintenance problem. We exploit the fact that each task depends on a small subset of training players and that similar tasks yield similar valuations, leading to utility locality and coalition locality. Based on these insights, we propose D-Shap, a dynamic valuation framework that enables efficient updates by modifying only a small portion of the matrix: new task valuations are inferred via structure-aware interpolation, while updates induced by new players are confined to affected local matrix blocks. To eliminate the need for pre-specified evaluation tasks, we introduce self-valuation, which constructs the initial matrix directly from training data, supported by scalable subset reuse and coverage-aware anchor selection. Experiments across diverse models show that D-Shap performs task updates in milliseconds and reduces the cost of player updates by up to three orders of magnitude, while achieving valuation quality competitive with full recomputation.

</details>


### [150] [GraphDiffMed: Knowledge-Constrained Differential Attention with Pharmacological Graph Priors for Medication Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20188)
*Krati Saxena,Tomohiro Shibata*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GraphDiffMed 是一种全新的药物推荐框架，通过双尺度差分注意力机制过滤 EHR 噪声，并引入药理学约束，在保证临床安全性的同时，实现了更精准的药物推荐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 EHR 大规模、高噪声和临床异质性强的特征下，现有的推荐模型难以同时实现有效的时序建模与药理学知识深度集成，导致推荐的安全性不足。

Method: 提出了 GraphDiffMed 框架，采用双尺度 Differential Attention v2（差分注意力机制）处理患者就诊内和就诊间的数据，以消除噪声，并结合药理学约束（如药物相互作用 DDI）指导模型学习。

Result: 在 MIMIC-III 数据集上的实验结果表明，该模型在推荐质量和安全性平衡方面均优于现有基准模型（Baseline），且在仅利用人口统计学辅助特征时表现依然稳健。

Conclusion: GraphDiffMed 通过结合噪声感知注意力机制与药理学约束，有效提升了药物推荐的质量、排名表现及安全性，证明了在处理电子健康记录（EHR）时，兼顾时间序列建模与领域先验知识是实现鲁棒性临床决策的关键。

Abstract: Recommending safe and effective medication combinations from electronic health records (EHRs) is a core clinical AI problem, yet it remains difficult because patient trajectories are long, noisy, and clinically heterogeneous. Existing methods typically excel at either temporal modeling across visits or pharmacological knowledge integration (e.g., drug-drug interactions, DDIs), but rarely achieve both while robustly suppressing noise. We present GraphDiffMed, a knowledge-constrained medication recommendation framework built on dual-scale Differential Attention v2. Differential attention is applied at both intra-visit and inter-visit levels to filter spurious signals within encounters and across longitudinal history, while pharmacological constraints are incorporated during learning. Experiments on MIMIC-III and ablation studies show that this design consistently improves recommendation quality and ranking over strong baselines while achieving a more favorable safety performance balance. We further find that the strongest-performing configuration uses only demographic auxiliary features under our experimental setting. Overall, GraphDiffMed demonstrates that combining noise-aware attention with pharmacological constraints yields more reliable and clinically meaningful medication recommendation. We open-source our code at https://github.com/saxenakrati09/GraphDiffMed.

</details>


### [151] [Provably Learning Diffusion Models under the Manifold Hypothesis: Collapse and Refine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20235)
*Wei Huang,Andi Han,Mingyuan Bai,Huanjian Zhou,Qixin Zhang,Taiji Suzuki,Kenji Fukumizu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了扩散模型通过得分函数的几何特性实现降维的机制，并基于此提出了无需 VAE 正则化的 SiLD 模型，成功降低了样本复杂度对环境维度的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型虽然生成质量高，但其在处理高维数据时如何绕过“维度灾难”并高效学习得分函数，缺乏相应的理论解释。

Method: 提出了“得分诱导潜空间扩散”（Score-induced Latent Diffusion, SiLD）框架，利用得分函数本身的几何特性，在小噪声尺度下驱动降维（流形投影），在中等噪声尺度下进行密度精炼，无需 VAE 的 KL 正则化。

Result: SiLD 在 Stacked MNIST、CelebA 及分子生成基准上表现优异，生成质量达到或超过基于 VAE 的潜在扩散模型（LDM），并显著提升了重构性能。

Conclusion: SiLD 通过单一去噪得分匹配目标实现了流形学习与密度估计的统一，并证明了其样本复杂度依赖于数据的本征维度而非环境维度，克服了高维数据的“维度灾难”。

Abstract: Diffusion models generate high-dimensional data with remarkable quality, yet how their training efficiently learns the score function, bypassing the curse of dimensionality when data is supported on low-dimensional manifolds, remains theoretically unexplained. We identify a collapse-and-refine mechanism driven by the geometry of the score function itself: at small noise scales, the diverging singularity of the score drives a rapid dimensional collapse of the induced denoising map onto the data manifold projection; at moderate noise scales, training refines the intrinsic density on the learned manifold. We instantiate this principle as Score-induced Latent Diffusion (SiLD), a two-stage framework in which both manifold learning and density estimation emerge from a single denoising score matching objective, replacing the heuristic KL regularization of VAE-based latent diffusion models. We prove that the resulting sample complexity depends on the intrinsic dimension rather than the ambient dimension. Experiments on Stacked MNIST, CelebA variants, and molecular generation benchmarks show that SiLD matches or outperforms VAE-based LDMs in generation quality and consistently improves reconstruction, validating our theoretical predictions.

</details>


### [152] [Efficient Banzhaf-Based Data Valuation for $k$-Nearest Neighbors Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21033)
*Guangyi Zhang,Lutz Oettershagen,Lixu Wang,Aristides Gionis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文解决了 kNN 分类器中 Banzhaf 数据价值评估的计算难题，通过利用局部特性引入动态规划算法，将计算复杂度大幅降低至可实用水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据价值评估在机器学习中至关重要，但基于博弈论（如 Banzhaf 值）的公平评估框架存在指数级计算复杂性，无法在实际应用中直接计算。

Method: 1. 理论证明 Banzhaf 值的计算在 kNN 分类器中是 #P-hard 问题。 2. 利用 kNN 的局部特性，开发动态规划框架：针对加权 kNN 提出复杂度为 O(Wkn^2) 的伪多项式算法；针对非加权 kNN 提出 O(nk^2) 的线性复杂度算法。 3. 提供高效的蒙特卡洛估计方法用于近似计算。

Result: 提出的算法在理论上显著降低了计算复杂度，实验证明在真实数据集上表现出极高的效率，并能有效完成数据价值评估任务。

Conclusion: 该研究通过利用 kNN 分类的局部特性，打破了计算 Banzhaf 值的计算瓶颈，并为有效评估大规模数据集的价值提供了切实可行的方法。

Abstract: Data valuation, the task of quantifying the contribution of individual data points to model performance, has emerged as a fundamental challenge in machine learning. Game-theoretic approaches, such as the Banzhaf value, offer principled frameworks for fair data valuation; however, they suffer from exponential computational complexity. We address this challenge by developing efficient algorithms specifically tailored for computing Banzhaf values in $k$-nearest neighbor ($k$NN) classifiers. We first establish the theoretical hardness of the problem by proving that it is \#P-hard. Despite this intractability, we exploit the locality properties of $k$NN classifiers to develop practical exact algorithms. Our main contribution is a dynamic programming framework that achieves significant computational improvements: we present a pseudo-polynomial algorithm with $O(Wkn^2)$ time complexity for weighted $k$NN classifiers, where $W$ is the maximum sum of top-$k$ weights, and a specialized algorithm for unweighted $k$NN that achieves $O(nk^2)$ time complexity, that is, linear in the number of data points. We also offer efficient Monte Carlo estimation methods. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate the practical efficiency of our approach and its effectiveness in data valuation applications.

</details>


### [153] [MagBridge-Battery: A Synthetic Bridge Dataset for Li-ion Magnetometry and State-of-Health Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20240)
*Sakthi Prabhu Gunasekar,Prasanna Kumar Rangarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发布了首个名为 MagBridge-Battery v1.0 的电池健康诊断磁场合成数据集与 benchmark，旨在解决该领域公开数据匮乏的问题，并验证了磁签名在电池健康监测中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前电池健康诊断主要依赖电化学信号，磁场感知虽能提供额外信息，但由于缺乏带有退化标签的公开电池磁场测量数据集，该领域的发展受到限制。

Method: 构建了名为 MagBridge-Battery v1.0 的合成数据集，该数据集结合了 Mohammadi-Jerschow OSF 的真实磁形态数据与 PulseBat 数据集的 SOH 标签，并进行了严格的样本划分（无重叠电池、无交叉父子样本），同时设计了包括 SOH 回归、分类及异常检测在内的评估基准。

Result: 通过 label-shuffle 消融实验证明，模型能够非平凡地编码 SOH 信息（R² 约 0.77），而非产生仅仅是标签对齐的伪影，验证了数据集的有效性。该数据集含 6,760 条磁场特征，并提供了完整的 Benchmark 套件。

Conclusion: MagBridge-Battery v1.0 填补了电池磁场诊断领域公开数据集的空白，通过提供高质量、经过严格划分的合成数据集，为电池健康诊断（SOH）、梯次利用分类及异常检测任务建立了基准，证实了磁场信号在电池诊断中的应用价值。

Abstract: Battery health diagnostics today rely overwhelmingly on electrochemical signals measured at the cell terminals. A parallel literature has shown that magnetic sensing can resolve information that terminal-only measurements miss, but method development is limited by the absence, to the best of our knowledge, of public battery magnetic-measurement datasets paired with degradation labels. We release MagBridge-Battery v1.0, a synthetic dataset of 6,760 magnetic-field signatures that bridges real magnetic morphology from the Mohammadi-Jerschow Open Science Framework (OSF) archive with state-of-health (SOH) labels from the PulseBat dataset. The release contains 5,600 PulseBat-conditioned grounded samples, 600 synthetic sensor-anomaly samples derived from clean parents, and 560 low-voltage Regime-B extrapolation samples. A cell-disjoint, parent-child-leakage-free primary benchmark split is verified to contain zero overlapping cells, zero cross-split parent-child pairs, and zero sample-ID overlap. We define three primary benchmark tasks: SOH regression, second-life classification, and anomaly detection, plus an auxiliary anomaly-subtype classification task. A controlled label-shuffle ablation collapses SOH regression from R^2 approximately 0.77 to approximately 0, confirming that the bridge encodes input SOH non-trivially rather than producing label-aligned artifacts. The dataset is released on Zenodo under CC-BY-4.0, and the bridge code and benchmark suite are released under Apache-2.0. This work provides a public benchmark for magnetic-sensing battery diagnostics while paired magnetic-electrochemical measurements remain scarce.

</details>


### [154] [Polynomial-Time Robust Multiclass Linear Classification under Gaussian Marginals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21428)
*Ilias Diakonikolas,Giannis Iakovidis,Mingchen Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了高斯分布下多类线性分类器的不可知学习问题，克服了以往算法的指数级依赖问题，提出了在多项式时间内实现高效误差保证的新算法框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的二分类不可知学习理论成熟，但在 $k \ge 3$ 的多分类场景下，现有算法在复杂度和表示大小上往往具有指数级依赖，缺乏高效且通用的多分类学习算法。

Method: 1. 分析多类感知机算法，证明其存在超多项式样本复杂度障碍；2. 提出基于成对非真学习（Pairwise Improper-learning）的通用框架；3. 构建基于局部化（Localization-based）的优化框架，针对 $k=3$ 及几何正则分类器提升误差界限。

Result: 1. 发现标准多类感知机在处理高斯边缘分布时即便在干净标签下也需要超多项式的样本和更新；2. 对于一般 $k$，实现了误差为 $\widetilde O(k^{3/2}\sqrt{\mathrm{opt}})+ε$ 的高效学习器；3. 对于 $k=3$，实现了 $O(\mathrm{opt})+ε$ 的最优误差，对几何正则分类器实现了 $\mathrm{poly}(k)\mathrm{opt}+ε$ 的误差界。

Conclusion: 该研究弥补了多类线性分类器不可知学习（Agnostic Learning）领域在 $k \ge 3$ 情况下的理论缺失，证明了多类感知机算法的局限性，并提出了在多项式时间内实现误差保证的学习框架。

Abstract: We study the task of agnostic learning of multiclass linear classifiers under the Gaussian distribution. Given labeled examples $(x, y)$ from a distribution over $\mathbb{R}^d \times [k]$, with Gaussian $x$-marginal, the goal is to output a hypothesis whose error is comparable to that of the best $k$-class linear classifier. While the binary case $k=2$ has a well-developed algorithmic theory, much less is known for $k \ge 3$. Even for $k=3$, prior robust algorithms incur exponential dependence on the inverse of the desired accuracy in both complexity and representation size. In this work, we develop new structural results for multiclass linear classifiers and use them to design fully polynomial-time robust learners with dimension-independent error guarantees. Our first result shows that the standard multiclass perceptron algorithm requires super-polynomially many samples and updates, even with clean labels and Gaussian marginals, revealing a basic obstruction absent in the binary case. Our main positive result is a pairwise improper-learning framework which yields an efficient learner with error $\widetilde O(k^{3/2}\sqrt{\mathrm{opt}})+ε$ for general $k$. Additionally, we develop a sharper localization-based framework which leads to error $O(\mathrm{opt})+ε$ for $k=3$, and error $\mathrm{poly}(k)\mathrm{opt}+ε$ for geometrically regular $k$-class linear classifiers.

</details>


### [155] [Geometry-Lite: Interpretable Safety Probing via Layer-Wise Margin Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20241)
*Woo Seob Sim,Yu Rang Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Geometry-Lite 工具，揭示了大语言模型中的安全性证据主要形成于持久的层级几何边界，而非层间的动态演变，并探讨了如何利用这种几何特征提高对安全提示检测的鲁棒性和解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然现有探针能在平均水平上区分安全与不安全提示，但缺乏对大语言模型内部“如何形成安全性证据”的几何解释，特别是对于低误报率决策的层级机制以及跨基准测试迁移时的几何稳定性缺乏深入理解。

Method: 引入 Geometry-Lite 探针，通过将每一层的提示标记（prompt-token）表示映射到质心、局部邻域和监督线性边界的符号间隔（signed margins）上，进而从边界位置、层间变化和整体形状三个维度对安全性信号进行分解。

Result: Geometry-Lite 在 9 个骨干模型和 7 个安全基准上表现优于单层探针；研究发现安全证据主要由持久的边界位置几何（特别是最终层或极值间隔）主导，而层间差异（drift）和结构汇总对整体 AUROC 贡献有限，且在面临基准偏移时，类条件均值几何结构比优化线性边界更具鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 大语言模型的安全性证据主要不是通过层间的动态信号（motion signal）表达的，而是表现为持久的层级边界几何形态（persistent layer-wise margin geometry）。在决策关键区域，这种几何结构的有效性及其偏差才会显著体现。

Abstract: Prompt-level safety probes for large language models use hidden-state representations to separate safe from unsafe prompts, but strong average detection performance does not explain the geometry of this separation. In particular, it remains unclear how safety evidence is formed across layers, which aspects of that layer-wise geometry support low-false-positive decisions, and which geometric biases remain stable under benchmark shift. We study this as an empirical decomposition problem and introduce Geometry-Lite, a compact prompt-level probe that maps each layer's final prompt-token representation to signed margins under centroid, local-neighborhood, and supervised linear-boundary readouts, then summarizes the resulting margin profiles by boundary position, layer-to-layer change, and coarse shape. Across nine instruction-tuned backbones ($1.2$B--$70$B) and seven safety benchmarks, Geometry-Lite improves over single-layer probes while remaining close to raw multi-layer score stacking, making it a useful instrument for analyzing the multi-layer safety signal. The decomposition shows that safety evidence is expressed primarily through persistent boundary-position geometry: final or extremal margins and unsafe-side layer occupancy dominate aggregate detection performance. In contrast, finite-difference drift and structural summaries add little to pooled AUROC, although drift can provide small recall-oriented corrections under shifted low-FPR thresholds. Under benchmark shift, optimized linear boundaries are sharp on the training mixture, whereas class-conditional mean geometry retains separation more reliably on a predefined hard held-out subset. Overall, prompt-level safety evidence is not primarily a layer-to-layer motion signal, but a persistent layer-wise margin geometry whose useful components and readout-level biases become visible in decision-critical regimes.

</details>


### [156] [LEAP: A closed-loop framework for perovskite precursor additive discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20242)
*Xin-De Wang,Zhi-Rui Chen,Ze-Feng Gao,Peng-Jie Guo,Cheng Mu,Zhong-Yi Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究提出了 LEAP 框架，通过结合领域专用大模型提取的文献机制描述符、贝叶斯优化算法和专家反馈，实现了钙钛矿太阳能电池添加剂的高效、智能筛选，显著提升了器件能量转换效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钙钛矿太阳能电池添加剂的候选化学空间巨大，传统的“试错法”筛选效率低下，亟需一种更高效、机制导向的发现策略来提升器件性能。

Method: 开发了名为 LEAP (LLM-driven Exploration via Active Learning for Perovskites) 的闭环框架。该方法结合了：1. 领域专用 LLM（从文献中提取机制知识并构建可解释的分子描述符）；2. 贝叶斯优化（利用上述描述符进行不确定性引导的迭代筛选）；3. 专家在环（Expert-in-the-loop，进行可行性审查）。

Result: 在基准测试中，领域专用模型表现优于通用模型；在实验验证中，经过三轮筛选，添加剂处理后的器件效率（PCE）显著超过对照组（19.25%），其中 6-CDQ 和 2-CNA 分别达到 20.13% 和 20.87%，最高效率达 21.32%。

Conclusion: LEAP 框架证明了将大模型驱动的机制知识提取与贝叶斯优化及专家反馈相结合，能够有效克服搜索空间大、样本数据少的问题，从而实现钙钛矿太阳能电池添加剂的高效筛选与性能优化。

Abstract: Efficient discovery of precursor additives is essential for improving the performance of perovskite solar cells, yet the large chemical space makes conventional trial-and-error screening inefficient. We develop LEAP(LLM-driven Exploration via Active Learning for Perovskites), an expert-in-the-loop closed framework that couples a domain-specialized large language model(LLM) with active learning for iterative additive prioritization. The LLM is trained to extract mechanism-relevant knowledge from the perovskite additive literature and to represent candidate molecules through interpretable descriptors, which are further integrated into a Bayesian optimization workflow for uncertainty-aware prioritization under low-data conditions. Benchmark results on unseen literature show that the domain-specialized model outperforms general-purpose models in mechanism-consistent reasoning. Experimental validation in an expert-in-the-loop proof-of-concept study suggests improved additive prioritization across three screening rounds, leading to average device PCEs of 20.13% and 20.87% for the later-round 6-CDQ- and 2-CNA-treated devices, respectively, compared with 19.25% for the control, with a champion PCE of 21.32%. These results provide preliminary evidence that literature-grounded mechanistic descriptors, when coupled with Bayesian optimization and expert feasibility review, can support mechanism-aware additive prioritization in perovskite photovoltaics.

</details>


### [157] [GROW: Aligning GRPO with State-Action Modeling for Open-World VLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20246)
*Xiongbin Wu,Zhihao Luo,Shanzhe Lei,Lechao Zhang,Xuhong Wang,Jie Yang,Zhonglong Zheng,Yuanjie Zheng,Xin Tan,Wei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GROW 是一种专为开放世界 VLM 智能体设计的强化学习框架。它通过将长轨迹分解为状态-动作样本来优化 GRPO 算法，解决了多轮交互中上下文冗长和噪声大的问题，并在 Minecraft 任务中取得了顶级表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 VLM 智能体多依赖于监督微调（SFT），且传统的 GRPO 算法因要求使用完整轨迹作为训练样本，在处理多轮长上下文任务时会导致上下文过长且包含噪声。

Method: 提出了 GROW 框架，该方法将全长轨迹分解为多个状态-动作样本，并基于这些样本计算优势（Advantage），通过替代分析证明了该简化形式仍能保留 GRPO 的核心相对策略优化信号。

Result: 在 Minecraft 的 800 多个任务上进行了实验，结果表明 GROW 方法实现了最先进（SOTA）的性能。

Conclusion: GROW 框架通过将长轨迹分解为状态-动作样本，有效解决了传统 GRPO 在多轮视觉语言模型任务中的长上下文和噪声问题，为开放世界 VLM 智能体提供了一种高效的强化学习路径。

Abstract: Recently, vision-language model (VLM) agents have shown promising progress in open-world tasks, where successful task completion often requires multiple turns of visual perception and action execution. However, existing methods still rely primarily on Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) with expert demonstrations, while the advanced reinforcement learning (RL) algorithm, specifically Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), has not been effectively employed for multi-turn RL in these tasks because standard GRPO requires full trajectories as training samples which leads to excessively long context and noise. To address this issue, we propose GROW, a RL framework for open-world VLM agents that decomposes collected trajectories into state-action samples, and computes advantages between these samples rather than treating a full trajectory as a single entity. We further provide a surrogate analysis indicating that, even though the grouped samples are conditioned on different local states rather than an identical prompt context, the objective can preserve the core relative policy optimization signal of GRPO under simplifying assumptions. Experiments on more than 800 Minecraft tasks show that our method achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance, demonstrating the effectiveness of our proposed RL framework for open-world VLM agents.

</details>


### [158] [CP-MoE: Consistency-Preserving Mixture-of-Experts for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20247)
*Yang Liu,Toan Nguyen,Flora D. Salim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对 MoE 架构在持续学习中面临的“知识迁移不足”与“灾难性遗忘”矛盾，本文提出了 CP-MoE 框架，通过引入瞬时专家机制，实现了参数保护与知识高效利用的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有基于 LoRA 的 MoE 持续学习方法中存在的困境：过度的专家隔离限制了知识迁移，而放宽约束则导致严重的灾难性遗忘。

Method: 提出了一种名为 CP-MoE 的持续学习框架，核心组件包括：1) 瞬时专家（Transient Expert）：用于捕捉早期的任务特定更新；2) 一致性保持路由偏差（Consistency-preserving routing bias）：利用瞬时专家估计表征相似度，引导路由选择；3) 瞬时专家引导的正则化机制（Transient expert-guided regularisation）：在合并过程中有选择性地保护历史重要参数。

Result: 在 SuperNI（语言任务）和 VQA v2（多模态任务）基准测试中均达到 SOTA 水准，有效降低了任务间的参数干扰与遗忘，并增强了未见任务的零样本迁移能力。

Conclusion: CP-MoE 有效解决了 MoE 架构在持续学习中知识迁移与灾难性遗忘之间的权衡问题，为大规模模型的高效持续学习提供了一种新的范式。

Abstract: Catastrophic forgetting remains a major obstacle to continual learning in large language models (LLMs) and vision--language models (VLMs). Although Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures offer an efficient path to scaling, existing LoRA-based MoE continual learning methods still face a fundamental trade-off: they either isolate experts too aggressively, limiting knowledge transfer across tasks, or allow task-specific updates to overwrite important existing parameters, leading to severe forgetting. To address this, we propose CP-MoE, a continual learning framework built around a transient expert that captures early task-specific updates and guides their integration into stable experts. CP-MoE introduces a consistency-preserving routing bias, which uses the transient expert to estimate representation similarity with stable experts and steer routing towards more compatible expert selection, and a transient expert-guided regularisation mechanism, which selectively protects important historical parameters during merging. Together, these components reduce parameter interference and forgetting while preserving cross-task knowledge transfer. We validate CP-MoE on both unimodal and multimodal continual learning benchmarks with LLM-based and VLM-based MoE models. On SuperNI benchmark, spanning diverse sequential language tasks, CP-MoE achieves state-of-the-art performance and stronger zero-shot transfer to unseen tasks. On VQA v2 dataset, it scales effectively to multimodal visual reasoning, consistently reduces forgetting, and outperforms strong MoE baselines.

</details>


### [159] [Graph Transductive Sharpening: Leveraging Unlabeled Predictions in Node Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20248)
*Brown Zaz,Mar Gonzàlez I Català,Ferran Hernandez Caralt,Moshe Eliasof,Pietro Liò*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 Transductive Sharpening (TS) 的训练损失改进方法，通过最小化模型对无标签节点的预测熵，利用未利用的转导信息提升了半监督节点分类的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的半监督节点分类研究过度关注模型架构创新，而忽视了训练目标。在转导设置下，模型在训练时对所有节点（包括无标签节点）都有输出，但标准的交叉熵损失丢弃了这些无标签节点的潜在信息。

Method: 提出了 Transductive Sharpening (TS) 训练目标：在保持对有标签节点监督的同时，通过降低（Sharpening）无标签节点的预测熵来提取自监督信号。

Result: 在多个主流节点分类基准测试中，无需更改 Backbone 架构，仅通过修改损失函数即可实现性能的稳定提升。

Conclusion: Transductive Sharpening 是一种有效的、与模型结构无关的方法，能够通过在训练阶段利用无标签节点的预测熵来提升半监督节点分类任务的性能。

Abstract: In the transductive setting, where the full graph is observed but node labels are only partially available, progress in semi-supervised node classification has largely focused on architectural innovation. In this paper, we revisit an orthogonal axis: the training objective. We start from a simple observation: transductive models produce predictions for every node during training, including nodes without labels. These unlabeled-node predictions may contain useful training signal, but standard supervised objectives discard them because no ground-truth labels are available. Inspired by the decomposition of cross-entropy into a label-dependent alignment term and a label-independent entropy term, we propose prediction confidence as a natural way to extract this signal in the absence of labels. This motivates Transductive Sharpening (TS): a loss-level modification that minimizes prediction entropy on unlabeled nodes while counterbalancing this effect on labeled nodes. We evaluate Transductive Sharpening across a wide range of node-classification benchmarks and observe consistent performance improvements without requiring any changes to the backbone architecture. Code is available at https://github.com/transductive-sharpening/tunedGNN.

</details>


### [160] [Automated Kernel Discovery Towards Understanding High-dimensional Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20249)
*Taeyoung Yun,Woocheol Shin,Inhyuck Song,Jaewoo Lee,Jinkyoo Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Kernel Discovery的LLM驱动进化框架，通过两阶段生成和创新的LOO-CRPS评价准则，实现了高维贝叶斯优化中Kernel的自动化发现，突破了传统方法在搜索空间和数据上下文限制上的瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的贝叶斯优化（BO）的Kernel设计依赖大量人工工程；既有的自动化方法局限于基础内核的加乘组合，且基于LLM的方法受限于上下文长度及难以从原始观测数据中提取模式。

Method: 提出一种基于LLM的进化式Kernel搜索框架（Kernel Discovery）：1. 两阶段生成法（先生成数学表达式，再转换为可执行代码）；2. 采用留一交叉验证连续排名概率评分（LOO-CRPS）作为评价准则，有效防止过拟合；3. 不直接依赖原始观测值，避开了上下文长度限制。

Result: 在五个高维BO基准测试中，Kernel Discovery取得了平均1.2/17的排名，显著优于现有对比方法。

Conclusion: 该研究通过提出的Kernel Discovery框架，成功解决了高维贝叶斯优化中人工设计Kernel的局限性。通过两阶段生成和LOO-CRPS评估准则，模型在高维基准测试中表现优异，证明了LLM在探索更广阔Kernel空间方面的潜力。

Abstract: Gaussian Process (GP) kernels are central to Bayesian optimization (BO), yet designing effective kernels for high-dimensional problems still relies on extensive manual engineering. Existing automated approaches struggle in high dimensions for two bottlenecks: their kernel search space is limited to additions and multiplications of base kernels, and LLM-based approaches require conditioning on raw observations, which becomes infeasible due to context-length limits and the difficulty of extracting meaningful patterns. We introduce \textbf{Kernel Discovery}, a LLM-driven evolutionary framework for high-dimensional BO that searches a broader kernel space beyond predefined composition rules and does not require conditioning on observations. Motivated by the observation that directly prompting an LLM to generate kernel code yields syntactically varied but functionally identical kernels, we adopt a two-stage approach: an LLM first proposes novel mathematical forms, then a second LLM call converts each form into validated, executable code. We also propose a leave-one-out continuous ranked probability score (LOO-CRPS) as a selection criterion that penalizes overfitted kernels. On five high-dimensional BO benchmarks, our method achieves an average rank of \textbf{1.2 out of 17}, outperforming competitive baselines.
  We further analyze the discovered kernels to identify which kernels lead to improvements in high-dimensional BO.

</details>


### [161] [LOSCAR-SGD: Local SGD with Communication-Computation Overlap and Delay-Corrected Sparse Model Averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20866)
*Yassine Maziane,Ammar Mahran,Artavazd Maranjyan,Peter Richtárik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了LOSCAR-SGD，这是首个整合通信压缩、本地训练和通信-计算重叠的分布式SGD理论框架，通过创新的延迟校正合并策略，显著提升了在异构计算环境下的分布式训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式学习中通信瓶颈严重，虽然现有的压缩、本地训练和通信-计算重叠技术在实践中广泛应用且效果显著，但目前缺乏将这三者结合的统一理论框架。尤其在异构计算环境下，需要更稳健的方法来优化性能。

Method: 提出LOSCAR-SGD算法：结合了三种关键技术：1. 通信压缩（稀疏更新）；2. 本地训练（允许不同工作节点执行不同步数的局部更新）；3. 通信-计算重叠（在通信期间并行进行优化）。核心创新点在于引入了一种“延迟校正合并规则（delay-corrected merge rule）”，能够有效整合延迟的同步信息而不会丢失重叠阶段的优化进展。

Result: 为平滑非凸目标函数提供了收敛性保证，理论分析了稀疏性、重叠和工作节点异构性对模型收敛速率的影响；实验表明，该方法能够降低训练时间，且延迟校正机制显著优于朴素的覆盖策略。

Conclusion: LOSCAR-SGD首次为包含压缩、本地训练和通信-计算重叠的分布式学习方法提供了理论保证，证明了该方法在处理异构系统中的有效性，且延迟校正合并规则优于简单覆盖方法。

Abstract: Communication is a major bottleneck in distributed learning, especially in large-scale settings and in federated learning environments with slow links. Three standard ways to reduce this cost are communication compression, local training, and communication-computation overlap. Methods that combine these ingredients are used in practice and have been found to be effective for large-scale training, but there is little theory for methods that combine all three. We study a heterogeneous-compute setting in which different workers may take different numbers of local steps, and we propose LOSCAR-SGD, a Local SGD method that communicates only a sparse subset of model coordinates and continues optimizing while communication is in flight. A key ingredient is a delay-corrected merge rule that incorporates delayed synchronized information without discarding the progress made during the overlap phase. We give convergence guarantees for smooth non-convex objectives and show how sparsity, overlap, and worker heterogeneity affect the rate. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first theory for this combination of ingredients. Experiments further show that communication-computation overlap reduces training time and that the delay-corrected merge outperforms naive overwriting.

</details>


### [162] [Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Safe Autonomous Driving Under Pedestrian Behavioral Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20255)
*Prakash Aryan,Kaushik Raghupathruni,Timo Kehrer,Sebastiano Panichella*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用多智能体强化学习（MARL）协同训练自动驾驶汽车与行人，通过引入隐式的行人个性特征，有效模拟了现实中行人的违规横穿行为，显著提升了自动驾驶系统在复杂场景下的安全性与交互 realism。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶仿真测试过于依赖脚本化或简化的行人模型，无法模拟真实世界中行人的异构性与不确定性，导致无法充分评估自动驾驶在面对隐式违规横穿行为时的安全性。

Method: 构建了一个包含自动驾驶汽车（SDC）和12个行人的MARL环境，使用多智能体近端策略优化（MAPPO）算法进行协同训练。行人 locomotion 采用 Dijkstra 寻路，高层决策（走/等）由 RL 策略控制，并引入受隐式个性特征驱动的违规横穿行为。

Result: 协同训练的自动驾驶汽车目标达成率达到78%，大幅优于基准线的35%；事故率从33%降低至14%；与单智能体强化学习相比，碰撞事故减少了30%。此外，实验量化了违规横穿行为对驾驶决策的影响，证实了该方法能有效捕捉行人行为的不可预测性。

Conclusion: 通过多智能体强化学习（MARL）进行自动驾驶汽车与行人的协同训练，比单纯针对固定规则行人进行训练能产生更真实、更具挑战性的交互场景，从而显著提升自动驾驶汽车处理不可预测行人（如违规横穿马路者）的安全性并降低事故率。

Abstract: Simulation-based testing of self-driving cars (SDCs) typically relies on scripted or simplified pedestrian models that do not capture the heterogeneity and uncertainty of real human crossing behavior. This limits the realism of safety assessments, especially in scenarios involving jaywalking, which is governed by latent personality traits that the vehicle cannot observe. We hypothesize that jointly training pedestrians and the SDC with multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) produces more realistic interaction scenarios than training the SDC against fixed pedestrian policies, and that the resulting behavior gap between predictable and unpredictable crossings can be measured directly from trajectories. This paper describes a MARL environment in which an SDC and 12 pedestrians are co-trained using Multi-Agent Proximal Policy Optimization (MAPPO). Pedestrian locomotion follows scripted Dijkstra pathfinding, while an RL policy controls high-level go/wait decisions. Jaywalking probability depends on a per-pedestrian personality trait sampled at episode start and hidden from the SDC. In 500-episode evaluations, the co-trained SDC reached 78% of goals with a 14% collision rate, compared to 35% goals and 33% collisions for the best rule-based baseline. A speed differential metric shows that the SDC traveled 2.65 m/s faster near jaywalkers than near crosswalk users at close range (0-3 m), indicating that jaywalking encounters were not anticipated. Jaywalking accounted for 13% of crossing events but was associated with 62% of collisions. Co-training with MARL pedestrians reduced collisions by 30% relative to single-agent RL, as pedestrians learned to wait when the SDC approached at speed.

</details>


### [163] [A Differentiable Measure of Algebraic Complexity: Provably Exact Discovery of Group Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.23152)
*Dongsung Huh,Lior Horesh,Halyun Jeong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 HyperCube 的可微张量分解方法，通过将代数规则发现建模为 Cayley 表补全问题，成功证明了可以通过梯度下降高效识别离散代数结构（群），无需进行昂贵的组合搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决机器学习中从数据发现离散代数规则这一难题，克服传统方法对组合搜索的依赖，寻求一种可微的度量标准来衡量代数结构（特别是结合律）的复杂性。

Method: 提出了基于算子值张量分解的 HyperCube 框架，将其建模为 Cayley 表补全问题。通过分析 HyperCube 的目标函数（几何对齐与逆 $\ell_2$ 惩罚），证明了其景观特征，并结合 Lean 4 形式化验证与小规模实验验证了理论结果。

Result: 证明了 HyperCube 目标函数存在绝对下界 $H(Θ) \ge 3 \, |δ|$；证明了该下界仅在目标表同构于群时达到；证明了全局极小解等同于底层群的正则表示；所有理论结果均通过 Lean 4 机械验证。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了离散代数结构（即群结构）可以通过可微度量准确表征，从而实现了无需组合搜索的梯度下降发现算法。该工作解决了 Huh (2025) 的中心猜想，并验证了代数规则发现的可行性。

Abstract: Discovering discrete algebraic rules from data is a fundamental challenge in machine learning. We formalize this problem through Cayley-table completion -- an algebraic counterpart to classical matrix completion -- where the degree of associativity violation replaces linear rank as the intrinsic measure of complexity. We provide a rigorous landscape analysis of HyperCube, an operator-valued tensor factorization, on the fully observed target table $δ$, proving that its global infimum $H_{\inf}(δ) := \inf_{Θ\in F_δ} H(Θ)$ implicitly defines an exact differentiable measure for this complexity. We show that HyperCube's native objective $H(Θ)$ decomposes into two components: geometric alignment (collinearity) and an inverse $\ell_2$ penalty. We establish that these continuous variational pressures induce core discrete properties: collinearity enforces associativity (Collinearity--Associativity Equivalence), and the inverse $\ell_2$ penalty reduces to an exact inverse rank penalty within the collinear manifold, driving the parameters toward full-rank unitarity. Consequently, we derive an absolute lower bound $H(Θ) \ge H_{\inf}(δ) \ge 3 \, |δ|$, where $|δ|$ is the target table size. We prove this absolute floor is attained if and only if the target is isotopic to a group, and characterize the global minimizer as the regular representation of the underlying group (up to unitary gauge), resolving the central open conjecture of Huh (2025). This work serves as an existence proof that certain discrete algebraic structures can be exactly characterized by differentiable measures, enabling gradient-based discovery without the need for combinatorial search. All theoretical results are mechanically verified in Lean 4 and confirmed via small-scale experiments.

</details>


### [164] [FBOS-RL: Feedback-Driven Bi-Objective Synergistic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20256)
*Xikai Zhang,Yongzhi Li,Likang Xiao,Yingze Zhang,Yanhua Cheng,Quan Chen,Peng Jiang,Wenjun Wu,Liu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对GRPO在处理困难任务时因缺乏高质量采样导致训练停滞的问题，FBOS-RL通过引入反馈驱动的探索增强及EPA与ECC双目标协同机制，显著提升了模型对齐训练的效率与最终性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO在处理超出模型当前能力的复杂任务时，因采样机制单一导致无法获取高质量的rollouts，致使梯度更新缺乏方向，训练容易陷入瓶颈或停滞。

Method: 提出FBOS-RL框架，引入基于反馈的探索增强机制，并设计了两个互补的优化目标：面向利用的策略对齐（EPA）和面向探索的思维能力培养（ECC）。

Result: 在相同采样数量下，FBOS-RL相比GRPO和现有基准展现出更快的收敛速度、更高的性能上限、更高的策略熵以及更稳定的梯度更新。

Conclusion: FBOS-RL通过EPA和ECC双目标的协同作用，解决了传统GRPO在任务超出模型能力时因缺乏高质量采样而导致训练停滞的问题，显著提升了强化学习的效率与性能上限。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has become a cornerstone for aligning and unlocking the reasoning capabilities of large-scale models. At its core, the training loop of GRPO and its variants alternates between rollout sampling and policy update. Unlike supervised learning, where each gradient step is anchored to an explicit ground-truth target, the optimal gradient direction for updating model parameters in this setting is not known a priori; the high-quality rollouts drawn during the sampling stage therefore act as the implicit "teacher" that guides every parameter update. However, GRPO adopt a simple sampling scheme that conditions all rollouts on the same original prompt. When a task lies beyond the policy model's current capability, this sampling scheme rarely yields a high-quality rollout, leaving the policy model without a meaningful gradient direction when updating its parameters, which causes training to stall. To address this issue, we propose FBOS-RL, a Feedback-Driven Bi-Objective Synergistic reinforcement learning framework. Specifically, we let the model perform Feedback-Guided Exploration Enhancement based on the feedback provided by the environment, and on top of this we design two mutually reinforcing training objectives: Exploitation-oriented Policy Alignment(EPA) and Exploration-oriented Capability Cultivation(ECC). Extensive experiments demonstrate that EPA and ECC can mutually reinforce each other, forming a positive flywheel effect that significantly improves both the training efficiency and the final performance ceiling of reinforcement learning. Specifically, under an identical number of rollouts, FBOS-RL learns substantially faster than GRPO and feedback-based baselines and ultimately attains a higher performance ceiling, while exhibiting higher policy entropy and lower gradient norms throughout training.

</details>


### [165] [Catching a Moving Subspace: Low-Rank Bandits Beyond Stationarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20269)
*Hamed Khosravi,Xiaoming Huo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了分段平稳的低秩线性上下文强化学习问题，首次界定了移动子空间的恢复条件，并提出了SPSC算法，成功在大规模漂移环境下将动态遗憾从依赖环境维度 $d$ 压缩至依赖本征秩 $r$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的上下文强化学习算法无法同时处理“奖励位于低维潜空间”和“子空间漂移”这两个现实挑战。现有的低秩算法在子空间变化时失效，而非平稳算法在面对高维问题时无法利用低本征秩特性，导致遗憾边界受限于环境维度 $d$。

Method: 1. 提出了SPSC（Subspace-Proj-Subspace-UCB）算法，该算法将各向同性探测与基于窗口的投影岭UCB（Ridge-UCB）相结合，并在 subspace 学习中使用CUSUM测试在线检测分段边界。
2. 证明了在已知噪声方差、有界状态-噪声耦合及全维探测支持的条件下，通过奖励的二次函数可实现移动子空间的恢复。

Result: 1. 理论上，实现了动态遗憾 $\widetilde{O}(r\sqrt{T} + T^{2/3})$，成功将维度依赖从 $d$ 降低为 $r$。
2. 实验上，在11个基准测试集（包括合成、UCI、MovieLens及生产日志数据）中，证明当 $d-r \gtrsim T^{1/6}$ 时，SPSC算法显著优于现有的非平稳及低秩基准方法。

Conclusion: 本文首次界定了分段平稳低秩线性上下文强化学习中子空间的“可识别性边界”，并提出了SPSC算法，实现了动态遗憾边界从环境维度 $d$ 向本征秩 $r$ 的压缩，理论与实验均证明了该方法在处理子空间漂移问题上的优越性。

Abstract: Many bandit deployments (recommendation, clinical dosing, ad targeting) share two facts prior work handles only in isolation: rewards live on a low-dimensional latent subspace, and that subspace drifts. Stationary low-rank bandits exploit rank but break under subspace change; non-stationary linear bandits adapt to drift but pay ambient rate $\widetilde{O}(d\sqrt{T})$. We study piecewise-stationary low-rank linear contextual bandits with scalar feedback: $θ_t = B_k^\star w_t$ with rank-$r$ factor $B_k^\star\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times r}$ constant within each of $K$ unknown segments and able to shift at boundaries. Our results are tight along three axes. (i) Identification boundary. With single-play scalar rewards, the moving subspace is recoverable through quadratic functionals of rewards iff three probe-side conditions hold: known noise variance, bounded state-noise coupling, and full-dimensional probe support. Each is necessary in the unrestricted-second-moment problem, and jointly they are sufficient, characterizing the boundary of the solvable region. (ii) Algorithm and dynamic regret. SPSC interleaves isotropic probes with windowed projected ridge-UCB exploitation inside the learned $r$-dimensional subspace; a CUSUM-style variant discovers segment boundaries online. The costed dynamic regret is $\widetilde{O}(r\sqrt{T})+\widetilde{O}(T^{2/3})+O(W\,V_{\mathrm{in}})$, replacing the ambient $d\sqrt{T}$ rate with the intrinsic rank. (iii) Empirics. On eleven benchmarks spanning synthetic, UCI/MovieLens, semi-synthetic clinical, and ZOZOTOWN production-log data, SPSC outperforms non-stationary and low-rank baselines whenever $d-r\gtrsim T^{1/6}$, matching the analytical crossover. To our knowledge, this is the first work to characterize the identification boundary and attain the intrinsic-rank dynamic-regret rate in this setting.

</details>


### [166] [Instance Discrimination for Link Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20257)
*Valentin Cuzin-Rambaud,Mathieu Lefort,Rémy Cazabet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将实例判别自监督学习从节点任务扩展至链路预测，通过提出基于链接表示的模型（L-GRACE/L-BGRL）及针对性的社区结构增强策略，显著提升了链路预测的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于实例判别的自监督学习已在图节点分类任务中取得成功，但针对链路预测任务的研究较少且效果待提升。

Method: 1. 评估现有图自监督模型在链路预测任务中的表现并分析数据增强的影响；2. 提出一种基于社区结构的图结构增强方法；3. 提出两个基于链接表示（而非节点表示）的新模型：L-GRACE和L-BGRL。

Result: L-GRACE和L-BGRL模型在链路预测任务中表现出比传统基于节点表示方法更好的性能，尤其在无属性图上展现了与现有先进方法相当或更优的竞争力。

Conclusion: 该研究通过引入基于链接表示的L-GRACE和L-BGRL模型，以及针对链路预测优化的社区结构数据增强方法，有效提升了自监督学习在链路预测任务上的性能，特别是在无属性图上表现优异，达到或超越了现有先进水平。

Abstract: Recently, instance discrimination models have emerged as a major solution for self-supervised learning. Having already demonstrated its effectiveness in the image domain, instance discrimination learning is now proving equally convincing in the graph domain, in particular for node classification. However, fewer contributions have tackled the link prediction task. In this contribution, we propose to adapt existing methods to this context. We first provide a rigorous evaluation of existing self-supervised models in the field of link prediction, showing that the main performance depends on the augmentation process (like in computer vision). We then propose a new structural augmentation based on the community structure that is relevant for link prediction. Our main contribution introduces two new models, L-GRACE and L-BGRL, based on link representations instead of node representations, which improve the performance of the existing methods, especially on unattributed graphs, and we show that they perform on par with the state of the art, both in supervised and self-supervised contexts.

</details>


### [167] [Conformal Selective Acting: Anytime-Valid Risk Control for RLVR-Trained LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20270)
*Hamed Khosravi,Xiaoming Huo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CSA是一种创新的部署封装器，通过保形选择执行机制，解决了在受监管环境下实时、自适应更新流中LLM部署的安全有效性认证问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在受监管机构中部署本地专家LLM时，必须为每个请求提供实时安全证书（无平均化、无跨部署合并），但现有的离线及在线保形预测（conformal prediction）方法无法在自适应更新流中同时满足此严格的时间和选择性风险要求。

Method: 提出Conformal Selective Acting (CSA)框架，利用基于Ville型e-process的Bonferroni网格校准，针对每个阈值进行实时风险控制，实现了 anytime-pathwise 的选择性风险保障。

Result: 在八个基准测试、十六个对抗性分布偏移场景以及真实Expert-Iteration RLVR测试中，CSA是唯一能保持路径维有效性且不拒绝部署的方法，证明了其在实际应用中的高稳健性和极低的释放率差距。

Conclusion: CSA提供了一种针对自适应、在线更新的本地LLM部署的实时安全认证方案，能够在满足严格路径维（pathwise）风险约束的同时，最小化对模型可用性的影响，是受监管环境下部署本地LLM的可行且稳健的封装器。

Abstract: A local specialist LLM, fine-tuned with reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) on operator-local data, is installed in a regulated organization with per-deployment error budget $α$. The operator needs a safety certificate for this deployment's stream at every round: no pooling across deployments, no waiting for a long-run average. Existing wrappers cannot deliver this on adaptive, online-updated streams: offline conformal-risk methods require exchangeability; online-conformal methods bound only long-run averages; non-exchangeable extensions are marginally valid; and the closest anytime wrapper, A-RCPS, controls marginal rather than selective risk. Using a (test statistic, validity guarantee, deployment rule) framework, we identify one empty cell forced by deployment requirements: e-process per threshold, selective risk, anytime-pathwise validity, max-certified-threshold rule. Conformal Selective Acting (CSA) fills it as a per-round wrapper maintaining a Ville-type e-process per threshold on a Bonferroni grid, evaluated against the RLVR filtration. Under predictable updates and isotonic-calibrated monotone risk we prove (i) an anytime-pathwise selective-risk bound $R_T^{\mathrm{act}}\leα+O(N_T^{-1/2})$, (ii) rate-optimal certification matching $Θ(\barη^{-2}\log(1/δ))$, and (iii) a horizon-independent release-rate gap. Across eight specialist benchmarks ($480$ streams), sixteen adversarial distribution-shift cells ($160$ streams), and five live Expert-Iteration RLVR cells with online LoRA over four base models in three architecture families ($10{,}300$ rounds), CSA is the only method among ten compared that satisfies pathwise validity and non-refusing deployment on every cell. We do not propose a new LLM, training algorithm, or policy class; CSA is the deployment-side complement, orthogonal to the model, for operators who cannot use a frontier API.

</details>


### [168] [It Takes Two: Complementary Self-Distillation for Contextual Integrity in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20258)
*Sangwoo Park,Woongyeong Yeo,Seanie Lee,Yumin Choi,Hyomin Lee,Kangsan Kim,Jinheon Baek,Seong Joon Oh,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对大模型隐私与效能的权衡难题，本文提出了 SELFCI 框架，通过自蒸馏和产品专家（PoE）机制，在不牺牲任务性能的前提下，有效实现了对情境完整性（CI）的隐私对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在处理敏感工作流时难以遵循“情境完整性”（CI），且传统的隐私缓解策略往往以牺牲任务完成度为代价，存在严重的隐私-效能权衡问题。

Method: 提出了一种基于自蒸馏的框架 SELFCI，利用产品专家（PoE）目标，通过联合优化两个独立的逆 KL 散度（分别针对任务效能和隐私披露）来解耦信息抑制与任务执行。该方法不依赖外部监督，而是通过反馈生成的教师分布进行训练。

Result: 实验证明 SELFCI 在多个基准测试中表现优于 GRPO 等线上强化学习基线，并在涉及智能体工作流和累积隐私上下文的域外场景中表现出良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: SELFCI 能够无需昂贵的外部监督，有效平衡大语言模型（LLM）的隐私保护（遵循情境完整性）与任务效能，为复杂智能体工作流的隐私对齐提供了一种实用方案。

Abstract: Contextual Integrity (CI) defines privacy not merely as keeping information hidden, but as governing information flows according to the norms of a given context. As large language models are increasingly deployed as personal agents handling sensitive workflows, adhering to CI becomes critical. However, even frontier models remain unreliable in making disclosure decisions, and existing mitigation strategies often degrade underlying task performance. To overcome this privacy-utility trade-off, we propose SELFCI, a complementary self-distillation framework that decouples information suppression from task resolution. SELFCI jointly optimizes two independent reverse KL divergences over distinct teacher distributions derived from feedback: one encourages preserving task-relevant information for utility, while the other enforces minimal and appropriate disclosure. This complementary formulation induces a Product-of-Experts (PoE) target, aligning the policy with the intersection of capability and privacy requirements. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that SELFCI, without relying on costly external supervision, consistently outperforms competitive baselines such as online reinforcement learning algorithms (e.g., GRPO). These trends further extend to out-of-domain settings involving agentic workflows and accumulated private context, suggesting that SELFCI provides a practical path toward CI alignment.

</details>


### [169] [Symmetrization of Loss Functions for Robust Training of Neural Networks in the Presence of Noisy Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20347)
*Alexandre Lemire Paquin,Brahim Chaib-Draa,Philippe Giguère*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了噪声标签下的鲁棒损失函数设计，通过对称条件推导出多分类无铰链损失，并提出 SGCE 和 alpha-MAE 两种新型平滑插值损失函数，有效提升了模型在含噪数据下的抗噪能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决标记训练集昂贵且容易出错的问题，旨在设计针对噪声标签具有理论稳健性的损失函数。作者关注如何通过对称条件来优化多分类任务中的损失函数设计。

Method: 1. 利用对称条件对多分类损失函数进行唯一分解，推导出满足对称性的多分类无铰链损失函数；2. 通过理论证明，确定该损失函数在局部线性逼近中的基础性地位；3. 引入两种新型损失函数（SGCE 和 alpha-MAE），通过插值方法结合了无铰链损失与平均绝对误差（MAE），并引入参数控制其平滑度。

Result: 1. 理论上确定了多分类无铰链损失在该类问题中的唯一凸性与基础性；2. 提出的 SGCE 和 alpha-MAE 损失函数在标准噪声标签基准测试中展现出优于或等同于现有鲁棒损失函数的性能。

Conclusion: 本文证明了多分类无铰链损失（unhinged loss）是唯一满足对称条件的凸多分类损失，并提出了一种通过插值构建鲁棒损失函数的新方法。实验结果表明，该方法在处理噪声标签任务时具有较强的竞争力和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Labeling a training set is often expensive and susceptible to errors, making the design of robust loss functions for label noise an important problem. The symmetry condition provides theoretical guarantees for robustness to such noise. In this work, we study a symmetrization method arising from the unique decomposition of any multi-class loss function into a symmetric component and a class-insensitive term. In particular, symmetrizing the cross-entropy loss leads to a linear multi-class extension of the unhinged loss. Unlike in the binary case, the multi-class version must have specific coefficients in order to satisfy the symmetry condition. Under suitable assumptions, we show that this multi-class unhinged loss is the unique convex multi-class symmetric loss. We also show that it has a fundamental local role: the linear approximation of any symmetric loss around score vectors with equal components is equivalent to the multi-class unhinged loss. We then introduce SGCE and alpha-MAE, two loss functions that interpolate between the multi-class unhinged loss and the Mean Absolute Error while allowing control of the beta-smoothness of the loss. Experiments on standard noisy-label benchmarks show competitive performance compared with existing robust loss functions.

</details>


### [170] [Residual Paving: Diagnosing the Routing Bottleneck in Selective Refusal Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20262)
*Bryce Hinkley,Peyman Najafirad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 Residual Paving 技术，通过将模型的“路由决策（是否介入）”与“残差编辑（如何修正）”解耦，在冻结模型参数的前提下，实现了对特定提示的高效编辑，同时较好地平衡了良性行为保持与有害拒绝功能的完整性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决模型编辑中的三方控制问题：在诱导模型对特定编辑提示不拒绝的同时，保持其对良性提示的正常响应以及对有害提示的拒绝能力，避免原有能力的退化。

Method: 提出“Residual Paving”方法，这是一种针对冻结指令微调模型的路由残差编辑技术。它通过早期层路由机制预测标量门控和专家混合，并在激活时通过特定于提示的瓶颈残差专家在后期层进行更新，从而实现对原始模型的最小化干预。

Result: 在 Gemma-3-4B-IT 上，将编辑提示的拒绝率从 88.6% 降至 4.0%，且良性分布保持率为 95.5%，有害分布保持率为 87.3%。相比基线方法（ActAdd 和 DIM），该方法在编辑成功率上具有显著优势。尽管存在部分“有害请求误判”的性能损失，但在六个骨干模型上的测试均验证了该方法路由选择性的有效性。

Conclusion: Residual Paving 有效地分离了路由选择性与残差编辑能力。实验表明，当前编辑模型的主要性能瓶颈在于“何时介入”的路由决策，而非编辑本身的能力不足。该方法在保持模型良性行为及拒绝有害请求能力的同时，显著降低了对特定目标提示的拒绝率。

Abstract: We study selective refusal editing as a three-way control problem: induce non-refusal on designated edit prompts while preserving benign behavior and harmful refusals outside the edit set. We introduce Residual Paving, a routed residual editing method for frozen instruction-tuned transformers that separates route selectivity, whether to intervene, from residual-edit capacity, what edit to apply. An early-layer router predicts a scalar gate and expert mixture; when active, prompt-conditioned bottleneck residual experts apply later-layer residual updates while leaving the backbone unchanged. This decomposition supports an oracle-routing diagnostic where only the learned scalar gate is replaced with the held-out edit/keep label, leaving the residual editor and frozen backbone fixed. On the primary Gemma-3-4B-IT held-out split, learned Residual Paving reduces edit refusal from 88.6% to 4.0%, with 95.5% benign distribution preservation and 87.3% harmful distribution preservation. Same-protocol one-direction steering controls are much weaker on edit success, leaving edit refusal at 86.8% for Edit-target ActAdd and 78.9% for DIM-style refusal steering. The remaining failure is off-target harmful-keep degradation: harmful refusal remains below the frozen-base rate, 65.3% vs. 81.6%. Across six backbones, oracle routing improves the keep-side diagnostic score on every reported row, with median gain +12.9 pp, supporting the interpretation that learned route selectivity is the main observed bottleneck. Trajectory diagnostics on two backbones further suggest directed movement toward edit-target continuations rather than generic refusal suppression.

</details>


### [171] [Score-Based Causal Discovery of Latent Variable Causal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20396)
*Ignavier Ng,Xinshuai Dong,Haoyue Dai,Biwei Huang,Peter Spirtes,Kun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种新型评分法，通过理论保证可识别性并统一了多种约束型方法，有效解决了含潜变量因果结构学习中的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的针对潜变量的约束型因果发现方法存在测试顺序依赖性、误差传播和显著性水平选择困难等问题，而传统的评分法（如GES）主要限于无潜变量模型。

Method: 提出了一种适用于含潜变量因果模型的新型评分函数，通过表征不同结构假设下观测变量边缘分布的自由度，开发了精确（exact）和连续（continuous）的评分搜索算法。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在包含潜变量的因果结构发现任务中表现有效，并为评估不同结构假设下的因果模型提供了一个统一的视角。

Conclusion: 该研究通过将评分法引入含潜变量的因果模型，解决了传统约束法在测试顺序依赖、误差传播等方面的局限，提供了一种具有理论可识别性保证且统一的因果结构学习框架。

Abstract: Identifying latent variables and the causal structure involving them is essential across various scientific fields. While many existing works fall under the category of constraint-based methods (with e.g. conditional independence or rank deficiency tests), they may face empirical challenges such as testing-order dependency, error propagation, and choosing an appropriate significance level. These issues can potentially be mitigated by properly designed score-based methods, such as Greedy Equivalence Search (GES) (Chickering, 2002) in the specific setting without latent variables. Yet, formulating score-based methods with latent variables is highly challenging. In this work, we develop score-based methods that are capable of identifying causal structures containing causally-related latent variables with identifiability guarantees. Specifically, we show that a properly formulated scoring function can achieve score equivalence and consistency for structure learning of latent variable causal models. We further provide a characterization of the degrees of freedom for the marginal over the observed variables under multiple structural assumptions considered in the literature, and accordingly develop both exact and continuous score-based methods. This offers a unified view of several existing constraint-based methods with different structural assumptions. Experimental results validate the effectiveness of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [172] [Chronicle: A Multimodal Foundation Model for Joint Language and Time Series Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20268)
*Paul Quinlan,Jeremy Levasseur,Qingguo Li,Xiaodan Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Chronicle是首个跨模态从头联合预训练的文本与时间序列基础模型，证明了统一架构在处理两种模态时的优越性，不仅在单一任务上达到SOTA，还验证了联合训练对双领域表现的有效提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列模型忽略了丰富的文本上下文；现有的多模态模型多采用预训练语言模型的后处理（post-hoc）适配，导致对时序数据的表征能力不足，且缺乏与最强单模态基础模型的比较，导致联合训练的必要性存疑。

Method: 提出了Chronicle模型，一个324M参数的解码器专用（decoder-only）Transformer。该模型通过统一的底层架构处理文本和时间序列数据，在预训练阶段大部分时间使用单模态批次（unimodal batches），随后通过短暂的交错阶段（alignment stage）实现跨模态对齐。

Result: 在19项NLU任务中表现与Gemma-3-270M-PT持平；在24个UCR/UEA数据集上刷新了冻结嵌入式（frozen-embedding）时间序列分类的表现；在Time-MMD多模态预测任务中超越了所有监督融合基线。

Conclusion: Chronicle证明了通过从头开始联合预训练（Joint Pretraining），可以在一个统一的Transformer架构中实现文本和时间序列数据的双向提升，无需依赖现有的预训练语言模型后处理适配。

Abstract: Real-world time series come with text: metadata, descriptions, news, reports. Yet time series foundation models process numerical sequences in isolation, and the multimodal text-and-time-series models that attempt to bridge the two all adapt a pretrained language model post hoc, inheriting representations shaped without ever seeing temporal data. These models are also evaluated almost exclusively against other multimodal baselines, not against the strongest unimodal foundation models in either domain, leaving open whether joint training is needed at all. We present Chronicle, a compact 324M-parameter decoder-only transformer trained from scratch on natural language and time series within a single unified architecture. Both modalities share the same transformer blocks, attention mechanism, and residual stream; the bulk of pretraining uses unimodal batches so cross-modal capability emerges purely from shared parameters, with a short alignment stage that interleaves the two. To our knowledge, Chronicle is the first model jointly pretrained on text and time series from scratch, and the first multimodal model evaluated against dedicated foundation models in both domains. It matches Gemma-3-270M-PT on 19 NLU tasks, sets a new bar for frozen-embedding time series classification on 24 UCR/UEA datasets, and produces multimodal forecasts on Time-MMD that beat every supervised fusion baseline, all from a single backbone.

</details>


### [173] [CASCADE Conformal Prediction: Uncertainty-Adaptive Prediction Intervals for Two-Stage Clinical Decision Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20468)
*Ricardo Diaz-Rincon,Muxuan Liang,Adolfo Ramirez-Zamora,Benjamin Shickel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 一种名为CASCADE的共形预测框架，通过利用辅助分类任务的认知不确定性动态优化帕金森病药物剂量预测区间，显著提升了预测的针对性与可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决帕金森病药物管理中病程异质性高、患者反应差异大导致的用药剂量预测偏差问题，以及标准置信度评估方法无法区分预测可靠性的局限性。

Method: 提出CASCADE框架，通过Venn-Abers多概率不确定性映射非一致性评分，将“是否需要调整用药”（分类任务）的认知不确定性传递至“调整剂量多少”（回归任务）的预测间隔中，实现动态缩放。

Result: 在保持覆盖率的前提下，CASCADE显著提升了预测效率：对比基准方法，高置信度群体的预测区间缩窄了38.9%，同时确保了低置信度患者预测结果的稳健性。

Conclusion: CASCADE通过结合分类任务的认知不确定性与动态缩放机制，成功实现了帕金森病药物剂量预测的精确度（高置信度下）与稳健性（低置信度下）的平衡，弥合了临床决策与剂量预测之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Effective medication management in Parkinson's Disease (PD) is challenging due to heterogeneous disease progression, variable patient response, and medication side effects. While AI models can forecast levodopa equivalent daily dose (LEDD) as a measure of medication needs, standard uncertainty quantification often fails to communicate the reliability of these predictions, treating high and low confidence clinical decisions identically. We introduce CASCADE (Calibrated Adaptive Scaling via Conformal And Distributional Estimation), a novel conformal prediction framework that propagates epistemic uncertainty from a screening classifier to adapt downstream predictions. Unlike standard conformal methods that rely on auxiliary residual regression, we leverage epistemic uncertainty from a primary classification task (identifying whether a medication change is needed) to dynamically scale the prediction intervals of a secondary regression task (predicting how much change). By mapping Venn-Abers multi-probabilistic uncertainty directly to non-conformity scores, our framework achieves continuous risk adaptation. We demonstrate that this ``cascade effect'' produces highly efficient intervals for confident patients (38.9% narrower than standard conformal baselines) while automatically expanding intervals to ensure robust coverage for uncertain cases, bridging the gap between discrete clinical decision-making and continuous dose forecasting in PD.

</details>


### [174] [Tippett-minimum Fusion of Representation-space Diffusion Models for Multi-Encoder Out-of-Distribution Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20502)
*Neelkamal Bhuyan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 EncMin2L 的高效 OOD 检测架构，通过动态整合多个不同感知的编码器，实现了在无需 OOD 标签的情况下对四种主要分布偏移类型的统一、高精度检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 OOD 检测方法通常难以应对广泛且多样的分布偏移（如全局域变化、语义差异、纹理差异及协变量损坏），且通常依赖大量 OOD 样本进行训练或调优。

Method: 1. 提出了 EncMin2L：一种编码器无关的双层 min-gate 架构，用于融合感知器的表示空间扩散模型（RDM）；2. 引入了两项仅需 ID 数据即可工作的诊断指标：η²（类条件 F 检验）和 Δμ（合成扰动下的对数似然偏移），以量化不同编码器的偏移敏感度；3. 使用 Tippett 最小 p 值组合法聚合各感知器的评分，以提升 OOD 检测的校准稳定性。

Result: EncMin2L 在所有四种分布偏移类型上同时达到了 ≥ 0.94 的 AUROC，在参数量降低 2.3 倍的同时，性能优于当前最先进的表示空间扩散 OOD 检测器。

Conclusion: EncMin2L 通过双层 min-gate 机制有效整合了多种感知器的扩散模型似然估计，在无需 OOD 标签的情况下，实现了对多种分布偏移类型的统一、高效且鲁棒的检测，显著超越了现有的单体多编码器方案。

Abstract: We address out-of-distribution (OOD) detection across the full spectrum of distribution shifts -- global domain changes, semantic divergence, texture differences, and covariate corruptions -- through a multi-encoder fusion of per-encoder representation-space diffusion models (RDMs). We statistically identify each encoder's sensitivity to specific shift types from ID data alone and introduce EncMin2L -- an encoder-agnostic two-level $\min(\cdot)$-gate that combines and calibrates per-encoder diffusion-based likelihood detectors without OOD labels, outperforming monolithic multi-encoder baselines at $2.3\times$ lower parameter cost. Two ID-data diagnostics: $η^2$ (class-conditional F-test) and $Δμ$ (log-likelihood shift under synthetic corruptions) -- quantify encoder specialization, while a Tippett minimum $p$-value combination aggregates per-encoder scores into a single, calibration-stable OOD signal. EncMin2L achieves $\geq 0.94$ AUROC across all four shift types simultaneously, outperforming the state-of-the-art representation-space diffusion OOD detectors across overlapping benchmarks.

</details>


### [175] [Smaller Abstract State Spaces Enable Cross-Scale Generalization in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20272)
*Nasehatul Mustakim,Lucas Lehnert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个关于RL智能体实现OOD泛化的理论模型，证明了通过状态抽象将任务压缩到有限且较小的抽象空间是实现跨复杂任务泛化的核心机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能够轻松地将抽象概念泛化到复杂或规模更大的任务，但现有的强化学习系统在实现此类分布外（OOD）泛化能力上仍面临挑战。

Method: 将现有的状态抽象框架扩展至部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDPs）；定义了“后继加权模型约简”（successor-weighted model reduction）以实现更高效的压缩；通过推导性能边界（bound），将损失分解为近似误差和估计误差。

Result: 推导出了智能体在OOD场景下的演化性能边界，揭示了减少抽象状态空间大小与提升泛化性能之间的定量关系。

Conclusion: 将强化学习智能体约束在有限的小型抽象状态空间内，是实现跨任务（OOD）泛化的必要条件，并为设计可扩展的RL架构提供了理论指导。

Abstract: While humans readily generalize abstract concepts to more complex or larger tasks, building Reinforcement Learning (RL) systems with this ability remains elusive. Here, we present the first theoretical model of how such Out-of-Distribution (OOD) generalization can be achieved in RL agents. Our approach considers Partially Observable Markov Decision Processes (POMDPs) and assumes that an intelligent agent uses an abstraction function to determine which experiences can be treated as equivalent and which must be distinguished. First, we extend the existing state abstraction framework and proof techniques to POMDPs. Then, we define a successor-weighted model reduction, a model reduction variant that enables compression into smaller abstract spaces than prior definitions allow. We derive a bound on the agent's OOD test performance, thereby defining the conditions under which OOD generalization is achievable. This bound decomposes an agent's performance loss into approximation and estimation errors, revealing how reducing an agent's abstract state space size improves test performance and OOD generalization. Our analysis suggests that constraining an agent to operate over a small, finite set of abstract states is necessary for achieving generalization to more complex tasks. Our results motivate further research into learning RL architectures that scale across tasks of varying complexity levels.

</details>


### [176] [Latent Process Generator Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20547)
*Lukas Billera,Hedwig Nora Nordlinder,Ben Murrell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“潜在过程生成匹配”框架，将生成模型中的辅助动力学过程形式化为潜在马尔可夫过程的投影，从而在理论上统一并扩展了流匹配等生成模型中对辅助状态的使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的流匹配和扩散模型常依赖辅助随机动力学来定义训练目标，但这些辅助状态在推理阶段通常无法直接采样或并非最终所需。现有的生成匹配理论仅涵盖静态潜在变量，缺乏对复杂时变辅助过程的统一理论描述。

Method: 构建了一个通用框架，将观测到的生成状态视为可处理马尔可夫过程 $Y_t$ 的确定性投影 $X_t=Φ(Y_t)$。通过该映射，推导出在图像空间上学习生成器的数学条件，使得生成的随机过程与投影过程具有相同的单时边缘分布。

Result: 证明了在该框架下，可以学习到一个在图像空间上的生成器，其生成的边缘分布与目标投影过程一致。该研究不仅涵盖了文献中现有的离散潜在过程结果，还扩展了生成匹配的应用范围。

Conclusion: 提出了“潜在过程生成匹配”（Latent Process Generator Matching）框架，成功将生成匹配（Generator Matching）从静态潜在变量推广至含时潜在条件过程，为生成模型提供了更通用的理论基础。

Abstract: Many recent flow-matching and diffusion-style generative models rely on auxiliary stochastic dynamics during training: a richer process is simulated to define conditional targets, but the auxiliary state is either intractable to sample at generation time or simply not part of the desired output. Existing Generator Matching theory formalises conditioning on static latent random variables, and several recent papers prove special cases of projection results for particular augmented-state constructions. We introduce latent process generator matching, a general framework that treats the observed generative state as a deterministic image $X_t=Φ(Y_t)$ of a tractable Markov process $Y_t$. We show that in this setting one may learn the generator of a stochastic process on the image space which has the same one-time marginal distributions as the projected process. This generalizes and subsumes the discrete latent process results from the literature, and extends Generator Matching from static latent variables to a rich family of time-dependent latent conditional processes.

</details>


### [177] [SURF: Steering the Scalarization Weight to Uniformly Traverse the Pareto Front](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20619)
*Liuyuan Jiang,Chentong Huang,Lisha Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多目标优化中均匀权重采样导致Pareto前沿（PF）覆盖不均的问题，本文提出SURF算法，通过几何分析反向推导权重采样规则，实现了PF的高效、均匀覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然标量化在多目标优化中应用广泛，但均匀的权重采样通常会导致Pareto前沿（PF）上的解分布不均匀，无法有效满足多样的用户偏好。

Method: 1. 几何分析：分析标量化权重与Pareto前沿（PF）上解的 traversal speed（遍历速度）之间的关系。
2. CDF映射：构建弧长累积分布函数（CDF）并求解其逆映射，以此修正权重分布。
3. SURF框架：针对结构化问题推导闭式解，针对通用问题采用迭代式CDF重构与权重采样方法。

Result: 1. 理论上证明了SURF在特定条件下能够线性收敛至有限采样下界。
2. 实验在多目标Bandits、Gymnasium环境以及大模型（LLM）对齐任务中验证了SURF相较于传统方法的优越性，实现了更均匀的PF覆盖。

Conclusion: 本文提出的SURF方法通过分析标量化轨迹的几何特性，导出了一种有效的权重采样规则，解决了多目标优化中均匀权重采样导致的Pareto前沿（PF）覆盖非均匀的问题。SURF在多种任务上展现了优越的覆盖均匀性和效率。

Abstract: Scalarization is widely used in multi-objective optimization owing to its simplicity and scalability. In many applications, the goal is to generate solutions that represent diverse user preferences, ideally with uniform coverage of the Pareto front (PF). However, uniformly sampling scalarization weights usually induces non-uniform coverage of the PF. We explain this mismatch through a geometric analysis of the scalarization path. As the scalarization weight varies, the corresponding solutions trace the PF with a generally non-uniform traversal speed. This speed induces an arc-length cumulative distribution function (CDF); inverting this CDF map yields a principled rule for selecting weights that produce uniform PF coverage. Building on this insight, we propose SURF (Sampling Uniformly along the PaReto Front). For structured problems, including bi-objective bandits, we derive closed-form expressions for this CDF map and the resulting PF-aware weight sampling rule. For general problems, SURF alternates between CDF reconstruction and weight sampling. Theoretically, we show that under provable conditions, SURF converges linearly to an unavoidable finite-sampling floor. Empirically, experiments on bandits, multi-objective-gymnasium, and multi-objective LLM alignment demonstrate that SURF efficiently achieves more uniform PF coverage than baselines.

</details>


### [178] [OmniISR: A Unified Framework for Centralized and Federated Learning via Intermediate Supervision and Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20276)
*Wei-Bin Kou,Guangxu Zhu,Ming Tang,Chen Zhang,Lisheng Wu,Lei Zhou,Yujiu Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OmniISR 是首个统一 CL 和 FL 训练框架的方法，通过在隐层引入互信息监督和负熵正则化，解决了异构数据分布下的模型漂移问题，并提供了严格的收敛与理论保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球边缘智能部署面临法律碎片化问题，导致集中式学习（CL）与联邦学习（FL）并存。由于两者在优化机制（无偏梯度 vs 偏差局部更新）上的根本差异，直接结合缺乏有效的理论保障，限制了跨模式学习的性能。

Method: 提出 OmniISR 框架，核心机制包括：(1) **中间监督（ISR）**：使用互信息（MI）对齐 CL 中的协变量偏移和 FL 中的客户端漂移特征；(2) **中间正规化**：利用负熵（NE）正则化项抑制过度自信，保持表征不确定性并防止模型崩溃。在理论上推导了统一的收敛界、联邦漂移界、梯度对齐保证及逃离鞍点的时间复杂度。

Result: OmniISR 在 CL 和 FL 任务中均取得了性能提升；相比基准方法，将 CL-FL 性能差异缩小了 22.60%；在多个 FL 实验中取得了 48 项指标中的 37 项优胜。

Conclusion: 提出 OmniISR 框架，通过中间监督（ISR）机制成功统一了 CL 和 FL 的优化范式，在保证收敛性的同时显著提升了两种模式下的模型表现，填补了 CL 与 FL 混合训练在理论与实践上的空白。

Abstract: The global deployment of edge intelligence operates across heterogeneous legal frameworks. While some regions permit centralized learning (CL) via cloud data aggregation, others enforce strict data localization, necessitating federated learning (FL). This operational dichotomy introduces two incompatible optimization regimes (i.e., unbiased global gradients yet coupled with internal covariate shift in CL versus biased, drift-prone local updates in FL), resulting in that any naive integration of the two lacks rigorous theoretical guarantees.
