To fill this gap, we propose OmniISR, a unified framework that fuses pure CL, pure FL, and hybrid CL-FL training modes via equipping intermediate supervision and regularization (ISR) signals at multiple hidden layers. Specifically, we propose (i) to use mutual-information (MI) as intermediate supervision to align shifting internal covariate in CL and client-drifting representations in FL, and (ii) to adopt negative-entropy (NE) as intermediate regularizer to penalize overconfident prediction, preserve representational uncertainty, and avoid device-specific collapse. On the theory side, we derive (i) a unified, ISR-agnostic, and non-asymptotic O(1/sqrt(T)) convergence bound that shows the introduced ISR does not violate standard SGD convergence, (ii) a federated drift-bound that quantifies the ISR-reduced client drift, (iii) a gradient-alignment guarantee that ensures non-conflicting CL and FL updates under mild bias, and (iv) an explicit escape-time bound that indicates that CL-FL hybrid mixing enlarges effective stochasticity and accelerates escape from strict saddles. Extensive experiments demonstrate that OmniISR consistently improves model performance in both centralized and federated paradigms, reduces the CL-FL gap by 22.60%, and yields 37/48 paired metric wins across multiple FL algorithms.

</details>


### [179] [ClaimDiff-RL: Fine-Grained Caption Reinforcement Learning through Visual Claim Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20278)
*Tianle Li,Xuyang Shen,Yan Ma,Rongxin Guo,Shaoxiang Chen,Jiacheng Chen,Haochen Wang,Hongyang Tang,Yucong Zhou,Yu Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对长文本标注任务中奖励信号过于粗糙的问题，本文提出 ClaimDiff-RL，通过将图像描述拆解为可细粒度验证的原子声明差异进行奖励计算，成功平衡了模型描述的准确性（无幻觉）与完整性（无遗漏）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的长文本图像描述强化学习（RL）通常使用整体序列（Sequence-level）奖励，导致模型难以区分幻觉（Hallucination）和遗漏（Omission），导致事实性和信息覆盖度之间的无效权衡。

Method: 提出 ClaimDiff-RL 框架：使用引用条件下的原子声明差异作为奖励单元。利用多模态评判器枚举视觉差异，并根据图像验证、分类（幻觉/遗漏）及严重程度，计算分段的奖励信号，而非单一的序列级标量奖励。

Result: 在诊断基准、常规描述和 VQA 任务中，ClaimDiff-RL 有效改善了幻觉与遗漏的平衡，并在对象计数、空间关系和场景识别等细粒度指标上超过了 Gemini-3-Pro-Preview。

Conclusion: 细粒度的原子声明差异（ClaimDiff）是解决长文本图像描述中“事实性”与“覆盖度”权衡问题的有效奖励单元，能够实现更精细、可诊断的模型优化。

Abstract: Long-form image captioning exposes a reward granularity problem in RL: captions are judged as whole sequences, while the important errors occur at the level of individual visual claims. A good dense caption should be both faithful and informative, avoiding hallucination without omitting salient details. Yet pairwise preferences, reference-based metrics, and holistic scalar rewards compress these local errors into a single sequence-level signal, obscuring the tradeoff between factuality and coverage. We introduce ClaimDiff-RL, a framework that uses reference-conditioned atomic claim differences as the reward unit for caption RL. Given an image, an actor caption, and a reference caption, a multimodal judge enumerates visually grounded differences, verifies each difference against the image, assigns open-vocabulary error types and severity levels, and produces per-difference statistics for reward composition. This makes hallucinated claims and omitted salient facts separately measurable and tunable. Experiments show that holistic scalar rewards can reduce hallucination by increasing missing facts, while ClaimDiff-RL exposes this faithfulness and coverage tradeoff and enables more balanced operating points. On a 160-image human-labeled diagnostic benchmark, public captioning benchmarks, and VQA benchmarks, ClaimDiff-RL improves the hallucination--missing-fact balance, preserves general capability, and even surpasses Gemini-3-Pro-Preview on several fine-grained Capability dimensions such as object counting, spatial relations, and scene recognition. These results suggest that typed, verifiable claim differences are an effective reward unit for fine-grained and diagnosable caption RL.

</details>


### [180] [Introspective X Training: Feedback Conditioning Improves Scaling Across all LLM Training Stages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20285)
*Brandon Cui,Ximing Lu,Jaehun Jung,Syeda Nahida Akter,Hyunwoo Kim,Yuxiao Qu,David Acuna,Shrimai Prabhumoye,Yejin Choi,Prithviraj Ammanabrolu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IXT通过引入一种基于反馈的“内省训练”机制，将后期的评价标准引入预训练阶段，从而显著提升了大型语言模型的训练效率和最终性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型训练流水线在多阶段扩展时效率较低，且各阶段之间缺乏有效的反馈协同。研究人员旨在探索如何利用后续阶段（如后训练）的动态特征来指导和优化早期阶段（如预训练）的训练过程。

Method: 提出了“内省训练”（Introspective Training, IXT）方法。核心机制包括：1. 使用“思考奖励模型”（thinking reward model）为数据标注自然语言批评反馈；2. 在模型训练过程中，通过前缀条件化（prefix-conditioning）将这些反馈作为输入的一部分，使模型能够区分标记质量，并在训练早期就关注高质量信息。

Result: 在7.5B至12B参数规模、高达18万亿Token的从零训练实验中，IXT实现了高达2.8倍的计算效率提升，并在数学和编程等下游任务中达到了传统训练方法无法比拟的性能水平。

Conclusion: IXT证明了利用后续训练阶段的反馈来指导早期训练（如预训练）是提高计算效率和模型性能的有效途径，为大语言模型训练流程的优化提供了新范式。

Abstract: We tackle the question of how to scale more efficiently across the many, ever-growing stages of current LLM training pipelines. Our guiding intuition stems from the fact that the dynamics of later stages of the pipeline, e.g. post-training, can be used to inform earlier stages such as pre-training. To this end, we propose Introspective Training (or IXT), inspired by offline reward-conditioned reinforcement learning and applicable to any stage of training. IXT uses a thinking reward model to annotate data with natural language critique based feedback, enabling quality aware training from the earliest stages of the pipeline. Models are then trained by prefix-conditioning the data with the generated feedback -- ensuring that not all tokens are treated equally starting much earlier in training than usual. Comprehensive experiments on 7.5-12B transformer-based dense LLMs trained from scratch all the way up to 18 Trillion tokens seen show that our method: bends scaling curves resulting in up to 2.8x more compute efficiency generally; and reaches performance levels unachievable for models trained otherwise in domains such as math and code.

</details>


### [181] [Decision-Path Patterns as Tree Reliability Signals: Path-based Adaptive Weighting for Random Forest Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20716)
*Youngjoon Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一种基于决策树路径拓扑信号的随机森林样本加权方法，通过类条件比率调整，在消除类偏见的同时显著提升了分类准确性，优于现有的传统加权与动态集成方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的随机森林通过简单的多数投票机制聚合决策树结果，未能区分不同树的预测可靠性，且忽略了决策路径中包含的结构信息。

Method: 利用决策树从根节点到叶节点路径中标签转换的拓扑模式，设计了一种类条件比率加权方法（class-conditional ratio weighting），以实现样本级别的动态重加权，并确保零预期类偏见。

Result: 在30个二分类基准数据集上，该方法是唯一在统计学上显著优于标准随机森林的方法（p=0.018）；相比于其他方法，该方案有效地避免了多数类召回率退化，并在不同森林规模下表现出鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过类条件比率加权（class-conditional ratio weighting）利用决策树路径中的拓扑信息，能够在保证无预期类偏见的前提下，显著提升随机森林的预测准确性，且优于现有的加权随机森林及动态集成选择方法。

Abstract: Random forests aggregate tree votes by simple majority, treating all trees as equally informative. We observe that the topological pattern along each tree's root-to-leaf decision path -- where and how often the dominant class label flips along it -- carries a signal of tree reliability that is exploitable for per-sample reweighting. The naive use of this signal is structurally confounded with the predicted class, so we propose a class-conditional ratio weighting that guarantees zero expected class bias by construction. On 30 binary classification benchmarks under a shared-forest, shared-split protocol with 30 repeats, the proposed method is the only one among four compared schemes -- RF, weighted RF, KNORA-Eliminate, KNORA-Union -- to yield a statistically significant accuracy improvement over RF (Wilcoxon p = 0.018), while the three alternatives all fail to do so (p > 0.5). It is also the only scheme without majority-recall regressions, with minority-recall regressions limited to 3/30 datasets -- a one-sided loss to which classical dynamic ensemble selection methods are susceptible. The gain is robust across forest sizes from 100 to 1000 trees.

</details>


### [182] [FusionCell: Cross-Attentive Fusion of Layout Geometry and Netlist Topology for Standard-Cell Performance Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20287)
*Haoyi Zhang,Kairong Guo,Bojie Zhang,Yibo Lin,Runsheng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FusionCell 是一种新型双模态标准单元性能预测模型，它将布局几何与网表拓扑相结合，在极速提升预测效率的同时，保持了比传统仿真方法更高的预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的标准单元延迟和功耗预测方法通常依赖于缓慢的电路仿真，或因忽略布局几何影响而丧失了对耦合效应及布局相关效应（LDE）的捕捉能力，导致预测精度不足。

Method: 提出了一种名为 FusionCell 的双模态预测模型，利用 DeiT 编码器处理路由布局几何信息，利用图 Transformer 处理异构器件/网表连接性，并通过“拓扑引导机制”将两者进行显式融合，从而实现对性能的联合分析。

Result: FusionCell 在基于 7nm ASAP7 PDK 构建的包含 19.5k 个单元的数据集上，实现了 0.92% 的平均 MAPE，并在排名相关性（Spearman/Kendall）指标上优于基准方法，同时将表征效率提升了几个数量级。

Conclusion: FusionCell 在数字电路标准单元性能预测方面表现优异，通过融合布局几何信息与拓扑结构，实现了高精度预测，并显著提升了表征速度，为自动化设计流程提供了有效的加速手段。

Abstract: Standard cells form the building blocks of digital circuits, so their delay and power critically influence chip-level performance; yet characterization still relies on slow simulation sweeps, and many fast predictors ignore layout geometry, missing coupling and layout-dependent effects. The challenge is to jointly represent layout geometry and netlist topology so models capture fine-grained spatial details together with structural connectivity for accurate performance prediction. We introduce FusionCell, a dual-modality predictor that treats routed layout geometry and netlist topology as inputs and fuses them explicitly in a unified model. A DeiT encoder processes three-layer routed layouts, while a graph transformer models heterogeneous device/net graphs. The modalities are integrated through a topology-guided mechanism, where the netlist acts as a structural "map" to actively query relevant physical regions in the layout for joint geometric and topological reasoning. We build a 7nm dataset based on the ASAP7 PDK with over 19.5k cells spanning 149 types using automatic tools, targeting six metrics: signal rise/fall delay, transition, and power. Experimental results demonstrate that FusionCell reduces regression error, with an average MAPE of 0.92 percent, and improves Spearman/Kendall ranking over baselines, while accelerating the characterization process by orders of magnitude compared to circuit simulation.

</details>


### [183] [Correcting Stochastic Update Bias in Preconditioned Language Model Optimizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20756)
*Nikhil Nayak,Julia White,Urchade Zaratiana,Kelton Zhang,Henrijs Princis,Dhruv Atreja,Henry Fawcett,Matthew Thomas,George Hurn-Maloney,Ash Lewis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究识别出大模型优化器中存在的两种有限样本更新偏置，并提出了一种单批次偏差校正框架，通过交叉拟合和方差校正提升了主流预处理优化器的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型训练中，预处理优化器（如AdamW等）的随机更新规则通常被视为总体预处理下降的直接近似。然而，这种观点忽略了两种有限样本偏置：由同一小批次估计梯度与预处理器带来的耦合偏置，以及因反演算子非线性导致的估计偏差。

Method: 提出了一种单批次偏差校正框架，包含两个核心策略：1. “交叉拟合预处理”（Cross-fitted preconditioning）：从独立的微批次组中估计梯度和预处理器，消除耦合偏置；2. “方差校正反演”（Variance-corrected inversion）：利用微批次变异性来减去反演产生的Delta方法偏置项。该框架被成功应用于AdamW、Sophia和Shampoo等主流优化器。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-0.5B模型上进行预训练实验，偏差校正使AdamW、Sophia和Shampoo的留存测试损失分别降低了0.15、0.07和0.11 nats，且对混合质量预训练和下游指令微调表现出中性偏正的影响。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了“偏差校正”是一种减少有限样本更新偏差并提升预训练优化器性能的实用机制。

Abstract: Preconditioned optimizers are central to language model training, but their stochastic update rules are usually treated as direct approximations to population preconditioned descent. We show that this view misses two finite-sample biases. First, the gradient and preconditioner are typically estimated from the same minibatch, introducing gradient--preconditioner coupling bias. Second, even when the preconditioner estimate is unbiased, its inverse or inverse-root is generally biased because inversion is nonlinear. We propose a single-batch bias-correction framework that addresses both effects: cross-fitted preconditioning estimates the numerator and preconditioner from independent microbatch groups, while variance-corrected inversion uses microbatch variability to subtract the leading delta-method bias term. The framework applies to diagonal moment, diagonal curvature, and matrix preconditioning methods, instantiated in AdamW, Sophia, and Shampoo. Bias correction reduces held-out pretraining loss on Qwen2.5-0.5B by $0.15$, $0.07$, and $0.11$ nats, respectively; the effects on mixed-quality pretraining and downstream instruction tuning are consistently neutral-to-positive. Together, these results establish bias correction as a practical mechanism for reducing finite-sample update bias and improving the performance of preconditioned optimizers.

</details>


### [184] [Weasel: Out-of-Domain Generalization for Web Agents via Importance-Diversity Data Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20291)
*Fatemeh Pesaran zadeh,Seyeon Choi,Xing Han Lù,Siva Reddy,Gunhee Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Weasel 是一种针对 Web LLM 智能体的优化框架，通过精选训练轨迹、修剪无关网页状态及增强推理逻辑，在大幅加速模型微调的同时，显著增强了智能体的跨域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Web 智能体训练存在两类问题：1. 泛化能力差，难以应对任务域外的环境；2. 传统离线训练效率低下，受冗余轨迹影响大，且访问树（AXTree）状态过长导致计算负担重。

Method: 1. 轨迹选择：基于贪婪算法，通过优化目标函数平衡步骤的“重要性”与“多样性”（涵盖状态、网站和交互模式）；2. 数据修剪：采用目标中心化策略对 AXTree 进行修剪，仅保留关键上下文；3. 数据增强：通过模型生成风格一致的推理路径（Rationales）替代原始专家轨迹，以适应推理型模型。

Result: 在 AgentTrek 和 NNetNav 数据集上训练，并在 WebArena 等基准测试中验证，相较于标准微调，Weasel 实现了 9.7-12.5 倍的训练速度提升，并提升了 OOD 场景下的任务完成性能。

Conclusion: Weasel 是一种高效的 Web 智能体离线训练优化方法，通过轨迹选择、网页辅助树（AXTree）修剪和理性生成策略，在显著降低训练成本的同时，有效提升了 LLM 在多种 Web 任务中的域外（OOD）泛化能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have enabled web agents that follow natural language goals through multi-step browser interactions. However, agents fine-tuned on specific trajectories and domain often struggle to generalize out of domain, and offline training can be compute-inefficient due to noisy, redundant trajectories and long accessibility-tree (AXTree) states. To address both issues, we propose Weasel, a trajectory selection method for offline training of web agents. Weasel selects a fixed-budget subset of trajectory steps by optimizing an objective that balances unary importance with pairwise diversity over states, websites, and interaction patterns, solving efficiently with a greedy algorithm. We further improve efficiency with target-centered AXTree pruning that keeps only content around the ground-truth action target, and we mitigate style mismatch for reasoning-native models by replacing expert traces with model-generated, style-consistent rationales. Across AgentTrek and NNetNav training datasets, evaluations in WebArena, WorkArena, and MiniWob, and experiments with Qwen2.5-7B, Gemma3-4B, and Qwen3-8B, Weasel improves out-of-domain performance while reducing training cost, producing roughly 9.7-12.5$\times$ training speedups over standard fine-tuning. We make the code available at https://github.com/fatemehpesaran310/weasel.

</details>


### [185] [Divide et Calibra: Multiclass Local Calibration via Vector Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21060)
*Cesare Barbera,Lorenzo Perini,Giovanni De Toni,Andrea Passerini,Andrea Pugnana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多分类校准中局部误差难以捕捉的问题，本文利用向量量化构建组合式校准映射，实现了局部校准性能的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多分类校准方法存在局限性：全局方法假设校准误差在整个潜在空间是均匀的，由于不实，效果有限；而局部方法往往依赖降维导致信息丢失。

Method: 提出了一种组合式校准框架，利用向量量化（VQ）对表征空间进行结构化划分，并引入索引Dirichlet浓度参数化方法，在不同区域间实现参数共享。

Result: 在基准数据集上的实验表明，该方法在显著提升局部校准精度的同时，保持了优异的全局校准能力和预测性能。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于向量量化的组合式多分类校准方法，通过在不同区域间共享参数，实现了局部与全局校准性能的平衡，有效解决了高维空间中局部校准困难的问题。

Abstract: Accurate and well-calibrated Machine Learning (ML) models are mandatory in high-stakes settings, yet effective multiclass calibration remains challenging: global approaches assume calibration errors are homogeneous across the latent space, while local methods often rely on latent-space dimensionality reduction, which leads to information loss. To address these issues, we propose a compositional approach to multiclass calibration, where region-specific calibration maps are constructed from shared codeword-dependent factors. We instantiate this idea via Vector Quantization (VQ), which induces a structured partition of the representation space, and an indexed parameterization of Dirichlet concentrations that enables parameter sharing across regions. Our approach learns heterogeneous calibration maps that generalize well even to sparse regions of the latent space. Experiments on benchmark datasets show significant improvements in local calibration while maintaining competitive global calibration and predictive performance.

</details>


### [186] [TreeText-CTS: Compact, Source-Traceable Tree-Path Evidence for Irregular Clinical Time-Series Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20292)
*Kwanhyung Lee,Juhwan Choi,Jongheon Kim,Joohyung Lee,Hyeongwon Jang,Eunho Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TreeText-CTS 是一种新型 EHR 时间序列分析框架，将临床测量数据转化为可溯源的树路径文本证据，在保证高预测性能的同时，实现了对临床决策证据的透明化解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数值时间序列模型虽效果好但缺乏可解释性；现有的文本化接口方案（如原始序列化或自由文本摘要）存在冗余度高、难以溯源或信息丢失等问题，迫切需要一种既易读又能追溯原始测量数据的 EHR 证据表示方法。

Method: 提出 TreeText-CTS 框架：1. 将不规则 EHR 轨迹转化为树路径（tree-path）证据单元；2. 利用冻结的 XGBoost 模型处理多尺度窗口摘要；3. 通过证据选择器筛选关键路径，并由语言模型编码器进行融合预测，无需传统的自回归解码。

Result: 在 PhysioNet 2012、MIMIC-III 和 PhysioNet 2019 数据集上，TreeText-CTS 的 AUROC 和 AUPRC 均优于现有的文本接口基准，AUPRC 提升了 6.0 至 9.7 个百分点，并与纯数值模型性能相当。

Conclusion: TreeText-CTS 有效解决了 EHR 时间序列分析中“预测性能”与“可解释性/溯源性”之间的矛盾，证明了将模型激活路径转化为结构化文本证据不仅能提升预测表现，还能保障医疗临床决策的可追溯性。

Abstract: Numerical time-series models can effectively process irregular electronic health record (EHR) trajectories, but they do not naturally expose the measurements and temporal patterns supporting each risk estimate as readable evidence. Existing text-based interfaces improve readability, but typically rely on either raw serialization, which is lengthy and redundant, or patient-level free-form summaries, which are difficult to trace to source measurements and time windows. To bridge this gap, we introduce TreeText-CTS (Clinical Time-Series), which converts irregular EHR trajectories into human-readable, compact, source-traceable tree-path evidence units without patient-level summarization or inference-time autoregressive decoding. TreeText-CTS routes multi-scale window summaries through frozen XGBoost models and verbalizes activated tree paths as deterministic, source-traceable evidence units composed of threshold conditions. An evidence selector assembles an informative subset of these units, which a language-model encoder then integrates for prediction. Across PhysioNet 2012 mortality, MIMIC-III mortality, and PhysioNet 2019 sepsis-onset forecasting, TreeText-CTS achieves the best AUROC and AUPRC among evaluated text-based EHR time-series interfaces, improving AUPRC by 6.0 to 9.7 absolute percentage points over the strongest prior text-based interface while remaining competitive with numerical time-series models. Ablations show that tree-path evidence construction, evidence selection, and language-model composition each contribute to performance. Because every span passed to the language-model encoder is constructed from activated tree-path threshold conditions, TreeText-CTS makes the evidence supplied to the final predictor inspectable and source-traceable.

</details>


### [187] [Improved Guarantees for Constrained Online Convex Optimization via Self-Contraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21107)
*Dhruv Sarkar,Abhishek Sinha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于投影的简单算法，通过利用自收缩曲线的几何特性，显著改善了约束在线凸优化（COCO）中的累积违规（CCV）界限，在保持最优遗憾的同时，实现了强凸损失下CCV从 $O(\sqrt{T \log T})$ 到 $O(\log T)$ 的突破。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决约束在线凸优化（COCO）中，在对抗性约束下实现更低累积约束违规（CCV）与遗憾（regret）平衡的问题，突破已有算法在CCV方面的界限。

Method: 提出了一种简单的基于投影（projection-based）的算法，并利用了关于自收缩曲线（self-contracted curves）的几何性质分析来优化算法的收敛行为。

Result: 对于强凸损失，实现了 $O(\log T)$ 的遗憾（regret）和 $O(\log T)$ 的 CCV（指数级改进）；对于凸损失，实现了 $O(\sqrt{T})$ 的遗憾和 $O(\sqrt{T})$ 的 CCV。

Conclusion: 通过引入针对自收缩曲线的几何新结果，该算法在约束在线凸优化（COCO）问题中实现了累积违规（CCV）的显著提升：对于强凸损失，CCV达到了最优的 $O(\log T)$；对于一般凸损失，CCV达到了 $O(\sqrt{T})$，同时保持了最优遗憾界。

Abstract: We consider Constrained Online Convex Optimization (COCO) with adversarially chosen constraints. At each round, the learner chooses an action before observing the loss and constraint function for that round. The goal is to achieve small static regret against the best point satisfying all constraints while also controlling cumulative constraint violation ($\mathsf{CCV}$). For strongly convex losses, state-of-the-art algorithms achieve $O(\log T)$ regret and $O(\sqrt{T \log T})$ $\mathsf{CCV}.$ The corresponding best-known bounds for convex losses is $O(\sqrt{T})$ regret and $O(\sqrt{T} \log T)$ $\mathsf{CCV}$. In this paper, we give a simple projection-based algorithm that simultaneously achieves $O(\log T)$ regret and $O(\log T)$ $\mathsf{CCV}$ for strongly-convex losses, yielding an exponential improvement in the $\mathsf{CCV}$. For the convex losses, our algorithm improves the $\mathsf{CCV}$ to $O(\sqrt{T})$ while maintaining the optimal $O(\sqrt{T})$ regret. The key to our improvement is a recent geometric result for self-contracted curves, which may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [188] [On the Regularity and Generalization of One-Step Wasserstein-guided Generative Models for PDE-Induced Measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21388)
*Likun Lin,Zhongjian Wang,Jack Xin,Zhiwen Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为PDE诱导的生成模型提供了理论框架，证明了最优传输映射的Hölder正则性，并从理论上论证了单步映射（如DeepParticle）在科学计算任务中的近似能力与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前关于生成模型在科学计算中统计精确性的理论分析大多持悲观态度，缺乏对PDE诱导概率测度的一步生成模型进行严谨的理论支撑。

Method: 1. 证明目标测度满足倍加条件（doubling conditions）；2. 利用最优传输的正则性理论，证明从均匀源测度到目标测度的最优传输映射是Hölder连续的；3. 基于逼近理论推导DeepParticle的过剩风险界（excess-risk bounds）及目标偏移下的稳健性估计。

Result: 建立了目标测度的正则性框架，证明了一步生成模型通过平滑映射逼近目标分布的可行性，并给出了具体的误差收敛速率及稳健性界。

Conclusion: 该研究通过理论证明了针对PDE诱导概率测度的一步生成模型（如DeepParticle）具有合理的统计近似性质，为该领域提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Despite the remarkable empirical success of generative models, the available theory on their statistical accuracy in scientific computing remains largely pessimistic. This paper develops a theoretical framework for understanding the regularity of transport maps and the generalization properties of one-step Wasserstein-guided generative models for PDE-induced probability measures. We consider normalized target densities associated with linear elliptic and parabolic equations on bounded domains, as well as diffusion and Fokker--Planck equations on the torus. Under standard structural assumptions, we prove that these target measures satisfy doubling conditions. By combining this fact with regularity theory for optimal transport between doubling measures, we show that the optimal transport map from a uniform source measure to the target measure is Hölder continuous. This regularity yields an approximation-theoretic justification for one-step generative models that learn PDE-induced distributions via a single pushforward map. As a representative instance, we study DeepParticle and derive excess-risk bounds characterizing the discrepancy between the learned map and the population-optimal map. We also establish a robustness estimate under target shift and illustrate the theory with experiments which support the derived rates.

</details>


### [189] [Quant.npu: Enabling Efficient Mobile NPU Inference for on-device LLMs via Fully Static Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20295)
*Jinghe Zhang,Daliang Xu,Chenghua Wang,Weikai Xie,Tao Qi,Yun Ma,Mengwei Xu,Gang Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Quant.npu 是一种专为移动端 NPU 设计的大模型全静态量化框架，通过旋转矩阵技术、精细化的参数初始化与分阶段优化策略，在实现全静态推理的同时显著降低了延迟并保持了模型精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大模型后训练量化（PTQ）方法多依赖动态激活量化，无法满足移动端 NPU 对全静态量化的硬件约束，导致推理效率提升受限。为了弥补高保真 PTQ 与 NPU 硬件推理约束之间的差距。

Method: 提出一种整数全静态（integer-only fully static）量化框架，核心技术包括：1. 引入可学习的量化参数与旋转矩阵；2. 提出“旋转与位宽感知”的初始化策略；3. 采用“分布感知选择性优化”的两阶段流水线；4. 引入灵敏度引导的自适应混合精度方案。

Result: 在真实移动端 NPU 上的实验表明，Quant.npu 在保持与最先进方法相当的精度的同时，推理延迟降低了高达 15.1%。

Conclusion: Quant.npu 成功解决了在移动端 NPU 上实现全静态量化与高精度推理之间的矛盾，为大模型在移动设备上的高效部署提供了实用且兼容性强的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed on mobile devices, where Neural Processing Units (NPUs) necessitate fully static quantization for optimal inference efficiency. However, existing post-training quantization (PTQ) methods predominantly rely on dynamic activation quantization, rendering them incompatible with NPU hardware constraints. To bridge the gap between high-fidelity PTQ and NPU-constrained inference, we propose Quant.npu, a integer-only fully static quantization framework. It incorporates learnable quantization parameters and rotation matrices, enabling low-bit activation-weight quantization without runtime quantization parameters re-computation. Crucially, we identify that initialization and selective optimization of quantization parameters is pivotal for optimization stability, as improper initialization and naive joint optimization induce gradient instability that disrupts the optimization of rotation matrices. To address this, we propose a rotation-and-bit-width-aware initialization tailored to diverse activation profiles and a distribution-aware selective optimization (two-stage quantization pipeline) tailored to rotated and unrotated tensors. Furthermore, we introduce a sensitivity-guided adaptive mixed-precision scheme to balance accuracy with inference efficiency. Extensive experiments on real-world mobile NPUs demonstrate that Quant.npu achieves comparable accuracy to state-of-the-art methods, while reducing inference latency by up to 15.1%.

</details>


### [190] [Quantifying Hyperparameter Transfer and the Importance of Embedding Layer Learning Rate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21486)
*Dayal Singh Kalra,Maissam Barkeshli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究量化了超参数传递性能，揭示了$\mu$P相较于SP的关键优势在于消除了嵌入层的学习率瓶颈，并证实了对齐嵌入层学习率可显著优化大模型的超参数传递效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的超参数传递理论不足以解释为何$\mu$P在训练大语言模型时比标准参数化（SP）表现更好，且缺乏量化评估超参数传递效果的标准框架。

Method: 1. 提出了用于量化超参数传递的三指标框架（拟合质量、外推鲁棒性、渐进损失惩罚）；2. 对比$\mu$P与SP进行消融实验，分析AdamW优化器下的超参数传递行为。

Result: 发现$\mu$P在高学习率下的优势主要归功于嵌入层学习率的提升；在SP中，通过将嵌入层学习率增加宽度倍数（即对齐$\mu$P），可以显著缓解训练不稳定并提升传递效果；量化了权重衰减对缩放规律拟合与外推鲁棒性的双重影响。

Conclusion: $\mu$P的优越性主要源于其优化了嵌入层的学习率，从而消除了SP中的训练瓶颈；此外，权重衰减会影响超参数传递的鲁棒性。研究提供了一个量化超参数传递质量的新框架。

Abstract: Hyperparameter transfer allows extrapolating optimal optimization hyperparameters from small to large scales, making it critical for training large language models (LLMs). This is done either by fitting a scaling law to the hyperparameters or by a judicious choice of parameterization, such as Maximal Update ($μ$P), that renders optimal hyperparameters approximately scale invariant. In this paper, we first develop a framework to quantify hyperparameter transfer through three metrics: (1) the quality of the scaling law fit, (2) the robustness to extrapolation errors, and (3) the asymptotic loss penalty due to choice of parameterization. Next, we investigate through a comprehensive series of ablations why $μ$P appears to offer high-quality learning rate transfer relative to standard parameterization (SP), as existing theory is inadequate. We find that the overwhelming benefit of $μ$P relative to SP when training with AdamW arises simply from maximizing the learning rate of the embedding layer. In SP, the embedding layer learning rate acts as a bottleneck that induces training instabilities; increasing it by a factor of width to match $μ$P dramatically smooths out training while improving hyperparameter transfer. We also find that weight decay improves the scaling law fits, while, in the fixed token-per-parameter setting, it hurts the robustness of the extrapolation.

</details>


### [191] [Spectral Unforgetting: Post-Hoc Recovery of Damaged Capabilities Without Retraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20296)
*Aarash Abro,Muhammad Tahir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对语言模型微调引起的“灾难性遗忘”问题，提出了一种基于谱分析的后处理修复方法DG-Hard。该方法通过对权重更新应用Donoho-Gavish硬阈值截断，在不使用额外数据的情况下，有效修复了被微调损坏的通用能力与安全性，同时保持了目标任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在微调过程中会出现灾难性遗忘，导致模型在未受微调数据明确覆盖的任务上能力下降。现有的解决方案往往依赖额外数据或计算资源，或在修复能力与保留微调收益之间难以平衡。

Method: 提出DG-Hard（Donoho-Gavish Hard Thresholding），这是一种仅需预训练检查点（W_base）和微调检查点（W_ft）的后处理修复方法。将权重更新增量（Δ = W_ft - W_base）进行奇异值分解（SVD），并应用Donoho-Gavish硬阈值处理，剔除谱噪声（即非任务相关的权重扰动），保留结构化的任务信号。此外，引入了分区条件指标（partition-conditional metric）来全面评估修复效果。

Result: 在14个模型-任务设置及9个跨域测试集上的实验表明，DG-Hard在所有后处理基线方法中表现最佳；同时，在未经额外对齐数据的情况下，该方法有效恢复了被微调损害的模型安全对齐能力。

Conclusion: 微调导致的能力丧失部分并非专精化的必然后果，而是权重更新中可移除的“谱残留”。DG-Hard通过无需数据的闭式SVD滤波，能够有效修复受损能力，同时保留目标任务的性能增益。

Abstract: Fine-tuning a language model for a target task routinely degrades capabilities the training data never explicitly threatened. We study this phenomenon, known as catastrophic forgetting, and propose a post-hoc repair solution that uses only the pretrained checkpoint $W_{\mathrm{base}}$ and its fine-tuned descendant $W_{\mathrm{ft}}$. The goal is not merely to revert the model toward the base checkpoint, but to recover capabilities damaged by fine-tuning while preserving both the target-task gains and any beneficial held-out improvements. We introduce DG-Hard, a checkpoint-only spectral repair method for the fine-tuning update $Δ= W_{\mathrm{ft}} - W_{\mathrm{base}}$. DG-Hard treats $Δ$ as a low-rank task-aligned signal embedded in an IID-like noise residual that gradient descent has no incentive to remove, and applies the Donoho-Gavish hard singular-value threshold to each weight-delta matrix, keeping the structured high-energy part of the update and removing the spectral bulk. This reduces repair to a closed-form SVD filtering step requiring no data-dependent tuning. A central difficulty is evaluation: average accuracy hides per-benchmark failures, while naive recovery scores reward models that simply revert toward the base. We therefore introduce a partition-conditional metric that separately tracks healing, preservation, non-damage, and target-task retention. Across $14$ (model, task) settings and nine cross-domain held-out benchmarks, DG-Hard achieves the strongest balanced repair among post-hoc baselines. DG-Hard also restores safety alignment degraded by benign fine-tuning on three independent safety axes, despite using no alignment data. These results suggest that part of fine-tuning-induced capability loss is not an unavoidable consequence of specialization, but a removable spectral residue in the weight update itself.

</details>


### [192] [Variance Reduction for Expectations with Diffusion Teachers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21489)
*Jesse Bettencourt,Xindi Wu,Matan Atzmon,James Lucas,Jonathan Lorraine*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CARV 是一种通过摊销昂贵的上游计算和优化采样策略（重要性采样+分层采样）来加速扩散模型下游任务的方法，在显著降低计算成本的同时，揭示了不同任务对梯度方差的敏感度差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于预训练扩散模型的下游任务（如文本生成 3D、单步蒸馏等）中，计算梯度时的蒙特卡洛 (MC) 估计方差导致了巨大的下游计算成本，因为每次采样都需要昂贵的上游工作。

Method: 提出了一种计算感知且计及方差的框架 (CARV)，通过分层蒙特卡洛 (MC) 估计，将昂贵的上游计算（渲染、编码等）与廉价的扩散噪声重采样进行摊销，并结合时间步重要性采样 (IS) 和分层逆累积分布函数 (Inverse-CDF) 技术来减小估计方差。

Result: 在文本生成 3D 和数据归因任务中，CARV 实现了 2-3 倍的有效计算加速；在单步蒸馏中，该方法将梯度方差降低了一个数量级，但未能改善 FID 指标，揭示了该场景下 MC 方差已非主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: CARV 框架通过分层蒙特卡洛估计显著降低了扩散模型下游任务的计算成本（通过摊销重用），在某些任务中（如 Text-to-3D 和归因）提升了计算效率，但在另一些任务中（如单步蒸馏）表明 MC 方差不再是性能瓶颈。

Abstract: Pretrained diffusion models serve as frozen teachers feeding downstream pipelines such as text-to-3D, single-step distillation, and data attribution. The teacher gradients these pipelines consume are Monte Carlo (MC) expectations over noise levels and Gaussian noise samples; their estimator variance dominates compute cost because each draw requires expensive upstream work (rendering, simulation, encoding). We introduce CARV, a compute-aware variance-accounting framework that motivates a hierarchical MC estimator: amortize the expensive upstream computation over cheap diffusion-noise resamples, sharpened by timestep importance sampling and a stratified-inverse-CDF construction. In our text-to-3D distillation and attribution experiments, CARV delivers 2-3x effective compute multipliers (most from amortized reuse; ~25% additional from IS+stratification) without changing the objective; in single-step distillation, the same techniques cut gradient variance by an order of magnitude but do not improve downstream FID, marking the regime where MC variance is no longer the bottleneck.

</details>


### [193] [Mechanisms of Misgeneralization in Physical Sequence Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20299)
*Kento Nishi,Raphael Tang,Karun Kumar,Core Francisco Park,Hidenori Tanaka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示了生成模型在物理任务中因局部预测误差累积导致全局物理属性分布偏差的现象（物理误泛化），并提供了一种通过数据偏差核进行分析与干预的有效机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成序列模型在物理领域（如机器人）应用时，虽能生成合理的独立轨迹，却往往难以维持训练数据预期的物理量（如旅行距离、能量）分布，导致“物理误泛化”现象，缺乏对 aggregate distribution 的控制。

Method: 1. 定义了“物理误泛化”现象；2. 通过受控合成任务分析误差传播机制；3. 引入“数据偏差核（Data deviation kernel）”来量化并预测物理量分布的偏移；4. 在迷宫导航和双摆运动任务中进行验证并提出干预方法。

Result: 证实了局部错误在物理测量下的传播是导致分布偏移的主要原因。利用提出的数据偏差核，成功预测了物理量在不同任务中的表现（增益或损失），且基于核的干预手段能显著改善最终的物理分布一致性。

Conclusion: 揭示了物理误泛化（Physical Misgeneralization）的成因，即局部预测误差累积导致物理分布偏移。基于此机制，提出了名为“核引导干预（Kernel-informed intervention）”的缓解策略，能有效纠正生成轨迹在物理量上的分布偏差。

Abstract: Generative sequence models are often trained to plan motion in physical domains, from robotics to mechanical simulations. When constructing a dataset to train such a model, engineers may curate demonstrations to specify how trajectories should be distributed over a physical quantity like travel distance or mechanical energy. For example, a roboticist building a maze navigation agent might choose demonstrations whose travel distances cover a fixed range uniformly, hoping to constrain the agent's expected power usage. We find that standard deep learning can violate this intent: each generated trajectory can seem plausible on its own, but the aggregate distribution over the physical quantity is wrong. We call this failure physical misgeneralization, and develop an account of its mechanism. Using controlled synthetic tasks, we show that physical misgeneralization arises when local errors typical of the model class propagate through the physical measurement to shift the recovered distribution. We estimate these errors with a data deviation kernel, and we use it to predict which physical quantities gain or lose mass in both our synthetic and more applied maze navigation and double-pendulum motion tasks. Finally, our mechanistic interpretation helps identify which mitigation strategies are structurally promising, and we use it to propose a kernel-informed intervention.

</details>


### [194] [Robust Subspace-Constrained Quadratic Models for Low-Dimensional Structure Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20300)
*Zheng Zhai,Xiaohui Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为SCQM的鲁棒子空间学习模型，通过支持多种噪声分布并结合高效的梯度优化算法，解决了高维数据处理中对不同噪声特性的适配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有子空间学习方法在处理具有不同尾部特性（重尾或轻尾）的噪声时不够鲁棒，且缺乏统一的数学框架来适应多种噪声分布。

Method: 1. 提出子空间约束二次模型（SCQM），扩展了SQMF框架以适应更广泛的噪声分布；2. 开发了基于梯度的回溯线搜索优化算法；3. 对l_p^p和l_2损失函数进行了灵敏度分析。

Result: 数值实验表明，SCQM在多种噪声环境下均表现出比现有方法更好的鲁棒性和更高的重构精度。

Conclusion: 本文提出的SCQM模型通过引入广义高斯和径向拉普拉斯噪声模型，显著提升了子空间学习方法在面对不同噪声分布时的鲁棒性和重构精度，理论分析与实验结果均验证了其优越性。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a robust subspace-constrained quadratic model (SCQM) for learning low-dimensional structure from high-dimensional data. Building upon the subspace-constrained quadratic matrix factorization (SQMF) framework, the proposed model accommodates a broad class of noise distributions, including generalized Gaussian and radial Laplace models. This generalization enables reliable performance under both heavy-tailed and light-tailed noise, thereby substantially enhancing robustness across diverse data regimes. To efficiently address the resulting nonconvex optimization problem, we develop a gradient-based algorithm equipped with a backtracking line-search strategy that ensures stable and efficient convergence. In addition, we present a sensitivity analysis of the $\ell_p^p$ and $\ell_2$ loss functions, elucidating their distinct behaviors under varying noise characteristics. Extensive numerical experiments corroborate the theoretical analysis and demonstrate that the proposed approach consistently outperforms existing methods in terms of robustness and reconstruction accuracy.

</details>


### [195] [Neural Collapse by Design: Learning Class Prototypes on the Hypersphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20302)
*Panagiotis Koromilas,Theodoros Giannakopoulos,Mihalis A. Nicolaou,Yannis Panagakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出通过在单位超球面上进行原型对比来优化监督学习，修正了交叉熵和对比学习的缺陷，实现了理论上的神经崩溃（NC），在提升分类准确率的同时，大幅缩短了训练时间并增强了模型的鲁棒性与迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有监督学习的两大主要范式——交叉熵（CE）和监督对比学习（SCL）均未能达到深度学习的理论最优状态：神经崩溃（NC）。CE存在几何退化问题，而SCL则在预训练后通过线性探测丢弃了已学到的NC结构。

Method: 1. 理论统一：通过数学证明指出CE和SCL本质上都是超球面上的原型对比优化。
2. CE修正：提出NTCE和NONL两类归一化损失函数，引入大负样本集及解耦的对齐与均匀性约束。
3. SCL修正：证明SCL可以直接使用类均值嵌入作为分类器权重，从而摒弃冗余的线性探测（Linear Probing）阶段。

Result: 1. 性能提升：在ImageNet-1K等基准上，改进后的模型分类精度优于传统CE。
2. 收敛效率：NTCE和NONL在极少数迭代（<7.5%）内达到与传统CE相当的NC指标。
3. 泛化能力：在迁移学习中平均相对提升5.5%，在强类不平衡下提升高达8.7%，并展现出更强的抗干扰能力（更低的mCE）。

Conclusion: 本文通过统一交叉熵（CE）与监督对比学习（SCL）的视角，提出在超球面上进行原型对比是实现神经崩溃（NC）的最优路径。通过修正两种范式各自的缺陷，作者不仅在分类精度上超越了传统CE，还显著加速了模型收敛，并提升了模型在迁移学习和类不平衡场景下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Supervised classification has a theoretical optimum, Neural Collapse (NC), yet neither of its two dominant paradigms reaches it in practice. Cross entropy (CE) leaves radial degrees of freedom unconstrained and converges to a degenerate geometry, while supervised contrastive learning (SCL) drives features toward NC during pretraining but discards this structure in a post hoc linear probing phase. We show that both paradigms are different appearances of the same method, prototype contrast on the unit hypersphere, and that closing the gap requires fixing each at its specific point of failure. From the CE side, we propose NTCE and NONL, two normalized losses that import contrastive optimization's missing ingredients into classifier learning: a large effective negative set and decoupled alignment and uniformity terms. From the SCL side, we prove that SCL's objective already optimizes throughout training for a principled classifier whose weights are the class mean embeddings, making linear probing both redundant and harmful. Empirically, on four benchmarks including ImageNet-1K, NTCE and NONL surpass CE accuracy, closely approximate NC ($\geq 95\%$), and match CE's converged NC on 4/5 metrics in under $7.5\%$ of its iterations, while SCL with fixed prototypes matches linear probing without the hours-long classifier training phase. The learned geometry yields $+5.5\%$ mean relative improvement in transfer learning, up to $+8.7\%$ under severe class imbalance, and lower mCE on ImageNet-C, recasting supervised learning as prototype learning on the hypersphere, with NC reached by design on both paths.

</details>


### [196] [AirfoilGen: A valid-by-construction and performance-aware latent diffusion model for airfoil generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20303)
*Zhijie Yang,Min Tang,Qiang Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 AirfoilGen，一种结合圆扫描约束与潜空间扩散模型的新型翼型生成框架，实现了在严格几何约束下对气动性能的精准控制，并配套发布了大规模翼型数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度生成模型在翼型设计中存在几何有效性差、气动性能可控性弱的问题，且缺乏适合复杂深度学习训练的大规模数据集。

Method: 1. 提出“圆扫描表示法”（circle sweeping representation）以确保生成翼型的几何有效性；
2. 构建基于 Transformer 的编码器将翼型映射到潜空间；
3. 设计条件扩散模型（conditional diffusion model），将目标气动性能作为条件嵌入生成过程，从而实现反向设计与性能控制。

Result: 1. 构建了包含超过 200,000 个翼型的大规模数据集；
2. 实验表明生成的翼型几何有效性大幅提升；
3. 实现了 98.41% 的平均性能条件化准确率，显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 提出 AirfoilGen 模型，通过创新的几何表示和扩散模型框架，实现了高几何有效性与精确的气动性能控制，为翼型设计提供了一种高效、可控的新范式。

Abstract: Airfoil shape design is a fundamental task in aerospace engineering, with a direct impact on flight stability and fuel consumption. Deep learning has recently emerged as a promising tool for this task, but existing deep generative approaches remain limited in both geometric validity and physical controllability. They offer little control over the generated shapes, yielding invalid geometries, and they typically do not condition effectively on aerodynamic performance. To address these issues, this paper proposes AirfoilGen, a valid-by-construction and performance-aware latent diffusion model for airfoil. It first introduces a novel airfoil representation scheme, the circle sweeping representation, to constrain the generative process so that output shapes respect essential airfoil characteristics. It then enables explicit control over aerodynamic performance (e.g., lift and drag coefficients) by operating in a learned latent space: a transformer model encodes airfoil shapes into vector embeddings, and a conditional diffusion model denoises Gaussian noise into these latent embeddings while incorporating target aerodynamic performance. In addition, this paper presents a new dataset of over 200,000 airfoils, which is substantially larger than the widely used UIUC airfoil dataset (1,650 airfoils) and more suitable for training modern deep generative models. Experiments demonstrate that AirfoilGen enables airfoil generation with far greater geometric validity and aerodynamic performance controllability than previously achievable, with an average performance-conditioning accuracy of 98.41%.

</details>


### [197] [Consistently Informative Soft-Label Temperature for Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20357)
*Hoang-Chau Luong,Nghia Van Vo,Kaiqi Zhao,Lingwei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对传统知识蒸馏中固定温度导致样本间信息一致性差及对齐过于僵化的问题，本文提出了CIST方法，通过样本级自适应温度实现稳定且高质量的知识转移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的蒸馏方法通常采用全局固定的温度，无法适应不同样本的逻辑值尺度与学习难度差异，导致教师软标签熵的不一致性（过度尖锐或过度平滑），且强制教师与学生模型进行刚性的逻辑值对齐，忽视了二者容量的不匹配。

Method: 提出了CIST（Consistently Informative Soft-label Temperature）框架，通过为教师和学生模型分配独立的、样本自适应的温度参数，并根据教师置信度与学生学习难度动态调整蒸馏目标权重，打破了传统蒸馏中的固定温度与硬性对齐限制。

Result: CIST通过保持教师软标签的持续信息量，显著缓解了固定温度带来的不一致性，在视觉和语言任务中均取得了比标准KD及现有强基线方法更优的性能。

Conclusion: CIST通过引入样本级自适应温度机制，有效解决了传统KD中固定温度带来的信息不一致问题，在视觉和语言领域均表现出优于标准KD及强基线模型的效果，且计算开销极低。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation (KD) transfers knowledge from a high-capacity teacher to a compact student by matching their predictive distributions, with temperature scaling serving as a central mechanism for smoothing teacher predictions and exposing informative "dark knowledge" beyond the hard label. However, the standard fixed-temperature design is inherently sample-agnostic. Since samples differ in logit scale and learning difficulty, a single global temperature produces teacher soft labels with highly inconsistent entropy: some predictions remain overly sharp and provide limited inter-class information, whereas others become over-smoothed and lose class-discriminative information. Moreover, sharing the same temperature between teacher and student further imposes rigid logit-scale alignment despite their capacity mismatch. To address these limitations, we propose CIST (Consistently Informative Soft-label Temperature), which assigns separate sample-wise adaptive temperatures to the teacher and student. This design produces consistently informative teacher soft labels while relaxing rigid teacher--student logit-scale matching. It also reweights the distillation objective according to teacher confidence and student learning difficulty. Theoretically, we show that teacher-label entropy is largely governed by the ratio between the maximum teacher logit and the temperature, providing a principled basis for adaptive smoothing. Empirically, CIST mitigates the inconsistency induced by fixed temperature, and experiments on both vision and language distillation tasks show consistent improvements over standard KD and strong baselines with negligible computational overhead.

</details>


### [198] [Nonlocal operator learning for fMRI encoding and decoding tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20389)
*Andreas Kramer,Saugat Acharya,Alice Giola,Emanuele Zappala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用神经积分算子建模fMRI数据的框架，通过引入非局部时空上下文信息，显著改善了fMRI信号的解码与重建效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: fMRI数据具有复杂的高维时空结构，传统的建模方法在处理非局部时空上下文依赖时存在困难。

Method: 提出了一种基于潜在神经积分算子的框架，通过在辅助空间中执行不动点迭代来捕获高维时空依赖关系，并结合解码器进行分类和刺激预测。

Result: 在两个公开fMRI数据集上验证，较大的时间窗口显著提升了解码和编码任务的性能，同时也带来了更具结构化的潜在空间表征。

Conclusion: 神经积分算子（Neural Integral Operators）是建模fMRI动力学的有效框架，更广泛的时空上下文（非局部结构）对预测和表征学习至关重要，且比原始数据能提供更好的特征分布和类别分离。

Abstract: Functional MRI data exhibit high-dimensional spatiotemporal structure, making both prediction and decoding challenging. In this work, we investigate neural integral-operator-based models for encoding and decoding tasks in fMRI, with particular emphasis on the role of nonlocal spatiotemporal context. We implement a latent neural integral operator framework that performs fixed point iterations in an auxiliary space from which classification and stimuli prediction is performed via a decoder. We evaluate our model on two open-source fMRI datasets.
  Our experiments examine both decoding of stimuli from fMRI recordings and encoding of fMRI dynamics from stimulus representations. A main focus is the effect of spatiotemporal context: we systematically compare short and long temporal windows, as well as the use of visual cortex vs whole brain recordings, and analyze their influence on performance and latent-space geometry. Across tasks and datasets, larger temporal windows generally improve results and produce more structured learned representations. In decoding experiments, the learned latent space often provides clearer class separation than the raw data. In encoding experiments, although absolute performance remains moderate due to the difficulty of the task, longer temporal windows still yield consistent gains.
  These findings suggest that neural integral operators provide a promising framework for modeling fMRI dynamics and that broader spatiotemporal context can be beneficial for both prediction and representation learning. More broadly, the results indicate that exploiting distributed nonlocal structure in brain dynamics requires model architectures specifically designed to capture such dependencies.

</details>


### [199] [Decomposing MXFP4 quantization error for LLM reinforcement learning: reducible bias, recoverable deadzone, and an irreducible floor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20402)
*Xiaocan Li,Shiliang Wu,Zheng Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将MXFP4量化误差分解为尺度偏差、死区截断和网格噪声，分别对应RL训练中的梯度误差、回滚质量下降和策略熵异常，并通过针对性修正技术显著恢复了LLM在低精度RL训练下的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MXFP4算术虽然能显著加速LLM的RL后训练，但其量化误差会导致严重的精度下降。现有研究将其视为均匀噪声，未能揭示其损害训练的具体机制。

Method: 提出了一种对MXFP4量化误差的三向分解理论，并针对各组成部分设计了针对性的优化技术：宏块缩放（Macro-block scaling）解决尺度偏差，离群值回退（Outlier Fallback）解决死区截断，以及自适应量化噪声（AQN）调节策略熵。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-3B和Qwen3-30B-A3B模型上，使用该方法后，RL训练精度在BF16基准的0.7%和3.0%误差范围内。

Conclusion: 通过有针对性地缓解MXFP4量化误差的三个组成部分（尺度偏差、死区截断、网格噪声），该方法能够有效解决低精度RL训练导致的性能下降，在Qwen模型上实现了接近BF16精度的训练效果。

Abstract: MXFP4 arithmetic can dramatically accelerate reinforcement learning (RL) post-training of large language models (LLMs), yet the quantization error introduces severe accuracy degradation. Existing work treats the quantization error as a monolithic noise term, missing the distinct mechanisms upon interpreting how quantization error damages training. We prove an exact three-way decomposition of quantization error and show how each component dominates a distinct RL training pathway. Our theoretical and empirical analysis decomposes the MXFP4 quantization error into three additive components: "scale bias" from power-of-two rounding, "deadzone truncation" from zeroing small values, and "grid noise" from rounding to the nearest 4-bit grid. Each component dominates a distinct RL failure mode: scale bias accumulates multiplicatively through the backward pass, affecting gradient accuracy; deadzone truncation degrades rollout quality; and grid noise raises the policy's entropy. We combine corrections that are RL failure mode-targeted but not component-exclusive: Macro-block scaling to reduce scale bias, Outlier Fallback recovers deadzone entries, but also partially reduces scale bias induced error, and Adaptive Quantization Noise (AQN) for controlling the policy entropy. On Qwen2.5-3B dense and Qwen3-30B-A3B-Base mixture-of-experts model, the targeted corrections recover BF16 accuracy to within 0.7% and 3.0% respectively.

</details>


### [200] [Spectral Souping: A Unified Framework for Online Preference Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20408)
*Yinlam Chow,Guy Tennenholtz,Ted Yun,James Harrison,Arthur Gretton,Andre Barreto,Bo Dai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对 RLHF 难以实现个性化对齐的问题，提出 Spectral Souping 框架，通过利用 LLM 的谱表示特性，在推理阶段进行模型或输出的快速合并，从而高效实现个性化偏好适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 RLHF 方法主要面向群体共识，难以满足不同个体用户多样化和冲突的偏好需求。

Method: Spectral Souping 框架。分为两个阶段：一是离线阶段，学习一组针对不同细颗粒度偏好维度的基础策略（basis of specialized policies）；二是在线阶段，通过算法在推理时对这些策略进行“配料”（soups）处理（参数合并或输出合并）。

Result: 在在线偏好对齐基准测试中，表现优于现有的先进（SOTA）方法，具有更高的计算效率和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的 Spectral Souping 框架证明了 LLM 内部存在一种通用的谱表示，通过离线学习基础策略并在推理时执行参数或输出层面的合并，能够实现高效、动态的个性化偏好对齐，无需昂贵的在线重新训练。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) effectively aligns Large Language Models (LLMs) with aggregate human preferences but often fails to address the diverse and conflicting needs of individual users. To overcome this issue, we introduce Spectral Souping, a unified framework for efficient, online preference alignment. Our contribution is the discovery of a universal spectral representation within LLMs, which is proven to be highly amenable to model merging. This theoretical insight enables a two-phase methodology: we first learn a basis of specialized policies offline, each focused on a distinct, fine-grained preference dimension. An online adaptation algorithm then efficiently ``soups'' these policies at inference time, either by merging their outputs or parameters, enabling rapid model adaptation without the need for costly online retraining w.r.t. tailored preference rewards. Experiments on online preference alignment benchmarks demonstrate that our method achieves significant performance improvements over existing state-of-the-art approaches, presenting a scalable and computationally efficient solution for dynamically adapting LLMs to individual user preferences.

</details>


### [201] [Supervised Latent Restructuring for Small-Data Quantum Learning in Plant Phenomics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20413)
*Alakananda Mitra,David H. Fleisher,Vangimalla Reddy,Chittaranjan Ray*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对高维生物小样本分类难题，本文提出一种由 LDA 重构引导的量子核对齐工作流，指出虽然重构能提升几何可分性，但过度压缩会导致非线性判别信息丢失，对量子机器学习的性能产生负面影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决高维生物数据在小样本环境下特征维度与样本量不匹配导致的分类困难，以及现有核方法在处理压缩后的表征时判别力下降的问题。

Method: 提出了一种混合工作流：将 1280 维深度图像嵌入压缩至 64 维 PCA 空间，再通过线性判别分析（LDA）重构为 11 维监督潜在空间，随后进行 GPU 加速的量子核对齐（QKA）实验。

Result: 监督式潜在重构显著提升了几何可分性（Silhouette 系数从 -0.006 提升至 0.197）；下游评估显示压缩存在权衡，线性模型性能提升，而非线性模型性能下降；QKA 在该受限 regime 下性能提升仍具挑战。

Conclusion: 在小样本高维生物数据情境下，仅通过表征几何学的改变不足以保证量子学习的强性能，激进的压缩会造成非线性辨别结构的丢失，强调了表征几何是小样本量子学习设计的关键变量。

Abstract: High-dimensional biological data often exhibit a severe mismatch between feature dimensionality and sample size, making reliable classification difficult in extremely small-data regimes. In these settings, kernel methods can lose discriminative power when latent compression fails to preserve class-separating structure. We study this problem in fine-grained plant phenomics and propose a hybrid workflow that compresses 1280-dimensional deep image embeddings into a 64-dimensional PCA space and then restructures them into an 11-dimensional supervised latent space using Linear Discriminant Analysis (LDA), followed by GPU-accelerated Quantum Kernel Alignment (QKA) on NVIDIA L40S hardware. Empirically, supervised latent restructuring substantially improves the geometric separability of the compressed representation, increasing the Silhouette coefficient from 0.003 in the raw embedding space and -0.006 in PCA-64 to 0.197 in the supervised LDA-11 space. However, downstream classical evaluation reveals a clear compression trade-off: Linear SVM and XGBoost improve in the restructured latent space, whereas RBF-SVM and Random Forest degrade under the same 11-dimensional bottleneck. Under a constrained optimization budget, QKA in this regime remains challenging, indicating that latent geometry alone is not sufficient for strong trainable quantum performance. These findings position representation geometry as a central design variable in small-data quantum learning and expose the practical difficulty of recovering nonlinear discriminative structure from aggressively compressed biological representations.

</details>


### [202] [Miller-Index-Based Latent Crystallographic Fracture Plane Reasoning with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20416)
*Qinwu Xu,Yifan Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了将结晶学米勒指数作为潜变量引入多模态大语言模型，证明了模型能利用该结构化表示进行断裂几何推理，并具备判断该表示是否适用于特定物理场景的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究多模态大语言模型（MLLM）是否能将结晶学平面指标（米勒指数）作为结构化潜表示，用于推理断裂几何结构。

Method: 将米勒指数 (Miller indices) 作为控制理想化平面断裂的潜变量 (Latent variable)，并评估模型的两项能力：(i) 潜变量推理（从视觉输入到平面假设的映射）；(ii) 潜变量适用性评估（判断该表示在特定图像中是否物理合理）。通过合成数据、2D-3D几何对照及跨多种材料（陶瓷、玻璃、金属、混凝土）的真实断裂图像进行实验验证。

Result: 实验证明，MLLM 能够在理想条件下可靠地进行潜推理，并能识别出当底层物理规律不支持该表示（即断裂非晶体学特征）时，拒绝使用该潜变量。

Conclusion: 多模态大语言模型（MLLM）在被赋予物理约束的潜变量先验后，能够作为具备物理感知能力的推理系统，前提是需要明确建模其适用边界。

Abstract: We study whether multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can leverage crystallographic plane indices (Miller indices) as a structured latent representation for reasoning about fracture geometry. We formulate Miller indices $z = (h,k,l)$ as a latent variable governing idealized planar fracture and evaluate two complementary capabilities: (i) latent inference, where the model maps visual observations to plane hypotheses under physically valid conditions, and (ii) latent applicability assessment, where the model determines whether such a representation is meaningful for a given fracture image.
  Through extensive experiments spanning synthetic data, controlled 2D--3D geometric pairs, and real-world fracture images across multiple material classes -- including ceramics, glass, metals, and concrete -- we show that MLLMs can reliably perform latent inference in idealized settings and, critically, can reject the latent representation when the underlying physics does not support it. These results suggest that MLLMs can act as physics-aware reasoning systems conditioned on structured latent priors, provided that the domain of validity is explicitly modeled.

</details>


### [203] [Design for Manufacturing: A Manufacturability Knowledge-Integrated Reinforcement Learning Framework for Free-Form Pipe Routing in Aeroengines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20644)
*Caicheng Wang,Zili Wang,Shuyou Zhang,Yongzhe Xiang,Zheyi Li,Liangyou Li,Jianrong Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对航空发动机管路设计与制造脱节问题，本文提出了集成制造知识的Frenet路径优化框架（FPRO），利用强化学习实现高精度路径规划，并支持自动化的自由弯曲加工，显著提升了设计制造效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航空发动机复杂零件的管路设计与制造过程脱节，导致人工试错成本高、周期长。急需一种能够将制造可行性知识集成到设计阶段的自动化优化方法。

Method: 提出基于Frenet标架的管路路由优化（FPRO）框架：1. 将路由问题转化为Frenet标架下的边值问题，利用三次Hermite插值表达管道路径；2. 将制造工艺限制（曲率、扭率）嵌入奖励函数；3. 采用近端策略优化（PPO）算法进行路径搜索；4. 构建统一映射公式，将路径直接转换为六轴自由弯管机的运动轨迹。

Result: 实验表明，FPRO在路径平滑度、终端对齐精度、路径长度及避障能力方面均优于主流的笛卡尔坐标系方法和现有强化学习基线；实际制造验证显示数字设计与实体管件高度吻合。

Conclusion: FPRO框架有效实现了航空发动机管路设计与制造环节的集成。通过将制造约束显式嵌入设计流程，该方法不仅提高了路径规划的效率和质量，还实现了从数字化设计到六轴自由弯曲加工的直接转化，验证了其在复杂航空零部件制造中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Design for manufacturing plays a critical role in advanced aeroengine development, where complex components necessitate careful consideration of manufacturability. However, current practices in pipe routing remain largely decoupled from down-stream manufacturing, leading to labor-intensive, trial-and-error iterations to achieve manufacturable designs. To address this problem, this study proposes the Frenet-based pipe routing optimization (FPRO) framework, a manufacturability knowledge-integrated reinforcement learning approach for free-form pipe design in aeroengines. FPRO formulates the routing problem as a boundary value problem in the Frenet frame. In this framework, the pipe path is represented by curvature and torsion profiles, which are generated using cubic Hermite interpolation. To integrate design and manufacturing, domain-specific manufacturing knowledge is embedded as constraints on the permissible ranges of curvature and torsion. The path optimization is performed using the proximal policy optimization algorithm with stochastic exploration and a stage-guided reward mechanism. A unified mapping formulation then translates the optimized path into motion trajectories for the bending die, enabling direct fabrication on a six-axis free-bending machine. Experimental results demonstrate that FPRO consistently generates collision-free, manufacturable paths with smoother geometric profiles compared to Cartesian-based methods. It also achieves faster convergence and superior performance in terminal alignment, path length, obstacle avoidance, and manufacturability compared to state-of-the-art reinforcement learning baselines. Real-world validation confirms the close geometric correspondence between the manufactured pipe and its digital design, validating the practical feasibility of FPRO.

</details>


### [204] [Can Conversational XAI Improve User Performance? An Experimental Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20439)
*Sven Kruschel,Julian Rosenberger,Lasse Bohlen,Mathias Kraus,Patrick Zschech*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文旨在探究对话式XAI对用户预测表现的提升效果。通过实验发现，虽然用户能利用XAI辅助超越模型，但对话式交互相比问答式交互并没有带来显著的性能差异，且用户参与度整体偏低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可解释AI（XAI）技术通常难以达到提升用户表现的预期。对话式XAI助理被认为是解决方案，但目前关于其对客观绩效影响的实证证据仍然不足。

Method: 提出了一套评估解释性系统（XAI）辅助效果的实验设计，包含三个评估维度：预测准确性、模型理解力和错误识别能力。实验使用“可解释设计”的预测模型，受试者（N=42）在识别并补偿模型系统性错误的情况下与模型进行对比。研究对比了“对话式AI辅助”与“问答式AI辅助”两种交互方式。

Result: 测试结果表明，两组受试者均显著优于模型，但在两种辅助交互方式之间未发现操作绩效差异，且受试者的整体参与度较为有限。

Conclusion: 当前实验设计初步显示，虽然用户可以通过模型辅助提高表现，但对话式交互（Conversational）与问答式（Q&A）交互在提升用户客观表现方面无显著差异。研究强调了未来需要更有效的用户参与机制（Engagement Interventions）和对决策机制的深入探究。

Abstract: Explainable AI (XAI) techniques aim to provide insights into predictive models and enhance user performance, yet they often fall short of these expectations. Conversational XAI assistants promise to overcome such limitations, but empirical evidence on their impact on objective performance measures remains limited. We propose an experimental design for evaluating explanation assistance through prediction accuracy, model understanding, and error identification. Using an explainable-by-design prediction model, we create conditions where users can outperform the model by identifying and compensating for systematic errors. We compare conversational assistance against Q&A-based assistance to assess which better supports users in working with model explanations. Preliminary results from testing our experimental design show that participants (N=42) in both treatments significantly outperformed the model but reveal no performance differences between assistance types and modest engagement overall. These findings inform refinements for our planned full study, including enhanced engagement interventions and investigation of the mechanisms driving improved predictions.

</details>


### [205] [Weight Decay Regimes in Grokking Transformers: Cheap Online Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20441)
*Lucky Verma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究发现权重衰减是控制Transformer在模运算中实现“Grokking”（泛化）的关键参数，并提出了通过观测注意力机制行为来低成本实时监控模型学习阶段的新方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探究Transformer模型在学习模运算时观察到的“Grokking”（突发性泛化）现象的动力学机制，特别是权重衰减如何影响模型在记忆与泛化之间的转变，并寻找计算成本更低的训练监控方法。

Method: 通过控制权重衰减（λ）作为主要变量，在11种实验条件及三种模型规模下观测Transformer的表现；提出两种基于注意力机制激发的在线诊断指标（均值成对注意力头余弦相似度、熵标准差）；利用逻辑斯谛回归和幂律拟合确定临界点；并在MLP、LSTM及Mamba架构上进行跨架构验证。

Result: 1. 确定了从记忆到泛化的临界点 λ_c ≈ 0.0158；2. 验证了权重衰减对学习阶段的强控制作用；3. 提出的训练动态监测指标（注意力诊断）能够有效替代高成本的损失景观分析；4. 证实了该现象在不同架构（MLP、LSTM、Mamba）中均存在。

Conclusion: 权重衰减（Weight decay）是调控Transformer在模运算任务中学习行为（记忆、grokking、崩溃）的关键控制参数。作者验证了该现象在不同模型规模及架构中的普适性，并提出了低成本的注意力机制在线评估指标。

Abstract: Transformers trained on modular arithmetic exhibit sharp transitions between memorization, generalization, and collapse. We show that weight decay acts as a scalar empirical control parameter for these regimes, and introduce two cheap online diagnostics, mean pairwise attention-head cosine similarity and entropy standard deviation, that track training dynamics from attention activations alone and complement loss-landscape diagnostics at lower compute cost. Across eleven experimental conditions and three model scales (0.82M to 85M parameters), the weight-decay axis separates memorization, developmental grokking, and collapse. A near-transition logistic fit localizes the memorization-to-developmental boundary at $λ_c=0.0158$ (95% CI [0.0109, 0.0200], N=210); a power-law fit gives an empirical exponent $ν=0.757$ (CI [0.725, 0.799]). Reference exponents $ν=1/2$ and 3D Ising $ν\approx 0.63$ lie outside this empirical CI under our four-bin grid, so we report $ν$ as empirical and defer universality-class identification to denser finite-size-scaling work. A horizon-matched multi-task replication (n=280, four modular operations) preserves the weight-decay control pattern; a paired attention-head re-initialization experiment at $λ=0.05$ changes Phase-2 amplitude (Cohen's $d=-1.190$, n=10, $p_t=4.5 \times 10^{-3}$), while matched weight-norm clipping does not. Three cross-architecture probes (4L MLP, 4L LSTM, and 4L Mamba; each n=70) replicate the weight-decay-controlled transition with architecture-specific $λ_c$ values. Main diagnostic claims are scoped to modular arithmetic in small transformer attention models; the non-attention experiments are scope probes, and architecture-wide, language-model, and universality-class claims are out of scope.

</details>


### [206] [LLM Pretraining Shapes a Generalizable Manifold: Insights into Cross-Modal Transfer to Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20449)
*Alexis Roger,Prateek Humane,Zhenghan Tai,Gwen Legate,Andrei Mircea,Vasilii Feofanov,Irina Rish*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了LLM迁移至时间序列预测的机制：预训练构建了通用的表征流形，微调则是将时间序列动力学投影至该流形上的低维对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言预训练模型（LLM）为何能够有效地用于时间序列预测，并分析其跨模态迁移的本质原因。

Method: 通过线性探测（Linear Probing）评估冻结的LLM状态，分析损失景观（Loss Landscape），并通过低秩更新分析（Low-rank updates）、子空间对齐（Subspace alignment）以及特征共享分析，探究微调过程中的权重变换与动力学特性。

Result: 研究发现，在大规模语言预训练后，模型无需配对监督即可解码真实的时间序列轨迹；预训练初始化通过构建高度各向异性的损失景观改善了优化过程；微调表现为一种低秩对齐，而非重新学习时间序列原始特征。

Conclusion: 语言模型的预训练过程构建了一个可重用的几何流形，微调则是将时间序列动力学映射到该流形中相关方向的低维对齐过程。此结论揭示了跨模态迁移的几何机理。

Abstract: Can language-pretrained transformers become effective time-series forecasters, and why? In this paper, we show that cross-modal transfer arises because language pretraining preconditions time series training with a reusable manifold. A linear probe on frozen LLM states decodes realistic time-series trajectories without paired supervision, and retrieval in this projected space yields competitive forecasts, showing that structure and dynamics exist before finetuning. Pretrained initialization also improves optimization, producing coherent gradients and a highly anisotropic loss landscape unlike random initialization. Finetuning then acts as low-dimensional alignment, reusing existing directions rather than learning temporal primitives from scratch, as evidenced by low-rank updates, subspace alignment, and shared features for periodicity, trend, and repetition. Together, these results support a geometric account of LLM-to-time-series transfer: language pretraining builds the manifold, and finetuning projects numerical dynamics onto task-relevant directions.

</details>


### [207] [Training Language Agents to Learn from Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20477)
*Yuval Shalev,Zifeng Ding,Mateja Jamnik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文引入了“上下文训练（ICT）”框架，通过强化学习训练语言智能体自动生成可复用的系统提示词，实现了跨任务的自我改进，并开源了MetaGym用于此类研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于反射（reflection）的方法仅局限于单个任务实例内的自我修正，尚未解决如何将单次任务经验转化为可复用的通用知识，以提升智能体在未来未见任务中表现的问题。

Method: 1. 提出“上下文训练（In-context Training, ICT）”框架，用于评估智能体的跨任务自我改进。
2. 设计了一个基于强化学习（RL）的训练流水线，通过观察角色模型（Actor）的轨迹，自动生成优化后的系统提示词（System Prompts），无需人工示例。
3. 构建了MetaGym库，用于创建元环境（Meta-environments）以支持此类研究。

Result: 在ALFWorld和MiniHack基准测试中，经过训练的反射模型在大多数未见过的任务族群中表现优于未训练的基线模型，且在某些情况下展现出跨越特定环境的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 研究表明语言智能体具备习得“经验总结”的能力，从而实现跨任务的自我提升。这种通过强化学习训练出的反射模型（Reflector）能够泛化到未见过的任务甚至不同的环境中，证明了从经验中学习的能力本身是可以被习得的。

Abstract: Language agents can adapt from experience in interactive environments, but current reflection-based methods can only self-correct within a single task instance. Whether such experience can be distilled into reusable lessons that improve performance on future unseen tasks remains unclear. We address this problem by introducing the In-context Training (ICT) task, a framework for evaluating cross-task self-improvement in language agents. In ICT, a reflector model observes trajectories collected by an actor model and generates system prompts intended to improve the actor's performance on future unseen tasks. We then propose an RL-based training pipeline for learning such reflections directly from experience, without human-provided examples. Across ALFWorld and MiniHack, our trained reflectors outperform an untrained baseline on most held-out task families, showing that the ability to learn from experience can itself be learned. In some cases, we observe generalisation beyond the benchmark on which the reflector was trained, to substantially different environments. Finally, we introduce MetaGym, a generic Python library for constructing meta-environments, enabling future research on self-improving language agents.

</details>


### [208] [ZEBRA: Zero-shot Budgeted Resource Allocation for LLM Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20485)
*May Hamri,Inbal Talgam-Cohen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ZEBRA 是一种零样本、推理时的多智能体预算分配框架，通过数学建模与算法求解，克服了大模型在多阶段任务中资源分配效率低下的问题，显著提升了受限预算下的任务执行质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法多侧重于单智能体步进控制或强化学习，缺乏针对多智能体流水线在推理时按阶段进行动态预算分配的通用方案。

Method: 提出 ZEBRA 框架，将多阶段预算分配建模为连续非线性背包问题：由 LLM 控制器估计各阶段效用曲线，通过基于拉格朗日乘子的注水算法（water-filling search）解出最优预算分配方案。

Result: 在 APPS 编码测试中，ZEBRA 在预算受限（为无约束消费的 50%）下达到 94.4% 的无约束质量，显著优于 LLM 直接分配（88.1%）；在 HotpotQA 测试中，ZEBRA 表现出针对不同任务结构（如任务平衡与侧重修正）的自适应分配能力。

Conclusion: 轻量级的推理时算法指导（如 ZEBRA）能够显著提升多智能体系统在有限预算下的经济决策效率和任务质量，通过数学建模实现比大模型直接分配更优的资源分配。

Abstract: As autonomous agents increasingly execute end-to-end tasks under fixed monetary budgets, the pressing open question shifts from whether the budget is respected, to how to spend it effectively. Existing budget-aware methods typically control reasoning step-by-step within a single agent, or learn resource allocation policies via RL. None address how to split a budget across the composing phases of a multi-agent pipeline at inference time. We propose ZEBRA, a zero-shot framework that reduces multi-phase budget allocation to a continuous nonlinear knapsack problem: an LLM controller estimates per-phase utility curves, and a water-filling search on the Lagrange multiplier returns the per-phase split. Additive and multiplicative aggregations are unified under the same solver. On a $150$-task APPS coding benchmark, both ZEBRA variants outperform LLM-direct (budget allocation directly by an LLM) on every aggregate metric. At a budget of $α= 0.5$ of the unconstrained spend, ZEBRA recovers $94.4\%$ of unconstrained quality, versus $88.1\%$ for LLM-direct. The advantage is statistically significant and transfers beyond coding: on a $3$-phase HotpotQA pipeline, ZEBRA beats LLM-direct by $14.3$pp, with allocations empirically robust to curve-estimation noise. On HotpotQA, ZEBRA arrives at a different budget split (near-balanced) compared to the APPS one (skewed towards a refinement phase), showing adaptation to the pipeline structure. More broadly, we show that lightweight algorithmic guidance at inference time can improve the economic behavior of autonomous multi-agent systems.

</details>


### [209] [Reinforcing Human Behavior Simulation via Verbal Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20506)
*Weiwei Sun,Xuhui Zhou,Jiarui Liu,Weihua Du,Haojia Sun,Yiqing Xie,Qianou Ma,Sihao Chen,Mengting Wan,Longqi Yang,Pei Zhou,Sherry Wu,Sean Welleck,Graham Neubig,Yiming Yang,Maarten Sap*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了DITTO框架，通过将口头反馈纳入强化学习（RL）流程来训练大模型模拟人类行为，并构建了SOUL基准测试集以评估模型在社交、角色扮演等复杂任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM反馈学习多局限于代码和数学等可直接验证的标量奖励场景，而模拟人类行为（如用户、患者等角色）需要处理更复杂、主观且多维的口头反馈，目前尚缺乏相关的训练范式和评估基准。

Method: 提出了DITTO模型：在强化学习循环中将口头反馈作为第一类信号，利用GRPO算法对模型输出及其改进后的反馈响应进行联合优化，从而实现反馈的策略蒸馏；同时构建了SOUL基准测试集，涵盖心理理论、角色扮演、社交技能等6大类的模拟任务。

Result: DITTO模型平均性能提升36%，在SOUL基准测试的10个任务中有6个超过GPT-4（原文注：GPT-5.4可能为文稿笔误，分析基于其表现优于强基线模型），证明了该方法的优越性。

Conclusion: 模型通过强化学习将口头反馈作为核心信号，证明了使大语言模型具备人类行为模拟能力的有效路径，超越了传统仅依赖标量奖励的优化范式。

Abstract: Humans learn social norms and behaviors from verbal feedback (e.g., a parent saying "that was rude" or a friend explaining "here's why that hurt"). Yet, learning from feedback for LLMs has largely focused on domains like code and math, where RL rewards are directly verifiable and condensed into scalar values. As LLMs are increasingly used to simulate human behavior, e.g., standing in for users, patients, students, and other personas, there is a pressing need to make them more human-like, which requires embracing a fundamentally different kind of signal: feedback that is verbal, subjective, and multi-faceted. We present DITTO, a model trained by treating verbal feedback as a first-class signal in reinforcement learning. After each rollout, DITTO receives verbal feedback and generates a feedback-conditioned improved rollout; both outputs are jointly optimized with GRPO, distilling verbal guidance into the base policy without requiring feedback at test time. We also introduce SOUL (Simulation gym Of hUman-Like behavior), a unified benchmark and training data suite spanning 10 tasks across six categories: Theory of Mind, character role play, social skill, learner simulation, user simulation, and persona simulation. DITTO achieves an average 36% improvement over the base model and exceeds GPT-5.4 on 6 of 10 SOUL benchmarks, demonstrating that RL with verbal feedback is a promising direction for training LLMs to simulate human behavior.

</details>


### [210] [Online Conformal Prediction with Corrupted Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20515)
*Bowen Wang,Matteo Zecchin,Osvaldo Simeone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对在线共形预测（OCP）在反馈数据受到污染（噪声或对抗干扰）时校准失效的问题，提出了基于过滤和主动补偿的两种鲁棒算法，有效保障了非理想环境下的不确定性估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI系统需在非平稳环境下获得可靠的校准不确定性估计。尽管在线共形预测（OCP）能提供长期误覆盖保证，但其严重依赖于“完美反馈”假设。在现实应用中，反馈数据常因噪声、通信故障或对抗攻击而失真，导致传统OCP校准失效。

Method: 1. 正式建模反馈污染为任意二元翻转序列；2. 分析了反馈污染对标准OCP误覆盖性能的影响；3. 设计“基于过滤的鲁棒OCP（利用阈值结构特征滤除噪声）”与“基于主动补偿的鲁棒OCP（引入补偿机制修正偏差）”两种方案；4. 对不同误差假设（独立随机翻转、有界记忆误差）推导了明确的误覆盖保证。

Result: 实验结果表明，在反馈受损的情况下，所提方法显著提升了模型校准的准确性，并保持了更小的预测区间，优于现有的基准OCP方法。

Conclusion: 本文提出的两种鲁棒在线共形预测（OCP）框架（过滤法和主动补偿法）有效地解决了反馈信息被污染时的校准性能下降问题，在存在噪声或对抗性干扰的场景中提供了更可靠的置信区间保证，且在性能上优于传统OCP方法。

Abstract: Modern artificial intelligence systems require calibrated uncertainty estimates that remain reliable in sequential and non-stationary environments. Online conformal prediction (OCP) addresses this challenge through adaptively updated prediction sets that provide deterministic long-run miscoverage guarantees. These guarantees, however, hinge on the assumption of perfect feedback about the coverage of past prediction sets. In practice, the observed miscoverage indicator may be corrupted by noise, communication failures, or adversarial manipulation, which can severely degrade OCP's calibration guarantees. In this paper, we study OCP under corrupted feedback. We first model feedback corruption as an arbitrary binary flip sequence, and analyze how feedback corruption affects and degrades the miscoverage performance of standard OCP. We then propose two robust schemes: robust OCP via filtering, which leverages the structural properties of the predicted threshold to filter corrupted feedback, and robust OCP via active compensation, which incorporates an active compensation mechanism to mitigate the effect of corrupted feedback. For both methods, we establish explicit miscoverage guarantees, which are further specialized for an independent stochastic flip model and for an arbitrary error model with memory bounds. Experiments on real-world datasets validate the proposed approach, showing markedly improved calibration and significantly smaller prediction sets compared with baseline OCP methods under corrupted feedback.

</details>


### [211] [An exponential mechanism based on quadratic approximations for fine-tuning machine learning models with privacy guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20521)
*Hoang Tran,Jorge Ramirez,Jiayi Wang,Alberto Bocchinfuso,Christopher Stanley,M. Paul Laiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于指数机制的差分隐私微调算法，通过多元正态分布抽样和随机投影策略，在保证隐私安全的前提下实现了高效、可扩展的模型微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调过程存在记忆敏感个体数据的风险，导致模型易受对抗攻击从而泄露隐私；需要一种既能保持微调效果又具备差分隐私保障的方法。

Method: 开发了一种基于指数机制的随机微调算法；构造了一个包含预训练模型二次近似信息与新数据集信息的效用函数；利用多元正态分布的闭式解进行精确采样；引入随机投影策略以处理高维模型。

Result: 在MNIST和MIMIC医疗数据集上的实验表明，该方法相比现有的差分隐私微调技术具有竞争力的性能，并成功实现了在大规模模型上的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于指数机制的差分隐私微调算法，通过理论证明和实验验证了其在保护数据隐私的同时，能够保持良好的模型性能，且具备较好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Fine-tuning adapts a pretrained machine learning model to a small, sensitive dataset, but this process risks memorizing individual new data points, making the model vulnerable to adversaries who seek to extract sensitive information. In this work, we develop a randomized algorithm based on the exponential mechanism for fine-tuning while ensuring differential privacy. Our key idea is to construct a simple utility function that combines a local quadratic approximation of the pretrained model with information from the new dataset. The resulting exponential mechanism admits exact sampling from a multivariate normal distribution in closed form. We establish theoretical privacy guarantees, sensitivity bounds, and accuracy estimations for our method. We further introduce a random-projection strategy that makes the approach scalable to high-dimensional models. Numerical experiments on the MNIST benchmark and the MIMIC clinical dataset demonstrate competitive performance against existing differentially private fine-tuning techniques.

</details>


### [212] [Ada2MS: A Hybrid Optimization Algorithm Based on Exponential Mixing of Elementwise and Global Second-Moment Estimates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20533)
*Meng Zhu,Quan Xiao,Weidong Min*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Ada2MS 是一种融合 AdamW 和动量 SGD 特性的新型优化算法，通过指数插值动态调整二阶矩估计策略，兼顾了算法的稳健性与泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的主流优化器中，AdamW 虽然稳健但泛化能力有时不及动量 SGD；而动量 SGD 虽然泛化性能好但对超参数高度敏感。为了融合两者优势，需要一种能结合两者特性的新算法。

Method: 提出了一种名为 Ada2MS 的优化算法，通过在逐元素（elementwise）二阶矩估计和全局（global）二阶矩估计之间进行连续指数插值，从而实现 AdamW 类行为与动量 SGD 类行为之间的平滑过渡。

Result: 在统一的优化器比较实验协议下，Ada2MS 在各类视觉任务中均表现出比肩（甚至优于）现有主流优化器的竞争性结果。

Conclusion: Ada2MS 有效平衡了 AdamW 的稳健性和动量 SGD 的泛化能力，在视觉任务中表现出极强的竞争力，是应对复杂训练场景的优选优化算法。

Abstract: Optimization algorithms are core methods by which machine learning models iteratively minimize loss functions, update parameters, learn from data, and improve performance. Momentum SGD and AdamW represent two important optimization paradigms. AdamW produces stable updates and usually has strong robustness across training scenarios, but its generalization performance is sometimes weaker than that of momentum methods. Momentum SGD can often obtain better generalization after careful tuning, but it is more sensitive to gradient-scale variation and hyperparameter settings. To balance the strengths and weaknesses of the two paradigms, this paper proposes Ada2MS, an optimization algorithm that achieves a smooth transition between AdamW-like behavior and momentum-SGD-like behavior through continuous exponential interpolation between elementwise second-moment estimates and global second-moment estimates. On the visual tasks evaluated in this study, Ada2MS obtains competitive results under a unified optimizer-comparison protocol. The code will be released at https://github.com/mengzhu0308/Ada2MS

</details>


### [213] [OpenSeisML: Open Large-Scale Real Seismic and well-log Dataset for Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20539)
*Ipsita Bhar,Huseyin Tuna Erdinc,Thales Souza,Charles Jones,Felix J. Herrmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 为了解决地震反演中高质量数据集匮乏的问题，本文推出了 OpenSeisML 数据集及自动化构建流程，旨在支持生成式 AI 学习地下属性分布，从而改进 seismic inversion 并提升不确定性量化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器学习地震反演研究受限于高质量真实速度模型数据的匮乏，且大多数优质数据为私有资源。

Method: 构建了名为 OpenSeisML 的数据集，利用 UK National Data Repository (NDR) 的公开数据，设计了自动化数据处理管道，通过测井数据（checkshot）进行时深转换，并使用插值法构建速度模型。

Result: 提供了一套可复现、标准化的地震数据集，能够用于训练捕捉地下属性统计分布的生成式 AI 模型，进而实现地震反演中的不确定性量化。

Conclusion: OpenSeisML 填补了高性能地震数据集的空白，通过自动化流程实现了数据的高质量准备，为生成式 AI 在地震反演和不确定性量化中的应用提供了标准化支持。

Abstract: The advent of machine learning (ML) and computer vision has significantly accelerated seismic inversion workflows by reducing the computational cost of traditionally expensive iterative methods. However, the development and evaluation of ML methods remain limited by the scarcity of realistic velocity models, as most high-quality data are privately owned by oil and gas companies. To address this gap, we present OpenSeisML, a collection of real seismic datasets designed to support generative AI (Gen-AI) workflows for seismic inversion. The datasets are curated from publicly available surveys in the UK National Data Repository (NDR). When seismic volumes are in the time domain and wells are in depth, a time-to-depth conversion is required. We use checkshot data to establish the time-depth relationship and construct a velocity model through interpolation for accurate conversion of post-stack seismic data. Here, we present an automated data curation pipeline that enables seismic data preparation while ensuring reproducibility. The objective is to train a generative model that captures the statistical distribution of subsurface properties, enabling the synthesis of multiple statistically consistent realizations for uncertainty quantification which can act as a prior for seismic inversion.

</details>


### [214] [Complementing reinforcement learning with SFT through logit averaging in the post training of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20555)
*Xingwei Gan,Ying Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种将SFT策略与训练中策略的logits进行平均的无KL/Critic的GRPO优化方法，在保持模型格式优点的同时提升了推理任务的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于强化学习的推理模型优化（如RLVR）通常依赖于KL正则化和Critic模型，这增加了训练复杂度和计算开销。研究旨在寻找一种更简化的方式，既能发挥可训练策略的推理能力，又能保持SFT的格式规范性。

Method: 提出了一种将冻结的参考策略（如SFT）与可训练策略的逻辑对数（logit）进行平均的新方法，并将其集成到GRPO框架中。该方法取消了常见的KL正则化和Critic（价值函数）组件。

Result: 在MATH、cn-k12和MMLU数据集上的评估表明，该方法在准确率上优于或至少持平于标准的带KL正则化的GRPO。

Conclusion: 通过逻辑对数（logit）平均耦合策略，无需KL正则化或价值函数（Critic）即可有效提升推理模型的性能，在保持SFT格式优势的同时，实现了与传统KL正则化GRPO相当甚至更优的效果。

Abstract: We introduce a novel method that averages the logits of a frozen reference policy (e.g., SFT) and a trainable policy, and incorporate the method into Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). In contrast to Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) methods, our proposal does not involve a Kullback Leibler (KL) regularization or critic; the trainable policy and the reference anchor are coupled through the logit averaging structure to leverage the reasoning expertise of the trainable policy while maintaining the formatting advantage of SFT. Our method is evaluated on MATH, cn-k12, and MMLU, and the results show a higher accuracy or at least comparable accuracy relative to the canonical KL-regularized GRPO.

</details>
