### [215] [TriForces: Augmenting Atomistic GNNs for Transferable Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20581)
*Ali Ramlaoui,Alexandre Duval,Hannah Bull,Victor Schmidt,Hugues Talbot,Fragkiskos D. Malliaros,Joseph Musielewicz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TriForces 是一种针对机器学习原子间势函数（MLIPs）的模型无关三流框架，通过解耦成分与结构信息并结合自监督学习，极大提高了模型在小样本环境下的迁移性能和预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 MLIPs 虽然在大规模数据集上表现优异，但在处理特定任务的小型数据集时，往往存在跨域迁移能力不足及结构/成分信息丢失的问题。

Method: 提出了一种名为 TriForces 的模型无关三流（three-stream）框架；将化学成分信息与结构信息进行分离，并引入自监督学习（Self-Supervised Learning）以增强表示的迁移能力。

Result: 在 MatBench 和 QM9 数据集上表现优于基准方法；在 OMat24 数据集上，即便只有 2 万个样本，能量预测平均绝对误差（MAE）降低了 57%，且在多种样本规模下均提升了力（force）的预测精度。

Conclusion: TriForces 通过分离成分与结构表示并结合自监督学习，有效解决了机器学习原子间势函数（MLIPs）在不同领域间迁移性差的问题，显著提升了在小样本数据下的预测性能。

Abstract: Machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) achieve excellent accuracy when trained on large Density Functional Theory (DFT) data. To be useful in practice, they must often be adapted to target chemistries using small and expensive task-specific datasets. However, MLIPs transfer inconsistently across domains, with representations that often loose accessible composition and structure information. To address this, we present TriForces, a model-agnostic three-stream framework that separates composition and structure information, combined with self-supervised learning to preserve transferable representations. TriForces improves performance on MatBench and QM9 over baselines without needing DFT labels and enables efficient similar structure retrieval through its learned latent space. On OMat24, in limited-data training regime, TriForces reduces energy MAE by 57% at 20K samples only and improves force MAE across sample sizes. We release pretrained TriForces variants across multiple MLIP architectures with code at https://github.com/Ramlaoui/triforces.

</details>


### [216] [ReversedQ: Opportunities for Faster Q-Learning in Episodic Online Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20592)
*Sofia R. Miskala-Dinc,Aviva Prins*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对模型无关 Q-learning，本文提出了 ReversedQ 算法，通过改变价值函数更新顺序、频率及初始化策略，消除了对延迟学习的依赖，显著加速了强化学习在复杂环境中的收敛并提高了绩效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的模型无关后验采样算法在证明理论界限时过度依赖“延迟学习（delayed learning）”，阻碍了样本效率的提升。研究旨在识别并利用优化空间以实现更快速的学习。

Method: 基于 Wang 等人的 RandomizedQ，提出了三种改进机制：优化价值函数更新顺序、调整更新频率以及改良价值函数初始化策略。

Result: 在 Bidirectional Diabolical Combination Lock (BDCL) 和 Chain MDP 测试中，ReversedQ 的归一化平均累积奖励分别由 9.53% 提升至 78.78%，以及由 21.76% 提升至 61.81%。

Conclusion: 通过对随机Q学习算法进行策略性修改，ReversedQ 在不依赖延迟学习的前提下，显著提升了模型在复杂探索环境下的训练效率与累积奖励。

Abstract: We study model-free Q-learning in finite-horizon episodic Markov Decision Processes (MDPs) with stationary dynamics across episodes. We identify a central issue in nascent model-free posterior-sampling works: the reliance on delayed learning in order to prove theoretical guarantees. In particular, we identify three opportunities for faster learning - (i) value-function update order, (ii) update frequencies, and (iii) value-function initialization. Using Wang et al.'s RandomizedQ as a basis, we illustrate these changes and their individual (as well as cumulative) impact in multiple empirical studies. We find that our combined modifications, termed ReversedQ, improve scaled mean cumulative reward compared to RandomizedQ, from 9.53% to 78.78% in the Bidirectional Diabolical Combination Lock (BDCL), and from 21.76% to 61.81% in a chain MDP.

</details>


### [217] [Compositional Transduction with Latent Analogies for Offline Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20609)
*Junseok Kim,Dohyeong Kim,Mineui Hong,Songhwai Oh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对离线目标条件强化学习中泛化能力不足的问题，本文提出了一种基于类比转导的新方法，通过学习任务内生类比表示，实现了在未知环境下的组合规划与泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的离线目标条件强化学习（GCRL）方法主要依赖于时间上连续的轨迹拼接，难以在不同上下文之间有效地组合行为以实现泛化。

Method: 提出了一种新的类比表示（Analogy Representation），该表示能够在任务执行过程中通过区分上下文不变特征和任务内生特征来实现规划合成；同时引入了针对未见过的类比-上下文组合的转导算法。

Result: 在 OGBench 操作环境中的实验表明，该方法在处理未见过的上下文组合方面显著优于现有的离线 GCRL 算法。

Conclusion: 通过在离线目标条件强化学习中引入类比转导，该方法有效地实现了在未见过的上下文中的组合泛化，克服了传统轨迹拼接方法的局限性。

Abstract: Compositional generalization is essential for reaching unseen goals under novel contextual variations in offline goal-conditioned reinforcement learning (GCRL), where a generalist goal-reaching agent must be learned from limited data. Most prior approaches pursue this via trajectory stitching over temporally contiguous segments, which limits composing behaviors across varying contexts. To overcome this limitation, we formalize analogy transduction as synthesizing new plans by composing task-endogenous analogies with given contexts and propose a novel analogy representation tailored for it. Grounded in our theory, this analogy representation captures what changes under optimal task execution, remains invariant to contextual variations, and is sufficient for optimal goal reaching. We further contend that generalization to unseen analogy-context pairs is a practical obstacle in analogy transduction, and introduce a new approach for offline GCRL that enables analogy transduction beyond seen pairs to unseen combinations. We empirically demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach on OGBench manipulation environments, substantially outperforming prior methods that do not perform analogy transduction. Project page: https://rllab-snu.github.io/projects/CTA/

</details>


### [218] [Matryoshka Concept Bottleneck Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20612)
*Ziye Chen,Hongbin Lin,Xinyue Xu,Jie Li,Lijie Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MCBM将概念嵌套分层，实现了单模型下的按需自适应概念干预，大幅降低了专家介入成本，告别了为不同预算训练多个模型的低效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的概念瓶颈模型（CBM）面临两难：要么需要穷尽式人工干预导致成本高昂，要么需要维护多个模型导致计算负担大，缺乏在不同预算下灵活调整的能力。

Method: 提出“俄罗斯套娃概念瓶颈模型”（Matryoshka Concept Bottleneck Model, MCBM），通过受俄罗斯套娃表征学习（Matryoshka Representation Learning）启发的方法，将概念按相关性最大化和冗余最小化原则组织成嵌套层次结构。

Result: 理论上将预期干预成本从线性 O(K) 降低至对数级 O(log K)，同时保证单调性能提升；实验证明其效果匹配独立训练模型，且具备动态适应性。

Conclusion: MCBM通过嵌套概念层次结构实现了在单一模型中进行自适应的概念利用，在保持模型性能的同时，显著降低了专家干预的成本并消除了维护多个独立模型的负担。

Abstract: Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) have emerged as a prominent paradigm for interpretable deep learning, learning by grounding predictions in human-understandable concepts. However, their practical deployment is hindered by the high cost of test-time intervention, as correcting model errors typically requires human experts to manually inspect and verify a large set of predicted concepts. Existing approaches suffer from a fundamental structural limitation: they either adopt a single static concept set, forcing experts to exhaustively annotate concepts and incurring prohibitive intervention costs, or train multiple models tailored to different concept budgets, resulting in substantial computational and maintenance overhead. To address this challenge, we propose the Matryoshka Concept Bottleneck Model (MCBM), a unified architecture that enables adaptive concept utilization within a single model. Inspired by Matryoshka Representation Learning, MCBM organizes concepts into a nested hierarchy based on maximum relevance and minimum redundancy, allowing inference at multiple levels of conceptual granularity without retraining. Theoretically, we show that MCBM reduces the expected intervention costs from linear to logarithmic order, $O(\log K)$, while guaranteeing monotonic performance improvement. Empirically, extensive experiments demonstrate that MCBM matches the performance of independently trained models while enabling dynamic and efficient expert interaction.

</details>


### [219] [Same Target, Different Basins: Hard vs. Soft Labels for Annotator Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20642)
*Mirerfan Gheibi,Gashin Ghazizadeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统对比了两种硬标签处理方法（Multipass 和 SLS）与软标签训练的效果，发现硬标签传递在低标注密度下表现更优，并能提升模型泛化性和 OOD 检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标注者的意见分歧往往反映了认知不确定性，而非简单的标签噪声。研究旨在解决如何有效利用这些带有不确定性的标注数据，寻找优于传统软标签合并处理或直接使用经验软标签分布的方法。

Method: 主要探讨了两种硬标签传递方法：Multipass（在固定数据集大小下循环遍历观察到的投票）和随机标签采样（SLS，在每个 epoch 开始时为每个样本采样一个标签）。并将其与传统的软标签分布训练进行对比。

Result: 在 CIFAR-10H 上，当每个样本的标注数量很少时，硬标签传递优于软标签训练，特别是当稀疏经验分布与完整标注分布差距较大时；当拥有完整分布时，两者效果持平。此外，实验表明硬标签传递能导向更平坦的损失函数盆地（Loss Basins），在 SVHN 和 CIFAR-100 上表现出更好的 OOD 检测能力。

Conclusion: Multipass 是处理原始投票数据的强力默认选择，而 SLS 在标注稀疏时具有竞争力，且在全标注分布下与软标签训练效果相当。硬标签传递往往能引导模型收敛到更平坦的损失盆地，从而提升泛化能力与OOD检测表现。

Abstract: When annotators disagree, that disagreement can reflect epistemic uncertainty rather than simple label noise. We study hard-label delivery as an alternative to the usual choices of collapsing votes to a single label or training directly on the empirical soft-label distribution. We focus on two primary hard-label methods: multipass, which cycles through observed votes while keeping the dataset size fixed, and stochastic label sampling (SLS), which samples one label per example at the start of each epoch. On CIFAR-10H, we find that when only a small number of annotations per example is available, hard-label delivery improves over soft-label training, with larger improvements where the sparse empirical target is farther from the full annotator distribution. When full annotator distributions are available, both hard-label methods match soft-label training. We use deterministic control as an ablation of multipass and shuffled SLS as a control that breaks the example-to-distribution match. We also show that SLS and soft-label cross-entropy optimize the same expected objective. Hard-label delivery also converges to flatter basins, with supporting descriptive evidence from OOD detection on SVHN and CIFAR-100. Overall, these results suggest that multipass is a strong practical default when raw vote counts are available, while SLS offers a lightweight alternative that remains competitive when only a few votes per example are available and matches soft-label training when full annotator distributions are available.

</details>


### [220] [AVSD: Adaptive-View Self-Distillation by Balancing Consensus and Teacher-Specific Privileged Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20643)
*Duy Nguyen,Hanqi Xiao,Archiki Prasad,Zaid Khan,Anirban Das,Austin Zhang,Sambit Sahu,Hyunji Lee,Elias Stengel-Eskin,Mohit Bansal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对大模型自蒸馏中特权信息利用不均衡与视图选择难的问题，提出 AVSD 方法，通过综合多视图共识与自适应残差，显著提升模型在推理与代码任务上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自蒸馏中， teacher 依赖的特权信息在推理时 student 不可获取导致不对称性，且单一特权视图的选择具有任务依赖性，难以通用。

Method: 提出自适应视图自蒸馏（AVSD）框架：1. 引入多视图特权信息；2. 通过分离跨视图共识信号（稳定监督）与视图特定残差信号（动态调整量），实现更鲁棒的令牌级监督学习。

Result: 在 AIME 和 HMMT 数学竞赛基准上，AVSD 较强基线平均提升 2.2%-3.1%；在代码生成基准上较强基线提升 2.4%。

Conclusion: AVSD 通过有效利用跨视图共识和自适应调整视图特定残差，解决了自蒸馏中信息不对称与视图选择困难问题，在数学推理和代码生成任务上表现优于现有的单视图自蒸馏方法和 GRPO。

Abstract: Self-distillation enables language models to learn on-policy from their own trajectories by using the same model as both student and teacher, with the teacher being conditioned on privileged information unavailable to the student. Such information can come in different types or views, such as solutions, demonstrations, feedback, or final answers. This setup provides dense token-level feedback without relying on a separate external model, but creates a fundamental asymmetry: the teacher may rely on view-specific information that the student cannot access at inference time. Moreover, the best type of privileged information is often task-dependent, making it difficult to choose a single teacher view. In this work, we address both these challenges jointly by introducing AVSD (Adaptive-View Self-Distillation), a novel method of self-distillation with multiple privileged-information views, which reconstructs token-level supervision by separating stable cross-view consensus from view-specific residual signals. AVSD identifies the consensus signal shared across views, which provides a reliable update direction, and then selectively adds the view-specific residual signal to adjust the update magnitude when it both aligns with the consensus direction and remains proportionate to the consensus signal. Experiments on math competition benchmarks (AIME24, AIME25, and HMMT25) show that AVSD consistently outperforms both single-view self-distillation baselines and GRPO, achieving average Avg@8 gains of 3.1% and 2.2% over the strongest baselines on Qwen3-8B and Qwen3-4B, respectively. Moreover, on code-generation benchmarks (Codeforces, LiveCodeBench v6) using Qwen3-8B, AVSD outperforms the single-view self-distillation baseline by 2.4% on average.

</details>


### [221] [LT2: Linear-Time Looped Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20670)
*Chunyuan Deng,Yizhe Zhang,Rui-Jie Zhu,Yuanyuan Xu,Jiarui Liu,T. S. Eugene Ng,Hanjie Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 LT2（线性时间循环 Transformer），通过将循环机制中的二次复杂度注意力替换为线性或稀疏注意力，显著提升了循环 Transformer 的推理效率与能力，实现了高效、可扩展的小型语言模型设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的 Looped Transformers（LT）虽然性能强大，但由于依赖全注意力机制（Full Attention），具有二次复杂度，导致计算昂贵且推理缓慢，限制了其大规模应用。

Method: 1. 提出 LT2 系列模型，使用线性注意力（LT2-linear）或稀疏注意力（LT2-sparse）替代标准二次 softmax 注意力。
2. 设计 LT2-hybrid 变体，将不同注意力机制（线性、稀疏、全注意力）在循环中交替使用。
3. 提出一种将预训练的传统 LT 模型转化为 LT2-hybrid 模型的方法。

Result: 1. LT2 在控制召回、状态追踪和语言建模等任务中表现出一致的经验增益。
2. LT2-hybrid (GDN+DSA) 以线性时间成本达到了标准 LT 的质量。
3. LT2-hybrid (Full+GDN) 在性能和效率上均超越了标准 LT。
4. 模型效果：Ouro-hybrid-1.4B 在 1B token 训练下，超越了一般 1B 模型，性能媲美 4B 模型。

Conclusion: LT2 架构通过在循环机制中引入线性/稀疏注意力，成功解决了 Looped Transformers (LT) 的二次复杂度问题，实现了在保持高效（线性时间复杂度）的同时，性能媲美甚至超越标准 LT 和同等规模模型，为构建高效、高性能的小型语言模型提供了明确路径。

Abstract: Looped Transformers (LT) have emerged as a powerful architecture by iterating their layers multiple times before decoding the final token. However, pairing them with full attention retains quadratic complexity, making them computationally expensive and slow. We introduce LT2 (Linear-Time Looped Transformers), a family of looped architectures that replace quadratic softmax attention with subquadratic, linear-time attention. We study two variants: LT2-linear with linear attention and LT2-sparse with sparse attention. We find that looping uniquely synergizes with these variants: it enables iterative memory refinement in linear attention and progressively expands the effective receptive field in sparse attention. We formalize these benefits theoretically and demonstrate consistent empirical gains across controlled recall, state-tracking, and language modeling tasks. We then explore LT2-hybrid, which combines different attention variants in a looped setting. Two variants are especially promising: LT2-hybrid (GDN+DSA), which interleaves linear and sparse attention to maximize efficiency and matches the standard looped transformer's quality at fully linear-time cost; and LT2-hybrid (Full+GDN), which interleaves GDN with a small fraction of full attention layers to maximize quality, surpassing the standard looped transformer in both performance and efficiency. We also show how to convert a pre-trained LT into an LT2-hybrid model. With about 1B tokens of training, our converted model, Ouro-hybrid-1.4B, outperforms industry-level 1B models and is competitive with industry-level 4B models while retaining the speed benefits of linear-time attention. Together, these results show a clear path toward making looped transformers more scalable and advancing efficient, capable small language models.

</details>


### [222] [Dynamic TMoE: A Drift-Aware Dynamic Mixture of Experts Framework for Non-Stationary Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20678)
*Jiawen Zhu,Shuhan Liu,Di Weng,Yingcai Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对非平稳时间序列预测，Dynamic TMoE 提出了一种具有动态演化专家池和时序记忆路由的架构，显著提升了模型对突发分布偏移的适应性与预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的混合专家（MoE）模型在非平稳时间序列预测中存在专家池固定、路由无记忆的问题，难以应对突发的分布偏移（regime shifts）。

Method: 提出 Dynamic TMoE 框架：1) 基于最大均值差异（MMD）检测分布偏移，动态实例化及剪枝专家；2) 引入带有循环状态与异常存储库的“时序记忆路由”（Temporal Memory Router），实现上下文感知的专家选择，无需测试阶段更新。

Result: 在九项基准测试中达到了 SOTA 性能，MSE 指标平均降低 10.4%，MAE 指标平均降低 7.8%。

Conclusion: Dynamic TMoE 通过融合架构演化与时序记忆路由，有效克服了传统 MoE 模型在处理非平稳时间序列时专家池固化与路由无记忆的问题，为动态多变的环境提供了更优的预测方案。

Abstract: Non-stationary time series forecasting is challenged by evolving distribution shifts that static models struggle to capture. While Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures offer a promising paradigm for decoupling complex drift patterns, existing approaches are limited by fixed expert pools and memoryless routing, hampering their ability to adapt to abrupt regime shifts. To address this, we propose Dynamic TMoE, a framework that unifies architectural evolution with temporal continuity during learning phase. By detecting distribution shifts via Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD), we dynamically instantiate heterogeneous experts and prune redundant ones to optimize capacity. Additionally, a temporal memory router leverages recurrent states and an anomaly repository to ensure stable, context-aware expert selection without requiring test-time updates. Experiments on nine benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, reducing MSE by 10.4% and MAE by 7.8%. Code is available at https://github.com/andone-07/Dynamic-TMoE.

</details>


### [223] [Distributed Direct Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20696)
*Zhanhong Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了针对分布式配置下直接偏好优化（DPO）的首个收敛性与复杂度分析框架，解决了联邦与去中心化学习中通信限制和偏好异质性带来的优化挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于偏好反馈的强化学习（RL）在对齐人类意图方面至关重要，但在偏好数据碎片化、异质性强且受通信约束的分布式（联邦/去中心化）场景下，主流的DPO（直接偏好优化）算法缺乏相应的理论收敛保证。

Method: 1. 在建模个性化离线RL的基础上，刻画了用户特定偏好分布下的全局优化景观。2. 针对联邦DPO，推导了包含客户端漂移（client drift）、通信频率及偏好异质性影响的收敛速率。3. 针对去中心化DPO，建立了基于一般通信图的收敛理论，分析了图谱连通性对优化速度与一致性的影响。

Result: 理论上推导出了分布式DPO的收敛界；实证方面在标准对齐基准测试中验证了算法的表现，证明了其在分布式环境下不仅具备理论保证，且具有强大的稳健性和扩展性。

Conclusion: 本文首次为分布式环境下的DPO提供了完备的理论收敛与时间复杂度分析，证明了其在联邦学习和去中心化学习设置下的有效性，并为处理用户偏好异质性提供了理论基础与实践指导。

Abstract: Preference-based reinforcement learning (RL) is a key paradigm for aligning policies with human judgments, yet its theoretical behavior in distributed settings where preference data are fragmented across heterogeneous users remains poorly understood. Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) avoids explicit reward modeling but lacks convergence guarantees under federated and decentralized training, where communication constraints and non-IID preferences fundamentally alter optimization dynamics. We provide the first convergence and time-complexity analysis of DPO in distributed environments. Modeling personalized offline RL with user-specific preference distributions, we characterize the induced global optimization landscape. For federated DPO, we derive convergence rates that quantify the impact of client drift, communication frequency, and preference heterogeneity; for decentralized DPO, we establish convergence over general communication graphs and show how spectral connectivity governs optimization speed and consensus. Empirically, we corroborate our theoretical insights on standard alignment benchmarks, demonstrating that our proposed methods not only enjoy strong theoretical guarantees but also deliver robust and scalable performance in practice. The code base is available here.

</details>


### [224] [Memory-Efficient Partitioned DNN Inference on Resource-Constrained Android Crowds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20723)
*Lakshani Manamperi,Disumi Pathirana,Thiwanka Pathirana,Nipun Premarathna,Kutila Gunasekera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CROWDio通过一种分布式流水线调度系统，将大型Transformer模型切分并在多台Android设备间协同推理，有效解决了移动设备内存受限的问题，并实现了优于传统同步机制的低延迟表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的模型压缩、剪枝和量化技术仍无法使大型Transformer模型适配移动设备（3.3-7.4 GB RAM）的内存限制。

Method: 引入了名为CROWDio的DNN流水线调度子系统，通过五个核心机制实现分布式推理：JIT延迟分区加载、单分区驻留约束、四层亲和力调度器、zlib压缩张量传输以及流式1:1依赖模型。

Result: 在5台Android手机上运行DistilBERT模型测试显示，单设备峰值内存占用（RSS）限制在43±2 MB，单次运行功耗为50±3 mAh，且流式并发处理使批处理延迟比基准同步降低了34%。

Conclusion: CROWDio成功证明在不修改模型的前提下，通过分布式推理机制可将大型Transformer模型部署在内存受限的移动设备上，在保持低内存占用与低功耗的同时显著降低了延迟。

Abstract: Deploying large deep neural networks on memory-constrained mobile devices is a central challenge in edge ML. While compression, pruning, and quantization reduce per-parameter cost, transformer-based models remain too large for the 3.3-7.4 GB RAM envelope of commodity Android handsets. We present the DNN pipeline scheduling subsystem of CROWDio, which achieves practical ONNX inference across resource-constrained Android workers without model modification, by distributing memory pressure across devices via five mechanisms: JIT deferred partition loading, a single-partition-resident constraint, a 4-tier affinity scheduler, a zlib-compressed tensor transport, and a streaming 1:1 dependency model. Evaluated on DistilBERT (Sanh et al., 2019) (approximately 67 M parameters, SST-2) across five Android handsets over ten runs, our system holds peak per-device RSS to 43+-2 MB and limits battery draw to 50+-3 mAh per run, while streaming concurrency cuts batch latency 34% below barrier synchronisation.

</details>


### [225] [The Hidden Signal of Verifier Strictness: Controlling and Improving Step-Wise Verification via Selective Latent Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20745)
*Yefan Zhou,Yilun Zhou,Austin Xu,Soroush Vosoughi,Shafiq Joty,Jiang Gui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 VerifySteer，通过发现验证器隐藏状态中关于决策严格度的信号，在不进行微调的前提下动态调节验证器的严格程度，有效平衡了错误检测与正确性验证，显著提高了验证效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式验证器存在“验证严格度偏差（Verifier Strictness）”问题（即过宽容或过严苛），导致推理步骤的验证不准确，且现有的直接干预方法容易出现错误检测与正确性判定之间的性能权衡。

Method: 1. 发现验证器严格程度的信号：验证决策被编码在验证段落边界的隐藏状态中。
2. 提出 VerifySteer：一种非微调的隐藏状态干预方法，通过潜在正确性信号进行样本级路由，并进行选择性干预，从而缓解错误检测与正确性认证之间的权衡。

Result: 在 ProcessBench 和 Hard2Verify 实验中，VerifySteer 表现优于提示词优化和激活值转向基线，且在推理计算量减少 4-7 倍的情况下达到与自洽性（Self-consistency）相当的性能，并能与微调后的验证器协同工作。

Conclusion: VerifySteer 通过调节隐藏状态有效地控制了生成式验证器的严格程度（Strictness），在保持高推理效率的同时，显著提升了验证准确性，并具有良好的通用性和互补性。

Abstract: Generative verifiers have emerged as a promising paradigm for step-wise verification, but their verification behavior is often poorly calibrated: they may be under-critical and miss erroneous steps, or over-critical and reject correct reasoning. We refer to this tendency to be overly lenient or overly critical as verifier strictness. In this work, we study whether verifier strictness can be controlled through hidden-state intervention. We uncover a verification-specific hidden-state signal: in step-wise verification, a verifier's tendency to accept or reject a solution step is encoded near the boundary of the corresponding verification paragraph. Exploiting this signal, we show that hidden-state steering can directly modulate verifier strictness without fine-tuning. However, uniform steering induces a trade-off between error detection and correctness certification. To address this, we propose VerifySteer, which exploits latent correctness signals for sample-level routing and selectively intervenes on paragraph boundaries. Experiments on ProcessBench and Hard2Verify show that VerifySteer outperforms prompt optimization and activation steering baselines, and is competitive with self-consistency while requiring 4-7x less inference compute. VerifySteer is also complementary to verification fine-tuning, providing further gains on top of fine-tuned verifiers. The code is available at https://github.com/YefanZhou/VerifySteer.

</details>


### [226] [Most Transformer Modifications Still Do Not Transfer at 1-3B: A 2020-2026 Update to Narang et al. (2021) with Downstream Evaluation and a Noise Floor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20798)
*Yang Zhao,Jiahao Lu,Bin Huang,Guhua Zhang,Jie Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重评估了20种新型Transformer架构修改，发现大多数修改无法在1-3B参数规模下带来实际性能提升，强调了严格的科学控制方法（噪声底线、下游评估、稳定性测试）对于架构研究的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去五年Transformer架构演进迅速，参数规模移至1-3B，评估方式从预训练困惑度转向下游任务。研究旨在重新评估2021年后提出的众多Transformer修改方案在当前主流规模下是否真的有效。

Method: 在1.2B和3B参数量下，严格控制数据量、计算量和训练配方（iso-data, iso-compute, iso-recipe），测试了20种2021年后的Transformer修改方案。使用多随机种子设定噪声底线，并采用CLIMB-12下游评测基准作为主要指标。

Result: 测试的20种修改中，仅有2种在1.2B规模下表现显著，且其中1种在3B规模下训练不稳定。研究还发现“损失函数与下游任务表现脱节”现象在注意力输出层修改中尤为严重，部分模型验证集损失仅与基线相差2-3%，但下游表现大幅下降。

Conclusion: 在大规模（1-3B参数）实验中，大多数Transformer修改方案无法实现性能迁移。除了噪声水平报告外，下游任务评估和跨尺度稳定性测试是架构评估的必要前提，且仅靠损失函数指标（loss）往往无法反映下游任务的实际表现。

Abstract: Narang et al. (2021) evaluated 40+ Transformer modifications at T5-base scale and concluded that most did not transfer. Five years later, the typical working regime has moved to 1-3B parameters, downstream evaluation has replaced pretraining perplexity, and a substantially different catalogue of modifications has emerged. We revisit their question by testing 20 post-2021 Transformer modifications at 1.2B and 3B under strict iso-data, iso-compute, iso-recipe control, with a multi-seed baseline noise floor and CLIMB-12 downstream evaluation as the primary metric. The central finding reproduces theirs at this curated set: most modifications do not transfer. Of the 20 modifications, only two clear Bonferroni correction at 1.2B; one of those two further fails to train stably at 3B under the shared recipe. We also find that the loss-downstream gap reported by Tay et al. (2023) enlarges several-fold for attention-output modifications: two significant failures converge to within 2-3% of baseline validation loss yet drop 6-16 CLIMB-points. We conclude that noise-floor reporting, downstream evaluation, and cross-scale stability testing are now prerequisites for architecture comparisons at 1-3B.

</details>


### [227] [Hack-Verifiable Environments: Towards Evaluating Reward Hacking at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20744)
*Amit Roth,Ankur Samanta,Matan Halevy,Yoav Levine,Yonathan Efroni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对AI奖励黑客行为难以度量的问题，本文提出了一种在环境中植入“可验证漏洞”的评估新范式，并开源了Hack-Verifiable TextArena测试平台，实现了对自主智能体奖励黑客行为的确定性与自动化测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对自主智能体与人类意图对齐中的核心难题——“奖励黑客（Reward Hacking）”，指出当前缺乏大规模、可靠且自动化的评估方法，传统的事后轨迹分析手段效率低下且不够精确。

Method: 提出了一种通过将可检测的“奖励黑客机会”嵌入环境来评估模型行为的新范式。通过设计特定的漏洞，使智能体的利用行为变得可验证，并基于此构建了Hack-Verifiable TextArena测试平台以进行自动化测量。

Result: 利用构建的Hack-Verifiable TextArena测试平台，对多种语言模型在不同任务环境中的奖励黑客行为进行了量化分析，证实了模型在特定条件下确实会通过利用这些注入的漏洞来获取不合理的高分。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一个可扩展、自动化的奖励黑客行为评估框架。通过在环境中植入可探测的“诱饵”，证明了现有的语言模型在目标奖励与实际任务追求之间存在偏差，为未来开发更安全的对齐技术提供了可靠的基准。

Abstract: Aligning autonomous agents with human intent remains a central challenge in modern AI. A key manifestation of this challenge is reward hacking, whereby agents appear successful under the evaluation signal while violating the intended objective. Reward hacking has been observed across a wide range of settings, yet methods for reliably measuring it at scale remain lacking. In this work, we introduce a new evaluation paradigm for measuring reward hacking. Whereas prior studies have primarily analyzed it post hoc by inspecting agent trajectories, we instead embed detectable reward hacking opportunities directly into environments. This makes their exploitation verifiable by design, enabling deterministic and automated measurement of whether and how agents exploit such vulnerabilities. We instantiate this approach in $\textit{TextArena}$ and release $\textit{Hack-Verifiable TextArena}$, a testbed in which reward hacking can be measured reliably. Using this benchmark, we analyze reward hacking behavior across language models in diverse environments and settings. We open source the code at https://github.com/MajoRoth/hack-verifiable-environments/.

</details>


### [228] [DelTA: Discriminative Token Credit Assignment for Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21467)
*Kaiyi Zhang,Wei Wu,Yankai Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对RLVR中奖励分配不精准的问题，本文揭示了其判别器原理，并提出了DelTA方法通过自适应调整令牌权重，抑制干扰噪声，显著提升了大模型的推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法在将响应级奖励转化为令牌级概率更新时缺乏透明度，且简单的算术平均质心构建容易受到高频共有模式（如格式化占位符）的影响，导致关键推理步骤的贡献被稀释。

Method: 提出了一种判别式令牌信用分配方法（DelTA），通过估计令牌系数来放大高收益与低收益响应之间差异化的梯度方向，并下调共有或低判别性令牌（如格式化符号）的权重，从而重塑RLVR的更新方向。

Result: 在七个数学基准测试中，DelTA在Qwen3-8B-Base和Qwen3-14B-Base模型上分别比最强基线平均提升了3.26和2.62分，并在代码生成及跨领域任务中表现出良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: DelTA通过优化RLVR中的令牌信用分配，成功克服了传统质心构建方法中高频模式干扰的问题，实现了更具对比度的策略梯度更新，显著提升了LLM在数学推理和代码生成任务中的表现。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) has emerged as a central technique for improving the reasoning capabilities of large language models. Despite its effectiveness, how response-level rewards translate into token-level probability changes remains poorly understood. We introduce a discriminator view of RLVR updates, showing that the policy-gradient update direction implicitly acts as a linear discriminator over token-gradient vectors and thereby determines which token probabilities are increased or decreased during learning. Under standard sequence-level RLVR, this discriminator is constructed from positive- and negative-side centroids formed by advantage-weighted averaging of token-gradient vectors. However, such centroid construction can be dominated by shared high-frequency patterns, such as formatting tokens, diluting sparse yet discriminative directions that better distinguish high-reward responses from low-reward ones. To address this limitation, we propose $\textbf{DelTA}$, a discriminative token credit assignment method that estimates token coefficients to amplify side-specific token-gradient directions and downweight shared or weakly discriminative ones. These coefficients reweight a self-normalized RLVR surrogate, making the effective side-wise centroids more contrastive and thereby reshaping the RLVR update direction. On seven mathematical benchmarks, DelTA outperforms the strongest same-scale baselines by 3.26 and 2.62 average points on Qwen3-8B-Base and Qwen3-14B-Base, respectively. Additional results on code generation, a different backbone, and out-of-domain evaluations further demonstrate the generalization ability of DelTA.

</details>


### [229] [You Only Need Minimal RLVR Training: Extrapolating LLMs via Rank-1 Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21468)
*Zhepei Wei,Xinyu Zhu,Wei-Lin Chen,Chengsong Huang,Jiaxin Huang,Yu Meng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了 LLM 在 RLVR 训练中的权重轨迹呈现极高的低秩性（rank-1），并据此提出了 RELEX 方法，仅需少量训练步数即可超前预测并实现高性能权重外推，大幅降低了计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR 虽能提升 LLM 推理能力，但其模型权重轨迹的几何结构尚未被充分探索。研究旨在发现权重演化的低秩特性，并利用该特性实现计算高效的训练加速与外推。

Method: 提出了 RELEX (REinforcement Learning EXtrapolation) 方法：先通过短时间观察 RLVR 训练轨迹，通过秩为 1 的近似提取变化空间，再利用线性回归对外推后的权重进行预测。

Result: 在多个模型上验证了 RELEX 的有效性：仅需 15% 的 RLVR 训练步数即可达到或超过完整训练的性能，并能实现 10-20 倍于观察窗口的外推，证明了这种简约方法的鲁棒性和降噪机制。

Conclusion: RELEX 证明了 RLVR 过程中的权重更新具有极高的低秩特性（秩为1），通过对更新轨迹进行线性外推即可获得性能提升，同时具备降噪效果，能够以极低的计算成本实现高效的模型优化。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become a dominant paradigm for improving reasoning in large language models (LLMs), yet the underlying geometry of the resulting parameter trajectories remains underexplored. In this work, we demonstrate that RLVR weight trajectories are extremely low-rank and highly predictable. Specifically, we find that the majority of downstream performance gains are captured by a rank-1 approximation of the parameter deltas, where the magnitude of this projection evolves near-linearly with training steps. Motivated by this, we propose a simple and compute-efficient method RELEX (REinforcement Learning EXtrapolation), which estimates the rank-1 subspace from a short observation window and extrapolates future checkpoints via linear regression, with no learned model required. Across three models (i.e., Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B, Qwen3-4B-Base, and Qwen3-8B-Base), RELEX produces checkpoints that match or exceed RLVR performance on both in-domain and out-of-domain benchmarks, requiring as few as 15% steps of full RLVR training. Remarkably, RELEX is able to extrapolate far beyond the observation window at no training cost, predicting checkpoints up to 10-20$\times$ beyond the observed prefix with continued improvement (e.g., observe only the first 50 steps and extrapolate to 1000 steps). Our ablation analysis confirms the minimalist sufficiency of RELEX: neither increasing the subspace rank nor employing non-linear modeling yields further gains in extrapolation. Finally, we show that RELEX's success stems from a "denoising" effect: by projecting updates onto the rank-1 subspace, the model discards stochastic optimization noise that would otherwise degrade performance during extrapolation. Our code is available at https://github.com/weizhepei/RELEX.

</details>


### [230] [Cumulative Meta-Learning from Active Learning Queries for Robustness to Spurious Correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20771)
*Kin Whye Chew,Jingxian Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 为解决机器学习中因伪相关性导致的泛化与公平性问题，本文提出了 CAML 框架，通过元学习累积并优化模型的归纳偏置，从而使主动学习更有效地利用查询样本纠正模型偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的主动学习方法（直接增量式更新）在深度学习中容易被大规模数据稀释或导致过参数化模型的记忆效应，从而难以克服真实数据集中的伪相关问题，导致模型泛化和公平性不足。

Method: 提出了累积主动元学习（CAML）框架。该方法将主动学习的每一轮视为一个元学习任务，将当前标签集作为元训练数据，新查询批次作为元测试数据，并通过维护和逐步优化一个累积的归纳偏置（Prior）来引导模型适应。

Result: 在多个处理伪相关性的基准测试中表现出色，在 Dominoes、Waterbirds、SpuCo 和 CivilComments 等数据集上，少数群体准确率提升幅度分别高达 27.8%、29.9%、14.3% 和 24.0%。

Conclusion: CAML 通过将主动学习重新定义为一种累积元学习过程，有效解决了模型对伪相关性的依赖问题。该方法利用序列化依赖更新归纳偏置，显著提升了模型在不同数据集上的鲁棒性和少数群体准确率。

Abstract: Spurious correlations in real-world datasets cause machine learning models to rely on irrelevant patterns, undermining reliability, generalization, and fairness. Active learning offers a promising way to address this failure mode by querying informative samples that distinguish core features from spurious ones. However, standard active-learning methods simply append queried examples to the labeled set, effectively updating only the likelihood term. In deep learning regimes, the influence of these informative samples can be diluted by the larger labeled set and memorized by overparameterized models. We propose Cumulative Active Meta-Learning (CAML), an active-learning framework that uses queried examples to meta-learn the prior, or inductive bias, governing how the model adapts. CAML casts each active-learning round as a meta-learning task: the current labeled set serves as meta-train data for adaptation, while the newly queried batch serves as meta-test data for evaluating generalization. Unlike conventional meta-learning, which treats tasks as independent and identically distributed, CAML exploits the sequential dependence between active-learning rounds by maintaining a cumulative inductive bias that is progressively refined. Theoretically, we show that this cumulative formulation introduces interaction terms that couple earlier meta-learned inductive biases with later query-induced objectives, capturing dependencies absent from standard meta-learning. Empirically, CAML improves minority-group accuracy across spurious-correlation benchmarks and acquisition strategies, with gains of up to 27.8% on Dominoes, 29.9% on Waterbirds, 14.3% on SpuCo, and 24.0% on CivilComments.

</details>


### [231] [Learning to Think in Physics: Breaking Shortcut Learning in Scientific Diffusion via Representation Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20780)
*Haozhe Jia,Pengyu Yin,Wenshuo Chen,Shaofeng Liang,Lei Wang,Bowen Tian,Xiucheng Wang,Nanqian Jia,Yutao Yue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 REPA-P 框架，通过在中间层施加 PDE 残差监督，提升了扩散模型解决偏微分方程的准确性与跨分布泛化能力，且推理阶段零开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的物理信息扩散模型通常仅在最终输出端施加 PDE 约束，导致中间层表示未受约束，在面对变边界条件时易产生快捷学习（shortcut learning）问题，泛化性弱。

Method: 提出 REPA-P 框架：在模型中间层附加轻量级 1×1 映射头（projection heads），将隐藏层激活解码为物理量，并在训练期间通过 PDE 残差进行约束；推理时移除映射头。

Result: 在多项 PDE 任务（达西流、拓扑优化等）中，相比基准模型，收敛速度提升 2 倍，物理残差降低高达 66.4%，OOD 鲁棒性提升 49.3%。

Conclusion: REPA-P 通过在中间层引入物理约束，有效解决了物理信息扩散模型在处理分布外（OOD）条件时存在的快捷学习问题，实现了精度与泛化能力的显著提升，且推理阶段零额外开销。

Abstract: Physics-informed diffusion models typically enforce PDE constraints only on final outputs, leaving intermediate representations unconstrained and prone to shortcut learning under shifted boundary conditions. We introduce **REPA-P**, a teacher-free, architecture-agnostic framework that aligns intermediate features with physical states using first-principles residuals. REPA-P attaches lightweight $1{\times}1$ projection heads to selected layers, decodes hidden activations into physical quantities, and applies PDE residual losses during training. These heads are discarded at inference, introducing **zero overhead**. Across four PDE tasks, including Darcy flow, topology optimization, electrostatic potential, and turbulent channel flow, REPA-P accelerates convergence by up to $2{\times}$, reduces physics residuals by up to $66.4\%$, and improves out-of-distribution robustness by up to $49.3\%$, with consistent gains on both U-Net and Diffusion Transformer backbones. Ablations show that supervising a small set of intermediate layers captures most benefits and complements output-level physics losses. Code is available at [https://github.com/Hxxxz0/REPA-P](https://github.com/Hxxxz0/REPA-P).

</details>


### [232] [Causal Machine Learning Is Not a Panacea: A Roadmap for Observational Causal Inference in Health](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20782)
*Donna Tjandra,Trenton Chang,Sonali Parbhoo,Rajesh Ranganath,Andre Kurepa Waschka,William Mitchell,Maggie Makar,Shalmali Joshi,Finale Doshi-Velez,Leo Anthony Celi,Jenna Wiens*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对临床观测数据，提出了因果机器学习的应用路线图，强调了满足因果假设对确保结果科学性和临床临床安全性至关重要，并提供了一套强化分析严谨性的实践框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大规模观测性临床数据集的普及，以及随机对照试验（RCT）的实施难度，学术界对利用因果机器学习从观测数据中进行因果推断的需求激增，但目前缺乏跨学科的应用规范。

Method: 本文通过梳理因果机器学习（Causal ML）在观测性数据中的应用，制定了一份详细的应用路线图，强调了评估因果推断假设的有效性，并为临床和机器学习领域的交叉研究者提供了严谨的分析模板和规范指导。

Result: 研究指出，因果机器学习的局限性常被低估，建模过程中的假设若未得到满足，会导致偏差或误导性结论，从而对临床决策产生负面影响。

Conclusion: 因果机器学习是探索因果假设的有力工具，作者提供了一个通用的分析框架，旨在增强临床研究中因果推断的严谨性与结果的可解释性。

Abstract: Objective: The growing availability of large-scale observational clinical datasets and challenges in conducting randomized controlled trials have spurred enthusiasm in using causal machine learning (ML) for causal inference in observational data. We present a roadmap for applying causal ML to observational data. Materials and methods: We outline the importance of assessing validity assumptions within available data and applying causal ML responsibly for clinical experts using causal ML and ML practitioners with limited clinical expertise. Observations: Despite advances in causal ML, its limitations remain largely under-appreciated across disciplines. This gap in shared knowledge may impact the validity of findings. Discussion: Causal assumptions must be satisfied and modeling choices justified. Otherwise, these approaches risk producing biased or misleading results, with consequences for clinical research and patient care. Conclusion: Causal ML can be a powerful tool for generating causal hypotheses. We provide a template to strengthen the rigor and interpretability of causal analyses.

</details>


### [233] [Beyond Numerical Features: CNN-Driven Algorithm Selection via Contour Plots for Continuous Black-Box Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20797)
*Yiliang Yuan,Xiang Shi,Mustafa Misir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种利用黑盒优化景观的“等高线图”作为输入，通过CNN模型进行算法选择的新方法，证明了视觉模型无需手工建模即可有效利用空间结构进行求解器推荐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的算法选择方法多依赖数值特征（如ELA）或复杂的嵌入学习，而图像化表示具有捕捉景观空间结构的潜力，旨在探索一种无需手工特征工程的自动化黑盒优化求解器选择方案。

Method: 提出了一种基于表示驱动的方法，利用黑盒函数的等高线图（contour-map）作为输入，通过CNN回归器预测不同求解器的性能，从而选择最佳算法。该方法采用了多视图堆叠或聚合的方式处理实例特定的景观信息。

Result: 在BBOB 2009标准协议测试中，所提出的图像选择器显著优于单一最佳求解器（SBS），并与基于特征的基准方法具有竞争力；在双目标评估中也表现出良好的性能。

Conclusion: 基于图像的表示方法（contour-map）能够有效捕捉黑盒优化问题中的空间特征，无需手动设计特征（ELA）即可实现有效的算法选择，为黑盒优化提供了鲁棒且高效的替代方案。

Abstract: The present paper introduces a new representation-driven approach to per-instance algorithm selection, applied to black-box optimization, for automatically choosing the most promising solver from a fixed portfolio. Prior work in continuous optimization largely relies on numerical descriptors, including Exploratory Landscape Analysis features and learned embeddings such as Deep-ELA. This work studies a complementary representation: contour-map visualizations of probed landscapes. A CNN regressor takes multiple instance-specific contour views (stacked or encoded per view and aggregated) and predicts per-solver performance, enabling selection by the predicted best value. On the standard BBOB 2009 single-objective protocol, the resulting selectors significantly outperform the single best solver (SBS) and are competitive with feature-based baselines. A subsequent bi-objective evaluation under the DeepELA setting further indicates that the same image-based principle can be competitive when using windowed contour views. Overall, the results suggest that simple vision models can exploit spatial structure in probed landscapes for algorithm selection without handcrafted ELA features.

</details>


### [234] [Tunable MAGMAX: Preference-Aware Model Merging for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20803)
*Kei Hiroshima,Kento Uchida,Shinichi Shirakawa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 为解决持续学习中模型合并缺乏灵活性、无法根据特定环境或用户偏好调节任务权重的问题，本文提出了 Tunable MAGMAX，通过偏好向量实现了任务级性能的可控调节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于模型合并（如 MAGMAX）的持续学习方法主要关注平均性能，缺乏对特定部署环境需求或用户偏好的适应性支持。

Method: 提出 Tunable MAGMAX 框架，通过引入偏好向量（Preference Vector）来控制模型合并过程中各任务参数的选取比例；同时引入了一种基于少量目标环境数据自动构建偏好向量的方法，减少了人工干预。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法能够精确控制各任务的性能表现，并成功将合并后的模型适应于多种不同的目标环境，性能与基线方法相当或更优。

Conclusion: Tunable MAGMAX 提出了一种在持续学习中实现任务性能偏好可控的新方法，能够灵活适应不同部署环境的需求，在保持整体性能的前提下实现了对任务权重的主动调节，具有实用价值。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) aims to train models sequentially on multiple tasks while mitigating catastrophic forgetting of previously learned knowledge. Recent advances in large pre-trained models (LPMs) and model merging techniques, such as MAGMAX, have demonstrated effective CL performance by combining task-specific parameters. However, existing methods primarily focus on average performance across all tasks and do not adequately address how to construct models accommodating different deployment environments or varying user preferences. This paper proposes a model merging framework, termed Tunable MAGMAX, which enables preference-aware control of task-specific performance in CL. Our method introduces a preference vector that controls the number of elements selected from each task vector during model merging, allowing us to adjust the merged model performance according to their deployment needs. We further propose a method for automatically constructing appropriate preference vectors by leveraging small amounts of target environment data and datasets from model training tasks, thereby eliminating the need for manual specification. The experimental result on CL benchmark tasks demonstrates that Tunable MAGMAX effectively controls task-wise performance and successfully adapts merged models to various target environments. The proposed Tunable MAGMAX achieves superior or comparable performance to baseline methods, making it a practical solution for deploying CL models to various environments where the preferences of each task performance differ.

</details>


### [235] [Markovian Circuit Tracing for Transformer State Dynamic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20824)
*Abdullah X*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MCT诊断框架，通过在可控的HMM任务中进行实验，证实了Transformer模型不仅能实现接近贝叶斯最优的预测，且其内部激活值确实编码了可提取、可干预的潜在状态转移结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究难以准确识别Transformer处理复杂序列时的内部状态演变，缺乏量化分析其是否具备隐式状态转移机制的可控基准（Benchmark）。

Method: 引入Markovian Circuit Tracing (MCT)诊断流水线，通过在已知隐马尔可夫模型（HMM）任务中训练小型因果Transformer，利用状态强制（state forcing）和激活补丁技术验证模型内部状态对Bayes最优预测的贡献。

Result: 小型Transformer在HMM任务上达到近Bayes水平的预测性能；残差流中提取的状态表征能恢复真实的转移信号；状态强制实验显示，通过将激活值修补为预测的“状态中心点”，能显著降低目标KL散度至0.0532。

Conclusion: MCT提供了一种有效的诊断流程，证明了Transformer在处理时序任务时会学习到类似马尔可夫过程的潜在状态表示，且这些状态在因果干预下具有可解释性。

Abstract: Many sequence computations are easier to study as movement through internal states than as isolated local circuits. We introduce Markovian Circuit Tracing (MCT), a diagnostic pipeline for testing whether transformer activations contain coarse state-transition structure. The benchmark uses synthetic Hidden Markov Model (HMM) tasks where latent states, transition matrices, Bayesian belief vectors, Bayes-optimal predictions, and forced-state counterfactual targets are known exactly. Across six HMM families and three seeds per family, tiny causal transformers learn near-Bayes next-token predictors, with mean excess loss over Bayes of 0.0138. Residual activations contain partial Bayesian belief information in this controlled synthetic benchmark. State abstractions extracted from these activations recover coarse transition signal, strongest in persistent and lower-state regimes, and weaker in ambiguous-emission and six-state regimes. The clearest result comes from state forcing. Patching a recovered-state centroid reduces KL to the exact HMM counterfactual target from 0.1957 in the unpatched model to 0.0532 on average, beating wrong-state, mean-activation, random-activation, and shuffled-label controls. The contribution is a controlled benchmark and evaluation framework for transformer state-dynamics interpretability, with MCT as a simple reference pipeline

</details>


### [236] [Finite-Time Regret Analysis of Retry-Aware Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20854)
*Bingkui Tong,Junpei Komiyama,Soichiro Nishimori,Paavo Parmas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于多尝试最佳结果目标（如 pass@k）的 ReMax 算法，通过理论分析证明了其次线性后悔界，揭示了其相比 Thompson 采样更激进的利用策略及其特有的欠估计问题，并给出了性能优化方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的强化学习探索机制（如基于 pass@k 的 ReMax）在多臂老虎机问题中的后悔（regret）理论性质尚不明确，需要从理论上解释其相对于 Thompson 采样（TS）为何更具利用倾向及其性能边界。

Method: 针对高斯奖励设定，研究了 M=2 时的 ReMax 算法；通过“预期改进平衡条件”（expected-improvement balance condition）刻画了最优重采样分布，并利用该分布推导了首个次线性后悔界（sublinear regret bound）。此外，分析中引入了针对 ReMax 的欠估计效应分解技术。

Result: 理论上证明了 ReMax 的次线性后悔界；实验表明 ReMax 在轻微欠估计情况下优于 KL-UCB 和 TS，同时验证了后验方差缩放机制在缓解严重欠估计时的有效性。

Conclusion: ReMax 算法在特定的探索与利用权衡中具有独特的表现，尽管存在潜在的“低估效应”（underestimation effect）导致最优臂被采样过少，但通过后验方差缩放可以缓解此问题。该算法在处理 retry-aware 目标（如 pass@k）时展现了优于 KL-UCB 和 TS 的性能。

Abstract: We study a stochastic bandit algorithm motivated by retry-aware objectives that value the best outcome among multiple attempts, such as pass@$k$ and max@$k$. Given a posterior over arm values, ReMax chooses a sampling distribution that maximizes the posterior expected maximum reward over $M$ virtual draws. Although this objective was introduced in reinforcement learning as an exploration mechanism under uncertainty, its regret properties in bandit problems have remained unclear. For Gaussian rewards and the first nontrivial case $M=2$, we characterize the optimal ReMax distribution through an expected-improvement balance condition and prove the first sublinear regret bound for ReMax. Our analysis separates the usual saturation behavior of suboptimal arms from a ReMax-specific underestimation effect, in which the optimal arm may be sampled too rarely after an unfavorable estimate. This explains why ReMax can be more exploitative than Thompson sampling (TS) and why its regret analysis is technically delicate. Experiments support this picture: ReMax often outperforms KL-UCB and Thompson sampling under mild underestimation, while posterior-variance scaling empirically mitigates severe underestimation.

</details>


### [237] [Runtime-Certified Bounded-Error Quantized Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20868)
*Dean Calver*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种支持“运行时认证注意力”的分层KV缓存系统，通过在线误差监测和确定性回退机制，在实现激进压缩的同时保证了LLM长文本推理的输出质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的KV缓存量化方法依赖于平均情况下的稳健性，缺乏运行时检测和从故障中恢复的机制，导致在高长文本任务中易出现不可控的近似错误。

Method: 提出了一种分层KV缓存架构：在GPU内存中存储INT8键和INT4值，在系统RAM中保留FP16原始数据以进行确定性回退。设计了一种两项误差分解方法，用于计算每个头、每一步的注意力分布失真和值重建误差，并基于此实现自适应精度选择和多阶段回退机制。

Result: 在LLaMA 3.1-8B模型上，上下文长度达128K时，该系统在语言建模和检索任务中的质量与FP16密集计算相当，且能有效恢复传统INT8/INT4基线中出现的灾难性失败。

Conclusion: 该研究将KV缓存量化从固定近似转变为可运行时验证的计算。通过分层架构和在线误差边界计算，在保证高质量输出的前提下，实现了激进的KV压缩部署，避免了传统量化方法中的灾难性故障。

Abstract: KV cache quantization reduces the memory cost of long-context LLM inference, but introduces approximation error that is typically validated only empirically. Existing systems rely on average-case robustness, with no mechanism to detect or recover from failures at runtime. We present a tiered KV cache architecture that enables runtime-certified attention: INT8 keys and INT4 values are stored in GPU memory, while FP16 originals are retained in system RAM for deterministic fallback. A two-term error decomposition yields per-head, per-step bounds on (i) attention distribution distortion from key quantization and (ii) value reconstruction error. These bounds are computed online and used to drive adaptive precision selection and a multi-stage fallback ladder, which guarantees recovery to the exact dense attention output when required. Across PG-19, NIAH, and RULER benchmarks on LLaMA~3.1-8B with contexts up to 128K, the system matches dense FP16 KV quality within noise for language modelling and retrieval tasks, while recovering catastrophic failures observed in naive INT8/INT4 baselines. Value-sensitive tasks at short context expose a controlled trade-off between compression and fidelity, which can be eliminated via tighter value tolerances or FP16-value fallback. The certification is local (per-head, per-step) and does not guarantee end-to-end model correctness, but ensures that each attention computation is either bounded relative to an FP16 reference or exactly recovered via fallback. This reframes KV cache quantization as a runtime-verified computation rather than a fixed approximation. The goal is not raw speedups, but enabling safe deployment of aggressive KV compression under strict quality constraints.

</details>


### [238] [CIG: Exploration via Conditional Information Gain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20878)
*Tim Joseph,Marcus Fechner,Philipp Stegmaier,Karam Daaboul,J. Marius Zöllner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为“条件信息增益（CIG）”的新型强化学习探索奖励，通过对集成不确定性的分解，实现了兼顾长短期记忆的高效且可扩展的探索策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有探索方法存在局限：生命周期奖励忽略了 rollout 内部冗余，而情景奖励丢失了历史经验；混合方法通常依赖不可扩展的启发式权重或高斯过程动态模型，且轨迹级信息增益计算不可行。

Method: 基于集成差异核（ensemble disagreement kernel）的对数行列式目标函数，利用Cholesky分解推导出可处理的因果每步奖励（causal per-step rewards），从而实现对回放缓冲区和 rollout 前缀的联合条件化。

Result: 在 MiniGrid 和 OGBench 的十二项任务（含清洁和随机干扰环境）中，CIG 在探索效率和抗干扰鲁棒性上均达到或优于现有方法。

Conclusion: CIG提供了一种可扩展且稳健的探索奖励机制，能够同时平衡生命周期（lifelong）探索和情景（episodic）探索，在多种控制任务中表现优于现有方法并具备处理随机干扰的能力。

Abstract: Intrinsic rewards for exploration in reinforcement learning condition on different contexts: lifelong rewards score each transition against accumulated experience but ignore within-rollout redundancy; episodic rewards penalize intra-trajectory repetition but discard lifetime progress. Hybrid methods combine both signals through heuristic weights or require Gaussian-process dynamics that do not scale beyond low-dimensional state spaces. Trajectory-level information gain decomposes into per-step terms that condition on the replay buffer and rollout prefix simultaneously, but remains intractable for deep models. We derive the Conditional Information Gain (CIG) reward as a tractable surrogate: a log-determinant objective over an ensemble disagreement kernel whose Cholesky factorization yields causal per-step rewards that retain both conditioning sets while scaling to high-dimensional state spaces. We instantiate CIG in a model-based setting, where rollouts are short and within-rollout corrections remain largely unexplored. Across twelve tasks spanning discrete (MiniGrid) and continuous control (OGBench), in both clean and stochastic-distractor settings, CIG outperforms or matches prior exploration methods while remaining robust to stochastic distractors.

</details>


### [239] [NeighborDiv: Training-free Zero-shot Generalist Graph Anomaly Detection via Neighbor Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20879)
*Kaifeng Wei,Teng Liu,Liang Dong,Xiubo Liang,Yuke Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NeighborDiv 是一种无需训练的通用图异常检测框架，通过度量“邻居间的特征多样性”而非“节点与邻居的一致性”，在保持极高效率的同时显著提升了异常检测的准确性和跨域泛化稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的通用图异常检测（GGAD）方法多依赖“节点-邻居”一致性，存在训练流程复杂、数据依赖性强、计算代价高以及跨域泛化能力不稳定等问题。

Method: 提出了一种无需训练（training-free）的框架 NeighborDiv，抛弃了传统的“节点-邻居”一致性范式，转而采用“邻居-邻居”多样性范式，通过计算邻居间特征相似度的方差来量化节点的局部结构离散度，从而识别异常。

Result: 在 SDIT 模式下，NeighborDiv 相比第二好基线平均 AUC 提升 10.25%，AP 提升 17.78%；在 UMDT 模式下，分别提升 6.89% 和 9.58%，且在所有数据集上表现稳定，实现了零性能波动。

Conclusion: NeighborDiv 通过引入“邻居邻居间多样性”范式，成功摆脱了对复杂训练流程和大规模标注数据的依赖，实现了零训练、低计算成本且具有高度鲁棒性的通用图异常检测，是该领域的一种高效且实用的新范式。

Abstract: Graph Anomaly Detection (GAD) is increasingly shifting to Generalist GAD (GGAD) for cross-domain "one-for-all" detection, but existing GGAD methods predominantly rely on the neighbor consistency principle, falling into the \textbf{Node-to-Neighbor Consistency Paradigm} for anomaly quantification. These methods suffer from complex training pipelines, heavy training data dependency, high computational costs, and unstable cross-domain generalization. To address these limitations, we propose NeighborDiv, a training-free generalist graph anomaly detection framework based on neighbor diversity. Departing from the dominant Node-to-Neighbor Consistency Paradigm, we shift the focus to the \textbf{Neighbor-to-Neighbor Diversity Paradigm}, and uncover that the internal structural dispersion of a node's neighbor set is a powerful, independently discriminative anomaly signal. We quantify neighbor diversity via the variance of inter-neighbor feature similarities, which captures how a node organizes its local graph environment, and operates independently of conventional node-to-neighbor consistency frameworks. Extensive experiments under two standard GGAD evaluation paradigms show NeighborDiv achieves state-of-the-art performance, with relative gains of 10.25% in average AUC and 17.78% in average AP over the second-best baseline under Single-Domain Independent Training (SDIT), and 6.89%/9.58% in AUC/AP under Unified Multi-Domain Training (UMDT), respectively. Notably, NeighborDiv yields zero performance volatility across all datasets, eliminating training-set dependency and establishing a lightweight and highly practical GGAD framework.

</details>


### [240] [Training distribution determines the ceiling of drug-blind cancer sensitivity prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20885)
*Taekyung Heo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究指出药物敏感性预测的性能停滞是由评估指标缺陷引起的，而非模型表示能力不足。通过引入机制（MoA）分层训练和响应匹配，可显著改善模型捕捉特定通路敏感性的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前精密肿瘤学中药物敏感性预测性能陷入停滞，尽管模型越来越复杂，但并未带来预期的预测增益。研究旨在探究这种停滞的根本原因。

Method: 1. 批判性分析基准指标：揭示全样本Pearson r受药物间效力差异支配，建议使用药内（Per-drug）Pearson r进行评估。
2. 对齐实验：验证机制作用（MoA）作为特征与作为训练约束对预测的影响。
3. 改进策略：提出基于机制分层的训练和基于试点观测的响应匹配方法。

Result: 1. 证明“标准指标”掩盖了模型的失效，简单的药物均值预测即可达到较好的全局相关性。
2. 发现将MoA作为特征并无显著增益；但利用MoA对训练进行分层能显著提升对靶向激酶抑制剂（Kinase inhibitors）的预测能力，因为传统的泛癌共训练会抑制通路特异性的敏感信号。

Conclusion: 药物敏感性预测的瓶颈并非药物表示学习的局限，而是现有基准指标导致的测量伪影。通过采用基于机制分层（MoA-stratified）的训练和响应匹配策略，可以有效解决泛癌共训练带来的信号抑制问题，从而提升预测性能。

Abstract: Precision oncology requires predicting which drugs will suppress a specific tumor from its molecular profile, but drug-blind sensitivity prediction has plateaued despite increasingly complex drug representations. Here we show that this stagnation reflects a metric artifact rather than a representational bottleneck. The standard benchmark, global Pearson r, is dominated by between-drug potency differences that a trivial drug-mean predictor captures without any cell-specific learning. Per-drug Pearson r, which isolates within-drug cell ranking, reveals that no drug encoding improves over cell-only features across four independent datasets. A controlled experiment channeling mechanism-of-action identity as either a drug feature or a training-distribution constraint identifies the cause. Supplying MoA as a feature yields negligible benefit, whereas using it to stratify training raises per-drug r substantially for targeted kinase inhibitors, because pan-cancer co-training suppresses pathway-specific sensitivity signals. Mechanism-stratified training and response matching from pilot observations provide two deployable strategies that together recover the principal sources of predictive gain in drug-blind sensitivity prediction.

</details>


### [241] [A Deployment Audit of Release-Side Risk in Conformal Triage under Prevalence Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20956)
*Chengze Li,Xiao Liu,Hanrong Zhang,Haiyang Peng,Yanghao Ruan,Huanhuan Ma,Chunyu Miao,Qichao Zhou,Xiangrong Qi,Philip Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对共形分诊在患病率偏移下的安全漏洞，本文提出了一种泄露感知审计框架，通过更精细的样本划分和分层评估，揭示了传统审查率指标的误导性，并能诊断关键的样本稀缺问题，从而保障临床部署安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的共形分诊（conformal triage）评估指标在患病率偏移（prevalence shift）下存在局限性，无法有效识别“真正发生目标事件但被错误放行（无审查）”的安全关键型风险。

Method: 提出一种“泄露感知部署审计”（leakage-aware deployment audit）方法，将目标对象划分为三个非重叠角色：患病率校正（prevalence correction）、共形校准（conformal calibration）和预留释放安全评估（held-out release-safety evaluation）。在此框架下，直接评估“事件阳性但被误放行”的情况，并诊断校准数据是否充足。

Result: 在非小细胞肺癌（NSCLC）回顾性试点中的应用表明，原本看似表现良好的低审查率策略，在经过患病率校正后，实际上会导致更多阳性病例被漏检。审计结果显示，目前的试点数据中事件标签过于稀缺，无法支撑安全地降低审查率。

Conclusion: 本文提出的“泄露感知部署审计”能够有效评估共形分诊在患病率偏移下的安全表现。研究表明，仅看总体审查率会产生误导；通过对样本进行角色分离审计，可以揭示由于缺乏事件标签而导致的不安全放行风险，为临床决策提供更严谨的诊断依据。

Abstract: Conformal triage converts predictive scores into deployment actions that either release a case, flag it for urgent attention, or defer it to human review. Under prevalence shift, however, the usual summaries of marginal coverage and human-review rate can miss the safety-critical question of whether patients who truly experience the target event are released without review. To address this gap, we introduce a leakage-aware deployment audit for release-side conformal triage. It first assigns target subjects to three non-overlapping roles: prevalence correction, conformal calibration, and held-out release-safety evaluation. This separation then lets the audit evaluate release directly: how many event-positive patients are cleared without review, whether the pilot has enough event labels for calibration, and how the safety-review trade-off shifts. Applying this audit to a retrospective NSCLC pilot shows why lower review can be misleading: after prevalence correction, the pooled conformal branch lowers review by releasing more patients, some of whom are event-positive. Within the audit, the classwise branch acts as a scarcity diagnostic: the pilot has too few event labels to certify safe low-review release.

</details>


### [242] [Choose Wisely and Privately: Proactive Client Selection for Fair and Efficient Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20975)
*Adda Akram Bendoukha,Heber Hwang Arcolezi,Nesrine Kaaniche,Aymen Boudguiga*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种主动式联邦学习客户端选择框架，通过差分隐私互信息量化数据相关性，并利用模拟退火算法在训练前筛选最优客户端组合，从而解决非IID数据下的性能瓶颈与公平性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中传统的平均化方法在处理非IID数据时性能受限，现有的响应式策略（如舍弃或稀释梯度）效率低下，浪费计算资源并带来不必要的隐私风险。需要一种主动的、训练前阶段的客户端选择机制。

Method: 1. 利用差分隐私（DP）列联表计算互信息，量化跨特征关联的相关性；2. 定义“潜在联合损失（PFL）”函数，旨在平衡集体数据效用与跨特征关联的公平性；3. 使用模拟退火算法求解最优客户端子集选择问题。

Result: 在四个基准测试中，该方法在收敛速度、模型公平性和最终准确率方面均优于均匀采样及前沿的自适应聚合/采样策略。

Conclusion: 通过在训练前主动选择满足数据效用与公平性要求的客户端集合，可以有效克服非独立同分布（non-IID）数据带来的挑战，实现比现有聚合或采样策略更快速、公平且准确的模型训练。

Abstract: Federated Learning enables collaborative model training across decentralized data sources without data transfer. Averaging-based FL is limited by the presence of non-IID data, which negatively impacts convergence speed and final model accuracy. Conventional alternatives suffer from significant inefficiency. Clients with noisy or highly heterogeneous data contribute expensive gradient computations that are either discarded or heavily down-weighted before aggregation. These reactive approaches waste computational resources, require more communication rounds and result in unnecessary privacy exposure. In this paper, we propose a proactive client selection framework that aims to find an optimal federation of clients whose combined data match utility and fairness requirements before training begins. Our method relies on mutual information computed from differentially private contingency tables to quantify the relevance of cross-feature correlations in the union dataset. We introduce a Potential Federation Loss (PFL) over the set of fixed-size federations, which balances two objectives. Maximizing collective data utility while ensuring fair cross-features correlations to prevent group unfairness. Client selection is expressed as an optimal subset search problem over the PFL objective, which we solve using simulated annealing under strong differential privacy guarantees for clients' local statistics. Experimental results on four benchmarks show faster, fairer, and more accurate models trained on optimally found federations, compared to uniform sampling, even when state-of-the-art adaptive aggregation or sampling strategies are employed.

</details>


### [243] [A Sharper Picture of Generalization in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20988)
*Paul Lintilhac,Sair Shaikh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过引入PAC-Bayes理论和傅里叶谱分析，揭示了Transformer在布尔域的泛化机制，证明了低度数稀疏布尔函数能够通过平坦极小值实现良好的泛化，并提供了首个非空泛化界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的针对Transformer在布尔域上的泛化研究多基于Rademacher复杂度，往往难以获得严谨的泛化界。本文旨在通过PAC-Bayes理论，通过分析目标函数的傅里叶谱特性，探索Transformer实现良好泛化性能的理论基础。

Method: 1. 引入PAC-Bayes理论替代传统的Rademacher复杂度用于推导泛化界；2. 利用傅里叶谱分析布尔函数，证明存在能实现低度数稀疏函数的“平坦极小值”（flat minima）；3. 结合理论推导与实验（预测评估及机制可解释性分析）进行交叉验证。

Result: 推导出了关于Transformer的一个非空（non-vacuous）泛化界，证明了当函数的傅里叶谱集中在低度数成分时，Transformer能够通过平坦极小值实现良好的泛化。实验结果与理论构造一致。

Conclusion: Transformer在处理布尔函数时，其泛化能力源于能够构建出具有“低锐度”（low-sharpness）且稀疏的解空间结构。利用PAC-Bayes框架成功导出了非空泛化界，并通过机制可解释性实验验证了该理论构造在实际Transformer模型中的合理性。

Abstract: We study transformers' generalization behavior on boolean domains from the perspective of the Fourier Spectra of their target functions. In contrast to prior work (Edelman et al., 2022; Trauger and Tewari, 2024), which derived generalization bounds from Rademacher complexity, we investigate the feasibility of obtaining generalization bounds via PAC-Bayes theory. We show that sparse spectra concentrated on low-degree components enable low-sharpness constructions with good generalization properties. Our idea is to show the existence of flat minima implementing any boolean function of sparsity no greater than the context length, and then apply a PAC-Bayes bound to an idealized low-sharpness learner, resulting in a non-vacuous generalization bound. We evaluate predictions empirically and conduct a mechanistic interpretability study to support the realism of our theoretical construction in real transformers.

</details>


### [244] [Modeling Temporal scRNA-seq Data with Latent Gaussian Process and Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20989)
*Mehmet Yigit Balik,Harri Lähdesmäki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用潜在高斯过程和最优传输建模单细胞时间轨迹的新框架，通过显式建模生物异质性能更准确地进行轨迹推断、插值、外推及扰动分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于神经微分方程和流的方法在处理单细胞RNA测序数据时存在过拟合风险，且未充分考虑生物学变异性，导致难以从静态快照中准确推断时间演化过程。

Method: 一种基于希尔伯特空间方法逼近潜在异方差高斯过程（GP）的生成框架；引入最优传输（OT）目标来对齐生成种群分布与观测分布；通过引入细胞特异性潜在时间及细胞类型条件化来解耦时间不同步与分化轨迹。

Result: 在复杂轨迹插值和外推任务中达到了当前最优（SOTA）性能；提出了一种新的基于梯度的策略，用于推断细胞扰动轨迹。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的基于希尔伯特空间高斯过程逼近的生成框架，结合最优传输目标，有效解决了单细胞RNA测序数据中提取时间序列过程的难题。该方法在处理生物异质性、插值/外推任务以及推断扰动轨迹方面均表现出色。

Abstract: Single-cell RNA sequencing provides insights into gene expression at single-cell resolution, yet inferring temporal processes from these static snapshot measurements remains a fundamental challenge. Current approaches utilizing neural differential equations and flows are sensitive to overfitting and lack careful considerations of biological variability. In this work, we propose a generative framework that models population trends using a latent heteroscedastic Gaussian process (GP) approximated by Hilbert space methods. To address the absence of genuine cell trajectories, we leverage an optimal transport (OT) objective that aligns generated and observed population distributions. Our method explicitly captures biological heterogeneity by incorporating cell-specific latent time and cell type conditioning to disentangle temporal asynchrony and trajectories to different cell types. We demonstrate state-of-the-art performance on complex interpolation and extrapolation benchmarks and introduce a novel gradient-based strategy for inferring perturbation trajectories.

</details>


### [245] [Beyond the Bellman Recursion: A Pontryagin-Guided Framework for Non-Exponential Discounting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20996)
*Hojin Ko,Jeonggyu Huh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对非指数折扣场景下贝尔曼递归失效的问题，本文提出了 PG-DPO 框架，通过引入庞特里亚金极大值原理实现非递归优化，显著提升了模型在复杂经济与生物生存模拟中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于贝尔曼递归的强化学习算法在处理人类偏好或生存过程等非指数折扣场景时会失效，因为指数折扣具有特殊的数学性质（乘法性和时间同质性），而一旦违反这些性质，动态规划将无法直接应用。

Method: 提出“庞特里亚金引导直接策略优化”（PG-DPO），该方法放弃了贝尔曼递归，通过结合庞特里亚金极大值原理与蒙特卡洛回报投影（Adjoint-MC projection）来实现逐点哈密顿量最大化。

Result: 在多维双曲折扣和生存折扣基准测试中，PG-DPO 在准确性和稳定性方面均优于传统的基于贝尔曼方程的求解器及基于 Critic 的基线模型。

Conclusion: 非递归的变分框架（PG-DPO）能够有效解决非指数折扣下的强化学习优化难题，提供了一种更稳健的策略优化途径。

Abstract: Most value-based and actor--critic reinforcement learning methods rely on Bellman-style recursions, yet these recursions collapse under non-exponential discounting common in human preferences and survival processes. We show the breakdown is structural: exponential discounting sits at a fragile intersection of multiplicativity and time homogeneity, and violating either property breaks standard dynamic programming. To overcome this, we propose Pontryagin-Guided Direct Policy Optimization (PG-DPO), a variational framework that abandons recursion and couples the Pontryagin Maximum Principle with Monte Carlo rollouts via an Adjoint-MC projection enforcing pointwise Hamiltonian maximization. Across multi-dimensional hyperbolic and survival-discount benchmarks, PG-DPO improves accuracy and stability where equation-driven solvers and critic-based baselines diverge.

</details>


### [246] [A Dialogue between Causal and Traditional Representation Learning: Toward Mutual Benefits in a Unified Formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21058)
*Yan Li,Yuewen Sun,Shaoan Xie,Gongxu Luo,Yunlong Deng,Kun Zhang,Guangyi Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出传统表征学习与因果表征学习（CRL）领域长期脱节，提出了一套统一的任务与约束分析框架，证明了将两者结合能促进理论与实践的互补，并揭示了因果约束的效用取决于具体的下游任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统表征学习侧重经验应用，而因果表征学习（CRL）侧重理论可标识性，两个领域长期割裂导致术语和研究方法脱节，限制了交流与进步。

Method: 提出了一个统一的表征学习分析框架，将表征学习拆解为“任务组件”（规定保留何种信息）和“约束组件”（规定潜在空间的结构）。在此框架下，通过实验研究了不同任务组件如何影响不同结构约束下CRL方法的表现。

Result: 在CausalVerse数据集上的实验表明，因果约束的有效性并非绝对，其效果显著依赖于任务设置。

Conclusion: 因果表征学习（CRL）与传统表征学习应相互融合。通过统一的框架，CRL可提供理论约束，而传统表征学习可提供任务设计的实践经验。实验验证了因果约束的有效性高度依赖于所配对的任务。

Abstract: Causal representation learning (CRL) and traditional representation learning have largely developed along different trajectories. Traditional representation learning has been driven mainly by applications and empirical objectives, whereas CRL has focused more on theoretical questions, particularly identifiability. This difference in emphasis has created a gap between the two fields in terminology, problem formulation, and evaluation, limiting communication and sometimes leading to disconnected or redundant efforts. In this paper, we argue that these two fields should be brought into dialogue rather than treated as separate paradigms. To this end, we introduce a unified formulation in which the representation learning is characterized by two components: a task component, which specifies what information the learned representation is required to preserve, and a constraint component, which specifies what structure is imposed on the latent space. Under this formulation, the benefits run in both directions. CRL provides theoretical tools for understanding when structured latent constraints are useful or necessary, while traditional representation learning offers practical insights on task design and objective choice that can improve the development of CRL methods. To illustrate this interaction, we experimentally study how different task components affect the behavior of CRL methods under different structured constraints. Results on CausalVerse show that the effectiveness of causal constraints depends strongly on the tasks with which they are paired.

</details>


### [247] [Robust Personalized Recommendation under Hidden Confounding in MNAR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21066)
*Zongyu Li,Wanting Su,Tianyu Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对推荐系统中的隐含混杂问题，本文提出了一种名为PUID的个性化敏感性界限框架，通过放松全局同质性假设并配合对抗优化，有效地在不依赖RCT数据的情况下解决了选择偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的去偏差方法（如IPW或DR）在存在隐含混杂因素时失效；而基于随机对照试验（RCT）的方法成本过高，基于全局敏感性界限的方法则因假设混杂效应在所有交互中同质化而受限。为了克服这些局限，需要一种能够捕捉个体差异的敏感性分析方法。

Method: 提出了“个性化未观测混杂感知交互去混杂”（PUID）框架，通过引入用户-物品层面的个体化敏感性界限（而非传统的全局常数界限）来处理隐含混杂因素；并开发了一种对抗优化策略，以及结合预训练模型的BPUID基准引导变体以增强鲁棒性。

Result: 在三个真实世界数据集上的实验结果表明，该方法在隐含混杂情况下显著优于主流的全局敏感性控制方法，且能够有效提升推荐准确性，同时无需昂贵的随机对照试验数据。

Conclusion: PUID及其变体BPUID通过引入用户-物品层面的敏感性界限并结合对抗优化策略，能够在存在隐含混杂因素的情况下，无需RCT数据即可有效缓解推荐系统中的选择偏差，且在性能和稳健性上均优于现有的全局敏感性方法。

Abstract: Recommender systems often rely on observational user--item interaction data, which is prone to selection bias due to users' selective interactions with items. Inverse propensity weighting and doubly robust estimators effectively mitigate selection bias under observed confounding, but are unreliable in the presence of hidden confounders. Existing approaches relying on randomized controlled trials (RCTs) or global sensitivity bounds are constrained in practice: RCTs demand costly experimental data, while global sensitivity bounds presume a uniformly bounded effect of unmeasured confounders on propensities through sensitivity analysis, thereby neglecting heterogeneity across user--item interactions. To overcome this limitation, we propose a novel framework, which estimates user--item level sensitivity bounds, thereby substantially relaxing the homogeneity assumption inherent in global sensitivity bounds named Personalized Unobserved-Confounding-aware Interaction Deconfounder (PUID). To ensure both robustness and predictive accuracy, we further develop an adversarial optimization strategy and propose a benchmark-guided variant (BPUID) that incorporates pre-trained models as stabilizing references. Extensive experiments on three real-world datasets demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms global methods under hidden confounding, without requiring RCT data.

</details>


### [248] [Reviving Error Correction in Modern Deep Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21088)
*Minh Hoang Nguyen,Dai Do,Huu Hiep Nguyen,Dung Nguyen,Kien Do,Hung Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对深度时间序列预测中自回归造成的误差累积问题，作者提出了UEC-STD方法。该方法通过季节趋势解耦实现跨模型的通用误差修正，在不重新训练原模型的前提下显著提升了长序列预测表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在长序列预测时，由于自回归推理（递归使用预测值作为输入）导致误差累积，性能显著下降。传统的误差修正机制（ECM）在深度学习中的应用效果有限，亟需一种通用的解决方案。

Method: 提出通用误差修正器（UEC-STD）：通过季节性-趋势（Seasonal-Trend）分解将时间序列预测结果解耦，并针对不同分量训练对应的误差修正模块；该方法具有架构无关性，无需重新训练原有的基模型。

Result: 在4种主流预测基模型（Backbones）和10个标准时间序列数据集上进行了验证，结果显示UEC-STD显著提升了预测准确性和模型的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 针对深度学习长序列时间序列预测中自回归推理导致的误差累积问题，提出了一种与架构无关的通用误差修正框架。通过季节性-趋势分解，该方法能有效提升现有预测模型的精度与稳定性。

Abstract: Modern deep-learning models have achieved remarkable success in time-series forecasting. Yet, their performance degrades in long-term prediction due to error accumulation in autoregressive inference, where predictions are recursively used as inputs. While classical error correction mechanisms (ECMs) have long been used in statistical methods, their applicability to deep learning models remains limited or ineffective. In this work, we revisit the error accumulation problem in deep time-series forecasting and investigate the role and necessity of ECMs in this new context. We propose a simple, architecture-agnostic error correction model that can be integrated with any existing forecaster without requiring retraining. By explicitly decomposing predictions into trend and seasonal components and training the corrector to adjust each separately, we introduce the Universal Error Corrector with Seasonal-Trend Decomposition (UEC-STD), which significantly improves correction accuracy and robustness across 4 backbones and 10 datasets. Our findings provide a practical tool for enhancing forecasts while offering new insights into mitigating autoregressive errors in deep time-series models. Code is available at https://github.com/DA2I2-SLM/UEC-STD.

</details>


### [249] [UOTIP: Unbalanced Optimal Transport Map for Unpaired Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21094)
*Donggyu Lee,Taekyung Lee,Jaewoong Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对非成对图像修复任务，提出了一种基于非平衡最优传输（UOTIP）的新型求解器，通过学习分布间的传输映射，有效提升了在噪声多样性和类别不平衡场景下的重构性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对非成对（unpaired）图像逆问题中训练数据缺乏配对样本的挑战，旨在解决噪声稳健性、类别不平衡以及不同类型噪声的泛化能力问题。

Method: 提出了基于非平衡最优传输（Unbalanced Optimal Transport）的映射学习方法（UOTIP）。通过松弛边缘约束，构建了从噪声测量分布到清晰信号分布的传输映射，并引入基于似然函数的代价函数及二次代价项以解决非配对数据和病态问题。

Result: UOTIP 在线性和非线性逆问题基准上均达到了当前最优（SOTA）性能，同时验证了其对多级观测噪声和数据不平衡的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: UOTIP 通过利用非平衡最优传输（UOT）框架，在非成对（unpaired）图像逆问题中表现优异。理论证明了引入二次代价项可确保传输映射的存在性和唯一性，实验证实了其在多种逆问题任务中达到了SOTA性能。

Abstract: We investigate unpaired image inverse problems, a challenging setting where only independent, non-paired sets of noisy measurements and clean target signals are available for training. We propose a novel inverse problem solver based on Unbalanced Optimal Transport, called Unbalanced Optimal Transport Map for Inverse Problems (UOTIP). Our method formulates the reconstruction task, predicting clean target signals from noisy measurements, as learning a UOT Map from noisy measurement distribution to clean signal distribution by incorporating a likelihood-based cost function. By relaxing the exact marginal constraint, the UOT framework provides key advantages to our model: robustness to multi-level observation noise, adaptability to class imbalance between noisy and clean datasets, and generalizability to diverse noise-type scenarios. Furthermore, we theoretically demonstrate that incorporating a quadratic cost term ensures the existence and uniqueness of the transport map by satisfying the twist condition, even for ill-posed inverse problems. Our experiments demonstrate that UOTIP achieves state-of-the-art performance on unpaired image inverse problem benchmarks, across linear and nonlinear inverse problems.

</details>


### [250] [A Typed Tensor Language for Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21103)
*Theofilos Mailis,Kalliopi-Christina Despotidou,Konstantinos Filippopolitis,Yannis Foufoulas,Thanasis-Michail Karampatsis,Andreas Ktenidis,Evdokia Mailli,Theodore Papamarkou,Yannis Ioannidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过提出一种类型化张量语言，将各类联邦学习协议统一为“编码-合并-解码”的数学结构，证明了其计算可通过固定维度的共享状态来实现，并为联邦可微编程提供了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前联邦学习和分析通常被视为一组零散的协议，缺乏统一的数学表示。作者旨在通过类型化张量语言将这些操作抽象为一个通用的计算框架，以简化分析、证明和实现。

Method: 提出了一种类型化张量语言（Typed Tensor Language），通过区分“联邦张量”和“全局共享张量”来形式化联邦学习过程。通过“共享状态因子分解理论”（Shared-State Factorization Theory）证明了程序的等价性，并开发了一个用于梯度计算的可微碎片（Differentiable Fragment）。

Result: 1. 证明了所有类型化单轮程序均可因子分解为大小与客户端/记录数无关的固定维度共享状态。
2. 建立了因子分解与“编码-合并-解码”流程之间的双向对应关系。
3. 提供了一套用于梯度下降和二阶优化的联邦可微计算范式。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了联邦学习计算的理论框架，证明了所有符合类型安全要求的单轮程序都可以简化为在固定维度共享状态下的“编码-合并-解码”模式，为联邦学习提供了正式的语义基础和可微分析范式。

Abstract: Federated learning and analytics are often described as collections of separate protocols, even when they share the same mathematical form: client-local tensor computation, mergeable aggregation into shared state, and shared-only post-processing. We introduce a typed tensor language that formalizes this structure. The language distinguishes federated tensors, whose records are partitioned across clients along a tracked record axis, from shared tensors, which are available globally. Its semantics are defined by comparison with a virtual global tensor, used only as a reference object. The main result is a shared-state factorization theory. We show that typed one-round programs factor through fixed-dimensional shared state whose size is independent of the number of clients and records, computed from client-local tensor expressions and merged across clients. We also prove a converse representability result; factorizations whose encoders and decoders are expressible in the language are realized by typed one-round programs, and the correspondence extends to iterative programs whose cross-round state is shared. This gives a formal account of the computations in the language that can be expressed as encode, merge, and decode procedures. We then develop a differentiable fragment for learning. If a per-record loss and its per-record gradient are represented by client-local tensor expressions, the global gradient is represented by record-axis summation of the federated gradient tensor. This yields typed iterative programs for server-side gradient descent and shared-linear-algebra second-order updates. The framework characterizes a broad class of federated learning computations whose communication passes through fixed-dimensional shared state.

</details>


### [251] [HORST: Composing Optimizer Geometries for Sparse Transformer Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21104)
*Tom Jacobs,Rohan Jain,Rebekka Burkholz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 HORST 优化器，通过非交换算子理论整合了自适应优化器的稳定性和双曲镜像映射的 L1 稀疏偏置，有效解决了 Transformer 稀疏化训练的稳定性与稀疏度平衡难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的自适应优化器（如 AdamW）具有 $L_\infty$ 偏置以保证训练稳定性，但不支持有效的 L1 稀疏化，导致难以同时实现 Transformer 的稀疏训练和训练稳定性。

Method: 提出了一种算子组合方法，将优化步骤视为非交换算子进行分析；设计了 HORST（Hyperbolic Operator for Robust Sparse Training）优化器，利用双曲镜像映射（Hyperbolic mirror map）在保证稳定性的同时引入 L1 稀疏偏差。

Result: 在视觉和语言任务的 Transformer 稀疏训练实验中，HORST 在所有稀疏度级别上均显著优于 AdamW 基准，特别是在高稀疏度下表现出更大增益。

Conclusion: HORST 是一个模块化优化器，通过将优化步骤建模为非交换算子，成功地将自适应优化器的稳定性与双曲镜像映射产生的 L1 稀疏度偏差结合，为 Transformer 的稀疏化训练提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Sparsifying transformers remains a fundamental challenge, as standard optimizers fail to simultaneously encourage sparsity and maintain training stability. Effective adaptive optimizers exhibit an implicit $L_{\infty}$ bias favoring stability, yet, sparsity requires an $L_1$ bias. To integrate sparsity, we propose a composition of optimizer steps, which we cast as non-commutative operators to analyze and combine their optimization geometry in a principled way. This yields HORST (Hyperbolic Operator for Robust Sparse Training), a modular optimizer that inherits stability from adaptive methods while inducing $L_1$ sparsity bias through a hyperbolic mirror map. Our experiments demonstrate its utility for sparse training of transformers on both vision and language tasks. HORST consistently and significantly outperforms AdamW baselines across all sparsity levels, with large gains at higher sparsity.

</details>


### [252] [PREFINE: Preference-Based Implicit Reward and Cost Fine-Tuning for Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21225)
*Richa Verma,Bavish Kulur,Sanjay Chawla,Balaraman Ravindran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PREFINE 是一种基于偏好的强化学习微调方法，利用 DPO 将预训练策略与安全约束对齐，能在高效保持奖励的同时显著降低系统违规及灾难性风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决如何在不全量重训练的前提下，通过提供的偏好（低成本/高成本轨迹）数据，使预训练的强化学习策略具备安全意识（即安全性约束）。

Method: 引入了 PREFINE（Preference-based Implicit Reward and Cost Fine-Tuning）方法，核心是针对连续控制环境调整了 DPO。通过构造策略采样的反事实轨迹（counterfactual trajectories）来建立明确的偏好对比，并联合优化以确保奖励保持和安全对齐。

Result: 在连续控制任务中，PREFINE 将约束违反和灾难性失败率降低了 60% 以上，同时保持了原始的高奖励性能，且在数据和计算效率上优于全量离线 RL 或模仿学习。

Conclusion: PREFINE 是一种有效的、计算高效的偏好驱动微调方法，能够引导预训练的强化学习策略在保持高奖励的同时显著提升安全性，证明了将大模型领域的直接偏好优化（DPO）迁移至连续控制任务进行安全对齐的可行性。

Abstract: We address the problem of making a pre-trained reinforcement learning (RL) policy safety-aware by incorporating cost constraints without retraining it from scratch.
