While costs could be numerically encoded, we assume a more general setting is when costs are provided as preferences. Given a reward-optimized policy and a small dataset of preferred (low-cost) and dispreferred (high-cost) trajectories, our goal is to fine-tune the policy to generate low-cost behaviors while retaining high rewards. Unlike standard RLHF in language models, where preferences are defined over responses to the same prompt, our setting involves trajectory-level preferences in continuous control environments. We introduce PREFINE: Preference-based Implicit Reward and Cost Fine-Tuning for Safety Alignment which is a preference-based fine-tuning method that adapts Direct Preference Optimization (DPO), which is now widely used for LLM fine-tuning, to the sequential decision making setting. PREFINE constructs policy-sampled counterfactual trajectories to establish meaningful preference contrasts and jointly optimizes for reward retention and safety alignment. Empirically, PREFINE reduces constraint violations and catastrophic failures by over 60% while maintaining original reward behavior. PREFINE produces policies that achieve low-cost, high-reward performance with significantly improved data and computational efficiency compared to full offline RL or imitation learning, bridging preference alignment and safe policy adaptation in continuous domains.

</details>


### [253] [OCTOPUS: Optimized KV Cache for Transformers via Octahedral Parametrization Under optimal Squared error quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21226)
*Mark Boss,Vikram Voleti,Simon Donné,Shimon Vainer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OCTOPUS 是一种创新的 KV 缓存压缩算法，通过对旋转后的三元组进行八面体参数化量化，实现了比现有旋转编码更优的压缩效果，且无额外计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文推断中 KV 缓存占用大量内存带宽和空间，现有的旋转预处理编码方式（如 TurboQuant、PolarQuant）虽具优势，但在压缩率和精度平衡上仍有提升空间。

Method: 提出了一种基于八面体参数化（octahedral parameterization）的联合量化方法，将旋转后的三元组映射到正方形，并结合非均匀位分配与 Lloyd-Max 量化。通过 Triton 实现算子融合，在解压过程中无需显式还原未压缩键。

Result: 在文本、视频和音频任务中，OCTOPUS 在所有位宽和指标下均优于现有的旋转编码方案，且在极端压缩场景下优势更明显；由于算子融合，该方法不增加额外的解码延迟和带宽成本。

Conclusion: OCTOPUS 是一种高效、通用的数据无关型 KV 缓存压缩技术，通过八面体参数化和三元组联合量化，在保持低延迟的同时实现了优于现有旋转编码器的压缩效果。

Abstract: The key-value (KV) cache dominates memory bandwidth and footprint in long-context autoregressive inference. Recent rotation-preconditioned codecs (TurboQuant, PolarQuant) show that a structured random rotation followed by a per-coordinate scalar quantizer matched to an analytically tractable marginal is a near-optimal recipe for KV compression. OCTOPUS advances this paradigm through joint quantization of rotated coordinate triplets. Each triplet's direction is mapped to a square via an octahedral parameterization, and the two resulting coordinates and the triplet norm are Lloyd-Max quantized against implementation-matched marginals. Optimizing the per-triplet squared error gives a strictly non-uniform bit allocation depending only on the total dimensionality of the keys. We find the finite-dimensional quality optimum with sweeps to be constant on every real decoder we test. The codec is data-oblivious, online, and deterministic given a seed. Across text, video, and audio, OCTOPUS matches or beats every prior rotation codec at every reported bit width and metric, with a lead that grows as bits drop for extreme compression. Furthermore, a fused Triton implementation reconstructs keys on the fly without materializing the uncompressed key, so the codec adds no decode-time bandwidth or latency over the existing dequantization. Project Page: https://octopus-quant.github.io/

</details>


### [254] [Advantage Collapse in Group Relative Policy Optimization: Diagnosis and Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21125)
*Xixiang He,Qiyao Sun,Ao Cheng,Xingming Li,Xuanyu Ji,Hailun Lu,Runke Huang,Qingyong Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了ACR指标用于量化GRPO中的梯度失效问题，并开发了AVSPO算法通过动态注入虚拟样本，在不增加推理成本的情况下有效缓解了优势坍塌，显著提升了LLM的推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO算法在处理奖励同质化（全对或全错）的训练组时，容易出现优势归零和梯度消失的问题，从而导致推理性能提升停滞。

Method: 1. 提出优势坍塌率（ACR）作为量化无效梯度训练批次的诊断指标；2. 设计自适应虚拟样本策略优化（AVSPO），根据ACR监测结果动态注入虚拟奖励样本，无需额外模型前向推理。

Result: 实验表明ACR能准确预测性能退化；AVSPO将优势坍塌减少了58-63%，在各参数规模模型上实现了4-6个百分点的准确率提升。

Conclusion: AVSPO通过引入虚拟样本注入机制，有效解决了GRPO中的优势坍塌（Advantage Collapse）问题，在保持泛化能力的前提下，显著提升了数学推理任务中的模型性能。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), a prominent algorithm within the Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) framework, has achieved strong results in improving the reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs). However, GRPO is prone to advantage collapse, a failure mode where homogeneous rewards within a group (e.g., all correct or all incorrect answers) yield near-zero advantages and vanishing gradients. To address this, we introduce the Advantage Collapse Rate (ACR), the first diagnostic metric quantifying the proportion of training batches with ineffective gradients. Across models from 0.5B to 14B parameters on mathematical reasoning benchmarks, we show that ACR strongly predicts training stagnation and final performance. We then propose Adaptive Virtual Sample Policy Optimization (AVSPO), a lightweight extension of GRPO that injects virtual reward samples, guided by real-time ACR monitoring, to enable learning from homogeneous groups without additional model rollouts. AVSPO reduces advantage collapse by 58-63% relative to GRPO and yields consistent accuracy gains of 4-6 percentage points across all model scales, while maintaining generalization on the evaluated out-of-domain task. Code and datasets are available at https://qingyonghu.github.io/AVSPO.

</details>


### [255] [Reasoning-Trace Collapse: Evaluating the Loss of Explicit Reasoning During Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21127)
*Lukas Twist,Helen Yannakoudakis,Jie M. Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究指出推理模型微调过程中常出现“推理轨迹坍塌”现象，即模型能答对问题但丧失了逻辑推导过程，并提出通过结构化评估和损失遮蔽策略来监测和缓解这一问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示了推理模型在下游任务微调过程中，由于训练数据缺乏推理轨迹，导致模型虽然能给出正确答案，但丧失了原本具备的显式推理能力（即“推理轨迹坍塌”）。

Method: 提出“结构化评估框架”以分离答案正确性和推理轨迹有效性；研究四种开源推理模型在标准监督微调下的表现；比较传统答案评估与结构化评估的差异；实验验证通过损失遮蔽（loss-masking）策略缓解坍塌。

Result: 标准监督微调会迅速抑制模型的有效推理轨迹；传统的仅靠答案的评估指标掩盖了推理能力的退化；实验证明简单的损失遮蔽策略可以在无需教师数据的情况下有效减缓坍塌。

Conclusion: 微调推理模型时，仅关注最终答案会导致“推理轨迹坍塌”。评估方法应从简单的答案评估转向包含结构化推理质量的综合评估，且使用简单的损失遮蔽（loss-masking）策略可有效缓解该问题。

Abstract: Explicit reasoning models are trained to produce intermediate reasoning traces before final answers, but downstream fine-tuning is often performed on ordinary instruction-response data that contains no such traces. We show that this mismatch can induce reasoning-trace collapse: a fine-tuned model continues to produce plausible final answers while losing the structurally valid explicit reasoning traces that made it a reasoning model in the first place. We introduce a structural evaluation framework that separates answer correctness from reasoning-trace validity, measuring valid, empty, missing, and truncated reasoning alongside reasoning-conditioned task performance. Using this framework, we study four open-weight reasoning models and find that standard supervised fine-tuning can rapidly suppress valid reasoning traces, and that answer-only metrics can substantially obscure this failure: in several settings, performance conditional on valid reasoning remains high while the rate of valid reasoning falls sharply. We further show that simple loss-masking strategies can substantially mitigate collapse without requiring teacher-generated reasoning traces. These results suggest that evaluations of fine-tuned reasoning models should report structural reasoning reliability metrics in addition to final-answer performance, especially when adaptation data does not contain explicit reasoning traces.

</details>


### [256] [How Much Online RL is Enough? Informative Rollouts for Offline Preference Optimization in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21266)
*Richa Verma,Balaraman Ravindran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: G2D是一种将在线RL（GRPO）预热与离线训练（DPO）相结合的新型训练范式；它通过“适度预热+高效离线微调”的策略，以极低的算力成本实现了优于纯在线RL方法的推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在线RL方法（如GRPO）计算开销大且难以扩展；传统的离线方法（如DPO）虽高效，但通常被认为在冷启动SFT策略上的表现逊于在线RL。旨在寻找一种既高效又高性能的RLVR训练范式。

Method: 提出G2D三阶段流水线：1. 短期GRPO在线预热；2. 构建静态偏好数据集；3. 进行离线DPO微调。探究了预热步数（K）对模型性能的影响及数据信息量机制。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-7B和Llama-3.1-8B模型上，G2D显著优于同等条件下的GRPO。在Qwen2.5-7B上，G2D以约4倍的效率提升实现了MATH-500跑分10.8%的超越。实验发现，模型性能取决于数据的“信息量”而非偏好对数量，适度的预热能产生更有利的对比信号。

Conclusion: 将RLVR（强化学习验证奖励）中的离线-在线性能鸿沟重新定义为“数据信息量”问题。提出了一种名为G2D的计算高效方案，证明了通过适度的在线强化学习预热并结合离线DPO微调，能够在显著降低计算成本的同时，达到甚至超越纯在线RL（如GRPO）的性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for reasoning in language models, with GRPO as its primary example. However, GRPO requires continuous online rollout generation, making it computationally expensive and difficult to scale. While Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) offers a stable and efficient offline alternative, it is typically expected to underperform w.r.t. online RL methods such as GRPO when trained on rollouts from a cold supervised fine-tuned (SFT) policy. We introduce G2D (GRPO to DPO)}, a three-stage pipeline that performs a short GRPO warm-up, constructs a static preference dataset, and fine-tunes a model offline with DPO. Across a set of values of the number of online steps (K) in GRPO on Qwen2.5-7B and Llama-3.1-8B, we find that offline DPO with moderate warm-up matches or outperforms GRPO at substantially lower compute cost in our setting. On Qwen2.5-7B, G2D at K=150 achieves 62.4% on MATH-500, outperforming GRPO (51.6%) by 10.8% at ~4x lower compute. On Llama-3.1-8B, G2D at K=500 achieves 49.4%, surpassing GRPO in our experimental setting. We show that performance is not governed by the number of preference pairs, which does not vary much w.r.t. K, but by their informativeness. Moderate warm-up produces rollouts with calibrated uncertainty, yielding stronger contrastive signal, while excessive warm-up leads to overconfident policies and less informative data. Our results recast the offline-online gap in RLVR as primarily a data informativeness problem, and identify short online RL warm-up with appropriate difficulty calibration of the fine-tuning dataset as a compute-efficient alternative to online RL.

</details>


### [257] [Learning First Integrals via Backward-Generated Data and Guided Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21160)
*Jingfeng Zhong,Zhengxiang Liu,Zhijie Wang,Shuai Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了FISolver，通过反向生成算法解决训练数据匮乏问题，并结合监督微调与强化学习，实现了一种在发现动力系统第一积分任务上高效、准确的新型AI数学求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发现动力系统的第一积分（守恒律）对于科学研究至关重要，但现有符号计算工具和通用LLM受限于高质量训练数据的匮乏及对数学直觉的特殊需求，难以高效求解。

Method: （1）“反向生成”（Backward Generation）算法：通过先采样第一个积分再推导微分方程，构建大规模训练数据集；（2）针对小型数学模型进行监督微调（SFT）；（3）基于Levenshtein距离的奖励函数进行强化学习（RL）优化；（4）设计了数据合成与混合策略，以应对稀有困难问题的迁移学习。

Result: FISolver在处理挑战性基准测试时，表现显著优于大型数学LLM（如GPT-4等）及商业软件（如Mathematica），且计算成本大幅降低。

Conclusion: FISolver证明了一种全新的数据驱动方法，能有效克服传统符号计算在发现动力系统守恒律任务中的局限性，其在低计算成本下超越了大型数学LLM和商业软件。

Abstract: The discovery of first integrals is of fundamental scientific importance for understanding conservation laws in dynamical systems. However, existing symbolic computation tools and Large Language Models (LLMs) remain limited on this task because high-quality training data are scarce and successful solutions often depend on mathematical intuition. This paper presents FISolver, an LLM-based solver developed to address this challenge. First, we introduce a "Backward Generation" algorithm that systematically builds large-scale datasets of (differential equation, first integral) pairs by deriving differential equations from sampled integrals, thereby alleviating the data scarcity bottleneck. Second, we apply supervised fine-tuning to a compact mathematical model and further improve its performance through reinforcement learning with a Levenshtein Distance-based shaped reward. In addition, we design data synthesis and blending strategies that support effective adaptation to difficult problem families from sparse examples. Experiments show that FISolver, while requiring substantially lower computational cost, significantly outperforms larger mathematical LLMs and commercial solvers such as Mathematica on challenging benchmarks, indicating a new data-driven route for automated discovery of first integrals.

</details>


### [258] [TimeSRL: Generalizable Time-Series Behavioral Modeling via Semantic RL-Tuned LLMs -- A Case Study in Mental Health](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21295)
*Yuang Fan,Lilin Xu,Millie Wu,Jingping Nie,Qingyu Chen,Yuzhe Yang,Zhuo Zhang,Xin Liu,Subigya Nepal,Xiaofan Jiang,Xuhai "Orson" Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TimeSRL框架，通过将原始时间序列传感数据转化为“语义抽象”作为中间瓶颈，并利用强化学习进行端到端优化，显著提升了心理健康预测在跨数据集分布偏移场景下的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向被动传感在跨数据集分布偏移（Distribution Shift）场景下，传统机器学习易过拟合特定群体产物，而LLM难以处理长且异构的时间序列。亟需一种更具泛化能力的建模方法。

Method: 建立了一个名为TimeSRL的两阶段LLM框架：1. 将原始传感信号抽象为高层自然语言（语义瓶颈）；2. 基于这些语义抽象预测行为结果。利用群组相对策略优化（GRPO）结合来自可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）进行端到端优化，无需人工标注中间过程。

Result: 在心理健康预测的留一数据集（LOSO）评估中，该模型在焦虑与抑郁预测上的平均绝对误差（MAE）较传统ML降低了3.1-10.1%和3.2-9.6%，较LLM基线降低了9.5-44.1%和27.4-57.6%，展现出强大的跨数据集迁移性能。

Conclusion: 语义抽象（Semantic Abstractions）能够跨数据集迁移且具有鲁棒性，基于强化学习微调的LLM提供了一种实现可泛化行为建模的新范式。

Abstract: Longitudinal passive sensing enables continuous health prediction, yet models often fail under cross-dataset distribution shifts. Traditional ML overfits cohort-specific artifacts, while Large Language Models (LLMs) struggle to reason reliably over long, heterogeneous time-series. We introduce TimeSRL, a two-stage LLM framework that routes predictions through an explicit semantic bottleneck. The model first abstracts raw signals into high-level natural language, then predicts behavioral outcomes from these abstractions alone. This forces the model to reason over semantic concepts that we argue generalize better than raw numbers. We optimize this process end-to-end using Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), learning outcome-aligned abstractions without gold intermediate annotations. Instantiated on mental-health prediction, TimeSRL achieves state-of-the-art performance on a benchmark designed to stress-test cross-cohort generalization under a rigorous leave-one-dataset-out (LOSO) protocol, reducing mean absolute error (MAE) over strong non-LLM ML and LLM baselines by 3.1--10.1% and 9.5--44.1% for anxiety, and 3.2--9.6% and 27.4--57.6% for depression (all $p$s<0.05). TimeSRL significantly outperforms prior methods in cross-benchmark transfer across different sensing pipelines, rivaling its own within-domain performance without target-domain fine-tuning. These results demonstrate that semantic abstractions are reusable and point to a new direction for generalizable behavior modeling via RL-tuned LLMs.

</details>


### [259] [Domain-Adaptable Reinforcement Learning for Code Generation with Dense Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21180)
*Erfan Aghadavoodi Jolfaei,Daniel Maninger,Abhinav Anand,Mert Tiftikci,Mira Mezini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于强化学习的框架，通过执行感知奖励和词元级信用分配微调大语言模型，显著提升了通用及机器人领域代码生成的正确性与可执行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽有较强的代码生成能力，但在功能正确性、安全性及特定领域（如机器人环境中的物理约束）规范上缺乏保障。

Method: 提出了一种基于近端策略优化（PPO）的强化学习框架。该方法引入了可定制的执行感知奖励公式来衡量语法、功能正确性、代码风格、安全性和模拟器可执行性，并设计了一种词元级（token-level）的奖励映射机制，以实现从执行结果到生成词元的有效信用分配。

Result: 在通用代码生成任务（MBPP/MBPP+）上，pass@1 指标提升了 19%；在机器人程序合成任务（RoboEval）中，执行失败率降低了 51%。

Conclusion: 结构化强化学习框架能够有效将预训练大语言模型对齐，使其在代码生成中满足功能正确性和特定领域需求，对于提升机器人程序合成任务的可靠性至关重要。

Abstract: Large language models show strong potential for automated code generation, but lack guarantees for correctness, quality, safety, and domain-specific constraints. For instance in robotics, where code generation is increasingly being used for planning and executing actions, awareness of the environment and physical constraints is critical. To facilitate the adaption of code-generating LLMs to diverse requirements, including domain-specific ones, we present a reinforcement learning framework that fine-tunes pre-trained LLMs using proximal policy optimization. Our customizable execution-aware reward formula captures and optimizes syntax, functional correctness, code style, security, and simulator executability. A token-level reward mapping mechanism enables effective credit assignment from execution outcomes to generated tokens. The framework is evaluated on general-purpose code generation (MBPP/MBPP+) and robotic program synthesis (RoboEval). The results show substantial improvements in functional correctness and simulator executability, including an absolute pass@1 increase of 19% on MBPP and a reduction in execution failures by 51% on RoboEval. These findings demonstrate that structured reinforcement learning can effectively align language models to correct program generation and domain-specific requirements.

</details>


### [260] [From Circuit Evidence to Mechanistic Theory: An Inductive Logic Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21303)
*Nura Aljaafari,Danilo S. Carvalho,Andre Freitas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过结合因果分析与归纳逻辑编程，构建了一套统一的电路特征化框架，实现了神经元电路在解释层面上的可比较化与跨模型迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机械可解释性往往产生孤立的实验结果，缺乏统一的形式化框架来描述电路的计算逻辑、关联性及其在不同模型间的普适性，难以形成累积性的科学知识。

Method: 1. Causal Functional Signature (CFS): 通过因果归因证据和Token角色配置来定义组件行为。2. 架构签名 ($\tau_{\mathrm{arch}}$): 利用归纳逻辑编程（ILP）从尺度不变的结构谓词中学习电路的结构特征。3. 形式一致性层: 通过 $\theta$-归纳（$\theta$-subsumption）实现机械化声明的显性化、可比性和跨模型迁移。

Result: CFS能够揭示跨任务的不同计算策略（如注意力机制的复制 vs MLP的绑定）；ILP签名在结构分离度上显著优于图核和特征向量基准，并支持跨模型尺度和架构族的原则性迁移。

Conclusion: 通过引入“因果功能签名（CFS）”和基于归纳逻辑编程（ILP）的架构签名，该研究为神经元电路的机制解释建立了形式化、可比较且可扩展的基础，推动了机械可解释性从零散的实验转向累积性的科学研究。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability produces circuit-level causal analyses of neural network behaviour, but discovered circuits often remain isolated experimental artefacts: there is no shared formal representation for what circuits compute, how they relate, or when two findings provide evidence for the same mechanism. This work provides a formal infrastructure for cumulative mechanistic science by treating circuit interpretation as inductive theory construction. Each circuit is characterised at two levels: a Causal Functional Signature (CFS), which grounds component behaviour in causal attribution evidence and token role profiles, and an architectural signature $τ_{\mathrm{arch}}$, learned by inductive logic programming (ILP) from scale-invariant structural predicates. Together, these constitute a formal coherence layer that makes mechanistic claims explicit, comparable via $θ$-subsumption, and portable across model scales. CFS reveals qualitatively distinct computational strategies across task types, including attention-mediated copying versus MLP-mediated binding. ILP signatures achieve substantially better structural separation than graph kernel and feature-vector baselines, and support principled transfer across model scales and architecture families.

</details>


### [261] [Divide and Contrast: Learning Robust Temporal Features without Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21241)
*Abdul-Kazeem Shamba,Kerstin Bach,Gavin Taylor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Di-COT 是一种高效的时间序列自监督学习框架，通过将序列窗口拆分为对比的子块而非单点，在提升表示学习效果的同时实现了比现有方法更快的训练速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的时间序列自监督学习方法往往计算成本过高，或者过度依赖在处理复杂时间动态时可能失效的假设（如固定的预测任务或特定的时间平稳性假设）。

Method: 提出了一种名为 Divide and Contrast (Di-COT) 的自监督学习框架：1. 抛弃传统的数据增强和多次编码器前向传播，通过将滑动窗口随机划分为重叠的子块进行对比；2. 采用了一种与序列长度无关的对比损失函数，显著提高了训练效率。

Result: 在六个大规模真实数据集及 UCR/UEA 基准测试上，Di-COT 在分类、聚类、kNN 和跨数据集迁移任务中均达到最优性能，并大幅缩短了训练时间。

Conclusion: Di-COT 通过针对时间序列的“分块对比”策略，成功在降低计算成本的同时提升了表示学习的质量，在多项基准测试中达到了 SOTA 水平。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning for time-series representation aims to reduce reliance on labeled data while maintaining strong downstream performance, yet many existing approaches incur high computational costs or rely on assumptions that do not hold across diverse temporal dynamics. In this work, we introduce Divide and Contrast (Di-COT), an unsupervised framework that avoids data augmentation and multiple encoder passes by contrasting informative substructures within a window rather than individual timesteps. Di-COT stochastically partitions each window into a small number of overlapping sub-blocks per iteration, enabling efficient and meaningful contrast while mitigating false positives during temporal transitions. To further improve scalability, we adopt a contrastive objective whose computation depends on the batch size and the number of sub-blocks, making loss computation independent of sequence length. Extensive experiments on six large-scale real-world datasets, as well as the UCR and UEA benchmarks, demonstrate that Di-COT learns semantically structured and transferable representations, achieving state-of-the-art performance on classification, clustering, $k$NN, and cross-dataset transfer, while substantially reducing training time. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/sfi-norwai/Di-COT.

</details>


### [262] [Data-Efficient Neural Operator Training via Physics-Based Active Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21348)
*Alicja Polanska,Lorenzo Zanisi,Vignesh Gopakumar,Stanislas Pamela*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种物理驱动的主动学习算法，利用PDE残差引导样本选择，在显著提高神经算子训练数据效率的同时，引入物理归纳偏置以提升模型的物理准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然神经算子显著降低了求解偏微分方程的计算成本，但其训练过程高度依赖大量的标注数据。作者旨在利用主动学习方法减少数据需求，并提高模型在物理求解空间中的数据利用效率。

Method: 提出了一种基于偏微分方程（PDE）残差的“物理驱动获取策略”（Physics-based acquisition），通过迭代引导数据采样，引导模型专注于在物理上最难以拟合的区域进行学习。

Result: 在1D Burgers方程和2D可压缩Navier-Stokes方程的实验中，证明了该方法在数据效率上持续优于随机采样，并达到了领域内先进水平。

Conclusion: 基于物理的获取策略不仅在数据效率上达到先进水平，还通过引入物理归纳偏置，确保计算资源集中于模型物理理解最薄弱的区域。

Abstract: Solving partial differential equations with neural operators significantly reduces computational costs but remains bottlenecked by high training data requirements. Active learning offers a natural framework to mitigate this by selectively acquiring the most informative samples in an iterative manner. We introduce physics-based acquisition - a novel physics-informed active learning algorithm that leverages the partial differential equation residual to guide data selection. We validate the method by presenting numerical experiments for the 1D Burgers equation and the 2D compressible Navier-Stokes equations. We show that, in our experiments, physics-based acquisition consistently outperforms random acquisition and matches the state of the art in data efficiency. At the same time, it has the unique advantage of injecting a physics inductive bias into the training process, ensuring that simulation cost is spent where the model's physical understanding is weakest.

</details>


### [263] [On the Cost and Benefit of Chain of Thought: A Learning-Theoretic Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21260)
*Yue Zhang,Zhiyi Dong,Tommaso Cesari,Yongyi Mao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过学习理论框架量化了CoT的利弊：OTR体现了CoT的推理增益，而TMR反映了因推理轨迹偏差导致的错误累积成本；研究表明，系统稳定性是决定CoT是提升性能还是导致逻辑崩溃的核心因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺乏对思维链（CoT）工作机制的系统化理论理解，特别是难以直观解释CoT在何种条件下有效、在何种条件下产生负面影响（幻觉或逻辑错误）。

Method: 构建了一个学习理论框架，将CoT建模为答案映射与自回归生成中间问题的“链式规则”之间的交互，通过风险分解（Canonical Decomposition）分析，推导出OTR和TMR两个核心风险项，并利用稳定性分析探讨了错误累积的边界。

Result: 1. 证明了CoT风险可分解为代表收益的OTR和代表代价的TMR。2. 指出如果损失函数、答案映射或链式规则缺乏稳定性，即便OTR为零，TMR也可能变得任意大（即错误累积）。3. 给出了在稳定性条件下TMR的严格上界，并识别出三种误差增长状态：有界控制、线性增长、指数增长。

Conclusion: CoT的有效性取决于“谕示轨迹风险（OTR）”与“轨迹失配风险（TMR）”之间的权衡。稳定性是控制TMR、防止错误累积的关键因素，决定了推理过程是保持受控、线性增长还是呈指数级崩溃。

Abstract: We develop a learning-theoretic framework for understanding Chain of Thought (CoT). We model CoT as the interaction between an answer map and a chain rule that generates intermediate questions autoregressively, and define the reasoning risk of a hypothesis under this interaction. Our first result is a tight canonical decomposition of this risk into two terms with opposing roles: an oracle-trajectory risk (OTR), which captures the benefit of CoT and reduces to a target-domain risk in a domain adaptation problem, and a trajectory-mismatch risk (TMR), which captures the cost of CoT through error accumulation along mismatched reasoning trajectories. We then show that this cost is unavoidable without structure: if any one of the loss, the hypothesis answer map, or the chain rule lacks stability, the TMR can be arbitrarily large even when the OTR is zero and the hypothesis is uniformly close to the ground truth. Conversely, under stability, we prove a tight upper bound on the TMR governed by an exact amplification factor that identifies bounded, linear, and exponential error-growth regimes. Together, these results give a precise theory of when CoT helps, when it hurts, and what controls the transition between the two.

</details>


### [264] [Nonparametric Learning and Earning with One-Point Feedback under Nonstationarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21263)
*Xiangyu Yang,Feng Xu,Jian-Qiang Hu,Jiaqiao Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对需求非参数化、观测极其受限且市场环境动态变化的难题，提出了一种基于收入梯度近似与自适应重启机制的动态定价算法，实现了高效的“边学边赚”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业在动态定价中面临两个核心难题：一是在每个周期仅能观测到单个价格对应的收入（反馈极度受限），二是在市场环境（客户偏好、竞争等）发生非平稳变化时如何快速适应。

Method: 1. 开发了一种仅基于单次观测的收入梯度近似方法来进行价格更新；2. 设计了定期重启机制来过滤过时信息；3. 引入元学习层，在多种重启调度之间进行自适应切换，以应对非平稳性程度未知的情况。

Result: 理论分析证明了该方法在总收入损失方面的性能保证；通过合成数据和真实世界数据的仿真实验，验证了所提方法在不同市场环境下的有效性和优越性。

Conclusion: 该论文提出的基于梯度近似和重启机制的学习框架，能够有效地在需求不确定和环境动态变化的约束下实现定价优化，且在未知非平稳性程度时通过元学习层表现出鲁棒性。

Abstract: Firms increasingly rely on dynamic pricing to respond to evolving customer demand, yet in many applications they observe only the revenue generated by a single posted price in each period. At the same time, market conditions may shift gradually or abruptly due to changes in customer preferences, competition, or external shocks. These features create two intertwined challenges: learning the revenue--demand relationship from limited feedback and adapting pricing decisions to a changing environment. We study how a seller can learn and earn effectively under these constraints, without assuming a specific parametric form for demand. We develop a learning framework that updates prices using revenue-based gradient approximations constructed from one observation per period. To address environmental changes, we incorporate a restarting mechanism that periodically refreshes the learning process so that outdated information is discounted. When the degree of nonstationarity is unknown, we further introduce a meta-learning layer to adaptively hedge across multiple restarting schedules. We provide performance guarantees for our approach, showing how cumulative revenue loss relative to a fully informed benchmark depends on both the time horizon and the magnitude of market variation. Simulation experiments using synthetic and real-world data illustrate the effectiveness of the proposed procedures.

</details>


### [265] [torchtune: PyTorch native post-training library](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21442)
*Mark Obozov,Maxime Griot,Joseph Cummings,Evan Smothers,Felipe Mello,Rafi Ayub,Philip John Bontrager,Salman Mohammadi,Ariel Kwiatkowski,Nathan Azrak,Mircea Mironenco*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Meta 开源了 torchtune，这是一个侧重于模块化设计、代码透明度及可扩展性的 PyTorch 原生 LLM 微调库，旨在平衡高性能与研究迭代的灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 微调框架通常在易用性、硬件性能或代码透明度之间进行权衡，缺乏一个既能兼顾高性能又具备高度灵活性和可调试性的 PyTorch 原生工具。

Method: 基于 PyTorch 原生构建，强调模块化和可扩展性（hackability）；设计了专门的模型构建器、训练配方（recipes）和分布式训练栈。

Result: 实验表明，torchtune 在多项代表性微调基准测试中展现出与 Axolotl 和 Unsloth 相当甚至更优的性能和显存效率，同时具备更强的灵活性。

Conclusion: torchtune 是一个高效、模块化且易于扩展的 PyTorch 原生 LLM 后训练库，为大模型的研究、实验和部署提供了一个理想的基石。

Abstract: Modern LLMs typically require multistage training pipelines to achieve strong downstream performance, with post-training serving as the main interface for adapting open-weight models. We introduce torchtune, a PyTorch-native library designed to streamline the post-training lifecycle of LLMs, enabling efficient fine-tuning, experimentation, and deployment-oriented workflows. Unlike many existing fine-tuning frameworks, which often optimize for ease of use, specialized recipes, or hardware efficiency at the cost of transparency and extensibility, torchtune emphasizes modularity, hackability, and direct access to the underlying PyTorch components. In this paper, we present the design principles behind torchtune, describe how they are reflected in its model builders, training recipes, and distributed training stack, and evaluate the library across representative post-training settings. We compare against popular fine-tuning frameworks, including Axolotl and Unsloth, and show that torchtune provides strong performance and memory efficiency across many settings while remaining flexible enough for rapid research iteration. These results position torchtune as a practical foundation for reproducible LLMs post-training research.

</details>


### [266] [A Mechanistic Study of Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21288)
*Marin Biloš,James T. Wilson,Anderson Schneider,Yuriy Nevmyvaka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过因果干预和机制分析，揭示了表格基础模型并非表现趋同便意味着逻辑一致，而是通过不同的相似性读取机制工作，并识别了影响其精度与鲁棒性的核心特征与脆弱点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管不同架构的表格基础模型在各类任务中表现趋同，但对其内部工作机制（如算法执行的一致性、不变性的来源及鲁棒性）缺乏深入理解，目前的排行榜评估无法回答这些关于机制的问题。

Method: 采用因果干预（causal intervention）分析模型的输出机制；通过移除特定位置参数验证排列不变性的来源；利用针对推断机制设计的扰动（如中心攻击和排序攻击）测试模型的鲁棒性。

Result: 1. 不同模型采用了质量上有区别的相似性读取方式（如加权投票与条件均值读取）；2. 排列不变性源于特定位置参数，移除后可实现精确不变性；3. 模型在针对性扰动下会发生预期的性能失效，证实了其潜在的脆弱性。

Conclusion: 现代表格基础模型在缺乏对底层机制理解的情况下，依靠不同的相似性读取（similarity-based readouts）实现了一致的准确率。研究明确了这些模型的归纳偏置、排列不变性的来源以及特定的失效模式，标志着对机器学习模型机制理解的进步。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models with different architectures converge in accuracy across a range of classification and regression tasks. This raises questions a leaderboard cannot answer: (i) whether the models execute the same in-context algorithm, (ii) where row, column, and class-permutation invariances originate, and (iii) how robust they are under perturbations engineered against the inferred mechanism. We characterize all three. The model families realize qualitatively distinct similarity-based readouts: from an attention-weighted vote over context labels to a class-conditional mean readout, each confirmed by causal intervention. We find that the representation collapse highlighted in prior work is not a practical concern for them. Each model's permutation invariances trace to specific positional parameters whose removal preserves accuracy and makes approximate invariance exact. Perturbations engineered against each readout reproduce predicted failure modes; hub and rank attacks isolate them from refit baselines. Together these results give a mechanistic account of contemporary tabular foundation models and identify which inductive biases govern both their accuracy and characteristic failures.

</details>


### [267] [Automatic Discovery of Disease Subgroups by Contrasting with Healthy Controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21301)
*Robin Louiset,Edouard Duchesnay,Benoit Dufumier,Antoine Grigis,Pietro Gori*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 Deep UCSL 的对比子组发现方法，通过对比患者与健康对照组，滤除共有杂质并提取纯粹由病理因素驱动的可解释患者亚组。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生物医学领域，现有的子组发现方法往往无法区分“病理特异性变异”与“健康对照组共有的背景变异”，导致提取的患者亚组缺乏足够的临床可解释性与同质性。

Method: 提出了 Deep UCSL 框架：使用深度特征提取器学习表征空间，设计了一种基于联合潜变量与类别似然函数的对比损失函数，并通过期望最大化（EM）算法交替更新亚组推断与特征编码器，同时引入正则化项以剥离与健康对照组共有的背景变异。

Result: 在 MNIST 示例和四个真实的医学影像数据集上进行了验证，结果表明该方法在定量指标（如亚组质量）上优于现有相关方法。

Conclusion: Deep UCSL 有效地隔离了与病理相关的变异，能够发现比现有方法更具可解释性和同质性的亚组，在多个医疗影像数据集上表现出优越性。

Abstract: In biomedical Subgroup Discovery, practitioners are interested in discovering interpretable and homogeneous subgroups within a group of patients. In this paper, assuming that healthy subjects (i.e., controls) share common but irrelevant factors of variation with the patients, we motivate and develop a Contrastive Subgroup Discovery method, entitled Deep UCSL. By contrasting patients with controls, Deep UCSL identifies subgroups driven solely by pathological factors, ignoring common variability shared with healthy subjects. Our framework employs a deep feature extractor to learn a discriminative representation space. Mathematically, we derive a novel loss based on the conditional joint likelihood of latent clusters and patient/control labels, optimized via an Expectation-Maximization strategy alternating between subgroup inference and feature encoder updates. A regularization term further encourages representations to capture disease-specific variability while ignoring variability shared with controls. Compared to previous related works, our approach quantitatively improves the quality of the estimated subgroups, as demonstrated on a MNIST example and four distinct real medical imaging datasets. Code and datasets are available at: https://github.com/rlouiset/deep_ucsl.

</details>


### [268] [CRAFT: Conflict-Resolved Aggregation for Federated Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21317)
*Ziqi Wang,Qiang Liu,Nils Thuerey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对联邦学习中数据异构引发的客户端更新冲突，提出CRAFT框架，通过几何投影约束和闭式求解，高效解决冲突并实现更公平、准确的模型聚合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在联邦学习中，由于异构数据分布，不同客户端的更新存在冲突。传统的全局模型平均聚合方式会导致“全局提升但部分客户端性能下降”的问题，损害了系统的整体性能及公平性。

Method: 提出CRAFT框架，将聚合问题建模为几何修正问题，旨在寻找一个满足无冲突对齐约束且最接近参考方向的更新。推导了该约束优化问题的闭式解（Closed-form expression），并采用层级自适应（Layer-wise adaptation）策略处理不同粒度的冲突。

Result: 理论分析表明CRAFT促进了共同下降（common-descent）结构并缓解了冲突。在异构基准测试上的实验结果显示，CRAFT在提升全局模型精度的同时，最小化了不同客户端之间的性能差异，且避免了迭代求解器带来的高计算开销。

Conclusion: CRAFT通过几何约束有效解决了联邦学习中异构数据带来的客户端冲突问题，既提升了全局模型精度，又显著增强了公平性，且在推理层面具备计算高效性。

Abstract: The aggregation of conflicting client updates remains a fundamental bottleneck in federated learning (FL) under heterogeneous data distributions. Naive averaging can produce a global update that improves the global objective while conflicting with specific clients, causing degradation for those clients. In this work, we propose CRAFT (Conflict-Resolved Aggregation for Federated Training), a new aggregation framework that treats the global update as a geometric correction problem. We formulate aggregation as finding the update closest to a reference direction while satisfying conflict-free alignment constraints. We derive a closed-form expression for the constrained optimization problem, avoiding the computational overhead of iterative solvers. Furthermore, we use a layer-wise adaptation to address conflicts at varying feature granularities. We provide a theoretical analysis showing that CRAFT promotes a common-descent structure and mitigates conflicts through its projection geometry. Extensive experiments on heterogeneous benchmarks demonstrate that CRAFT improves the accuracy of the global model while reducing performance disparity across clients compared with state-of-the-art baselines. The source code for CRAFT is available at https://github.com/tum-pbs/CRAFT.

</details>


### [269] [Optimized Federated Knowledge Distillation with Distributed Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21322)
*Chaimaa Medjadji,Sylvain Kubler,Yves Le Traon,Guilain Leduc,Sadi Alawadi,Feras M. Awaysheh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种名为 FedKDNAS 的框架，通过集成神经架构选择与模型知识蒸馏，实现了联邦学习在异构环境下的高效率、高精度协同训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习方案多假设固定模型架构，难以同时应对现实中的数据异构性（Non-IID）、系统设备性能差异以及通信带宽限制，导致精度与效率之间难以取得平衡。

Method: 提出 FedKDNAS 框架，核心机制包括：1. 客户端在资源限制下自动选择轻量级架构；2. 采用混合学习目标（监督学习+知识蒸馏）；3. 仅共享评估集上的预测结果（Logits），实现轻量级通信；4. 服务端通过聚合和平滑预测结果生成稳定的蒸馏目标。

Result: 在六个数据集上对比六种主流基线，FedKDNAS 在 Non-IID 条件下精度提升最高达 15%，CPU 使用率降低约 28%，通信开销减少最高达 44 倍。

Conclusion: FedKDNAS 证明了结合客户端神经架构搜索（NAS）与知识蒸馏（KD）可以有效解决联邦学习中的系统与统计异构性问题，在提升模型精度的同时，显著降低了计算与通信开销。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables collaborative model training without centralizing data. However, real-world deployments must simultaneously address statistical heterogeneity across client data (non-IID), system heterogeneity in device capabilities, and communication efficiency. Existing FL approaches mitigate these challenges through improved aggregation, personalization, or knowledge distillation, but they almost universally assume a fixed client architecture, limiting adaptability to heterogeneous data complexity and hardware constraints. This architectural constraint often leads to suboptimal trade-offs between accuracy and efficiency in real-world FL systems. This work introduces FedKDNAS, a distillation-driven FL framework that combines client-side neural architecture selection with distillation of server-coordinated knowledge. Each client autonomously selects a lightweight model under accuracy-resource constraints. It then trains it locally using a hybrid objective combining supervised learning and knowledge distillation and shares only predictions on a public reference set. The server then aggregates and smooths these predictions, optionally combining them with a teacher model, to produce stable distillation targets for the next round. Extensive evaluation on six datasets against six representative FL baselines (FedAvg, Ditto, FedMD, FedDF, FedDistill, Local-KD) demonstrates that FedKDNAS consistently achieves superior Pareto efficiency, improving accuracy by up to 15\% under non-IID conditions, reducing client CPU usage by approximately 28\%, and decreasing communication overhead by up to 44 times while maintaining lightweight logit-based communication.

</details>


### [270] [Fast and Stable Triangular Inversion for Delta-Rule Linear Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21325)
*Aleksandros Sobczyk,Gioele Gottardo,Christos K. Matzoros,Mirko De Vita,Filip Skogh,Anastasios Zouzias,Jiawei Zhuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文解决了线性注意力模型中Delta规则三角矩阵计算的瓶颈，通过优化求逆算法，在保持精度的前提下在NPU上实现了显著的性能加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性注意力模型（如Qwen3.5/3.6, RWKV-7）中基于Delta规则的三角矩阵求逆操作是性能瓶颈，且由于对数值误差高度敏感，不当实现会导致模型精度大幅下降。当前缺乏针对现代硬件优化且具备数值稳定性的通用高效实现。

Method: 系统地分析并评估了多种直接法与迭代法三角矩阵求逆算法；重点研究了适合现代硬件并行计算（矩阵乘法）的实现方案，并针对低精度浮点数表示环境下的数值稳定性与计算复杂性进行了rigorous实验验证。

Result: 通过优化算法，在NPU上对比SGLang的最优实现获得了高达4.3倍的加速比。实验证明该方法在各种实际场景及低精度格式下能够保持与原始实现一致的端到端模型准确率。

Conclusion: 研究确立了针对线性注意力中Delta规则的高效、数值稳定的三角矩阵求逆算法选择方案。通过算法优化，在保持模型精度的前提下，在NPU上实现了高达4.3倍的加速，显著提升了长上下文大模型的推理效率。

Abstract: Linear attention has emerged as a cornerstone for efficient long-context architectures, as evidenced by its integration into state-of-the-art open-source models including Qwen3.5/3.6, Kimi Linear, and RWKV-7. Models that incorporate linear attention layers with the so-called Delta-Rule involve the inversion of triangular matrices as a core sub-routine. This operation often forms a performance bottleneck, and, due to its high-sensitivity to numerical errors, it can significantly deteriorate end-to-end model accuracy if it is not carefully implemented. This work provides a systematic analysis of both direct and iterative triangular inversion algorithms, targeting methods that are rich in matrix products, and, therefore, have the potential to efficiently utilize modern hardware. To that end, our analysis covers a broad spectrum of mathematical and practical aspects, with a heavy focus on numerical stability, computational complexity, and, ultimately, hardware efficiency and practical considerations. We provide a rigorous experimental evaluation to verify these properties in practical scenarios, and in low-precision floating-point representations, highlighting the strengths and limitations of each method. Performance benchmarks on NPUs reveal up to $4.3\times$ speed-up against the state-of-the-art implementations of SGLang for triangular matrix inversion, leading to significant performance improvements on the entire layer level, while maintaining full end-to-end model accuracy.

</details>


### [271] [Adaptive Signal Resuscitation: Channel-wise Post-Pruning Repair for Sparse Vision Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21426)
*Qishi Zhan,Ziheng Chen,Minxuan Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对高稀疏环境下一次性修剪导致的精度剧降问题，本文提出 ASR 方法，通过通道级方差匹配与收缩规则，在无需再训练的情况下有效修复受损模型，在大模型稀疏化任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在极高稀疏度下，一次性量级修剪会导致严重的精度下降。这是因为现有的层级修复方法颗粒度过粗，无法处理同一层内不同通道受损程度不一致的问题，导致修复时过度放大受损严重的通道。

Method: 提出了自适应信号复苏（Adaptive Signal Resuscitation, ASR）：一种训练无关的通道级修复方法。它为每个输出通道估算方差匹配校正，并应用数据驱动的收缩规则来抑制不可靠的校正。该方法位于批量归一化（BatchNorm）重校准之前。

Result: 在 CIFAR-10 上的 ResNet-50（90% 稀疏度）测试中，ASR 将 Top-1 精度提升至 55.6%，显著优于层级修复（41.0%）和仅使用 BatchNorm 重校准（28.0%）。该方法适用于多种架构和稀疏设置。

Conclusion: ASR 是一种有效的训练后修剪修复方法，通过将修复粒度与受损粒度（通道级）相匹配，显著提升了高稀疏度下的模型精度。有效的收缩规则是避免过修复的关键。

Abstract: One-shot magnitude pruning can cause severe accuracy collapse in the high-sparsity regime, even when the pruning mask preserves the largest weights. We argue that this failure reflects a granularity mismatch in post-pruning repair. Under global magnitude pruning, nearly collapsed channels can coexist with channels that retain informative activation variance within the same layer. Existing layer-wise activation repair methods apply a single correction to the whole layer, and can therefore over-amplify damaged channels while trying to restore the layer-level signal. We propose Adaptive Signal Resuscitation (ASR), a training-free channel-wise repair method that matches the granularity of repair to the granularity of damage. ASR estimates a variance-matching correction for each output channel and stabilizes it with a data-driven shrinkage rule, suppressing unreliable corrections for channels with weak post-pruning signal while preserving corrections for healthier channels. Applied before BatchNorm recalibration, ASR requires only forward passes on a small calibration set and no retraining. Across three datasets, four convolutional architectures, and both unstructured and structured sparsity settings, ASR generally improves over layer-wise repair, with the clearest gains in high-sparsity regimes. On ResNet-50 at 90% sparsity, ASR recovers 55.6% top-1 accuracy on CIFAR-10, compared with 41.0% for layer-wise repair and 28.0% for BatchNorm-only recalibration. Ablations show that naive channel-wise variance matching is insufficient, and that shrinkage stabilizes post-pruning repair.

</details>


### [272] [A Machine Learning Framework for Weighted Least Squares GNSS Positioning based on Activation Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21461)
*Pin-Hsun Lee,Harry Leib*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对城市环境GNSS定位精度下降问题，该论文提出了一种结合集成学习与激活函数的动态加权最小二乘定位框架，有效过滤了低质量信号，实现了高精度定位，并验证了模型的跨地域适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市峡谷环境中普遍存在的遮挡、非视距（NLOS）和多径效应会导致GNSS定位精度大幅下降，而盲目增加卫星数量可能引入劣化信号，加重定位误差。因此，需要一种能够实时准确识别高劣质信号并自动优化定位权重的机制。

Method: 1. 利用信号质量指标作为特征，采用集成学习算法对GNSS信号进行质量评估打分。2. 引入激活函数（特别是Sigmoid函数）将模型打分转化为WLS算法的加权系数，以动态调整信号权重。3. 使用香港和东京的实测数据进行验证。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在单星座和多星座场景下均显著降低了定位误差；相较于传统方法，引入Sigmoid激活函数的框架在不同测试区域均取得了最优的提升效果，且模型在不同城市间的迁移表现稳健。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的基于激活函数的加权最小二乘（WLS）定位框架能够有效识别并抑制城市环境中的伪距误差，不仅在不同卫星星座下显著提升了定位精度，还表现出良好的跨区域迁移能力。其中，Sigmoid函数在多种环境下的性能表现最为优异。

Abstract: Global Navigation Satellite Systems (GNSS) are widely used to provide position, velocity, and timing (PVT) information for various applications, including transportation, location-based communication services, and intelligent agriculture. In urban canyons, high-rise buildings and narrow streets can cause signal obstruction, non-line-of-sight (NLOS) reception, and multipath effects that introduce errors in GNSS pseudorange measurements. Although multi-constellations GNSS effectively increase the number of available satellites, the inclusion of degraded signals can lead to severe positioning errors. This study proposes a machine learning framework for the weighted least squares (WLS) algorithm incorporating activation functions to enhance positioning accuracy. Several signal quality indicators are employed as training features for ensemble learning algorithms to identify poor quality signals by providing quality scores. Then, activation functions are employed to transform the machine learning predicted scores to appropriate weights for WLS positioning. To evaluate the performance of our approach, experiments are conducted using real-world datasets from Hong Kong and Tokyo urban areas. Comparative analysis of activation functions reveals that sigmoid functions consistently yield the greatest improvements with different machine learning algorithms and GNSS constellation configurations. The proposed algorithm demonstrates substantial reductions in positioning errors for both single- and multiconstellation scenarios. Furthermore, our results indicate that the proposed algorithm exhibits strong geographical transferability. The proposed algorithm maintains comparable level of performance when trained on data from other regions with similar levels of urbanization.

</details>


### [273] [Is Fixing Schema Graphs Necessary? Full-Resolution Graph Structure Learning for Relational Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21475)
*Yi Huang,Qingyun Sun,Jia Li,Xingcheng Fu,Jianxin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FROG 是一种用于关系深度学习（RDL）的结构优化框架，通过将表角色建模为可学习对象和引入函数依赖约束，实现了关系数据库图中结构学习与 GNN 预测的联合优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的关系深度学习（RDL）方法在处理关系数据库时多采用“全分辨率”原则构建静态图，限制了模型捕捉复杂关系语义的灵活性，且缺乏对图结构本身的可优化性。

Method: 提出 FROG 框架，1) 将关系结构学习建模为可学习的表角色建模问题（使表既可作为节点也可作为边参与消息传递）；2) 设计角色驱动的消息传递机制以捕获关系语义；3) 引入函数依赖约束以确保表级和实体级表示的语义一致性。

Result: 大量实验表明，FROG 在关系预测任务上的性能超越了现有主流方法，并验证了通过学习表角色有助于提升下游任务的表现及提供更好的可解释性。

Conclusion: FROG 框架通过引入可学习的表角色建模和函数依赖约束，成功实现了关系数据库任务中图结构与 GNN 表示的联合优化，优于现有的固定图结构方法，并揭示了不同表角色对下游预测任务的影响。

Abstract: Relational prediction tasks are fundamental in many real-world applications, where data are naturally stored in relational databases (RDBs). Relational Deep Learning (RDL) addresses this problem by modeling RDBs as graphs and applying graph neural networks (GNNs) for end-to-end learning. However, the full-resolution property is commonly adopted as a design principle in graph construction for RDBs to preserve relational semantics, which leads most existing methods to rely on fixed graph structures. In this paper, we propose FROG, a Full-Resolution and Optimizable Graph Structure Learning} framework for RDL that formulates relational structure learning as a learnable table role modeling problem, allowing tables to contribute as nodes and edges in message passing. We further design role-driven message passing mechanisms to capture relational semantics, enabling joint optimization of graph structure and GNN representations. To ensure semantic consistency, we introduce functional dependency constraints that regularize representations across table and entity levels. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method outperforms existing approaches and reveal how table roles impact downstream tasks, offering new insights into graph construction for RDL

</details>


### [274] [Equilibrium Reasoners: Learning Attractors Enables Scalable Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21488)
*Benhao Huang,Zhengyang Geng,Zico Kolter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EqR框架，通过学习潜在的动力系统吸引子来驱动推理，证明了测试时迭代计算（深度与广度）是提升模型解决复杂推理问题能力的关键，并提供了有效的机制解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有迭代推理模型虽然表现出强大的性能，但其背后的机制（如为何能超越记忆模式进行泛化）仍不明确。研究旨在探究通用的推理是否源于学习“任务条件下的吸引子”动力系统。

Method: 提出“均衡推理器”（Equilibrium Reasoners, EqR），通过迭代更新潜在状态（Latent State）来逼近解空间的稳定不动点。该方法通过增加迭代深度（Depth）和聚合多种初始化的随机轨迹（Breadth）来扩展测试时计算。

Result: EqR在Sudoku-Extreme等任务上表现显著，通过高达40,000层的等效计算深度，将前馈模型的准确率从2.6%提升至超过99%。研究进一步揭示了测试时计算的增益与模型收敛至解对齐吸引子的程度高度相关。

Conclusion: EqR通过学习任务条件的吸引子（Attractors）来实现可扩展的推理。这种吸引子视角不仅解释了迭代模型的泛化机制，还为理解如何提升测试时计算效率提供了有力的理论支撑。

Abstract: Scaling test-time compute by iteratively updating a latent state has emerged as a powerful paradigm for reasoning. Yet the internal mechanisms that enable these iterative models to generalize beyond memorized patterns remain unclear. We hypothesize that generalizable reasoning arises from learning task-conditioned attractors: latent dynamical systems whose stable fixed points correspond to valid solutions.
  We formalize this process through Equilibrium Reasoners (EqR), which enable test-time scaling without external verifiers or task-specific priors. EqR scales internal dynamics along two axes: depth, by running more iterations, and breadth, by aggregating stochastic trajectories from multiple initializations. Empirically, gains from test-time scaling are tightly coupled with stronger convergence toward solution-aligned attractors.
  This attractor perspective allows neural networks to adaptively allocate test-time compute based on task difficulty. While simple cases converge within 1 to 5 iteration steps, harder cases benefit from massive test-time scaling. By unrolling up to the equivalent of 40,000 layers, scalable latent reasoning boosts accuracy from 2.6% for feedforward models to over 99% on Sudoku-Extreme. These results suggest that learned attractor landscapes provide a useful mechanistic lens for understanding scalable reasoning in iterative latent models.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [275] [Efficient Table QA via TableGrid Navigation and Progressive Inference Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20254)
*Amritansh Maurya,Navjot Singh,Mohammed Javed,Omar Moured*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文针对大模型在表格问答中推理不透明的问题，提出了无需训练的TGN和PIP提示框架，通过显式的行列导航和渐进式逻辑约束，显著提升了模型处理表格数据的准确性与可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型（LLMs）在表格问答（TQA）领域面临挑战，主要原因在于缺乏对表格导航和推理过程的可验证控制，单纯的微调或专项训练效果有限。

Method: 提出两种结构化提示框架：1. TableGrid Navigation (TGN)：通过三模块循环迭代导航行列以定位证据并细化答案；2. Progressive Inference Prompting (PIP)：强化列识别机制，对行选择实施显式的渐进式约束。

Result: 在TableBench数据集上，TGN超过最强基线3.8个百分点；在FeTaQa数据集上，PIP的表现超越了ReAct和思维链（CoT）达到SOTA。此外，该方法可辅助小模型实现同等高质量的推理表现。

Conclusion: 本文提出的TGN和PIP框架证明了无需训练的提示工程在处理复杂表格问答任务中具有显著竞争力，同时该方法还可作为小模型微调的监督范式，实现了性能与计算效率的平衡。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown promising results on NLP tasks, however, their performance on tabular data still needs research attention, because Table Question-Answering (TQA) requires precise cell retrieval and multi-step structured reasoning. Existing work improves TQA either by fine-tuning or training LLMs on task-specific tabular data, but often lacks verifiable control over how the model navigates tables and derives answers. In this work, we propose a training-free TQA approach with two structured prompting frameworks: TableGrid Navigation (TGN), which iteratively navigates rows and columns via a three-module loop to locate evidence and refine answers, and Progressive Inference Prompting (PIP), which enforces columns identification for explicit progressive row selection constraint according to the query. We evaluate 17 LLMs against 6 baselines on TableBench and FeTaQa dataset. On TableBench, TGN improves over the strongest baseline by 3.8 points, and on FeTaQa, PIP achieves SOTA performance over ReAct and Chain-of-Thought. Beyond inference-time gains, PIP and TGN can also serve as supervision templates to fine-tune small models, narrowing the performance gap to much larger architectures in resource-constrained settings, offering versatile and cost-efficient solution for TQA.

</details>


### [276] [Layer-wise Token Compression for Efficient Document Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20683)
*Shengyao Zhuang,zhichao Xu,Ivano Lauriola*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了Layer-wise Token Compression (LTC) 技术，通过在中间层进行自适应标记池化，有效解决了交叉编码重排序器的性能瓶颈，并在提升推理速度的同时保持了排名质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer交叉编码重排序器存在计算开销大的问题；现有的嵌入层标记压缩在交叉编码器中效果不佳，因此需要更有效的压缩策略。

Method: 提出层级标记压缩（Layer-wise Token Compression, LTC），通过在Transformer中间层实施自适应标记池化（Adaptive Token Pooling）来减少计算负担。

Result: 在MS MARCO数据集上，段落重排序吞吐量（QPS）提升高达25%，文档重排序提升高达116%；在列表式LLM重排序器中同样有效，且在跨任务场景下表现出更好的泛化性能。

Conclusion: LTC在保持重排序质量的前提下，显著提高了推理速度；此外，压缩作为正则化手段，有助于提升模型在长文档任务上的泛化能力。

Abstract: Transformer-based document cross-encoder rerankers are a central component of modern information retrieval systems. Despite their success, these models suffer from high computational costs due to processing long query-document sequences at inference time. A known approach to improve efficiency is token compression, which consists of aggregating groups of tokens together in the initial embedding layer, reducing the effective number of tokens, and making the computation faster. While token compression has proven to be successful for bi-encoder retrievers, we empirically observed that this approach may be ineffective for cross-encoder rerankers. In this paper, we propose Layer-wise Token Compression (LTC), which applies adaptive token pooling at intermediate transformer layers. Through extensive ablation studies on MS MARCO passage and document ranking tasks, we demonstrate that compression at middle layers preserves ranking quality while increasing inference QPS by up to 25% for passage ranking and up to 116% for document ranking. We also extend LTC to listwise LLM rerankers and show that the same approach can be easily applied to long-context listwise reranking, where the QPS improvements are even greater. More surprisingly, when applying rerankers trained on short passages to long-document ranking tasks, models trained with compression outperform their uncompressed counterparts, suggesting that compression may act as a beneficial regularizer that encourages length-invariant representations.

</details>


### [277] [CALMem : Application-Layer Dual Memory for Conversational AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20724)
*Rajendra Narayan Jena,Rajan Padmanabhan,Sankar Arumugam*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: CALMem 是一种无需修改模型、插件式应用层记忆架构，通过结合情景与语义记忆以及动态内存注入（MOIM），解决了 LLM 对话中上下文窗口限制导致的历史信息丢失问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 存在固定上下文窗口限制，且现有的扩展方案（如超大窗口、RAG、MemGPT 等）存在需要修改底层模型、绑定供应商或无法解决会话内压缩导致的历史信息丢失等问题。

Method: 提出了 CALMem 架构，包含两个子系统：1) 基于滑动窗口向量嵌入的“情景记忆（Episodic Memory）”；2) 可读写的结构化“语义记忆（Semantic Memory）”。核心技术为 MOIM（Message of Injected Memory）适配机制，根据当前上下文压力动态调整检索信息的深度，并支持跨会话及会话内被压缩内容的检索。

Result: 实现了基于 Rust 的生产级应用层解决方案；成功实现了对 LLM 上下文的“虚拟无限”扩展；支持对已压缩历史记录的检索；具备无损降级回原始行为的能力，且在实际部署中表现出良好的可控性。

Conclusion: CALMem 实现了无需模型修改的、与提供商无关的、具备长短期结合的增强记忆架构，有效解决了 LLM 在长对话中的上下文受限及信息丢失问题，且具有良好的灵活性和可移植性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) operate within fixed context windows that fundamentally limit conversational continuity. When context fills, compaction discards history irreversibly; when sessions end, all memory resets to zero. Existing solutions-larger context windows, retrieval-augmented generation for knowledge bases, and memory-augmented architectures such as MemGPT-either require model modification, impose provider lock-in, or do not address the compaction continuity problem. We present CALMem (Conversational Application-Layer Memory), an application-layer dual memory architecture that gives LLM-based conversational assistants virtually unbounded effective context without any modification to the underlying model. CALMem combines two complementary memory subsystems: an episodic memory layer built on sliding-window vector embeddings of conversation history, and a semantic memory layer of agent-writable structured facts. A token-budget-adaptive injection mechanism, called the MOIM (Message of Injected Memory), automatically retrieves and injects relevant past context each turn, scaling injection depth inversely with context pressure. A key contribution is intra-session retrieval: compacted away turns from the current session remain searchable, closing a gap unaddressed by prior work. The system is implemented as a pure application layer in a production Rust codebase, is provider-agnostic, and degrades to original LLM behaviour with zero overhead when disabled. We describe the architecture, design decisions, and performance characteristics, and analyse the trade-offs that guided each implementation choice.

</details>


### [278] [MemConflict: Evaluating Long-Term Memory Systems Under Memory Conflicts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20926)
*Zhen Tao,Jinxiang Zhao,Peng Liu,Dinghao Xi,Yanfang Chen,Wei Xu,Zhiyu Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出了 MemConflict 框架，用于深度诊断 LLM 长短期记忆系统在处理有时效性、事实性及上下文冲突信息时的检索与推理能力，填补了现有评估在冲突处理机制分析上的缺失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估多关注结果或时序更新，缺乏对 LLM 记忆系统在存在冲突信息（如时间冲突、事实冲突、语境冲突）时如何进行检索、排序及决策的深度诊断，难以保证长短期记忆的可靠性。

Method: 1. 提出了 MemConflict 诊断框架，将记忆有效性定义为“查询条件下的使用适应性问题”。2. 形式化了动态、静态和条件冲突（涉及时间、事实和语境）。3. 构建了基于结构化用户画像的受控多轮对话基准测试，引入跨会话冲突和语义干扰项。4. 支持黑盒（回答评估）与白盒（检索与排序过程）双重分析。

Result: 对六个代表性长短期记忆系统进行的实验表明：系统性能在不同冲突类型上表现不均匀；回答正确率与检索排序表现往往不一致；系统在面对更长的历史记录、更多干扰项、隐式查询以及较大的冲突距离时，性能显著下降。主要失效原因在于检索遗漏或检索后有效信息利用不足。

Conclusion: MemConflict 提供了一个评估长短期记忆系统在面对多重冲突信息时可靠性的关键框架。研究表明，现有的 LLM 记忆系统在面对时效性、事实性和上下文冲突时表现不均衡，且最终回答准确率与检索排序质量之间存在偏差。该工具通过诊断检索与推理阶段的失效，为提升长久记忆治理提供了有力支持。

Abstract: Long-term memory systems enable conversational agents based on large language models (LLMs) to retain, retrieve, and apply user-specific information across multi-session interactions. However, existing evaluations mainly assess outcome-level performance or temporal updating, providing limited insight into how systems retrieve and rank temporally valid, factually correct, and contextually applicable memory evidence under conflicting alternatives. To address this gap, we propose MemConflict, a diagnostic framework that treats memory validity as a query-conditioned fitness-for-use problem. MemConflict formalizes dynamic, static, and conditional conflicts over temporal validity, factual correctness, and contextual applicability. It simulates controlled long-horizon histories from structured user profiles, introduces cross-session conflicts, and injects semantically similar distractors to create competition among memory candidates. The resulting multi-session dialogue benchmark supports black-box evaluation of final answers and white-box analysis of supporting-memory retrieval and ranking. Experiments on six representative long-term memory systems show uneven strengths across conflict types, with answer correctness often diverging from memory retrieval and ranking. Sensitivity analyses reveal that longer histories, distractors, implicit queries, and larger conflict distances degrade performance. Diagnostics show failures from missing supporting memories and ineffective use of retrieved memories. Collectively, MemConflict advances principled long-term memory governance through retrieval-aware, conflict-aware reliability assessment.

</details>


### [279] [SG-LegalCite: A Principle-Augmented Benchmark for Legal Citation Retrieval in Singapore Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21057)
*Shannon Lee Yueh Ern,Kaidong Feng,Yingpeng Du,Chloe Lee En Jia,Zhu Sun*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对法律引文检索中因缺乏核心原则导致的相关性偏差，本文提出了融合“事实+显式法律原则”的检索范式，并发布了SG-LegalCite数据集，证实了引入明确法律原则能有效提升判例检索的准确度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的法律引文检索基准往往忽略或隐含了核心法律原则，导致模型容易检索出“事实相似但判例/教义无关”的案例，无法满足法律实务中对法律原则论证的需求。

Method: 提出了一种结合“案件事实”与“显式法律原则”进行检索的新范式；构建了包含100,890对法律原则与案例的SG-LegalCite数据集；通过11种基线模型对比评估该新方法的有效性。

Result: 实验结果表明，在检索查询中显式融入法律原则能显著提升引文检索的效果，验证了该范式的优越性和判别能力。

Conclusion: 显式法律原则是提升法律引文检索准确性的关键判别信号，提出的基于事实与原则融合的检索范式优于仅依赖事实或上下文的模型。

Abstract: Legal citation in common-law systems depends not only on factual similarity, but also on the legal principle for which a precedent is invoked. However, existing benchmarks for legal citation retrieval use case facts, citation context, or full judgments as inputs, where the governing legal principle is often missing or only implicitly expressed and entangled with broader context. As a result, models may retrieve precedents that are factually similar yet doctrinally irrelevant. This limitation is particularly consequential in Singapore, where the legal system has evolved independently: only domestic precedents are binding, while foreign authorities serve merely as persuasive references. Thus, we propose a new retrieval paradigm that ranks cited cases based on queries integrating case facts and explicit legal principles, inspired by real-world legal reasoning workflows. To support this paradigm, we introduce SG-LegalCite, a dataset of 100,890 case-principle pairs extracted from 8,523 Singapore Supreme Court judgments spanning from 2000 to 2025. Experiments across 11 baselines demonstrate the effectiveness of our principle-augmented retrieval paradigm, showing that explicit legal principles provide strong discriminative signals for legal citation retrieval.

</details>
